Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.11

Chapter 1001
Qiu Ren's expression froze when he heard the soldier's words.
Qiu Ren was an ambitious and ruthless person. He had done well in the Lower Realm. Even after he ascended, he was still like a fish in water.
In his creed, if someone offended him, there was no way they could live!
He had lost all his face just now. On his way back, he was still planning how to deal with Luo Zheng! Now, this "Elder Chen" was telling him not to touch him. What kind of logic was this?
"Why?!" Qiu Ren asked indignantly.
Soldier Huang smiled coldly. "No reason. I'm just warning you not to touch Luo Zheng. It's fine if you listen to my advice, but if you don't, it's up to you. That's all I have to say!"
Then, the armored soldier with the surname Chen added, "When Luo Zheng ascended, he only took a few breaths to adapt to the pressure of the Primordial Qi."
After saying that, the two soldiers hurriedly left without looking back.
A complicated expression appeared on Qiu Ren's face as he gazed at the departing figures of the two armored soldiers. He could disregard the words of others, but since he was so familiar with these two people, even though their words sounded harsh, it was probably for his own good ….
Luo Zheng only took a few breaths to adapt to the Vitality pressure after leaving the Ascension Well? Qiu Ren's face revealed a trace of surprise.
When Qiu Ren had just ascended, his ability to adapt was extremely strong, but it still took him a full five days. This Luo Zheng had probably caught the eye of the Lingwu Holy Land …
Although most warriors born and raised in the Upper Realm had a prejudice against those who ascended from the Lower Realm, as a tenth-rank Holy Land, their thirst for talents was almost limitless.
Luo Zheng's performance after leaving the Ascension Well had already been reported by the soldiers.
The foundation of ascenders might not be as strong as warriors in the Upper Realm, but there were always some exceptions among them. Some outstanding people. No Holy Land would welcome such people!
Therefore, when Luo Zheng was mining, the Lingwu Holy Land had some soldiers secretly observe Luo Zheng.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng was extremely cautious in his actions. Two days ago, he had used the Roaring Command to set up divine patterns that isolated the outside world from prying eyes. Otherwise, there would have been a huge problem …
Now, Luo Zheng was sitting in the wilderness, cultivating with his eyes closed. After his Martial Heart had been sublimated, he felt a sense of urgency. There were still many things he needed to do, such as helping Xun recover her former glory and strength, helping Qinglong and the others, and finding Luo Yan …
And all of this required great strength to back it up.
The ascenders around Luo Zheng were also secretly sizing up Luo Zheng with surprised gazes. They sighed inwardly. This brat actually pretended to be crazy and played a fool with Qiu Ren. They wondered what his fate would be after tomorrow.
Unless he never left the settlement and never went mining, Qiu Ren would hunt him down as soon as he entered the mines.
But the next day, Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue continued to mine and returned safe and sound.
On the third day, when they returned from mining, there was still no movement from Qiu Ren …
On the fourth day, Qiu Ren appeared in the settlement. When he saw Luo Zheng, he actually put on a smile and greeted Luo Zheng!
Because of this, the cultivators in the mines immediately understood that Qiu Ren should have admitted defeat. As for the reason, they didn't know. Everyone had all sorts of guesses in their hearts.
Luo Zheng's mining efficiency was very high. Although the bottom layer of the mines was very dangerous, it wasn't a big problem for Luo Zheng. The most important thing was that he ran fast!
Moreover, Luo Zheng had already made plans. If he really couldn't escape, he still had a trump card, and that was to activate the "Gen Token" and escape into the immortal mansion. However, he hadn't used it even once in the past few days to dig out Zhen Yuanyu …
But Luo Zheng also couldn't dig out all of the Zhen Yuanyu. For example, one day, Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue found a Zhen Yuanyu plant with at least fifty to sixty pieces of Zhen Yuanyu condensed on it!
But around this Zhen Yuanyu plant, there were three level 12 Vicious Beasts guarding it. Under such circumstances, how could Luo Zheng court death? He could only take Mu Mingxue with him and leave first …
Time passed very quickly in the mineral vein.
Unknowingly, the number of Zhen Yuanyu registered by Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue had increased to eighty pieces. The two of them only needed to pay another twenty pieces of Zhen Yuanyu to leave the mines and join the Lingwu Holy Place.
Mu Mingxue was naturally very happy. After joining the Lingwu Holy Place, she could rely on her uncle.
However, Luo Zheng told Mu Mingxue that he planned to accumulate two hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu.
At this stage, Luo Zheng didn't want to join the Lingwu Holy Place. If he wanted to become an independent warrior, he had to pay two hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu …
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mu Mingxue's face revealed a strange expression. The Lingwu Holy Place was a tenth grade Holy Place. He had also ascended to this world. Why did he want to leave here and become an independent warrior?
Luo Zheng didn't explain too much to Mu Mingxue. He and Mu Mingxue were just temporary partners.
On the contrary, Mu Mingxue was curious. But since Luo Zheng didn't want to say, she didn't ask.
However, things often went against one's wishes. Several months later, when Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue finally accumulated one hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, the Lingwu Holy Place sent two people here.
When the other soldiers in the settlement saw the two people, they all looked very respectful. Obviously, the two blue-clothed soldiers were important figures in the Lingwu Holy Place.
"This subordinate respectfully welcomes Hall Master Shen and Hall Master He!"
As early as the day before, the soldiers guarding the mine had tidied up the entire settlement. At this time, the soldiers were standing in a neat row.
The area of the Great World was really too large. Even though the Lingwu Holy Place didn't occupy a lot of area and was ranked last among the three Holy Places of the Spiritual World, it was still unimaginably large …
The Lingwu Holy Place had thirty-six halls, and each hall was in charge of a thousand big cities. In other words, there were a total of thirty-six thousand big cities in the Lingwu Holy Place!
The two people in front of them were the two Hall Masters of the thirty-six halls!
Most of the soldiers in charge of the ascenders were warriors in the Divine Sea Realm. If they couldn't make great progress in their cultivation in this lifetime, their final goal was to fight for the position of City Lord. Most of them wouldn't succeed. After all, in a big city of a tenth-rank Holy Place, there were many fifth-rank and even sixth-rank clans under its jurisdiction. There were even all kinds of "small Holy Places" …
These thirty-six Palace Lords were indeed rare and important figures in the Spirit Martial Holy Lands!
In addition to the leaders and the core figures of the Holy Place, there were many elders and the thirty-six Hall Masters!
How could these armored soldiers not be respectful to such a character who had graced this remote mine with his presence?
The two Hall Masters responded indifferently. The leader of the soldiers asked again, "What orders do the two Hall Masters have for us? As long as it's within this subordinate's power … "
Before the soldier finished speaking, Hall Master Shen said indifferently, "In your mine, there's an ascender called Luo Zheng, right?"
When the armored soldier heard this, he hurriedly nodded his head. "Yes, Luo Zheng is indeed in our mine!"
"Where is he?" Hall Master Shen asked again.
The leader of the soldiers looked around. Naturally, he couldn't keep an eye on Luo Zheng all day long. How could he know where Luo Zheng had run off to?
The other soldier immediately responded, "Hall Master Shen, Luo Zheng has already entered the mine today."
Hall Master Shen nodded indifferently, "Then we'll wait for him here."
The two Hall Masters were actually waiting for Luo Zheng here. The hearts of the soldiers suddenly jumped. They couldn't help but wonder what kind of background Luo Zheng had.
Previously, they had received a secret order from their superiors to secretly observe Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng's performance at the Ascension Well was very outstanding. This wasn't strange. However, just based on this performance, it was enough to attract the attention of the two Hall Masters? The soldiers found it hard to understand. At the same time, they were secretly envious of Luo Zheng. Although these soldiers were very superior in front of ascenders, many ascenders would achieve much more than these soldiers in the future …
The leader of the armored soldiers seemed to have guessed the purpose of the two hall masters' visit. He opened his mouth and said, "Hall Master Shen, Hall Master He, since the two of you are here for Luo Zheng, this subordinate has something to say!"
Hall Master Shen said indifferently, "Speak!"
"Luo Zheng has already dug up a hundred True Elemental Jades. Originally, he could have left the lode, but he still entered the lode. I guess he wants to gather two hundred True Elemental Jades. He wants to gain freedom and become an independent warrior!" The armored soldier said.
Hall Master Shen didn't say anything. Hall Master He chuckled, "Independent martialist? Ha ha, I'm afraid it's not up to him. "

Chapter 1002
The soldiers had volunteered to enter the mine to find Luo Zheng, but they were rejected by the two Temple Masters.
The two Temple Masters were very patient.
This deepened the soldiers' speculations. What was Luo Zheng's background that made the two Temple Masters wait for him in person?
Luo Zheng waited from noon until dusk before he retreated from the mine.
After Mu Mingxue had reached 100 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, she chose to leave the mine and seek refuge with her uncle. They had only left a Voice-transmitting Token to each other …
However, leaving a Voice-transmitting Token in the Great World was probably a joke.
The Voice-transmitting Token was a means of communication in the Lower Realm. The range of transmission was only sixty to seventy thousand miles at most, but the territory occupied by the Lingwu Holy Place was tens of thousands of times larger than this. In most cases, the Voice-transmitting Token was useless! Unless Luo Zheng was within sixty to seventy thousand miles of Mu Mingxue!
Ever since Luo Zheng jumped out of the spiritual dilemma, Luo Zheng had been clear about his goal. Luo Zheng had always had a long-term goal, but he had just entered the Upper Realm and was still unfamiliar with the place. Luo Zheng naturally had to rely on himself to find out.
During this period of exploration, Luo Zheng prioritized waking up Xun. She had been sleeping for too long and showed no signs of waking up until now. Luo Zheng planned to accumulate 200 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu before looking for a treasure to restore Xun's soul.
Today's luck was good. He had encountered the Hydra again and snatched seven pieces of Zhen Yuanyu from it. Luo Zheng had also discovered this secret. If he wanted to find Zhen Yuanyu, he had to follow the Ominous Beasts. These Ominous Beasts were entrenched in the mine all day long to absorb the Qi of Heaven and Earth in the Zhen Yuanyu.
If it weren't for the fact that these Ominous Beasts were too powerful, Luo Zheng really wanted to leave a mark on them to save himself the trouble of looking for them every time …
However, as soon as Luo Zheng returned to the settlement, he found that the atmosphere in the settlement was a bit off.
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed as he glanced at the crowd …
"These two people are quite powerful!"
So far, Luo Zheng had seen a lot of unimaginably powerful people!
Needless to say, the master he worshiped in the Immortal Mansion was just an afterimage left behind by his master. He couldn't estimate his strength, but Luo Zheng only knew that he was an existence that surpassed the universe.
Next was the young man who had taken his sister away from the Void Spirit Sect. That guy's strength was also unimaginable. With Luo Zheng's current vision, he still couldn't judge who was stronger between him and his Master.
Then there was the old man from the Demi-Immortal Tribe whom he had met during the dual cultivation ceremony with Ning Yudie. According to Luo Zheng's speculations, the old man was most likely a world overlord.
As for the two people in front of him, Luo Zheng felt that they were very strong, but they should be below the world overlord level …
No matter who they were, Luo Zheng didn't want to provoke them. After he landed, he planned to find the armored soldier to register the Zhen Yuanyu. However, looking at the armored soldiers standing in a row, it seemed that no one was coming to register. Luo Zheng didn't know who to give the Zhen Yuanyu to.
At this moment, Hall Master He walked towards Luo Zheng and smiled faintly, "You're Luo Zheng?"
As soon as he said this, Luo Zheng instantly understood that he was most likely coming for him …
No matter what, he couldn't avoid it. He nodded, "Yes, I'm Luo Zheng."
Hall Master He said directly, "Congratulations, you've completed your service and become a member of the Spirit Martial Holy Land!"
As soon as he said this, the armored soldiers looked at each other. Just now, the leader of the armored soldiers had already warned the two Hall Masters that Luo Zheng intended to become an independent warrior.
Luo Zheng frowned, then smiled faintly and said, "Sir, is there a mistake? I don't intend to join the Spirit Martial Holy Land. "
"That's not up to you. This is an order from the Holy City. We'll take you directly into the Holy City," Hall Master Shen said from the side.
Hearing Hall Master Shen's words, the jaws of the armored soldiers almost fell to the ground!
The Spirit Martial Holy Land's territory was really too big. Generally, warriors who joined the Spirit Martial Holy Land could only do some job or position in the 36 thousand cities!
Just like these armored soldiers, although they were warriors in the Divine Sea Realm, their cultivation was not weak even in the Upper Realm. However, being a city lord in these 36,000 cities was already a very good job.
The truly outstanding warriors had always set their sights on the 36 palaces!
If they were able to join the 36 palaces, for many warriors, it would be like smoke rising from their ancestors' graves.
However, the difficulty of joining the 36 palaces was unimaginable. For the majority of warriors, it was just an unattainable dream.
However, the 36 palaces weren't the core of the Spirit Martial Holy Land. The core of the Spirit Martial Holy Land was the Holy City!
The Holy City was the most important place in the entire Spirit Martial Holy Land. It was also the capital that allowed the Spirit Martial Holy Land to compete with the other two Holy Lands of the Spirit Martial Holy Land!
Those who entered the Holy City didn't care about cultivation, only talent. Because as long as you had talent, if the Holy City wanted to give you the cultivation of the Spirit Sea Realm, it was a very simple matter.
Generally speaking, warriors who were chosen to enter the Holy City were usually below the Spirit Sea Realm. As for warriors above the Spirit Sea Realm, unless they were especially outstanding, the Holy City wouldn't take them in.
There was only one reason, and that was the Life and Death Realm tribulations!
After warriors broke through the Life and Death Realm, they would go through minor tribulations.
Many warriors with average cultivation would only go through five or six minor tribulations to open the Spirit Sea. Although they could enter the Spirit Sea Realm early, it wasn't a good thing. The less tribulations one went through in the Life and Death Realm, the less potential one had in the future!
Just like most of the ascenders, they were eager to enter the Spirit Sea Realm. These Spirit Sea Realm warriors weren't worthless. After all, their strength was there, but it would be difficult for them to achieve much in the future!
Warriors who could enter the Holy City often went through eight minor tribulations, and some even went through nine minor tribulations!
Therefore, there was a saying in the Upper Realm called the Nine Tribulations Realm …
It meant that if one could go through nine minor tribulations, there was a considerable possibility of becoming a Realm Lord!
Luo Zheng didn't know about these things, but he had swallowed the "Calamity Flower" in the Kingdom of God. According to Qinglong, this thing only existed in the Lower Realm. After he accidentally swallowed it, he was bound to go through nine minor tribulations.
At this moment, the armored soldiers were also dumbfounded.
They originally thought that Luo Zheng had caught the eye of the two Palace Lords and was qualified to enter the 36 Palaces. They didn't expect the two Palace Lords to directly bring Luo Zheng to the Holy City …
The soldiers surnamed Chen and Huang also felt a sense of lingering fear.
Fortunately, the two of them reminded Qiu Ren in time to not cause trouble for Luo Zheng. If they hadn't stopped him at that time, if Qiu Ren really did something bad, the two of them wouldn't be able to bear the consequences.
Under this lingering fear, the two of them made up their minds. They couldn't let Qiu Ren do anything rash. Although they had received many benefits from Qiu Ren, if Qiu Ren offended an extraordinary ascender, they would be the ones to suffer in the future!
Under the envious gazes of the armored soldiers, Luo Zheng suddenly asked, "Holy City? Where is that? "
These words almost made the armored soldiers fall down. However, after thinking about it, this guy had ascended. It was normal that he didn't know about the Holy City.
Palace Lord He said, "It's the core of our Spiritual Martial Holy Land. If the entire Spiritual Martial Holy Land is a big Holy Land, then the Holy City is the Holy Land of the Holy Lands!"
This metaphor was very vivid. Luo Zheng immediately understood, but he still said, "What if I insist on refusing?"
Hearing that Luo Zheng wanted to refuse, the armored soldiers' hearts were bleeding. If it were them, as long as they could enter the Holy City, they would give up everything except their lives.
The two Palace Lords didn't show any surprise on their faces. After all, they were people who had seen the world. Of course, they knew that there were many tenth rank Holy Lands in this world. Some peerless geniuses might not be interested in the Spiritual Martial Holy Land.
Palace Lord He just said faintly, "With your current strength, you don't have the qualifications to refuse. Moreover, we were only asked to bring you to the Holy City."
Since these words were said, Luo Zheng naturally had nothing to say. They were all smart people. With his current strength, there was no room for resistance.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1003
Luo Zheng was also very curious. There were so many ascenders, so why did they choose him?
In fact, what Luo Zheng didn't know was that he wasn't the only one who was curious. The soldiers, the other ascenders, and the two Temple Masters were also very curious.
Even if this boy was talented and could adapt to the pressure of the World Qi in a very short time, it wasn't a big deal.
The Great World was so big, and there were countless strange people in the Lingwu Holy Land. How could he go to the Holy City with just this? They wouldn't believe it even if they were beaten to death!
Since Luo Zheng no longer refused, the two Temple Masters were relieved. Temple Master He and Temple Master Shen didn't know the depth of this matter, but the person who paid attention to this matter should have a considerable background. They didn't want to offend Luo Zheng.
Generally speaking, when leaving the lode, one would need to go through some handover procedures with the soldiers, but this was naturally unnecessary.
Not only that, the leader of the soldiers stood up at the appropriate time and said very seriously, "Luo Zheng is a special case to leave the lode. Logically speaking, he shouldn't pay these Zhen Yuanyu. These 100 Zhen Yuanyu naturally have to be returned in full!"
In fact, what kind of logic was this? It was just that this soldier was very clever. In front of the two Temple Masters, he naturally wanted to curry favor with Luo Zheng.
These 100 Zhen Yuanyu were still a considerable amount of wealth for Luo Zheng at this stage, so Luo Zheng naturally accepted it with a smile.
The two Temple Masters naturally didn't care about these 100 Zhen Yuanyu. Of course, they also knew that the soldier wanted to curry favor with them, so they just smiled faintly and directly took Luo Zheng away.
After Luo Zheng left, the lode immediately underwent a major reorganization. The ascender named Qiu Ren was the first person to bear the brunt of it. He was very unlucky. All the Zhen Yuanyu he had accumulated over the years was confiscated, and he had to pay 100 Zhen Yuanyu again before he could leave the lode.
As for the other ascenders, when they learned that Luo Zheng was taken away like this, they could only sigh secretly in addition to envy.
Along the way, Temple Master He used a very cordial tone and constantly chatted with Luo Zheng.
The two Temple Masters were very curious about Luo Zheng. They also wanted to know who brought him directly into the Holy City. They also wanted to inquire about the "big shot" behind Luo Zheng.
However, even Luo Zheng himself was a little confused about this kind of thing. How could he answer the two Temple Masters? In this huge Lingwu Holy Land, there was no big shot.....
After following the two Temple Masters into the Space Passage, Luo Zheng didn't know how far they had traveled. Even in the Ascension Passage, they flew for five days and five nights. Finally, they turned into another huge Space Passage and traveled for another three days. Only then did the two Temple Masters bring Luo Zheng out of the Space Passage.
When the light of the spatial tunnel dissipated, Luo Zheng discovered that he was standing on a ten thousand feet tall teleportation platform. When he lifted his head and looked around, what entered his eyes was a vast and boundless city. Luo Zheng was unable to see the borders of this city at a glance. Moreover, there were buildings that were ten thousand feet tall and were comparable to large mountains standing all around him!
He didn't know what material these buildings were made of, but they were all shining with a light blue luster. Therefore, when he looked at it, it felt like the city was carved out of crystal. It was very beautiful.
"Is this the Holy City of the Lingwu Holy Land?" Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh.
Based on Luo Zheng's understanding of the whole universe, the Lingwu Holy Land was not a particularly powerful force in the whole universe.
Previously, Luo Zheng had a rough understanding that the Human Race controlled a total of 10,000 Great Worlds. This was just one of the 10,000 Great Worlds. Moreover, the Lingwu Holy Land was not the most powerful Holy Land in this Great World. It was only ranked third.
However, the Holy City of the Lingwu Holy Land was actually of such a scale. Luo Zheng naturally sighed. The vastness of this Great World seemed to be far beyond his imagination.
When the two Temple Masters heard Luo Zheng's sigh, there was a faint look of pride on their faces. After all, they were warriors from the Mortal World and had not seen the world. Temple Master He said, "Just the Holy City alone has nearly 100 billion creatures living in it!"
100 billion creatures … How big is the Holy City? "Luo Zheng asked again.
Temple Master He smiled and said, "If we use an image to make an analogy, it is about one-tenth of the area of a Mortal World."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was stunned again.
Just the Holy City alone was one-tenth of the area of a Great Thousand World. This was …
Luo Zheng finally understood why it took 10 days to reach the Holy City through the Teleportation Channel.
Cities like Apocalypse City and Cloud Sea City were already considered large cities in the Central Domain. Even the capital of the Divine Kingdom's continent was probably comparable to Apocalypse City.
The area of Apocalypse City was at most one-thousandth of the area of the Central Domain's continent!
However, in the Mortal World where Luo Zheng lived, 70% to 80% of the area was ocean. The area occupied by the entire Central Domain was only about 3% of the area of the Great Thousand World.
In other words, the area of the city in front of him was already three times larger than the area of the Central Domain.
Some of the mortals living in such a large city would probably be unable to leave it for the rest of their lives.
The facts were indeed as Luo Zheng had guessed.
Not everyone in the Mortal World was a warrior. Most of them were still ordinary humans who were unable to cultivate or had no desire to cultivate. Most of them were unable to leave the city. After all, the Holy City was really too large. Even if a warrior in the Divine Sea Realm didn't use the Teleportation Channel, he would still have to fly for several days to leave the city completely.
"Okay, let's continue our journey!" Temple Master He was very satisfied with the shock on Luo Zheng's face.
After leading Luo Zheng away from the Teleportation Channel, Luo Zheng discovered that there were many straight thin lines in the air. These thin lines were intertwined with each other, but their heights were different. Currently, many warriors were flying along these thin lines at an extremely fast speed.
Seeing that Luo Zheng had noticed these thin lines, Temple Master He continued, "There are too many warriors in the Holy City. If you fly too fast, you will collide with each other. Therefore, we used Divine Glyphs to arrange tens of thousands of thin lines that run through the entire Holy City. You see, the red line is the incoming warrior, and the blue line is the departing warrior. These thin lines are of different heights to prevent people from colliding with each other."
Luo Zheng finally understood the purpose of these thin lines.
Flying in the air could only be done after breaking through to the Divine Reflection Realm. However, in the Mortal World, although there were many Divine Reflection Realm warriors, it wasn't enough to make the entire sky overcrowded.
In the Holy City, the number of warriors in the Divine Reflection Realm and above was unknown. It was impossible to count. There were too many people flying in the air, so there must be a plan.
Therefore, these thin lines actually had the same function as the roads in the city.
Then, the two Temple Masters brought Luo Zheng to one of the thin lines and continued to move forward.
Just like Temple Master He said, all the warriors flying around this thin line were flying in the same direction. Although everyone's speed was different, the slow ones still gave way to the fast ones. In the end, there was only one line.
If it weren't for these thin lines, the sky above the Holy City would be so crowded that it would be impossible to fly.
However, Luo Zheng had just flown along this thin line for a short while when a group of martial artists came crashing over from the front!
This group of warriors didn't have the slightest intention of following the rules. They were all shouting and chasing each other. Moreover, their flying speed was extremely fast.
These warriors looked to be in their early twenties, but their cultivation wasn't low. Basically, they had already stepped into the Divine Sea Realm. Only a few of them were Life and Death Realm warriors.
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
Seeing these guys charging head-on, the cultivators who were in the same direction as Luo Zheng also dodged one after another. A guy with such cultivation at such a young age was mostly from a prestigious family. How could ordinary people afford to offend him?
Seeing this scene, the two Temple Masters slightly wrinkled their brows.
As the Temple Masters of the Spirit Martial Holy Land, their status was extremely high. However, even so, the two of them didn't want to stir up trouble in the Holy City. After all, the Holy City was a place of hidden dragons and crouching tigers. The thirty-six Temple Masters were indeed peak existences in the Spirit Martial Holy Land. However, there were many Elders and other characters above them. The less trouble, the better.

Chapter 1004
These guys were flying in the opposite direction of the thin line, breaking the flying rules of the Holy City. The bystanders were angry, but they didn't dare to say anything.
Luo Zheng had already retreated to the side, trying to avoid these guys.
Since he had just arrived and was unfamiliar with the place, he didn't want to cause any trouble.
What he didn't expect was that these two young warriors would charge straight at him with a trace of coldness on their faces. Obviously, they wanted to collide with him!
Luo Zheng didn't want to cause trouble, but he was never afraid of trouble. Just like those guys in the mineral vein, if they wanted to die, Luo Zheng wouldn't hesitate to make a move.
He had already taken a step back, but the other side still didn't let him go. Luo Zheng's face didn't change at all. He was accumulating power in his body.
Seeing this, the two Palace Masters were also shocked. They were entrusted with bringing Luo Zheng into the Holy City. Now, Luo Zheng hadn't been handed over yet. If something happened to him, how could they explain it?
The two Palace Masters were quite far from Luo Zheng. The young warriors were flying at a high speed, so it was too late for them to move away from Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng was hit by these two Divine Sea Realm warriors. Palace Master He and Palace Master Shen only had time to warn Luo Zheng.
"Get out of the way!"
Luo Zheng stood in the air without moving an inch. His gaze was indifferent as he looked at the two guys who were about to crash into him.
The moment these two young warriors collided with Luo Zheng, they even laughed. At the same time, the corners of Luo Zheng's mouth also curled up.
Seeing the strange smile on Luo Zheng's face, the two young warriors felt a little strange. What was this Life and Death Realm kid laughing about?
In the next moment, the three of them collided with each other.
Under the collision of their bodies, a muffled sound burst out.
"Puff!"
"Not good!"
The two Palace Masters' faces were also very unsightly. How safe was the Spirit Martial Holy Land? They didn't expect Luo Zheng to be so unlucky.
The group of male and female warriors in front of him were obviously the playboys of the Holy City. They didn't put others in their eyes. In their dictionary, bullying others was a matter of course, and they didn't need any reason. How could they have known that they would find Luo Zheng so coincidentally?
However, what happened next left everyone dumbfounded.
Luo Zheng only retreated a distance of several dozen meters before coming to a steady stop. After all, his opponents were two Divine Sea Realm warriors. Luo Zheng was unable to use "Power Transfer", so he could only rely on the body of a Saint Tool to forcefully resist. However, the power that erupted from his body had also neutralized part of the impact. Under the impact, Luo Zheng's Qi and blood were madly tumbling, but they were quickly digested by his tough body. The reason why he retreated so far was only to make his body feel better. If Luo Zheng wanted to show off, he could do it without retreating a single step.
However, the situation of the two young Divine Sea Realm warriors was very bad.
The moment the two young warriors collided with Luo Zheng, they felt as if they had collided with a mountain!
However, with the strength of a Divine Sea Realm warrior, even if it was a mountain, they would be able to smash it into pieces …
Therefore, in the entire Holy City, this group of playboys ran rampant. A few days ago, when they fought with others, they smashed more than ten thousand meters high buildings. Still, no one dared to say a word!
There was a reason for deliberately colliding with Luo Zheng. The reason was ridiculous. It was nothing more than seeing that Luo Zheng was a Life and Death Realm warrior, and Luo Zheng's appearance was only in his early twenties. To have such a cultivation at this age was not considered slow in the Upper Realm. Therefore, they took the initiative to provoke Luo Zheng, wanting to embarrass Luo Zheng.
They thought that Luo Zheng would inevitably dodge.
Who knew that not only did Luo Zheng not dodge, but he also stood still in the same place.
When they collided with Luo Zheng, they finally understood why he did not move …
Both of them let out a muffled groan from their noses. Under this high-speed collision, coupled with the explosive power of Luo Zheng, their internal organs were displaced. Even the opened Inner Worlds were constantly tumbling, and the True Essence Sea inside was surging with monstrous waves. There was also a tearing feeling in their Dantian.
"Puff puff …"
Blood spurted out from their mouths. Like two kites with broken strings, they fell straight down and finally smashed into the cold ground.
Below them was a mortal market. Today, there were countless mortals coming and going in this market. Suddenly, two Divine Sea Realm warriors fell down, startling those mortals.
However, the mortals living in the Sacred City were in a completely different world from the warriors. They also knew how to protect themselves. Seeing the two warriors falling down, no one dared to watch. Instead, everyone dispersed in a hubbub, fearing that the two warriors would get up from the ground and vent their anger on them.
It was too easy for warriors to kill mortals. Moreover, if they killed mortals, they would die unjustly …
"This …" For a moment, Hall Master He and Hall Master Shen did not know what to say. Although they were glad that Luo Zheng was fine, the result was too different from what they had expected.
As for the other dozen or so young warriors, they also looked at Luo Zheng in a daze. However, the first two warriors to react flew down and carried the two unconscious young men on their backs.
Luo Zheng glanced at Hall Master He and Hall Master Shen indifferently, then said, "Hall Masters, shall we continue on our way?"
The two Hall Masters nodded at the same time, muttering in their hearts, "No wonder this kid was chosen by a big shot. He does have some skills …"
Just as the three of them were about to leave, those young warriors suddenly became unhappy.
This group of people was tyrannical in the Sacred City. It had always been them who made others suffer losses. Since when did others make them suffer losses? The key was that after suffering such a loss today, this guy still wanted to run away? How could it be so easy?
"Stop. You want to run away after bumping into someone?" One of the young warriors said coldly.
Luo Zheng glanced at that warrior indifferently and said, "He bumped into me first."
"Hey, he bumped into you? Don't you know how to dodge? Instead, you stood there and let him bump into you? "Another young warrior continued.
Luo Zheng sneered, "Nonsense. You are a group of trash who have nothing to do. You don't have any skills. You deserve to die like this! You are looking for death, yet you still want to blame others. You should be the trash of trash! "
As soon as these words came out, those young warriors were stunned.
Everyone thought that Luo Zheng would continue to defend himself. Even the two Hall Masters thought that Luo Zheng would continue to reason. After all, along the way, they saw that Luo Zheng had a good temper. They didn't expect that he would suddenly become so arrogant halfway through his defense. For a moment, they couldn't accept it.
After experiencing the spiritual dilemma last time, Luo Zheng had understood that there were some guys in the world who didn't need to be reasoned with. For this group of people in front of him, only strength could make them understand what was reality.
In the Sacred City, few people dared to talk so arrogantly in front of this group of young warriors, even those warriors with strength. After all, this group of guys in the Spirit Sea Realm was extremely difficult to deal with. More importantly, their families were supported by a group of seniors in the Lingwu Holy Place. Therefore, even if some warriors suffered losses, they would just endure it.
Today, Luo Zheng was different from his usual self. He didn't give them any face and scolded them to their faces.
Foppish playboys cared about face the most. In their eyes, they could lose anything but face. Being called trash of trash by Luo Zheng, this group of people's faces turned from red to white. Soon, one of the young warriors said in a cold voice, "Do you know who we are?"
"Whoosh!"
A cyan light flashed in Luo Zheng's Sumeru Ring. He held a long sword in his hand and pointed it at that young warrior, "Don't tell me who you are, and don't tell me who your seniors are. This is a common excuse used by trash! Come at me together or one by one. I'll let you choose! "
With this attitude, he didn't put these guys in his eyes at all.

Chapter 1005
In the air, countless cultivators were flying and moving on the thin lines that crisscrossed like spider webs.
These martial artists were not as timid as those mortals. When they saw that there was a dispute here, they also stopped to watch.
Hearing Luo Zheng calling these Divine Sea Realm martial artists trash and even threatening to challenge them, their faces were full of excitement. They knew that there was a good show to watch. They didn't know where this Life and Death Realm martial artist came from to be so arrogant.
However, this kind of arrogance had to be fulfilled. Without strength, this kind of arrogance was no different from courting death.
"Hahaha, you're just relying on your strong body and you dare to be so arrogant. You want us to challenge you alone? You think too highly of yourself. I alone can deal with you! "A long-haired youth walked out from the group of martial artists.
Luo Zheng faintly smiled and raised the longsword in his hand, ready to fight.
At this time, the two palace masters couldn't take it anymore. If you want to fight, it's your business. However, the two of them were escorting Luo Zheng. How could they let Luo Zheng challenge these guys?
Palace Master He stood out at this moment and said to those young martial artists, "Sorry, we have important matters to attend to. We can't accept your challenge right now. You juniors should know when to stop!"
"Know when to stop? Who do you think you are? How dare you stop us! "The long-haired youth didn't care about Palace Master He's words and even scolded him.
At this moment, Palace Master He's face turned ugly.
The two of them were the palace masters of the thirty-six palaces. Although there were many experts in the Spirit Martial Holy Land, apart from the leaders of the Holy Land, there were more than a hundred elders and some other important people. The next in line were the palace masters of the thirty-six palaces.
In other words, in the tenth grade Holy Land, their positions were enough to be in the top two hundred!
Moreover, Palace Master He was old and his cultivation base was only second to the Realm Lord. Being scolded by these juniors in their early twenties, his face turned ugly.
Hall Master He sneered, "Brat, no matter who your elders are, you should know your limits!" To be honest, since these guys were so arrogant, they must have a powerful background. Therefore, Hall Master He had to restrain his anger. If it were any other warrior of the Spirit Sea Realm, he would have crushed them into ashes with a wave of his hand.
"Hmph, so what if I don't know my limits?" The long-haired young man clearly knew that these two Palace Masters had high cultivation levels, but he still didn't take it to heart.
"If you don't know your limits, then I'll have to teach you a lesson on behalf of your family's elders." Palace Master He was finally enraged. This group of reckless debauchees had really gone too far.
The long-haired young man smiled faintly. "My grandfather is Elder Crane Heart of the Lingwu Holy Land. Teach me a lesson? I'm afraid you don't have the qualifications! "
When the two Hall Masters heard Elder He Xin's name, their expressions froze. As expected …
In fact, the two Hall Masters had a rough idea from the beginning. Those guys who dared to be so arrogant in the Sacred City must have a powerful background. Otherwise, they would have been taken down long ago.
Probably seeing that the two Hall Masters were discouraged, this group of young warriors became even more arrogant. The long-haired young man sneered, "I wonder if you have the qualifications to teach me a lesson?"
Palace Master He was very embarrassed at this time.
Elder He Xin was an extremely powerful elder in the Lingwu Holy Land. He had a bad temper and was very protective of his own people. He was rude and unreasonable. Many Hall Masters had suffered in the hands of this elder. No matter Hall Master Shen or Palace Master He, they didn't want to provoke this old guy.
"Do I have the qualifications? Tell me!"
"Tell me loudly!"
"Who do you think you are? Just because your cultivation is higher, you pretend to be an elder here!"
This group of young warriors became aggressive when they saw the two Hall Masters softening their stance.
In particular, the long-haired young man took a step forward and sneered, "Tell me, how do you want to teach me a lesson?"
"I … This …" Although Palace Master He had powerful strength, he was forced to take a step back by the long-haired young man.
"Tell me! Looking at you, you should be the Hall Master or Deputy Hall Master of that Hall. You might be a big shot in your little place, but in the Sacred City, hehe! "The long-haired young man continued to press forward as he spoke.
Just as Palace Master He took another step back, a figure suddenly flashed by!
Luo Zheng broke through the air at this moment. At the same time, the Chaotic Energy in his body turned into an extremely powerful Law of Wind. With the help of the Chasing Cloud Movement Technique, Luo Zheng slapped the long-haired young man's face with lightning speed.
Luo Zheng's slap was not easy to bear.
The strength of this slap, even if it landed on a ferocious beast with a strong body, it would not be able to bear it.
As for the long-haired young man, he was like a spinning top as he spun rapidly in the air …
By the time the long-haired young man had resolved the strength of Luo Zheng's slap, half of his face was already red and swollen. The imprint of five fingers had also swelled up, like a relief sculpture inlaid on his face. It was incomparably clear.
Luo Zheng waved the long sword in his hand and sneered, "I said you were trash, but you didn't believe me. In the end, didn't you still have to bring out your family's elders?"
"You!" The long-haired young man was furious. The humiliation in his heart was completely covered by the pain on his face, "I will kill you!"
"Kill this brat …"
"Kill him with a thousand cuts. I want him to die in the dirtiest place in the Sacred City!"
At this time, the young warriors finally decided to swarm forward …
Luo Zheng also prepared to fight. However, although he was extremely arrogant, he did not intend to take the lives of these guys in this battle.
However, the two Hall Masters with profound cultivation would certainly not allow this to happen.
Palace Master Shen stretched out his hand and pointed, "Stop!"
Whoosh …
A power containing the Law of Time spread out and immediately shrouded these young warriors.
Palace Master Shen nodded towards Palace Master He and grabbed Luo Zheng. Palace Master He then used the Great Teleportation Technique, grabbed Luo Zheng and Palace Master Shen, and turned around. The three of them disappeared in a white spiral light, directly leaving this place of trouble.
The Great Teleportation Technique was something that could only be comprehended when one cultivated to a high level of the Law of Space. The principle was actually similar to Luo Zheng's space travel, but Luo Zheng's space travel could only travel a distance of several dozen feet. Even after two years of secluded cultivation and comprehending more of the Law of Space, his space travel limit was only maintained at a distance of 300 to 400 feet.
However, the Great Teleportation Technique could directly travel a distance of several hundred miles. This was also a major life-saving method for warriors who cultivated the Law of Space! After cultivating to a very high level, the Great Teleportation Technique could even travel an unimaginable distance. It could even teleport between great worlds!
After the three of them disappeared, the restriction of the Law of Time slowly disappeared. The young warriors all had ugly expressions. Today, they had suffered a great loss!
"Escape? As long as we're in the Holy City, do you think we can escape?" The long-haired young man sneered as he stuffed a pill into his mouth.
This eighth-grade pill was very expensive. For many cultivators, they would only swallow it when they were on the verge of death. In fact, although Luo Zheng's slap had left him in a sorry state, it was far more than just swallowing this pill. However, for him, the handprint on his face was too shameful. Naturally, he hoped that it would disappear as soon as possible.
The pill's effect was amazing. After swallowing it, the handprint from Luo Zheng's slap began to fade at a visible speed.
However, although the handprint on his face faded, his heart was far from calm!
Whoosh …
Under the Great Teleportation, the three of them instantly appeared in another part of the Holy City.
Palace Master He sighed and said, "Forget it, let's continue on our way!"
Luo Zheng saw the expression on Palace Master He's face and said indifferently, "Palace Master He, why are you afraid of that group of juniors? This kind of person should be taught a harsh lesson!"
Palace Master He didn't speak. Instead, Palace Master Shen said, "Hey, Luo Zheng, you don't know. Our thirty-six palaces may look impressive, but we can't compare to the Holy City. This group of people's background is really not small. I'm afraid they won't let you go.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1006
After all, the Holy Land of Lingwu was a tenth-grade Holy Land, one of the three overlords of a big world.
The core of the Holy Land was in the Holy City.
As the saying went, a higher official could crush people to death. How could Temple Master He and the others dare to provoke those elders?
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He had encountered this kind of thing too many times and was almost numb to it. However, he still smiled and said, "Thank you for your concern, Temple Master Shen. I'll be fine."
"I hope so …" Temple Master Shen nodded.
The three of them continued on their way …
Several hours later, a huge building appeared in front of Luo Zheng. The size of this building was about the same as the area of Tianqi City.
What surprised Luo Zheng even more was that this building was not built on the ground, nor was it suspended in the air like the Cloud Hall. Instead, it was built on the back of a giant tortoise!
"This giant tortoise …" The giant tortoise under the building was obviously a living creature. It was just quietly crawling on the ground, motionless. However, the aura emitted from this giant tortoise gave Luo Zheng a vast and ethereal feeling!
"This is not a tortoise. It's called 'Feiyi'. It's actually a giant snake with a shell." Temple Master Shen said.
"Feiyi … It allows humans to build this on top of it?" Luo Zheng looked at this scene with his eyes wide open.
Temple Master Shen laughed and said, "Of course, it's the contract beast of our Holy Land Master. It also protects the safety of the entire Holy City!"
"This guy is pressed under such a huge building. Won't it move?" Luo Zheng asked again. He was really curious.
"It hasn't moved for 3,000 years, right? I remember that 3,000 years ago, it should be here. Later, it moved once … "Temple Master Shen said.
There were indeed too many interesting things in the Upper World. Just the Holy Land of Lingwu was enough to make Luo Zheng curious. He wondered how many miracles existed in other big worlds.
After watching for a while, Luo Zheng flew toward the building with Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He.
As soon as they approached the top of the "Feiyi", a team of armored soldiers rushed out of the building.
These armored soldiers wore brown dragon scale armor, and every single one of them had a cultivation level of the Divine Realm. After charging out like this, they blocked the path of the three people.
"We're waiting for someone to visit the Spiritual Martial Palace!" The leading soldier asked coldly.
Hall Master Shen smiled and took out a nameplate in his hand.
The leader of the armored soldiers glanced over and said, "So it's Hall Master Shen! May I ask what brings you here? "
"I was summoned by the Holy Lord. Please pass on the message," Hall Master Shen said.
Sacred Master? In other words, the leader of the Holy Land?
Did this mean that the ruler of the Lingwu Holy Land had summoned him? Luo Zheng looked even more confused.
He had no idea who the Holy Lord of the Spirit Martial Holy Land was. Why would he be summoned by the Holy Lord?
His mind was in a mess, but he couldn't come up with an answer. However, the answer should be revealed soon, right?
After the armored soldiers passed on the message, the two Temple Masters and Luo Zheng entered the huge Spiritual Martial Palace. The Spiritual Martial Palace had three inner layers and three outer layers. There were a total of six layers of barriers. Luo Zheng casually looked around and judged that these barriers were at least seven layers or even eight layers of barriers. This kind of protection was too tight.
After passing through these barriers, they directly entered the center of the Spiritual Martial Palace. In the center, there was a spacious hall. In this hall, there was a middle-aged man wearing a purple-gold crown. He was sitting alone at a table, and the table was filled with all kinds of strangely shaped fruits. These fruits emitted an alluring fragrance, and the middle-aged man was eating them one by one.
After Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He entered the hall, they stepped forward and bowed to the middle-aged man. "Subordinate greets the Temple Master!"
The middle-aged man faintly nodded and waved his hand. His gaze fell on Luo Zheng.
When this gaze fell on him, Luo Zheng suddenly felt like he was completely seen through. This middle-aged man was the Holy Lord of the Spirit Martial Holy Land?
Then this person's cultivation should be at the Sector Lord level!
Luo Zheng tried to guess this person's cultivation level. However, when the middle-aged man looked at him like this, he felt a little conflicted. The feeling of being seen through with a single glance was not good!
The middle-aged man could feel Luo Zheng's emotions. He only gave a faint "hmm" before continuing, "He's a good seedling, but at this level, he shouldn't be qualified … Why is that?"
These words were a little strange. Not only did Luo Zheng not understand, but Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He also didn't understand.
"Strange, strange." The middle-aged man didn't understand and continued to look at Luo Zheng. "Warriors of this level, our Spirit Martial Holy Land should have quite a few … It shouldn't be!"
These mutterings made Luo Zheng even more puzzled. He couldn't bear it any longer. Luo Zheng was too lazy to care about etiquette and asked, "May I know why the Holy Lord summoned me?"
Seeing Luo Zheng talking so casually to the Holy Lord without any etiquette, Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He immediately became nervous. They knew that the Holy Lord was always very strict!
Who knew that the middle-aged man would only faintly smile. "Nothing much. It's just that throwing you into the mines is not very suitable!"
Speaking up to here, Luo Zheng immediately said, "If that's the case, can I leave the Spirit Martial Holy Land?"
"Hehe …" The middle-aged man faintly smiled. "Of course not. Not only can you not leave the Spirit Martial Holy Land, you can't even leave the Holy City!"
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng frowned and asked, "Are you going to imprison me?"
Temple Master Shen couldn't bear to listen anymore. You're just an Ascendant. What do you have that's worthy of being imprisoned by the Holy Lord of the Spirit Martial Holy Land? Are you kidding me? Temple Master Shen said, "Luo Zheng, the Holy Lord wants you to stay in the Holy City. Naturally, he won't treat you unfairly. You don't have to ask about anything else."
The middle-aged man waved his hand and said, "It's okay. It's not that I want you to stay in the Holy City. Someone is coming here. However, it will take some time. You can wait here for a while."
"Someone? Who is that person? " Luo Zheng stared at the middle-aged man and asked. This mystery was getting bigger and bigger. His curiosity was getting stronger and stronger.
"You'll know when the time comes." The middle-aged man refused to tell him. After thinking for a while, he said, "Shen Gongfan, take him to apply for a nameplate to enter the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall. During this time, you can use the cultivation area of the Spirit Martial Holy Land."
"Subordinate obeys!" Temple Master Shen hurriedly nodded.
"Okay, if there's anything, you can find me directly." The middle-aged man glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently.
After saying this, Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He directly led Luo Zheng away.
Although Luo Zheng didn't talk much with the middle-aged man, it made Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He even more shocked.
Their Holy Lord was a Sector Lord level existence. He was one of the top experts in the entire Spirit Martial World. Although he didn't seize the authority of a Sector Lord, his strength didn't lose to any Sector Lord.
But today, the Holy Lord was so amiable. He allowed them to take Luo Zheng to apply for a nameplate. He even told Luo Zheng to find him directly if there was anything!
Just who was behind Luo Zheng …
Originally, Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He guessed that Luo Zheng might be related to one of the Elders. But now, it seemed that the "big shot" behind him had an even bigger background than the Holy Lord. Otherwise, the Holy Lord wouldn't give Luo Zheng face.
One must know that the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall was the core of the Spirit Martial Holy Land!
Only the most outstanding disciples in the Spirit Martial Holy Land had the qualifications to enter the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall to cultivate.
Temple Master Shen and Temple Master He were originally from the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall. Even if they weren't the most outstanding existences, they were qualified for the position of Temple Master.
This kind of place would only allow the most talented core disciples to enter. But now, Luo Zheng didn't seem to be a member of the Spirit Martial Holy Land. This kind of "outsider" couldn't be allowed to enter the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall. But now that the Holy Lord had spoken, there was no problem.

Chapter 1007
Luo Zheng was only staying in the Lingwu Holy Land for a short while. He wasn't particularly interested in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall.
However, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't refuse the other party's kindness.
After Luo Zheng left, the Holy Lord of the Lingwu Holy Land gently clapped his hands. From the side of the main hall, two old men stepped on the air and arrived.
"Holy Lord, what's the matter?" The old man asked.
The Holy Lord immediately said, "Inform Yulong to challenge this brat and test his strength."
The two old men revealed strange expressions. One of them asked curiously, "Holy Lord, you want Sima Yulong to challenge this brat?"
"Hehe, I'm afraid this brat isn't qualified?" The other old man said disdainfully.
The Holy Lord nodded, "Yulong has already broken through the ninth tribulation. As for the legendary tenth tribulation, it's hard to say now. Luo Zheng has only broken through the fifth minor tribulation. There's indeed a gap between their cultivation bases. However … there must be a reason why this brat was chosen by the Alliance. If Yulong can defeat this brat, I'll be able to open my mouth and send him to the Alliance."
Hearing the Holy Lord's words, the two old men revealed an incredulous expression. "Holy Lord, you're saying this ascender was chosen by the Alliance?"
The Holy Lord nodded with a puzzled expression, "This ascender is somewhat special, but it's not enough for the Alliance to pay attention to. I can't figure it out either … However, it won't be long before the Alliance takes this brat away. I want Yulong to challenge this brat and then recommend him to the Alliance. If Yulong can join the Alliance, it'll be greatly beneficial for him."
After hearing this, the two old men's eyes suddenly flashed. They said almost simultaneously, "Holy Lord is wise!"
Sima Yulong was the most outstanding genius of the Lingwu Holy Land's younger generation. The entire Lingwu Holy Land had high hopes for him!
Although he hadn't broken through to the Divine Sea Realm, he had already broken through the ninth minor tribulation!
In the whole universe, most of the Life and Death Realm warriors would have broken through to the Spirit Sea Realm by the fifth or sixth minor tribulation. Some outstanding warriors would have to go through seven minor tribulations. As for geniuses, they would have to go through eight minor tribulations. As for those who had to go through nine minor tribulations, they were extremely rare!
The more minor heavenly tribulations one overcame, the greater one's future potential would be.
This Sima Yulong had already broken through the ninth minor heavenly tribulation, but he still hadn't opened up a divine sea. It was very likely that he would attract the legendary tenth minor heavenly tribulation!
Not only that, when Sima Yulong went through the first minor tribulation, his lotus flower had already opened. When he went through the ninth minor tribulation not long ago, his lotus flower had already opened seven leaves!
This was also the reason why the Sacred Lord placed so much importance on this matter.
However, the entire Great Alliance controlled 3,000 Great Worlds. Sima Yulong was naturally the core of the Spiritual Martial Sanctuary. However, with so many Great Worlds competing, it would be difficult for the Sacred Master to send Sima Yulong into the Great Alliance.
In contrast, this Luo Zheng was a mere Ascendant. He had just ascended, but he was actually selected by the Great Alliance. This naturally made the Sacred Lord feel somewhat unbalanced.
In front of Luo Zheng, the Sacred Lord naturally wouldn't show any expression on his face. However, he had already made plans in his heart. This was also the reason why the Sacred Lord was willing to make an exception and let Luo Zheng enter the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall.
The Myriad Spirit Martial Hall was the place that all the warriors in the Spiritual Martial Sanctuary yearned to enter.
However, ordinary warriors couldn't join it at all. The number of warriors in the Spiritual Martial Sanctuary was probably more than a billion, but only a mere 9,000 people could enter the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall! For every person who entered, there had to be a person who came out. There couldn't be more or less.
Those sixth and seventh grade aristocratic families would do anything to send their disciples into the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall. After all, once they entered the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, their future was almost limitless.
However, the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall had made an exception today. There was actually one more person in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, making it 901 people!
Moreover, this extra guy was actually an ascender!
This news spread like wildfire in the Sacred City. It was unknown how many warriors were envious of it.
There was once a seventh grade aristocratic family that was the wealthiest family in the Spiritual Martial Sanctuary. The entire Spiritual Martial Sanctuary had 4,800 mineral veins, and this aristocratic family controlled more than 2,000 mineral veins. In order to send a genius from their family into the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, they directly threw out millions of Zhen Yuanyu!
One could imagine how the other warriors would feel if they made an exception for this Ascendant.
However, although everyone felt extremely unhappy in their hearts, when they heard that it was the Sacred Lord's personal order, no one dared to say anything.
In the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, there were all kinds of cultivation techniques and inheritances. Furthermore, there was a Spirit Gathering Array that the Sacred Lord had personally set up. The purity of the Zhen Yuanyu's Zhen Yuanyu was astonishing. In addition, there were also Spiritual Item Manuals, high-grade Law Stone Tablets, and …
There were all sorts of benefits. Some martial artists stubbornly believed that entering the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall to cultivate would allow them to advance at a tremendous pace, allowing them to be reborn!
In any case, this was a legendary cultivation holy land.
Therefore, the moment Luo Zheng stepped into the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, he was destined to attract all kinds of envy and jealousy.
Many aristocratic families were also secretly observing this Ascendant, wondering if they should rope him in. After all, the 9,000 geniuses in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall all had their own affiliations. They either came from aristocratic families or were direct descendants of the Elders of the Spiritual Martial Sanctuary. As for Luo Zheng, he was just an Ascendant who had jumped out from some unknown corner.
However, when Luo Zheng stepped into the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, he did something extremely inconceivable.
The Myriad Spirit Martial Hall in front of him was indeed extraordinary. As a cultivation holy land in the Upper Realm, it was definitely several levels higher than the Central Domain and the Divine Kingdom Continent. The Heaven Earth Yuan Qi that spread out from this holy land was rich and pure, but it did not benefit him much.
From the beginning, Luo Zheng's Body Refining Dao had embarked on an extraordinary path, which was to use his own body as a weapon to cultivate.
After cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Technique in the Immortal Mansion, he could be considered to have abandoned the True Essence system. The True Essence in his Dantian was completely replaced by Primal Chaos Qi.
Ordinary cultivation methods were almost useless to Luo Zheng.
Previously, he could not refuse the Spiritual Martial Sanctuary's Holy Lord. Since he was told to wait in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, he could only wait.
Therefore, after stepping into the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, Luo Zheng found a corner, sat down cross-legged, and closed his eyes to meditate.
As for Hall Master He and Hall Master Shen, who had led Luo Zheng into the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, they were dumbfounded. Along the way, the two of them had bragged to Luo Zheng for a long time about the various benefits of the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall. Where did this weirdo come from? Was he not interested in these things?
"This … Luo Zheng, the Cultivation Secret Realm in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall is very useful for Life and Death Realm martial artists …" Hall Master He could not help but remind him.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "I appreciate Hall Master He's kindness, but Luo Zheng has his own plans on the path of cultivation."
The two Hall Masters knew that they could not persuade Luo Zheng. This time, by sending Luo Zheng to the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, their task was completed. As for other matters, they had nothing to do with them, so they bid farewell and left.
Luo Zheng sat in the corner and cultivated, but the bystanders were not pleased with him.
"This kid is most likely showing off! Entering a treasure mountain and returning empty-handed, hehe, even if you beat me to death, I won't believe it. Most likely, in less than a day, he will eagerly apply for the right to use the Cultivation Secret Realm! "
"Most likely. Our Holy Lord is willing to make an exception for a small ascender. This kindness is greater than the heavens, but he still wants to put on airs …"
"I really want to teach this guy a lesson!"
Luo Zheng did not know that just by sitting here, he had already infuriated countless people.
However, there was no one to disturb his cultivation. There were not many cultivators in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall. At most, those who came and went only glanced at Luo Zheng before hurriedly leaving.
Unknowingly, three days passed.

Chapter 1008
Seeing that Luo Zheng really had no intention of entering the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall to cultivate, those people changed their words.
"Is this guy stupid?"
"He's so conceited. Does he think that our Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall can't give him anything?"
"Since when can an ascender from the lower realms be so arrogant? Bastard!"
This kind of discussion spread in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall and even spread to the Holy City.
Many warriors in the Spirit Martial Hall knew that an awesome ascender had appeared in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall. It seemed that he didn't even care about the Holy Land that they yearned for.
Because of this, everyone became even more angry with Luo Zheng. At first, everyone thought that Luo Zheng was pretending. Now, they felt that he had hurt their feelings.
On the sixth day, a group of warriors appeared in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall.
Luo Zheng had seen this group of warriors before. It was the group of young warriors that had clashed with Luo Zheng when he had just entered the Holy City.
The two warriors in the lead had been violently attacked by Luo Zheng. They had been knocked half-dead by Luo Zheng and had barely recovered after recuperating for a few days.
Most of the warriors in this group were direct disciples of the Holy Land's Elders. Their talents were not bad, but each of them had a flamboyant personality. When they heard that a weirdo had appeared in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall and seemed to look down on this place, how could they let such a thing go?
When the two of them had recovered, the group returned to the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall. They were also members of the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall.
According to the rumors, they found a guy sitting in the corner, meditating with his eyes closed. However, when they swept their eyes over Luo Zheng's face, they were all shocked.
"How could it be this guy!"

Because it's a pure reflection, all of our weapons, treasures, and Cultivation Methods are completely copied inside. If we exchange pointers like this, we can show our strength, but we won't get injured."
Hearing Sima Yulong's explanation, Luo Zheng suddenly realized, "Is this an Illusory Array?"
Sima Yulong thought for a moment, then nodded and said, "You can understand it that way, but this Illusory Space is different from an Illusory Array."
In fact, an Illusory Array used an Array to create illusions, and the cultivator's own body was inside the Illusory Array. As for the Illusory Space, it actually used the fifth layer of the Space Law's "Refraction" ability to project the cultivator's own reflection inside. It was like looking into a mirror, completely copying the cultivator and entering the Illusory Space to fight. Moreover, the cultivator's own weapons and other things would all be retained.
From this point of view, the Illusory Space was naturally much more complicated than an Illusory Array!
Sima Yulong didn't want to explain too much to Luo Zheng. It was fine to reluctantly describe it as an Illusory Array.
"Then how do we start?" Luo Zheng asked.
Sima Yulong waved his hand, and the two diamond-shaped jade talismans floating in the Dragon Tiger Palace Hall quietly fluttered over. Then, he stretched out his hand and gently pushed the diamond-shaped jade talismans towards Luo Zheng.

Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Sima Yulong thought for a moment and actually guessed his thoughts. Then, he said, "This reflection only uses the Space Law. It doesn't copy your body after understanding you, so it won't remember any of your secrets. It's like looking into a mirror …"
A mirror could clearly reflect a person's face, but it couldn't remember the person's face.
This analogy dispelled Luo Zheng's doubts, but it was this hesitation that immediately attracted countless mouthfuls of saliva.
"This ascender is still so cautious, afraid that his secrets will be exposed, hehe."

"Hu hu …"
