"Yes! They must be mastered. That's why I've been in seclusion during this period of time and have been painstakingly comprehending these two Elemental Laws. "Sima Yulong's voice continued to be transmitted through the fluttering wind.
This information was also very crucial to Luo Zheng.
The saying that time was king and space was supreme was not without reason.
These two Elemental Laws involved things that were too powerful. Let's talk about the Time Elemental Law first. When the other party changed the flow of time at will, they could move a hundred times faster than you. No matter how powerful your Wind Elemental Law was or how fast your body technique was, how could you escape the restrictions of time?
The way to resist the Time Elemental Law was to cultivate the Time Elemental Law yourself.
It was the same for the Space Elemental Law!
When two living beings were fighting, if they cultivated to a high level of the Space Elemental Law, the other party could teleport tens of thousands of miles away. There was no way to fight!
These two Elemental Laws were the foundation of the foundation. They were also compulsory courses for Upper Realm cultivators!
As Luo Zheng and Sima Yulong exchanged blows and conversed, the Dragon Tiger Palace was already in an uproar.
"I didn't expect this guy to block two of Senior Brother Yulong's swords!"
"Don't get excited. Senior Brother Yulong didn't use his full strength at all. Have you forgotten? Senior Brother Yulong uses three swords to defeat even the weakest opponent!"
"But this kid didn't use his full strength either! Look at him, he looks like he's fine. He's even asking questions! "
"Hmph, he's just pretending to be calm. I'm afraid he's scared to death!"
"Everyone, don't talk! Senior Brother Yulong is going to use his third sword … "
Someone in the Dragon Tiger Palace shouted, telling everyone to stop arguing. Sima Yulong had a habit. No matter who he sparred with, he would never use one move to defeat his opponent. The first sword was just a symbolic greeting. The second sword intent was to suppress. As for the third sword, it was when he used his true strength.
Over the years, Sima Yulong had fought countless enemies, but he had never broken this rule. Many cultivators in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall were naturally exceptions who had experienced Sima Yulong's third sword!
Therefore, some cultivators would use this "honor" to brag in front of outsiders, saying, "I'm the one who blocked Sima Yulong's first two swords …"
In the Illusory Space, a fierce aura was suddenly transmitted from the wildly surging gale. Luo Zheng faced the gale directly in front of him, and his expression became serious at the same time.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1012
Sima Yulong could be considered the first genius he fought after ascending to the Upper Realm.
The outcome of this battle had no effect on Luo Zheng, and death in the Dreamscape was not a big deal. However, it was a crucial guide for Luo Zheng's judgment.
Facing Sima Yulong's third sword, Luo Zheng was naturally very serious.
"Huff … huff … huff …"
In this boundless Dreamscape, hundreds of violent gales suddenly appeared and circled around Luo Zheng.
Immediately after, those invisible gales began to compress violently, forming an extremely strong spiral of wind in front of Luo Zheng. And this spiral of wind was plated with a layer of golden brilliance!
"The law of metal …" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly, "The perfect fusion of two laws?"
As the spiral of wind continued to merge, Sima Yulong's figure appeared once again. He no longer needed to hide his figure.
Sima Yulong was very surprised when Luo Zheng easily parried his second sword.
However, although this was unexpected, it was still reasonable.
He did not treat Luo Zheng as an ordinary ascender. Although he disdained the judgment of the Thirteen Palaces of the Alliance, he believed that the Thirteen Palaces' judgment would not be so bad. This Luo Zheng should have some skills after all.
However, his evaluation of Luo Zheng was limited to "some skills". An opponent of this level was not enough for him to use his full strength. Luo Zheng could not block the third sword.
When the spiral of wind and the golden brilliance fused to the extreme, Sima Yulong stabbed his jade sword into the spiral of wind.
Although this jade sword was a Secondary Grade Three Divine Tool, it was still made of jade and its texture was extremely soft. After being stabbed, it was immediately crushed into powder by the spiral of wind that was compressed to the extreme!
However, after the jade sword was crushed, Sima Yulong held the sword hilt and smiled at Luo Zheng, "I think we should talk outside the Dreamscape …"
When the physical body in the Dreamscape was killed, Luo Zheng's reflection would disappear. In other words, Luo Zheng would definitely "die" under this sword.
"Sou!"
Without waiting for Luo Zheng's response, Sima Yulong gently lifted the sword hilt, and the shape of the wind suddenly changed from a ball to the "sword body" of his jade sword. Then, Sima Yulong stretched out two fingers and gently pointed at the "sword body", while his other hand waved vigorously.
"Phew …"
A wind blade flickering with golden light shot straight towards Luo Zheng!
"Golden Wind Blade!"
Luo Zheng's expression changed abruptly when he saw this scene.
Luo Zheng had seen this kind of golden wind blade before! In the center of the Chaotic Astral Ocean, there existed a large number of golden wind blades, and these golden wind blades seemed to have "intelligence."
The golden Wind Blades were many times more powerful than the black Wind Blades and the two-colored Wind Blades. Even if Luo Zheng used "Power Transfer", he still didn't dare to approach the golden Wind Blades.
According to Luo Zheng's judgment, the Law of Wind contained within the golden Wind Blade had probably reached an unimaginable level!
"No!"
In a flash, Luo Zheng immediately denied his judgment. The golden Wind Blade was only similar in appearance. The power contained within this sword should be far inferior to the golden Wind Blades in the Chaotic Astral Ocean.
This Sima Yulong was admittedly the number one genius of a tenth-rank Holy Land.
But in the Demi-Immortal Clan, which one of them wasn't a genius?
At that time, several God-Realm warriors of the Demi-Immortal Clan found it very difficult to deal with the black Wind Blade, let alone the two-colored Wind Blade and the golden Wind Blade …
It was impossible for Sima Yulong to have such terrifying strength. This simply didn't conform to common sense.
Thinking of this, the pressure in Luo Zheng's heart suddenly dissipated, and the chaotic energy in his body surged wildly!
In the Immortal Mansion, Luo Zheng had also completed several types of Origin Source Laws. His affinity with all kinds of Laws was basically the highest, so it wasn't difficult for him to comprehend them. Before entering the Immortal Mansion, Luo Zheng had naturally made all the necessary preparations.
As Luo Zheng gently waved the long sword in his hand, a Wind Blade suddenly appeared on the blade and attached itself to the surface of the long sword. The cyan Wind Blade crazily revolved around the long sword. He actually wanted to use the Law of Wind to resist Sima Yulong's sword.
Seeing Luo Zheng display the Law of Wind, a trace of disdain flashed in the depths of Sima Yulong's eyes.
He was a warrior who majored in the Law of Wind, and the Law of Gold was his secondary cultivation. The cultivation method he cultivated just happened to be compatible with the Law of Wind. Seeing Luo Zheng display the Law of Wind, he naturally felt like he was showing off his slight skill in front of an expert.
The key point was that Luo Zheng seemed to be purely displaying the Law of Wind, and it was only at the third level. How could this level of power block his sword?
I overestimated you, Sima Yulong said inwardly.
As for the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall warriors in the Dragon Tiger Hall, they were all amused.
"Showing off your slight skill in front of an expert! You're asking for humiliation! "
"Ha ha ha! This kid is an ascender. It's already good that he can cultivate to the third level of the Law of Wind!"
"Let him resist in despair this time. When he comes out of the Fantasy Space, we'll humiliate him again!"
The martial artists outside the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall also roared with laughter.
As more and more people rushed back from the outside, even the huge Dragon Tiger Hall seemed crowded. There were probably six to seven thousand people in the three inner and three outer layers. The other warriors who hadn't rushed back were either unable to make it back in time or were in a very important seclusion and couldn't come out.
What these warriors didn't know was that even the Spirit Martial Sacred Land's Holy Master and a group of Elders were silently watching everything that was happening in the Fantasy Space.
However, unlike these warriors, the Holy Master and the other Elders also had puzzled looks on their faces.
"Elder Gong, what do you think?" the Holy Master asked as he watched the wind blade condensed by Luo Zheng in the Fantasy Space.
Elder Gong, who was also one of the Founding Elders of the Spirit Martial Sacred Land, said cautiously, "Strange, strange … This kid is indeed using the Law of Wind, and it's only at the third level. It's far inferior to Yulong's! But why do I feel that this wind blade is completely different from ordinary wind blades? "
The Holy Master nodded. "I thought I was the only one who had this misconception. Luo Zheng didn't use any secret technique. He just condensed the power of the Law of Wind purely with his True Essence. He … How did he do it?"
The Holy Master and the Elders had a keen sense of smell. The moment Luo Zheng condensed the wind blade, they had already noticed the abnormality.
It was just that they couldn't see where this abnormality came from!
Putting aside the fact that it was only the third level of the Law of Wind, the level of the Law of Wind was the key. However, the quality of the cultivation technique could also determine the power of the wind blade.
The problem was that they couldn't see what kind of move Luo Zheng had used. At best, they could only see that his swordsmanship was quite exquisite and strange …
However, this was also the mindset of the Holy Master and the Elders, so they couldn't see any clues.
This was because they didn't know that Luo Zheng hadn't used his True Essence at all. They also didn't know that in this universe, there was actually someone who could use Chaos Qi …
Luo Zheng indeed hadn't used any other cultivation technique. This was because the Chaos Qi that was circulating within his body was a cultivation technique, a cultivation technique that they had never heard of before.
Martial artists absorbed Heaven and Earth Origin Qi and transformed it into True Essence for their own use. This was borrowing the good fortune of Heaven and Earth for their own use, and then using the Laws to use True Essence to erupt with the greatest power of True Essence.
As for Chaos Qi, it was something even purer. It was the origin of the entire world. From the grass and trees to the mountains and rivers, everything was transformed from Chaos Qi. The power of the Law that erupted from it was incomparable to True Essence …
The pain that Luo Zheng had suffered in order to cultivate the "Secret Primal Chaos Technique" was unimaginable to ordinary people. However, the rewards that he would receive in the future were also unimaginable.
Sima Yulong's sword was so powerful that it seemed to be able to blow away an entire continent. If he hadn't used it in this illusory space, the entire Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall would've been destroyed by this sword.
In contrast, Luo Zheng's sword was much weaker. When this sword was brandished, a cyan wind blade condensed within the sword light.

Chapter 1013
In the Illusory Space, Luo Zheng and Sima Yulong maintained a certain distance.
Two wind blades, one large and one small, collided at this moment.
Regardless of power or size, these two Wind Blades were completely disproportionate. The one that Sima Yulong used contained the power of gold and iron. It was exceptionally sharp and seemed to be able to grind everything in the world into dust.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng's wind blade looked like an ordinary wind blade with only a small amount of Luo Zheng's sword light.
Therefore, in the eyes of the bystanders, it looked like a lion pouncing on a defenseless white rabbit. There was no suspense at all.
However, the moment the two wind blades collided, the entire Illusory Space resounded with a shrill sound that made one's teeth ache.
Over there, it was the sound of wind blades grinding against each other!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
At this moment, the power contained in Luo Zheng's cyan wind blade suddenly erupted.
The cyan wind blade was like a ferocious beast that had hidden its fangs. The moment it collided with the golden wind blade, it revealed its ferocious side.
In just an instant, the golden wind blade was completely defeated and swallowed up by Luo Zheng's golden wind blade. At the same time, its momentum did not decrease and it actually rolled back towards Sima Yulong!
"Crash …"
After seeing this scene, the cultivators of the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall in the Dragon Tiger Temple fell silent for a moment before an uproar broke out.
"This, this, this is …"
"This doesn't make sense. How can Senior Brother Yulong's fourth level of the Law of Wind be defeated by a mere third level of the Law of Wind?"
"Not only that, but Senior Brother Yulong's cultivation method is also very special. Senior Brother Yulong has also comprehended the fourth level of the Law of Gold. When the two of them collide, the power of the two of them will be multiplied several times!"
In the midst of the uproar, the cultivators all opened their mouths wide. Some cultivators even rubbed their eyes hard as if they were seeing things!
At this moment, the Holy Lord of the Spirit Martial Holy Land suddenly stood up and stared blankly at the scene in the Illusory Space.
As for the other elders, their faces were also filled with silence.
"What's going on with this power of law?" The Holy Master frowned as he stared at the illusory space.
"It's a genuine third level Wind Law, but its power is not what a third level Wind Law should have!"
With the cultivation of the Holy Lord and the Elders, their control over the power of Laws had already reached the fifth level, or even the sixth level. They had already touched upon the threshold of the seventh level, so they knew the power of every level of the Wind Laws like the back of their hands. As for the other cultivation methods and martial skills, they could also calculate the power of Laws very accurately.
Therefore, in comparison, the shock that this scene brought to them was far greater than the shock of the geniuses in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall.
Elder Gong pondered for a long time and finally said with a frown, "Yes, it's the third level of the Law. However, Luo Zheng didn't use any martial skills. Even if he did, he wouldn't be able to exert such a strong power to resist Yulong's Golden Wind Chaotic Cloud Sword. This is simply impossible!"
Elder Gong used the word "impossible" to conclude Luo Zheng's sword. The problem was that this "impossible" scene had happened right under their noses.
However, at this moment, the Holy Lord slammed his palm on the wide purple-gold chair. The armrest of the chair instantly turned into dust. He had always been gentle and refined, but now he lost his composure and cursed, "Damn it, this is a loss!"
The other elders silently looked at the Holy Lord and sighed in their hearts. It was indeed a loss.
At the beginning, the Thirteen Palaces of the Alliance had personally selected Luo Zheng and asked them to send this ascender over. At that time, the Holy Lord had also paid special attention to this matter.
This ascender had ascended from the Lingwu Holy Land and was taken over by the Thirteen Palaces. Generally speaking, this kind of thing would be very unpleasant to any Holy Land.
After all, there were also some geniuses among the ascenders who were worthy of being nurtured. Therefore, during the service in the mine, the cost of becoming an independent warrior was greater than the cost of joining the Lingwu Holy Land. Joining the Lingwu Holy Land only required 100 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. However, if one wanted to be completely independent, it would cost 200 pieces. The extra 100 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu was the price to pay for borrowing the Ascension Well. After all, the Lingwu Holy Land had built the Ascension Passage and dug many Ascension Wells to help the ascenders get used to the pressure of Heaven and Earth's Origin Qi. These were not given for free. There was a price to pay.
Countless Holy Lands in the Upper Realm did this.
After knowing that Luo Zheng had been selected, the Holy Lord had naturally observed this ascender. At that time, he did not see any clues. At most, this kid was born with divine strength. It was not enough for the Lingwu Holy Land to pay attention to him. Since the Thirteen Palaces wanted him, he would just treat it as a favor.
Therefore, the Holy Lord wanted Sima Yulong to challenge Luo Zheng. This was to give him an excuse to recommend Sima Yulong to the Thirteen Palaces.
The problem now was that the Holy Lord felt that he had suffered a great loss.
This Luo Zheng was indeed not an ordinary ascender. There was a reason why the Thirteen Palaces had selected him.
Of course, he could not refuse Luo Zheng being selected by the Thirteen Palaces. However, he could ask for great benefits from the Thirteen Palaces.
Now, the Holy Lord was incomparably conflicted. Now that he had seen Luo Zheng's strength, he could not get anything other than jealousy. After all, it was not very appropriate to ask the Thirteen Palaces for a large sum of money.
Therefore, he was naturally very depressed.
Elder Gong let out a long sigh and said, "However, we can use this opportunity to send Sima Yulong to the Thirteen Palaces."
Of course, the Holy Lord understood Elder Gong's words. In fact, he had thought of this a moment ago. If he sent this ascender to the Thirteen Palaces for free, along with Sima Yulong, the Alliance would definitely accept this favor.
In other words, Sima Yulong would definitely enter the Thirteen Palaces. But so what? The Holy Lord still felt that he had suffered a great loss. He could only make up for it a little. However, this little bit of compensation was like a cup of water on a burning cart of firewood. It was not worth it.
In the illusory space, after Luo Zheng's wind blade crushed Sima Yulong's Metal Wind Chaotic Cloud Sword, it continued to sweep towards Sima Yulong.
Sima Yulong never thought that he would lose to Luo Zheng. Therefore, when he saw the wind blade sweeping towards him, his always calm face was filled with astonishment. When the wind blade swept towards him, his figure suddenly flashed and he began to frantically retreat.
While retreating, the jade sword in his hand, which had already been crushed, began to condense again. At the same time, he slashed out five or six times in a row, gradually dispersing Luo Zheng's wind blade.
In this exchange, Sima Yulong did not suffer any substantial injuries. However, his heart of martial arts was greatly injured.
Sima Yulong's vision was very farsighted, and he was very broad-minded. However, this did not mean that he was not proud. Although such a proud person treated others kindly, it was because others had not touched the pride in the depths of his heart. In other words, they could not pose any threat to him. He was so proud that he disdained to show his pride.
But now, the pride in Sima Yulong's heart had been crushed by Luo Zheng's sword. His eyes were filled with confusion, and his mind was filled with the word 'why'. Luo Zheng's sword had directly disintegrated the ideas he had accumulated over so many years.
It was really too unreasonable …
However, at this moment, he suddenly stretched out his hand and bit his arm. This bite was extremely forceful, and blood instantly flowed out from the bite mark. The intense pain instantly cleared his mind. At this moment, the contempt he had for Luo Zheng in the depths of his heart had completely disappeared. It was replaced with respect, seriousness, and even a feeling of facing a formidable enemy.
"You are really strong."
"The Thirteen Palaces have good foresight. I was thinking too much before …"
"Among the younger generation of the Spirit Martial Holy Land, only you are worthy of my full strength."
After saying that, Sima Yulong took out the jade sword that he had previously stored in his Sumeru Space.
The Jiangshan Sheji Sword was a lower first-grade divine tool.

Chapter 1014
When he was in the four Divine Kingdoms, Luo Zheng had seen the power of the divine weapons in the hands of the Monarchs.
However, according to the Upper Realm's classification of divine weapons, the jade bracelet on Huo Yuner's body was a Secondary Grade Three divine weapon.
As for the divine weapons in the hands of the Monarchs, they were Secondary Grade Three divine weapons. Compared to Sima Yulong's Secondary Grade One divine weapon, there was a gap of Secondary Grade Two and Secondary Grade Two.
Therefore, the power of the Mountain and Stream Sword was indeed enormous.
When Sima Yulong activated the Mountain and Stream Sword, he seemed to have transformed into a monarch who ruled over everything. He seemed to have become the supreme ruler of the land!
As for Luo Zheng, he was like a subject of the Mountain and Stream Sword. He had no choice but to bow down to this aura!
Looking at Sima Yulong's eyes, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. "You want to suppress me?"
"Rumble!"
A sharp soul pressure was suddenly released from Luo Zheng's body!
When a martial artist cultivated, they usually needed to make breakthroughs in three aspects. One was Body Refining, another was Cultivation, and the third was Soul Power!
In terms of Body Refining and Cultivation, Luo Zheng had embarked on a completely different path from others.
However, in terms of Soul Refining, Luo Zheng still followed the rules.
During the last period of his stay in the Central Domain, Qinglong had finally planted a Soul Seed in him and used it to break through to the Blue Soul Realm.
Luo Zheng had stayed in the Battle Soul Realm for a long time, so it took him a lot of time to break through to the Blue Soul Realm. However, at this time, he didn't have much of an advantage over other martial artists.
Just like the Saint Lord of the Spiritual Martial Sacred Land, he could also sense the strength of Luo Zheng's soul when he examined Luo Zheng. Although breaking through to the Blue Soul Realm at the fifth stage of the Life and Death Realm was extremely terrifying in the Lower Realm, it could only be considered as outstanding in the Upper Realm.
However, since it was a Soul Seed made by Qinglong, it was extraordinary.
This Soul Seed had been lurking in Luo Zheng's soul all this time, and this sudden burst of power instantly expanded Luo Zheng's soul pressure by more than three times!
Most importantly, Luo Zheng's Soul Willpower had completely merged with the Willpower of the Great Wide World. As he was not in the Great Wide World, he could not use his power to transfer it. However, after the merging of the Willpower, they were inseparable. The Soul Willpower that erupted contained the aura of the Great Wide World.
The Mountain and Stream Sword was a lower first-grade divine weapon forged by a Monarch of a tenth-grade Divine Nation. The Monarch's comprehension of the Mountain and Stream Sword was infused into the longsword, and it could be said to be a painstaking work …
Therefore, the aura and pressure they emitted made all the living beings feel that they were inferior by a head!
However, although the imposing aura of the Mountains and Rivers was powerful, it was ultimately forged based on comprehension. On the other hand, Luo Zheng's spiritual pressure contained the will of the Vast Thousand Realms, which was a genuine imposing aura!
This imposing aura was originally the accumulation of the Vast Thousand Realms over countless years.
In the Lower Realm, Luo Zheng's imposing aura made people feel that he represented everything. Now that he was in the Upper Realm, after releasing this imposing aura, others felt that he represented the entire world!
Just by relying on this spiritual pressure, he forcibly suppressed the imposing aura of the Mountain and Stream Sword.
This time, Sima Yulong's expression became even more solemn. When Luo Zheng stood in front of him, he felt like he was facing an entire world …
He originally wanted to use the Mountain and Stream Sword to suppress Luo Zheng, but he did not expect this guy's soul to be so strange! How many secrets did he have?
Seeing that Sima Yulong did not move, Luo Zheng slightly unfolded the longsword in his hand and said with a smile, "The fourth sword?"
Sima Yulong shook his head. "It's not the fourth sword. It's the seventh sword …"
Just now, in order to resist the remaining power of Luo Zheng's sword, Sima Yulong had consecutively slashed out several times to neutralize it. However, he had counted those slashes.
"What a serious guy …" Luo Zheng said with a smile, "Then it's the seventh sword!"
Sima Yulong nodded. "If I can't defeat you with this sword, I'll admit defeat."
Luo Zheng's sword hilt trembled slightly, and his eyes became incomparably clear. Although Sima Yulong was prideful to the bone, he was very frank. He said, "Then make your move!"
At this time, all the cultivators in the Dragon Tiger Palace understood that Luo Zheng was indeed not someone to be trifled with.
As for Tie Feiyu, he naturally understood that he was indeed not qualified to challenge Luo Zheng … Even Sima Yulong would find it difficult to defeat Luo Zheng. If he was to fight against Luo Zheng, he would not be able to last even one round!
As for those debauchees from the Holy City who were looking for trouble with Luo Zheng, they now deeply understood that they were simply courting death by recklessly provoking Luo Zheng. Fortunately, this guy had a good temper. Otherwise, they would have died countless times already. Even if they were Divine Sea realm cultivators and Luo Zheng was only in the fifth layer of Life and Death realm …
The next move would most likely determine the victor. They all held their breaths and quietly observed the movements of the two people in the Illusory Space.
As for the Sacred Lord and many Elders, they were somewhat dispirited.
Victory or defeat was no longer important.
In fact, with Sima Yulong's cultivation, challenging Luo Zheng was originally unfair.
The Sacred Lord did his best to facilitate this challenge only to help Sima Yulong enter the Thirteenth Palace. Sima Yulong had already undergone the ninth heavenly tribulation. Although he was not a Divine Sea realm cultivator, he was far stronger than ordinary Divine Sea realm cultivators.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng was only in the fifth layer of Life and Death realm. There was a huge gap between their cultivation bases.
With Luo Zheng's cultivation base, it should not be difficult for him to obtain the ninth minor heavenly tribulation. At that time, Luo Zheng's strength would most likely far surpass Sima Yulong's.
Therefore, they did not care about the outcome of this battle.
However, at this moment, the Sacred Lord suddenly thought of something important.
"This brat has already undergone five minor heavenly tribulations. Has he opened his lotus flower? How many leaves has he opened? "the Sacred Lord suddenly asked.
At the side, Elder Gong slapped his head and said, "That's right. With such talent, he must have opened his lotus flower. I wonder how many leaves he has …"
The Sacred Lord looked at Luo Zheng indifferently and said softly, "I'm afraid this brat has opened seven leaves."
This time, an expression of disagreement appeared on Elder Gong's face. "Actually, it's very likely that he's at the ninth leaf …"
In truth, the Sacred Lord also wanted to say that Luo Zheng was at the ninth leaf. However, his current state of mind was extremely unbalanced. He was like a bag of gold that had fallen on the ground and was picked up by someone else. Naturally, he hoped that there was less gold in the bag. The less gold there was, the more balanced he would feel.
Unfortunately, Elder Gong was not as self-deceiving as the Sacred Lord. Instead, he voiced his own opinion.
The Sacred Lord could only sigh faintly. "If I knew this would happen, I would've crossed out Luo Zheng's name when the alliance asked us to submit the list of ascenders."
Elder Gong chuckled. "At that time, we didn't know there was such a person among the ascenders of the Spirit Martial Saint Land. If it weren't for the Thirteen Palaces, I'm afraid this brat would still be mining!"
The Sacred Lord glared fiercely at Elder Gong after hearing Elder Gong's words that displeased him. Elder Gong jumped in fright. Then, the Sacred Lord said coldly, "Check all the ascenders in the mines within a month. Evaluate their strength and talent, then report back to me!"
Elder Gong's expression was extremely unsightly. The Sacred Lord was so angry that he went crazy. Naturally, the Spirit Martial Saint Land had a test for ascenders. Was there a need to test them in such a big way? Not everyone was like Luo Zheng …
Unfortunately, the Sacred Lord's order could not be disobeyed. Since the Sacred Lord was willing to do so, then so be it. However, Elder Gong believed this was destined to be a futile effort.
While the Sacred Lord was venting his frustrations, Sima Yulong finally began to prepare his final sword move. It was also the sword move that would determine the victor!
"Sword painting, mountains and rivers, a wisp of golden wind turns into myriad colors …"
The Sword of Mountains and Rivers in Sima Yulong's hand began to fly rapidly!
In front of him, a magnificent pattern began to form rapidly. It was as if the Sword of Mountains and Rivers was a brush in his hand. He was using the sword to paint the entire landscape!
The golden light was the Law of Gold, and the green light was the Law of Wind!
As he continued to paint, he would cut his arm from time to time. Spurts of blood essence flew into the air and dyed the golden landscape red!

＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1015
This Sword Painting Rivers and Mountains consumed an enormous amount of blood essence!
It was not an easy task for any cultivator to replenish their blood essence after they had lost it.
However, he didn't want to lose to Luo Zheng.
Sima Yulong didn't think that he was invincible in the same realm.
In the eyes of many martial artists, the Spiritual Martial Saint Land was a colossus. However, in the Spiritual World, it could only be ranked third.
As for the first two Holy Lands of the Spirit Channeling World, for example, the Spirit Channeling Holy Land had many outstanding geniuses, such as the son of the Spirit Channeling World Lord …
However, Luo Zheng was just an ascender.
In the Lower Realm, whether it was the resources or the environment, it was far from comparable to the Upper Realm. Therefore, the starting point of ascenders was much weaker than the martial artists of the Upper Realm.
This kind of impression was engraved in the bones of the martial artists from the Upper Realm. Even Sima Yulong was no exception.
As his blood essence continued to splatter, the golden mountains and rivers were dyed with a trace of bloody aura!
This country was not a peaceful one with songs and dances. Instead, it was a country where heroes fought for hegemony!
Countless blood-stained soldiers were displayed in this sword painting. The bright red color served as the finishing touch to this enormous painting of mountains and rivers!
At this point, Sima Yulong's sword was ready! Immediately after, he slashed out with his sword.
"Pop …"
The sword light was like a pair of sharp scissors, cutting the Landscape Painting into two!
All the images on the Landscape Painting began to distort and converge towards his sword light. At the same time, they all rushed towards Luo Zheng!
"He's here!"
Facing Sima Yulong's all-out attack, Luo Zheng quickly retreated.
The power contained in this sword strike was extremely special. Although the Mountain River State Painting had been drawn using the Metal and Wind laws, and it had been combined with his Essence Blood …
However, it created an extremely unique concept!
This kind of artistic conception not only brought psychological pressure to Luo Zheng, but also the power it contained could not be underestimated.
However, Luo Zheng was no longer the same as he had been in the past. He had several ways to deal with this attack. Among them, the simplest and crudest method was to use the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain in his hand.
Not even a Divine Realm warrior could do anything to the Divine Essencefused Mountain. His sword couldn't even move it a bit.
However, Luo Zheng did not intend to use this move.
After all, he and Sima Yulong were just fighting. Although Sima Yulong had used a Secondary Grade One Divine Tool, Luo Zheng had also used the Divine Magnetic Mountain.
However, Luo Zheng didn't intend to rely on the Divine Essencefused Mountain. Instead, he wanted to break this sword with his own strength. He needed to verify the difference between himself and the geniuses from the Upper Realm …
The perfect sword intent in Luo Zheng's hand spiraled out, and the chaotic energy in his body also slowly rotated!
His Sword Intent hadn't been tempered since it had reached perfection. He only had his own comprehension of sword moves, and as Chaotic Qi poured into it, his Sword Intent was constantly improving!
"I'll use a Divine Tool to break this sword!"
After Luo Zheng rapidly retreated to a certain point, he had already made up his mind. He actually rushed towards Sima Yulong's sword-light head-on.
Seeing this, Sima Yulong's face once again revealed a look of surprise. What was this kid trying to do?
He originally thought that Luo Zheng had some special trump card, or some defensive magic treasure that could block his move, or perhaps he had some practical movement technique to avoid the sharpness of his sword.
However, this guy didn't dodge or evade. He was still alone. Was he going to use a high-grade Divine Tool to meet his attack?
In the Dragon Tiger Palace, the thousands of warriors were also confused. Just now, Luo Zheng had erupted with the power of the Law of Wind. They finally realized how powerful Luo Zheng was. Therefore, although they were full of confidence in Sima Yulong's Sword Painting the Landscape, they were also worried in their hearts.
Who knew what kind of trump card Luo Zheng had?
However, Luo Zheng didn't seem to have used any trump cards. He was still using Qi to drive his Sword Intent. This meant that Luo Zheng either didn't take Sima Yulong seriously, or he was courting death!
The Holy Lord of the Spiritual Martial Holy Land didn't pay much attention to this battle. The result didn't matter. He simply couldn't keep Luo Zheng in the Holy Land. However, seeing Luo Zheng use this method to meet Yulong's strongest move, a strange look appeared on his face …
"This kid is really unconventional. Every move is unexpected …" Elder Gong murmured, "But he's probably overthinking this move. He's just using the perfect Sword Intent to fight against the Sword Painting. This is a bit of a fool's talk. But then again, there's no danger to his life in the Illusory Space. Perhaps this is also his first attempt … This kid's thoughts are too terrifying."
Elder Gong actually guessed some of Luo Zheng's thoughts, but he didn't guess the next result!
When Luo Zheng was caught in the sword light, countless soldiers suddenly appeared in front of him. Each soldier held a sword and slashed at Luo Zheng!
"This sword light … is really like an army of tens of thousands. It's invincible!"
What was more terrifying was that the illusions of the soldiers seemed to overlap. Every time they attacked, there were tens of thousands of soldiers attacking. These tens of thousands of swords slashed at Luo Zheng. If he relaxed even a little bit, he would be cut into pieces.
Faced with such a scene, Luo Zheng's attack didn't stop at all!
The long sword in his hand slashed out countless times in an instant. In order to increase the speed of his attack, Luo Zheng also operated the Law of Time at this moment!
The sword light in his hand danced out thousands of times in a breath's time!
"Ding … Ding! Ding! Ding! Ding!
Ding! Ding! "When the sword light collided, the sound of explosions rang out!
"Strangle!"
The Sword Intent Cyclone spread out and shredded the tens of thousands of illusions into pieces. However, the number of soldiers in the Landscape Painting was almost infinite. Luo Zheng crazily killed them, but the soldiers in the Landscape Painting kept pouring out. It was impossible to kill them all, and the number was increasing.
Sima Yulong's face was calm. The Landscape Painting's Concept was a combination of endless vitality. How could it be destroyed by the long sword in your hand? You're too arrogant to use a single sword to resist my move. The result will only be humiliation …
Just as Sima Yulong thought, Luo Zheng was indeed in trouble!
Although Sima Yulong's sword light was powerful, it wasn't particularly sharp. However, in this endless realm, the illusions of the soldiers surged forward like a tidal wave. There was no end to them, no end to them!
In addition to the ordinary soldiers, there were also some generals wearing red armor! The illusions of those generals were even more powerful, and Luo Zheng found it even more difficult to deal with them.
At this moment, Luo Zheng was in a dilemma!
"Sima Yulong's move can't be endless …" When Luo Zheng released the Sword Intent, his face revealed a contemplative expression.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed upwards. Sima Yulong's sword light was like a maggot in a tarsal bone, following closely behind Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng's figure paused for a moment before the sword light engulfed Luo Zheng again. Then, Luo Zheng was still immersed in the endless illusions.
"Found it!"
This sword-light had absorbed the Landscape Painting. This landscape was a human's landscape, and it had a ruler!
Just now, when Luo Zheng pulled away, he found that a yellow-robed monarch was hidden in the depths of the sword light.
These soldiers, generals, and monarchs should also be a kind of visualization technique, but it was more profound and extensive than the ones in the lower realm …
"It's him!"
After confirming his target, Luo Zheng's figure didn't retreat at all. Instead, he rushed straight towards the monarch!
As long as he killed the monarch, the sword light condensed from the Landscape Painting would naturally disperse!
Seeing Luo Zheng's actions, Sima Yulong's mouth revealed a trace of a smile. "You finally found him, but can you really defeat him?"
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …

Chapter 1016
The Sovereign King exuded a heavy and majestic aura. Seeing Luo Zheng charging towards him amidst the thousands of soldiers and horses, he suddenly stood up, his face revealing an unquestionable majesty.
Then, with a loud roar, the Sovereign King's Intent Realm suddenly turned into a whole, condensing into a long sword in his hand. Then, he directly slashed down at Luo Zheng!
The power of this sword hid the fiercest killing intent within the sword light!
However, facing this sword, Luo Zheng's face revealed a smile!
Facing the endless illusory killing intent, Luo Zheng might have found it difficult to deal with, but now that the entire Empire was in the hands of the Sovereign King, Luo Zheng was unafraid!
"Dragon Scale Power!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng activated the power of three thousand dragon scales. His sword slashed from bottom to top, and the two sword lights intertwined.

"Bang!"
A crisp sound rang out as the broken sword spun and flew toward Sima Yulong!

Even though he was seriously injured, Sima Yulong didn't move. He was still staring at Luo Zheng, who was in the middle of the sword light. In the illusory space, even if he died, it didn't matter. He just wanted to know if Luo Zheng could withstand this move of his! If he lost, then it would be his victory. Therefore, he didn't have any means to deal with the broken sword that pierced his chest.
Right now, Luo Zheng's sword was broken. What could he use to withstand the sword of the Sovereign King of the Empire?
Nowadays, high-grade sacred artefacts were no longer worthy of Luo Zheng. An ordinary long sword was only two inches wide, with a sharp blade and an extremely thin body.
Just like Luo Zheng's body, it could only withstand the strength of five to six thousand dragon scales. He had just used the strength of three thousand dragon scales, so the Saint artifact sword naturally could not withstand it. Coupled with the heavy pressure from the Sovereign King's sword, it was broken!
It was just that Luo Zheng couldn't get a better sword in the Mortal World. If he could open the weapon-refining workshop in the Immortal Mansion, the situation might be very different. However, Luo Zheng had only walked through two floors of the Path of True Desperation, and it would take a long time for him to completely open it.
Without his sword, Luo Zheng still had his fists!
At this moment, Luo Zheng threw away the other half of the sword and grabbed the Sovereign King's sword with his bare hands!
He had carried the weight of the country with his bare hands!
"Pu!"
Luo Zheng gripped the edge of the sword in his hand. Instantly, blood spurted out, but at the same time, his other fist was already smashing towards this sovereign king!

But they saw Luo Zheng brandishing his fist, and for some reason, smashing Sima Yulong's sword light into pieces.
Many warriors of the Dragon Tiger Palace also felt that it was a little inconceivable. They couldn't understand how Luo Zheng did it. It seemed like a divine skill, but Luo Zheng really did it.

Sima Yulong forced a smile and said, "It's too painful for us to talk like this. Let's go back to the Dragon Tiger Palace first …"
Although one wouldn't die in the illusory space, the projection still felt pain. Having a broken sword pierced through the chest was not a pleasant feeling.
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng and Sima Yulong in the Dragon Tiger Palace opened their eyes.
After Luo Zheng stood up, he looked around. The genius martial artists of the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall all had similar thoughts in their hearts. Although they couldn't see how Luo Zheng had broken through Sima Yulong's sword technique, by relying on a single person and a high-grade saint artifact, defeating Sima Yulong was a foregone conclusion!
On the other hand, Sima Yulong was the number one person in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall. More importantly, his Jiangshan Sheji Sword was a Secondary Grade One Divine Artifact. In comparison, one could imagine how big the gap between Sima Yulong and Luo Zheng was!
Luo Zheng might have some trump card that he hadn't used yet. They didn't want to know. The strength that Luo Zheng had displayed was enough for them to look up to.
Sima Yulong moved in front of Luo Zheng and bowed deeply to Luo Zheng. "I used to have some prejudice against those who ascended from the Lower Domain. Now it seems that I was wrong. The Lower Domain … is not as weak as I thought!"
The other warriors in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall couldn't help but nod.
Warriors from the Upper Domain did have an inexplicable sense of superiority in front of warriors from the Lower Domain. Even if some ascenders had become Celestial Venerables, the warriors below Celestial Venerables still seemed to be disdainful when they mentioned it. They thought that ascenders becoming Celestial Venerables was just luck.
They didn't know that any warrior who reached a certain level of achievement relied on luck and strength. Similarly, warriors from the Upper Domain couldn't jump out of this rut.
Luo Zheng just smiled and didn't say anything.
He didn't care about other people's contempt. As the saying went, the strong were always strong. If he was swayed by other people's views, what qualifications did he have to stand in this world?
After leaving the Dragon Tiger Palace, Luo Zheng returned to that corner and continued to cultivate with his eyes closed …
The genius warriors of the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall came and went. When they looked at Luo Zheng again, their faces revealed reverence. This guy had the right to look down on the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall! He had the strength to be proud! Naturally, there wouldn't be any idiots who would make irresponsible remarks …
In fact, there were still some people in the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall who were unconvinced. All the top ranked genius warriors didn't watch the battle at the Dragon Tiger Palace because of various reasons. When they rushed back from outside or came out of seclusion, they wanted to challenge Luo Zheng.
However, these people were all stopped by the people of the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall. If they wanted to challenge Luo Zheng, they had to defeat Sima Yulong first. Otherwise, they would only be humiliating themselves.
As for the Holy Lord of the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, he was very unhappy these days, very unhappy …
He wanted to have a talk with Luo Zheng. He wanted to find some leeway in this matter. He wanted to see what price he could pay to keep this kid in his Spirit Martial Holy Land.
If Luo Zheng could stay, his Spiritual Martial Holy Land would have a big advantage in the competition with the other two tenth-rank Holy Lands in the Spiritual World among the younger generation. They could even rely on Luo Zheng to make a comeback and suppress the Spiritual Martial Holy Land!
The competition between the younger generation of warriors might not determine the structure of a big world, but it could determine the ups and downs of these Holy Lands in the future!
However, when he thought about the Thirteen Palaces, he dismissed this idea. Since the Thirteen Palaces had appointed Luo Zheng, they must have a good understanding of Luo Zheng.
Originally, when he saw Luo Zheng's Divine Weapon broken in the Illusory Space, he thought about giving Luo Zheng a second-rank or even a first-rank divine weapon to win him over. However, on second thought, if the Spiritual Martial Holy Land could offer something, how could the Thirteen Palaces not be able to?
Seeing such a little guy slip away from his hands, the Holy Lord's heart was filled with deep unwillingness!
In the Ten Thousand Spirit Martial Hall, Luo Zheng meditated quietly and cultivated. As he cultivated, he felt that the sixth minor tribulation was approaching. However, he didn't know if there was a difference between undergoing the tribulation in the Upper Realm and the Upper Realm.
As he waited and cultivated, time passed quietly. However, the "big shot" who had appointed him didn't appear …

Chapter 1017
All along, Luo Zheng had been curious about this' big shot 'and was even wary of him.
After all, he seemed to be spying on him from the shadows.
Where did he find out about his existence? Where did he find out that he was ascending to the Spirit Martial Sacred Land?
Hence, after hearing the elder's words, Luo Zheng stood up abruptly.
Under the elder's lead, he once again headed towards the hall from before.
In this hall, all the elders of the Spirit Martial Sacred Land were already seated. The one sitting at the head wasn't their sacred lord but another woman!
The woman was wearing a nine-colored dress. The nine colors on the dress were woven from the feathers of nine different divine beasts. The light blue feathers were the tail feathers of an ice phoenix. Even a single tail feather exuded a piercingly cold aura. As for the dark red feathers, they were taken from the tail feathers of the fire phoenix. As for the green feathers, they were taken from the softest fur of the Tong Tian Rat's back …
Just the value of this nine colored long dress was immeasurable!
The problem was that even though so many precious materials were used, they were only used for decoration. The person who made this nine-colored dress was extremely extravagant and wasteful.
A pair of small white feet stepped on wooden clogs and swayed on the chair in boredom. Her eyes indifferently swept across the many elders in the large hall. Every time their eyes met with this girl's eyes, their hearts would tremble slightly.
Actually, this woman's cultivation wasn't very terrifying. She was only at the peak of half step sector lord.
And in this hall, their sacred lord was already a quasi-sector lord. Of course, there was only one great realm master in each realm. The Spirit Connecting Realm's realm master was the sacred lord of the Spirit Martial Sacred Land, not their Spirit Martial Sacred Land's sacred lord. Even Elder Gong was already at the peak of half step sector lord. He was only a step away from quasi-sector lord!
What everyone was afraid of was not her cultivation or strength, but her identity.
She was one of the thirteen palace masters. The most terrifying thing was that her father was an Empyrean. She was an Empyrean descendant! Her position in the alliance went without saying!
At this time, several cultivators suddenly appeared in the hall. One of them exuded an aura that was several times stronger than the Black Tortoise Holy Lord!
After he entered the main hall, he bowed to the lady and said, "I am Tong Ling Zi, the Realm Lord of the Spirit Channeling World. I welcome the Palace Lord of the Vast Cloud Heavenly Palace!"
This martial artist was the true master of a realm, and also the master of the strongest tenth-rank Holy Land in the Spiritual Communication World!
This time, the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace's Palace Lord came to the Psychic World personally. Although he didn't pay a visit to the Psychic Sacred Ground and came straight to the Spiritual Martial Sacred Ground, Tong Ling Zi still had to be there to welcome him personally!
However, a trace of impatience appeared on her face. The Spiritual World was only a remote great world. If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, she probably wouldn't have come here in her entire life. As for the title of Realm Master, she wasn't interested in it at all.
However, she was the Realm Lord of a Great World after all. She could only smile and reply. Tong Ling Zi sat on the second seat, above the Holy Master of the Spirit Martial Saint Land.
When Tong Ling Zi heard that the Palace Lord of the Vast Cloud Heavenly Palace had come personally, he was also very surprised!
Although he had received news that an extraordinary ascender had appeared in the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground and was chosen by the Thirteen Palaces, there were a lot of ascenders.
It wasn't strange for some powerful geniuses to appear occasionally. Even if they were chosen by the Thirteen Palaces, it wasn't strange either. The world was so big, and there would always be such lucky people.
The question was, what kind of person was worthy of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace's Palace Lord coming personally?
Wasn't it just an ascender? Did he have such a big face? Although the Psychic Sacred Ground couldn't take this ascender in, he still wanted to take a good look and see what kind of person this ascender was. Was it worth it for the Psychic Sacred Ground to make such a big fuss?
"Is he not here yet?" Yun Luo muttered in a low voice.
Although her voice was soft, how could the people in the main hall not hear her? Tong Ling Zi's face revealed a slight smile. Although the Psychic Sacred Ground and the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground belonged to the alliance, they were still in the same great world. After all, they were competing with each other …
As for the Sacred Leader of the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground, his face revealed a trace of awkwardness. He immediately replied, "He should be here soon. Palace Lord, please wait a little longer …"
As soon as he finished speaking, Luo Zheng had already stepped into the main hall. He walked directly into the depths of the main hall. When he walked halfway, his eyes were attracted by the nine-colored long dress. When their eyes met, Luo Zheng laughed and said, "So it's you, Yun Luo!"
After being puzzled for so long, the mystery was finally revealed.
However, at this time, many elders in the main hall sucked in a breath of cold air. At the same time, they stared at Luo Zheng with uncertainty.
Even the Sacred Leader of the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground and the Realm Lord of the Psychic Sacred Ground stared at Luo Zheng with a strange gaze. This kid directly called the Palace Lord's name. Wasn't it a little too casual?
Naturally, they didn't know that Luo Zheng had encountered Yun Luo's doppelgangers twice. He had always treated Yun Luo as someone of the same generation as him. After a long time, Luo Zheng was used to it. When he saw Yun Luo this time, a trace of surprise appeared on his face. Although he knew that she had a high status, he couldn't make Luo Zheng treat Yun Luo with the respect of an elder.
However, Luo Zheng also noticed that the temperament of the Yun Luo in front of him was completely different from that of the Yun Luo in the Lower Realm.
Of course, this confusion had already been solved by Qinglong.
The Yun Luo in the lower realm was just a clone of hers. The Million Spirit Clones Technique had many drawbacks. The clone's emotions and memories were incomplete. In other words, there were flaws in their personalities. Therefore, whether it was the Yun Luo in the Eastern Region or the Yun Luo in the Sin Tower, their words and actions were very strange.
It wasn't the indifference of those ice beauties, but the flaws in their personalities. They lacked the emotions of joy, anger, sorrow, and joy.
As for the Yun Luo in front of him, she should be a complete person. The temperament she exuded was completely different. It could be said that she looked more like a "person"!
Yun Luo smiled faintly. She stared at Luo Zheng and said, "I said that I can't decide where you go, but you still ascended to this place! Since you're in my territory, I have no choice but to accept you! I'm afraid that the people of the Demi-Immortal Clan won't find trouble with me! "
Hearing Yun Luo's words, the Realm Lord of the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground, the Sacred Leader of the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground, and the elders' expressions changed again.
This kid had been targeted by the Demi-Immortal Clan?
Although there were few people in the Demi-Immortal Clan, they were all elites. Although their actions and intentions were quite strange, no force dared to look down on them!
The key was that joining the Demi-Immortal Clan was even better than joining the Alliance. Of course, the conditions for joining the Demi-Immortal Clan were more stringent. In the Upper Realm, it was not easy for ordinary people to be recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan!
Luo Zheng smiled and continued to ask, "How did you find me?"
Yun Luo smiled and rolled her eyes. She looked very mischievous. Then, she smiled and said, "I don't know who shouted so loudly. If I didn't hear it, wouldn't I be deaf?"
When Yun Luo heard Luo Zheng use the Roaring Command and deduced that it was Luo Zheng from the name Luo Yan, she collected a list of all the people who had ascended in the past year in the 3,000 Realms. This list was given to Yun Luo. It only took her 30 minutes to find Luo Zheng among countless names! Moreover, there were two Luo Zheng …
There were many people who had ascended, and there were always times when there were people with the same name. However, after Yun Luo casually ordered to investigate the appearance and other characteristics of the two Luo Zheng, she was sure that Luo Zheng had ascended to the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground!
However, at that time, her Million Spirit Clones Technique was in the final stage, so it was naturally inconvenient for her to go there. She had almost absorbed all her clones and hurried over from the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace …
Hearing Yun Luo's words, Luo Zheng immediately understood that she had identified his voice from the Roaring Command. However, if she knew that he had the Roaring Command, he was afraid that she would find out that he had the Roaring Command!
As for the Realm Lord of the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground and the Sacred Leader of the Spirit Martial Sacred Ground, their expressions suddenly became serious. "Why are you shouting so loudly? What do you mean? "

Chapter 1018
This Sector Lord, Saint Lord, and even those elders were all extremely intelligent people.

In the universe, only the Roaring Token could do that!

Thinking of this, they all stared at Luo Zheng in shock.

The Realm Lord of the Spiritual Realm and the Sacred Lord of the Spiritual Martial Sacred Land naturally felt strange and inconceivable. . This explanation.......

.... It's fine if you know that I have a Roaring Command, but why did you hint it in front of so many people? Are you trying to kill me?

Although the Spiritual Martial Sacred Land had obtained one quota, the Sacred Land's Realm Lord thought that this quota was created because of Luo Zheng, so there was nothing wrong with them taking one more quota. However, the Spiritual Martial Sacred Land's Realm Lord didn't think so. Luo Zheng ascended to the Spiritual Martial Sacred Land was just lucky. Therefore, the other tenth-grade Sacred Land thought that with three quotas, each Sacred Land could get one quota. Wouldn't that be perfect?

…
A carriage was floating in the sky above the Spiritual Martial Sacred Land.
The carriage's craftsmanship was incomparably exquisite. Its entire body was purple, and there were strings of exotic flowers and plants growing around the carriage.
The most surprising thing was the two horses in front of the carriage. These two horses were a kind of Divine Beast!
Generally speaking, creatures that had reached the level of Divine Beasts were extremely arrogant and prideful. They could sign a contract with a warrior and become a warrior's contracted beast, but they couldn't be enslaved or tamed by a warrior!
This "Heavenly Luan Horse" was one of the few Divine Beasts that could be tamed. However, even if one wanted to tame them, it would take a lot of effort and time. Therefore, this Heavenly Luan Horse was not only an excellent mount, but also a symbol of status!
The others respectfully sent Yun Luo off. She then entered the carriage with Luo Zheng.
From the outside, this carriage looked ordinary. However, after opening the door, it was a completely different world. The inside was as spacious as a palace.
"First, tell me, where did you get so many Roaring Tokens?" Yun Luo asked with a smile.
"This …" Luo Zheng didn't know how to explain.

Chapter 1019
Yun Luo was not an untrustworthy person.
Moreover, she already knew that he seemed to have an unlimited number of Roaring Tokens.
However, it would be quite troublesome to explain the Roaring Tokens to Yun Luo. It also involved the immortal mansion, his master, and many other problems.
Therefore, Luo Zheng could only choose not to explain.
In fact, Yun Luo also knew that Luo Zheng would not tell the truth. Since he did not want to explain, Yun Luo did not force him. At least, Luo Zheng did not deny it.
However, Yun Luo immediately asked, "The mysterious woman who sang that day, do you know her identity?"
Luo Zheng subconsciously shook his head.
That mysterious woman's Roaring Token seemed to be the same as his, also endless. It was very likely that her Roaring Token was the same as the one in Luo Zheng's hand, which could be used countless times.
But who knew who she was?
"Oh …" Yun Luo nodded, then continued to look at Luo Zheng.
The current Yun Luo was her complete body. In a sense, this was the first time she and Luo Zheng "met".
Her doppelganger made the decision to let Luo Zheng ascend freely, and the Demi-Immortal Clan made the same decision. They did not forcibly bring Luo Zheng into the Demi-Immortal Clan. Yun Luo was very satisfied with this. However, by coincidence, Luo Zheng still ascended to the Alliance. This was Luo Zheng's fate.
Fateless people did not have a definite path, but no matter what Luo Zheng's future path was, she did not mind giving Luo Zheng a push from behind.
Luo Zheng's endless Roaring Tokens were worth it. The Roaring Tokens were very useful to many races. In the future, she might have to trouble Luo Zheng with many things. For example, she did not have many Roaring Tokens, so she could ask Luo Zheng to help her pass on messages.
When Luo Zheng was looked at by Yun Luo like this, he suddenly felt as if he had been seen through. It was as if he was standing naked in front of Yun Luo, as if there were no more secrets he could hide.
Luo Zheng also knew that even though Yun Luo's cultivation was very strong, she didn't have this ability. It was only because the gap between their cultivations was too big that she felt this uncomfortable …
Luo Zheng felt the same way when the Holy Lord of the Lingwu Holy Land was sizing him up.
It could only be said that Luo Zheng's cultivation was too low. In the lower world, he was already a peak existence, but in the upper world, the Life and Death Realm was just the beginning!
Most importantly, there was still no response to Luo Yan's message. Perhaps it was just as Xun had said. The young man who had appeared in the Void Spirit Sect was the person whom the entire Vast Thousand Domains feared. He was an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao. Luo Yan was already outside the Heavenly Dao …
Luo Zheng was very unwilling to come to this conclusion, but the possibility was undoubtedly quite high. If he wanted to find Luo Yan, his current strength was far from enough.
After thinking about Xun, Luo Zheng suddenly looked at Yun Luo and asked, "Yun Luo, do you have any way to awaken Xun?"
"Xun?"
Yun Luo was stunned when she heard Luo Zheng's words. The memories of her million doppelgangers were too vast. Her million doppelgangers had seen too many people and things. It was very difficult for her to accurately remember a person, but Luo Zheng was an exception.
However, she still managed to find the existence of "Xun" through Luo Zheng's memories …
"If I remember correctly, only a strand of her soul remains of the Demon Night Clan's King of Massacre," Yun Luo faintly smiled.
The disappearance of the Demon Night Clan's King of Massacre wasn't a secret in the universe. The three kings were their symbols. If Xun completely fell, a new King of Massacre would be born in the Demon Night Clan to replace her.
Luo Zheng nodded and didn't say anything.
Yun Luo continued to stare at Luo Zheng and smiled, "You want to help her take back the Demon Night Clan's throne?"
Luo Zheng nodded again.
Yun Luo continued with a smile, "The Demon Night Clan is an existence that we humans can't ignore. Although the other two kings are only sector lords, they are the kings of the Demon Night Clan. Many times, their words are more effective than Sky Sovereigns. Xun's position is very important. Your current strength is far from enough."
"I will improve my cultivation," Luo Zheng said with a straight face.
Seeing Luo Zheng's serious expression, Yun Luo suddenly chuckled, "I'm not your master, so I can't control your thoughts. You can control your own thoughts. I didn't interfere when you were on the Sea God Continent, so I won't interfere now. Once we reach Misty Cloud Palace, I will help you awaken Xun and repair her shattered soul."
"Repair!" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up.
Strictly speaking, Xun's main soul had already fallen. The strand of soul that accompanied Luo Zheng was only a remnant soul.
Luo Zheng was naturally happy to be able to awaken Xun, but he didn't expect Yun Luo to promise to repair her!
Seeing Luo Zheng's happy expression, Yun Luo smiled faintly and said, "Let's go."
At the same time, the sound of horses neighing came from outside the carriage. Then, the entire carriage trembled and sped forward.
In that instant, the carriage had already charged into the spatial passageway!
"Eh …" There was no spatial tunnel here, but the carriage entered it as soon as it moved. A trace of curiosity appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
"It's the Heavenly Luan Horse's innate ability. It can freely enter spatial tunnels," Yun Luo explained.
Luo Zheng nodded. Ever since he ascended, he was full of curiosity about the Upper World. He was afraid that he would need some time to adapt to it.
To a martial artist, crossing boundaries was an extremely extravagant matter. In fact, 99% of martial artists would never be able to leave their own great world in their entire lives. Thus, the 100,000 great worlds that existed in the universe were nothing more than a concept and a legend to martial artists. Everyone knew of the existence of these great worlds and had heard the roars of many Empyreans and World Masters, but they had never seen them in their entire lives.
Now, Luo Zheng was following Yun Luo. They had to cross thousands of worlds to reach the core world of the Alliance.
After the two Heavenly Luan Horses entered the spatial tunnel, they galloped at an incredible speed. What surprised Luo Zheng was that the Heavenly Luan Horses seemed to be accelerating. This kind of divine beast's initial speed was not particularly fast. Luo Zheng might be able to catch up with it if he used all his strength.
The problem was that the Heavenly Luan Horses seemed to have no limit to their speed! After running for a day, their speed had reached a level that Luo Zheng could not imagine. On the second day, their speed was more than twice as fast as the first day. As for the third day, their speed was doubled again on the basis of the second day …
A few days ago, Luo Zheng was still able to observe the surrounding scenery in this world passage, such as some warriors flying in the same direction and so on.
However, on the fourth day, Luo Zheng could no longer see the scenery outside the window. After reaching such a speed, everything turned into a line. It was impossible to distinguish whether it was a person or something else …
This world passage originally had countless checkpoints that required a fee to pass. If it was an ordinary warrior, the cost of passing through a world would be an astronomical figure. However, from the beginning to the end, no one dared to stop Yun Luo's ride.
Even if they wanted to stop her, it was impossible to do so at this speed …
On the sixth day, Yun Luo told Luo Zheng that they had left the World of Spirits.
"Leaving the World of Spirits just now …" Luo Zheng originally thought that with this speed, they would have already entered another world.
Based on their current speed, it would take them about 20 years to cross more than 1,000 great worlds before they could reach their destination!
However, the Heavenly Luan Horses' speed was still increasing!
After leaving the Spirit Channeling Realm, they entered the trans-realm passageway. This trans-realm passageway was much wider than the ones built by great realm passageways. Luo Zheng couldn't even begin to imagine building these passageways and connecting thousands of great realms together like pearls.

Chapter 1020
It took them about three days to cross the second great world.
As for crossing the third great realm, it only took a day's time.
As time passed, they passed by one great world after another. All of these great worlds were occupied by humans. This was only one of the three great forces of the Human Race. It was easy to imagine how powerful the Human Race was in the whole universe.
During this period of time, Luo Zheng also immersed himself in cultivation.
A month later, Luo Zheng felt the exquisite carriage finally stop. He slowly opened his eyes and looked out the window. He saw an orange crescent moon hanging in the sky.
"We're here." Yun Luo pushed open the door of the carriage and walked out first.
Luo Zheng followed Yun Luo out of the carriage. Suddenly, he thought of something and asked, "Then … if Sima Yulong wants to come here, how long will it take?"
Yun Luo thought for a moment and said, "If the Sacred Lord of the Sacred Land doesn't help him, he will have to find a way to get here. Even if he continuously passes through the passages, it should take him two or three hundred years. It should be enough …"
Two or three hundred years? Even his hair would turn white by this time. Luo Zheng was speechless. Of course, Sima Yulong was the pride of the Lingwu Sacred Land. The Sacred Lord naturally wouldn't let him waste hundreds of years on this journey. Moreover, crossing so many great worlds would cost a lot of money. Sima Yulong probably couldn't afford it.
"Let's go. When you are strong enough, you will understand that the universe is actually not that big." After saying this, Yun Luo still walked in front.
Watching her slowly walk forward, her nine-colored dress constantly swaying. Luo Zheng smiled and followed her.
In fact, this Yun Luo felt very unfamiliar to Luo Zheng. The two Yun Luos he met were very different from her. Although he knew the reason, the feeling of unfamiliarity still existed. Only the swaying of her long dress made Luo Zheng connect them.
After walking for a while, a big city appeared in front of Luo Zheng. The big cities in the Upper Realm were huge for Luo Zheng. Each city was comparable to the Central Domain. Luo Zheng was too lazy to find out how big this city was.
And in this large city, Luo Zheng saw a huge stone pillar. Because it was so huge, even from hundreds of miles away, Luo Zheng could still see it clearly. This stone pillar was tens of thousands of feet tall, and a majestic palace stood at the top of this stone pillar. This palace was like the crown of a tree, and it spread out at the top of this stone pillar. Luo Zheng wondered how the architect built this palace up there …
"This is the Misty Clouds Palace, one of the Thirteen Palaces," Yun Luo said calmly. "Follow me in!"
After saying that, Yun Luo waved her hand and an invisible force enveloped him.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng felt his body flash once in space. In the next moment, he appeared in another place.
Space Shift …
Luo Zheng could travel in a small space, but the Misty Clouds Palace was so far away from him. He was brought in with just a space jump. It could only be Space Shift.
Just like Sima Yulong said, warriors in the Upper Realm must master the two great Laws. They must be the Law of Space and the Law of Time. Yun Luo's control of the Law of Space was quite profound.
After entering the Misty Clouds Heavenly Palace, there were thousands of stairs in front of them. Every three steps, there was a warrior standing there. On top of the stairs, there were hundreds of warriors. Every one of them was at the Divine Sea Realm. They all looked solemn and stood straight.
Every time Yun Luo stepped on a new step, those soldiers lowered their heads and saluted her. But Yun Luo acted as if she didn't hear them and continued walking.
Right at this moment, a few streaks of light appeared in the sky. They arrived from the horizon in an instant and landed by Yun Luo's side in the blink of an eye.
Luo Zheng's eyes swept over this group of people. The one leading them was a thin middle-aged martial artist. He had a hooked nose and his eyes were slender like willow leaves. His gaze was sharp and piercing. Luo Zheng still couldn't see through this person's cultivation, but the aura he exuded was no different from Yun Luo's!
"He's also a Sector Lord?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
"Palace Lord, you're back. I'm here to welcome you!" The aquiline nose bowed to Yun Luo, but there was no respect in his words.
This made Luo Zheng a little confused. As he now understood, Yun Luo was the Palace Master of the Misty Cloud Palace of the Thirteen Palaces. Since the person in front of him called himself a subordinate, it seemed that he did not take Yun Luo seriously.
Yun Luo didn't seem to be happy to see the aquiline nose. "No need to welcome me! I still have things to do. You can leave now. "
The hook-nosed man stroked his chin and didn't retreat when he heard Yun Luo's words. Instead, he looked at Luo Zheng with interest and even sniffed his nose. He chuckled and said, "I don't know where Palace Master found this little guy. He smells like an Ascender, right?"
Hearing this, Yun Luo blinked and stared at the aquiline nose, asking, "What do you think?"
The aquiline nose smiled, "Nothing. I don't pick up trash. Palace Lord, do you want to see the two newcomers I brought back this time?"
As soon as his voice fell, two young cultivators behind him looked over, their faces filled with pride. These two cultivators were only in the Life and Death Realm, but like Sima Yulong, they were also cultivators who had undergone nine minor Heavenly Tribulations!
The two of them naturally did not dare to be disrespectful to Yun Luo, but their eyes were full of disdain when they looked at Luo Zheng intentionally or unintentionally.
Yun Luo looked at the two and said indifferently, "Not bad. It seems that my Misty Clouds Palace can cultivate two more geniuses."
The aquiline nose smiled, "Of course. This one is the son of the Realm Lord of the Great Wheel Realm. When he was just born, his father used the power of the Great Wheel Realm to purify his body … The other one has inherited the bloodline of the Heavenly Rat. His lotus flower has already sprouted seven leaves. Hehe, I guess he'll be qualified to join Swallow Cloud Hall after he breaks through the Divine Sea Realm!"
"Oh," Yun Luo replied indifferently. It seemed that she didn't want to argue with the aquiline nose here. She was about to leave with Luo Zheng.
Unexpectedly, the aquiline nose still stopped in front of Yun Luo and continued, "Palace Lord, I wonder what this boy said that made you bring him back in person? He seems to have a strong background. "
"I can see that. I wonder where this ascender came from!"
"Hehe, I can smell the dirt on him from several feet away!"
The two cultivators with extraordinary backgrounds behind the hook-nosed man spoke up.
In fact, they were very respectful to Yun Luo, but this time, they saw that the hawk-nosed man didn't put Yun Luo in his eyes. They also saw that the hawk-nosed man originally wanted to trample on Yun Luo's face. The two of them were quick-witted, so they naturally opened their mouths to target Luo Zheng.
Yun Luo's face showed a trace of anger. She seemed to be a little afraid of the aquiline nose, but it was inconvenient for her to lose her temper. She couldn't think of an appropriate way to deal with it.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled, sniffed, and said, "Why can't I smell it?"
One of them said sarcastically, "How can you smell yourself if you don't smell it after staying in Baosi for a long time?"
"Oh!" Luo Zheng sniffed and said, "So that's how it is. I guess the two of you can't smell yourselves either!"
"What smell do we have?" One of the warriors asked faintly.
"Of course it's the smell of idiots," Luo Zheng said with a smile and covered his nose, "Although it's not smelly, it's smelly enough. We shouldn't stay here for long!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Yun Luo laughed. Although she knew that Luo Zheng was a guy who wouldn't suffer losses, she didn't expect his words to be so sharp.
"How dare you!" One of them said angrily.
"How dare you!" Luo Zheng said immediately.
"Boy, what are you …"
"Boy, what are you? Do you know who my father is …" Luo Zheng continued to say, and even said the second half of his sentence first.
