Chapter 1021
Luo Zheng's imitation was perfect, and he was even able to say what the other party wanted to say first. This immediately stunned the other party.
The martial artist was stunned, "How did you know that I …"
"How did you know what I wanted to say?" Luo Zheng continued, "Isn't that simple? Because it's easy for an idiot to guess!"
"Pfff …"
Even as the Palace Master of Misty Cloud Palace, Yun Luo couldn't help but laugh.
As for the aquiline-nosed man, his face was extremely ugly. His eyes were cold as he stared at Luo Zheng.
The eyes of the two geniuses from the Great World were like knives, wanting to cut Luo Zheng to death …
Luo Zheng's move was learned from the old man from the Demi-Immortal Clan. Every time he spoke, he would speak first. Sometimes, it really made people feel depressed.
He knew how to observe people's expressions. He could see that the aquiline-nosed man and Yun Luo didn't get along. Since they weren't on the same side, Luo Zheng didn't have any scruples. He had always been like this.
The hook-nosed man had originally recruited two good seedlings and wanted to show off in front of Yun Luo. Originally, the results were indeed good. Martial artists who could open the Seven Petal Lotus were rare in the universe. Naturally, he was somewhat proud of himself. However, he did not expect that his good mood would be ruined by this kid.
"Brat, martial artists don't rely on their mouths to eat." The aquiline-nosed man stared coldly at Luo Zheng and said.
"I'll have to trouble you. I have my own master, I don't need you to teach me." Luo Zheng replied with a straight face. Normal martial artists would be very nervous when facing a Sector Master level martial artist, but some arrogant martial artists might be very excited, but Luo Zheng's mentality was very stable!
If he was too nervous, he would lose face for Yun Luo, but if he was too aggressive and didn't control his words well, he would give the other party something they could use against him. Although Luo Zheng had just scolded someone, he hadn't scolded the hook-nosed man, but the two cultivators behind him who were of the same generation as him. Facing the hook-nosed man, he was neither servile nor overbearing, and his performance was flawless.
"Master?" The aquiline-nosed man laughed, "I don't know what cultivation level the master of a martial artist from the lower realm has."
Yun Luo's face also revealed a strange expression. Luo Zheng … had a master? In her memory, all along the way, Luo Zheng relied on himself. He didn't have a master.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng said seriously, "I don't know."
"You don't even know your master's cultivation level." The aquiline-nosed man shook his head.
But then, Luo Zheng's master. "A faint smile appear on Luo Zheng's face.............
Hearing this, the hook-nosed man was stunned. Even Yun Luo was shocked. An existence that the entire universe looked up to? .............
. Luo Zheng then added, "Of course, that's in my mind!"
When the hook-nosed man heard these words, he realized that he had lost his composure. His emotions seemed to have been unintentionally stirred up by this little brat, and his train of thought followed this little brat. He was a dignified Sector Lord, and no matter how big the matter was, he would not be affected by it. However, he was stunned by a few words from this little brat. This was indeed embarrassing!
If they weren't in the Misty Clouds Palace, he would have killed this kid ten thousand times already!
But Luo Zheng's words to him were not disrespectful at all. He wanted to reprimand Luo Zheng, but he didn't know where to start. Finally, he could only grit his teeth and said coldly, "Let's go! To the Divine Weapon Hall! "
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
These people didn't seem to want to stay here for a moment. In the blink of an eye, they left quickly.
As for Yun Luo, she was beaming with joy. She stared at Luo Zheng and said, "I really underestimated you!"
Luo Zheng sighed softly and said, "I can't help it. I've met too many people like this. I'm used to it …"
In this world, there were always some people who were looking for trouble and would unintentionally jump in front of him. If he could fight, of course, Luo Zheng would 'teach' the two boys a lesson. But in the current situation where he couldn't fight, he could only use his mouth.
After this incident, Yun Luo's mood suddenly improved. As she walked, she told Luo Zheng that the aquiline-nosed man was called Xu Can, and he was the Deputy Palace Master of the Misty Clouds Palace!
Xu Can had a strong background. He was also the son of a Celestial Master. After Xu Can entered the Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace, he wanted to compete with Yun Luo for the position of Palace Master. He wanted to force Yun Luo down, so the two of them had never gotten along.
This time, Xu Can wanted to take the opportunity to ridicule Yun Luo, but he was driven away by Luo Zheng. Of course, Yun Luo was very happy.
After walking up the stairs, Yun Luo stretched out her hand to Luo Zheng and said, "Give it to me!"
"What?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Don't you want to help Xun recover her soul?" Yun Luo asked.
"Now?" Luo Zheng's face was filled with astonishment.
Yun Luo shook her head and said, "Xun was once a half step sector lord. It's impossible to completely recover her soul in a short period of time. But if you want to wake her up and recover half of her soul, I have a way."
Hearing Yun Luo's words, Luo Zheng happily took out the Soul Nurturing Wood. He checked Xun in the Soul Nurturing Wood and felt a warm feeling in his hand. Xun had been asleep for quite some time. Now that she could finally wake up, what would her expression be when she woke up and found herself in a great world?
After passing the Soul Nurturing Wood to Yun Luo, Yun Luo gave Luo Zheng a jade scroll and a small jade pendant. She said, "This jade scroll contains the map of the Misty Clouds Palace. Those who enter the thirteen palaces will have the chance to pick a weapon for free from the God Weapon Hall! It's up to you what weapon you can pick. "
Weapon …
"What's the grade of weapon? Is there a god artifact? "Luo Zheng asked.
Yun Luo didn't know whether to laugh or cry. "What do you think the thirteen palaces are? You don't even have a god artifact … "Yun Luo shook her head. She naturally knew that Luo Zheng didn't understand the meaning of the thirteen palaces in the alliance!
The top geniuses of the three thousand worlds gathered here. If there were any weapons below god artifact in the God Weapon Hall, wouldn't it be a joke?
Luo Zheng nodded. In the battle with Sima Yulong, his saint artifact was broken. Of course, the illusion space was just a projection. His high grade saint artifact naturally wasn't broken. But it did reveal that Luo Zheng didn't have a suitable weapon.
Since he had the chance to pick a weapon, why would Luo Zheng let it go?
While Yun Luo was helping Xun recover her soul, Luo Zheng was following the map in the jade scroll and traveling through the Misty Clouds Palace.
Previously, in the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall of the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, there were top geniuses of a tenth grade Holy Land. But in the Misty Clouds Palace, as one of the thirteen palaces of the alliance, there were thousands of geniuses of the Holy Lands gathered here!
Any one of them was enough to become a core member of a tenth grade Holy Land. For example, the Spiritual Martial Holy Land also wanted to put Sima Yulong into the Thirteen Palaces to train him. They hoped that he could get better resources and opportunities.
After traveling through the vast Misty Clouds Palace for a long time, Luo Zheng found the God Weapon Hall!
At the entrance of the God Weapon Hall, there were also two guards. The cultivation of these two guards was much stronger than the guards on the stairs. They were two experts of the Divine Realm.
In front of the armored soldiers, there were quite a number of warriors surrounding them.
These warriors were very young, about the same age as Luo Zheng. The older ones were only in their thirties. They gathered together and pointed at the God Weapon Hall.
"Today, the God Weapon Hall seems to have added a few more first grade god artifacts!"
"So what if there are more? Ordinary people can't get them … "
"I'll try my luck!"
"Hey, it costs 300 Zhen Yuanyu to try. If you don't feel the pinch, go ahead and try it!"
These warriors should be the geniuses sent to the Thirteen Palaces by various big realms. Hearing their words, Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression.
Entering the God Weapon Hall to pick a weapon required 300 Zhen Yuanyu?

Chapter 1022
Standing at the entrance of the Divine Weapon Hall, Luo Zheng observed for a while. Then, he saw two people walking over.
They were the two people who had a conflict with Luo Zheng on the steps of the Misty Cloud Palace. They held a small jade pendant in their hands and waved it in front of the two Ultimate Divine Realm armored soldiers. Then, they walked into the Divine Weapon Hall.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng suddenly understood.
He was stupid. Yun Luo had given him a jade pendant. Of course, there was a use for it.
So, he walked in front of the armored soldiers and waved the jade pendant. Then, he followed the two warriors and entered the Divine Weapon Hall.
As for those martial artists behind, they actually wanted to squeeze in behind Luo Zheng with a hubbub.
The two armored soldiers at the entrance had gloomy faces and blocked them.
"Uncle, we're just going in to take a look. Anyway, without the jade pendant, we can't choose a weapon …"
"It doesn't affect anything. Just make an exception."
Luo Zheng looked back. He was speechless. It was just choosing a weapon. What was there to see? It seemed that there was no lack of lively people everywhere. It was the same in the Upper Realm.
Unexpectedly, their pleading was effective. The armored soldiers only said, "After entering, don't make a noise! If you violate it, you will be expelled immediately! "
After getting the approval of the two armored soldiers, the warriors followed Luo Zheng into the Divine Weapon Hall.
"Hey, hey, hey, newcomer …" One of the warriors at the eighth level of the Life and Death Realm patted Luo Zheng's shoulder from behind.
However, before he could pat Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng lowered his shoulder and twisted his body slightly. He easily avoided the pat. Then, he asked indifferently, "What's the matter?"
The warrior was stunned for a moment. Then, he said, "What grade of weapon do you want to choose?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng replied, "Naturally, the more suitable the weapon, the better."
"Hehe, that's a good answer. Nowadays, these juniors are eyeing those high-grade divine weapons. They don't know that the one that suits them is the best!" An old man with white eyebrows sat at the side of the Divine Weapon Hall.
Luo Zheng nodded at the old man and smiled faintly. The old man said, "If you want to choose a weapon, just give me the jade pendant."
After giving the jade pendant, Luo Zheng looked at the Divine Weapon Hall.
In the center of the Divine Weapon Hall was a wide dome. There were many balls of light floating in the dome..
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and looked over. He saw that the ball of light contained weapons of various shapes and sizes.
"Divine weapons …"
No wonder Yun Luo didn't know whether to laugh or cry when she talked about it. She probably thought that she had underestimated Misty Cloud Palace.
Every weapon in the balls of light was a divine weapon.
Perhaps Luo Zheng's words made the old man with white eyebrows feel comfortable, so he said a few more words for Luo Zheng. "The weapons in the Divine Weapon Hall are all divine weapons. However, the divine weapons below the dome are divided into good and bad grades. The divine weapons at the bottom are the Secondary Grade Three, which is the worst grade."
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. The Secondary Grade Three was the Semi-Divine Weapon in the four Divine Kingdoms.
"The higher you go, the higher the grade of the divine weapons. However, it is more difficult to get them. If you want to get a suitable weapon, you have to rely on your own ability!" The old man with white eyebrows said faintly.
At this moment, the two warriors who had a bad relationship with Luo Zheng came from the other side of the Divine Weapon Hall. There was one more person beside them. This person's cultivation was already in the Divine Sea Realm.
On the stairs, Luo Zheng had ridiculed the two warriors in front of the Vice Palace Master. When the two saw Luo Zheng, how could they be happy?
One of them saw Luo Zheng and said to the Divine Sea Realm warrior, "Brother, that's the guy!"
"Oh?" The Divine Sea Realm warrior looked at Luo Zheng faintly and walked over. "A newcomer in the Life and Death Realm, and an ascender. How can you be so arrogant? It's really rare …"
This warrior of the Spirit Sea Realm was not that old, so it was inappropriate to call Luo Zheng "little guy".
The warriors who were watching the show became cautious when they saw the Divine Sea Realm warrior. They were muttering in their hearts, "How did this newcomer offend this guy?"
The Thirteen Palaces of the Alliance was originally a place to cultivate the core talents of the 3,000 Great Worlds. The purpose was to gather the most talented warriors and nurture more powerful warriors.
The Misty Cloud Palace was only one of the palaces. It was interdependent and competitive with the other twelve palaces.
In the Misty Cloud Palace, the status of warriors was divided into two halls.
Warriors who had just entered the Heavenly Palace were members of the Lingyun Hall. Luo Zheng, for example, had not gone through a series of procedures yet. He was purely a newcomer. If he went further, he could join the Swallow Cloud Hall.
The Divine Sea Realm warrior in front of Luo Zheng was called Xie Mang. Although he was also a member of the Lingyun Hall, his ranking was very high. Maybe he could be promoted to the Swallow Cloud Hall in the next batch.
Xie Mang was not only powerful, but also very talented. He was also the son of a Realm Lord. Today, his younger brother Xie Jun was also invited to the Heavenly Palace. Naturally, he came personally and brought his younger brother to pick a weapon.
Along the way, Xie Jun told him that there seemed to be a very arrogant newcomer who dared to talk nonsense in front of the Vice Palace Lord. When he saw Luo Zheng, it was inevitable that he would ridicule him.
Hearing Xie Mang's words, Luo Zheng looked indifferent. Previously, he only spoke to help Yun Luo. Now that the other party was clearly provoking him, Luo Zheng did not even bother to speak. Instead, he asked the old man with white eyebrows, "Is there only one chance to pick a weapon?"
The old man with white eyebrows nodded, "Yes. According to the rules of the Divine Weapon Hall, there is only one chance!"
Luo Zheng smiled and was about to enter the dome to pick a weapon. Unexpectedly, Xie Mang said, "My younger brother came first. Of course, they can pick first."
The old man with white eyebrows seemed to see eye to eye with Luo Zheng. This time, he looked at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng shrugged and revealed a casual expression. The old man with white eyebrows said, "It doesn't matter who came first. Both of you can enter and pick a weapon."
Xie Mang patted Xie Jun's shoulder and glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng. The people who came to the Thirteen Palaces were all geniuses of various Great Worlds. However, Xie Mang was able to establish himself in the Lingyun Hall. How could he put Luo Zheng in his eyes? In the future, there would be plenty of opportunities to make this kid suffer. There was no need for him to do it personally. He said to his younger brother, "Do your best and strive to get a Secondary Grade One Divine Weapon!"
Most of the cultivators here actually had a background. However, not everyone could get a Secondary Grade One Divine Weapon.
Sima Yulong's Jiangshan Sheji Sword was given to him by the Sacred Lord. If he left the Myriad Spirit Martial Hall, it would return to its original place.
Xie Jun nodded and walked to the center of the dome. After glancing at the sky, he suddenly leaped up!
"Buzz …"
The moment Xie Jun leaped up, the light spots on the dome flew up in an instant!
When Jun Xie reached a height of six meters, his speed began to slow down. It seemed that he was having a hard time doing so ….
Seeing this scene, the surrounding warriors also began to discuss in low voices.
"Xie Mang's strength is valiant, but his younger brother is ordinary …"
"Let alone a Secondary Grade One Divine Weapon, I'm afraid he won't even be able to get a Secondary Grade Two Divine Weapon."
"I wasted a spot. I might as well give him a Secondary Grade Two Divine Weapon in exchange for the jade pendant. Let me go up and try …"
Luo Zheng originally didn't understand what was going on with the dome. Now that he saw it, he understood. Xie Jun was a ninth stage Life and Death Realm cultivator, which was similar to Sima Yulong's cultivation. However, it wouldn't be easy for him to get a better weapon.
Xie Jun was struggling more and more. In the end, he was barely able to catch a light spot before falling down from the dome. His face was red from holding his breath.
He had only obtained a single Third Grade Divine Artifact. It was a golden handaxe, and its value was estimated to be around twenty to thirty True Essence Jades. Putting aside the question of whether it was worth anything, the main thing was that he had lost a great deal of face!
"I don't want this!" Xie Jun said stubbornly after getting up. He threw the golden hand axe aside and tried to fly up to the dome again.
However, the white-browed old man stretched out his hand and gently squeezed. An invisible force suddenly pressed down on Xie Jun. Xie Jun, who had just left the ground, felt like he was hit by a hammer and fell to the ground again!
"The weapons in the Divine Weapon Hall are free for the first time. The second time you get them, you have to pay three hundred Zhen Yuanyu," the white-browed old man said indifferently. "If you want to go up again, you have to pay the Zhen Yuanyu first!"

Chapter 1023
Xie Mang couldn't help but feel embarrassed when he saw that his younger brother had only managed to obtain a third-grade divine artifact despite his efforts.
Xie Jun crawled up from the ground with an unwilling expression on his face.
With their status, they didn't even place a Tier 3 Divine Artifact in their eyes. Xie Jun said angrily: "I underestimated the pressure of the dome! Brother, I want to try again! "
The price for another attempt was 300 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, which was equivalent to 3,000,000 top-grade True Origin Stones. It was an astronomical figure for most cultivators.
Of course, it was also a considerable sum for the cultivators of Misty Cloud Palace. Although it was painful, Xie Mang waved his hand and took out 300 pieces of True Origin Stones and threw them on the white-browed elder's table.
The white-browed elder smiled faintly and accepted the 300 pieces of True Origin Stones. Then, Xie Jun jumped up again and headed for the dome.
In fact, with Jun Xie's strength, he could have easily grabbed a secondary grade two divine artifact. However, when he fell down, he only managed to grab a grade three divine artifact. He was extremely unwilling to give up and fell into the heart of a gambler. Since he had wasted a chance, this time he was going to get it back with interest!
His target turned out to be a Secondary Grade One Divine Weapon …
He had set his sights on a first-grade divine artifact, but in the end, he was knocked down by the enormous pressure and landed heavily on the ground. He didn't manage to obtain anything.
Xie Mang frowned when he saw this.
As for the other warriors in the Divine Weapon Hall, they also let out a sigh. These three hundred True Origin Jades were equivalent to being thrown into the water.
This time, Xie Jun was too embarrassed to ask him to try again. He could only silently retreat to the side.
At this time, Xie Mang took out another 400 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu and handed them to the white-browed elder. He then said, "Here are 400 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. Let me try this time!"
"400 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He had only asked for 300 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. Why did Xie Mang ask for an extra 100 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu?
However, Luo Zheng's confusion was immediately solved by the cultivators' discussion.
"I'm going to faint. Xie Mang is really ambitious. He's eyeing the first-grade divine artifacts!"
"But Xie Mang is already very strong. Now that he's a Divine Sea Realm cultivator, he might be able to obtain a first-grade divine artifact!"
"Hehe, he wants to earn back what his younger brother lost with interest. However, the pressure in the dome is adjusted according to the cultivation of the cultivator. These first-grade divine artifacts have been here for who knows how many years, but how many have been taken away? I heard from a senior in the Lingyun Hall that the last time a first-grade divine artifact was taken away was probably a hundred years ago. "
First-grade divine artifact?
Luo's eyebrows's's.
When he was in the Lower Realm, whether it was the Central Domain or the Sea God Continent, the weapons he used were all top-grade. However, when he came to the Divine Kingdom Continent, they fell behind.
However, the most important reason was that Luo Zheng relied too much on his own body and not the weapon in his hand. This was because his body was a weapon. However, when he came to the Divine Realm, Luo Zheng also understood the importance of a top-grade divine artifact.
Although the Black Star Pickaxe was a supreme divine artifact, it was not suitable to show it in public. Moreover, it was not suitable to use it in battle.
"Let's observe for a while first," Luo Zheng thought to himself.
After the white-haired old man received the 400 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, he still had a faint smile on his face. Then, he stretched out his hand and a picture scroll appeared in his hand. The picture scroll was engraved with illustrations of various divine artifacts.
"The Divine Weapon Pavilion's first-grade divine artifact, the Blood Dripping Spear, has been borrowed by a deacon. Apart from that, there are 16 other first-grade divine artifacts. You can choose any four of them. I will place them on the dome," said the white-haired old man.
"I know the rules." Xie Mang nodded. His gaze swept across the picture scroll.
After that, Xie Mang casually gave some pointers and picked out four weapons from the sixteen first-grade divine artifacts.
From the looks of it, Xie Mang was a sword cultivator. The four weapons he chose were all first-grade divine artifacts. They were the Scarlet Flame Immortal Melody Sword, the Ancient Book Sword, the Shadow Sky Sword, and the Ten Directional Howl Sword.
After Xie Mang made his choice, the old man stretched out his hand and lightly flicked it. Everyone saw four red dots of light enter the dome. The red dots of light were very eye-catching among the white dots of light. However, after floating for a while, the red dots of light floated to the highest point of the dome.
Obviously, these first-grade divine artifacts were priceless items. It would not be easy to obtain them.
Not to mention a Divine Sea Realm martial artist, even a Divine Reaching Realm martial artist or someone of a higher realm would not be able to obtain a first-grade divine artifact so easily.
For example, when the elders and deacons of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace did not have a suitable weapon, they would need to borrow these weapons from the Divine Weapon Hall. When they returned, they would also need to return these weapons.
However, placing these first-grade divine artifacts here was actually just a form of encouragement. Whether it was the Lingyun Hall or the Swallow Cloud Hall, very few martial artists could obtain them. Those who could obtain first-grade divine artifacts were as rare as phoenix feathers and unicorn horns.
Xie Mang had the intention to challenge him. Moreover, his younger brother had given him a free opportunity. In addition to the three hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu just now, it was not a small expense for him. If he could obtain a first-grade divine artifact, it would be a hundredfold profit!
Moreover, if he obtained a first-grade divine artifact, not only would he obtain treasures, but he would also become famous in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, Xie Mang walked to the center of the dome. He did not rush to the top of the dome. Instead, he made some preparations. First, he swallowed an elixir. This elixir could increase the power of the Zhen Yuanyu in his Inner World by at least twenty percent. This elixir was worth thirty to forty pieces of Zhen Yuanyu. However, since he had already given away four hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, he naturally had to throw in this elixir as well.
After swallowing the elixir, he took a deep breath and then slowly left the ground.
He knew that there would be a delay in the gravity of the dome. Although the delay was very short, less than a blink of an eye, it was very precious to him.
This was because the gravity of the dome would sense the power of the cultivator. When the cultivator increased his power, the gravity of the dome would increase accordingly!
Therefore, Xie Mang only slowly flew up into the sky. Even so, the force was still very powerful. Moreover, as he flew higher and higher, the gravity he felt also became stronger and stronger.
At this moment, there were many white specks of light floating around Xie Mang. Those specks of light contained Secondary Grade Three Divine Artifacts. Weapons of this level were naturally not worthy of his attention. He did not even spare them a glance as he continued to increase his own strength.
Soon, the light spots floating around him were Secondary Grade Two divine artifacts. Then, they were Second Grade Two divine artifacts …
The cultivators in the Divine Weapon Hall began to discuss again.
"He has already reached the area of Second Grade Two divine artifacts. In fact, he won't lose anything by casually grabbing a Second Grade Two divine artifact!"
"Yes, Second Grade Two divine artifacts are not bad. However, since he took out an extra hundred pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, his target must be the First Grade Two divine artifacts …"
"First Grade Two divine artifacts are still very difficult. If he can't grab a First Grade Two divine artifact, I'm afraid his efforts will be in vain."
At this moment, Xie Mang's face was flushed red. Under the pressure of the terrifying gravity, even an inch higher was comparable to ascending to the heavens. However, he still gritted his teeth and slowly increased his altitude. He was too lazy to even look at the white light spots floating around him.
In his eyes, there were only those red light spots. They were the First Grade Two divine artifacts!
As he increased his altitude inch by inch, he swept past the Second Grade Two divine artifacts and arrived at the area of Secondary Grade Two divine artifacts.
At this moment, he was less than ten feet away from those red light spots. Looking at the four red light spots floating in the sky, Xie Mang's eyes flashed with a hint of malevolence.

Chapter 1024
Xie Mang's original plan was to explode when he was about five feet away from the red dots of light. He wanted to take advantage of the fact that the gravity had not abruptly increased to rush up.
This experience was said to have been left behind by a senior who had once used this method to obtain a first-grade divine artifact.
However, Xie Mang was already nearing his limit. It would be extremely difficult to ascend even an inch. At that moment, he was only relying on his willpower to forcefully hold on.
After a few breaths, he finally managed to ascend a quarter of a foot. He realized that he was already an arrow at the end of its flight. If he did not explode now, he might not have another chance. He clenched his teeth and the True Origin Sea in his body suddenly surged with monstrous waves.
Xie Mang, who had been slowly ascending, suddenly sped up.
However, the moment Xie Mang unleashed his power, the gravity pressing down from the dome increased exponentially!
"I've been tricked!"
Didn't they say that there would be a delay in the increase in gravity? Why did the gravity increase the moment he exploded?
The old man sitting at the table sneered, "Walking the crooked path and not relying on your own strength, you will fail in the end!"
In fact, Xie Mang had not been tricked.
Using this method to obtain the divine artifact on the dome was indeed feasible for a period of time.
The gravity runes on the dome were also constantly detecting the situation of the cultivators. However, there was a certain delay in this detection. Some cultivators discovered a loophole and flew into the dome to suppress their strength. When they were close enough, they would suddenly erupt with all their strength.
Although the delay was very short, not even the blink of an eye, it was enough to allow the cultivators to ascend by quite a bit.
Since it was a loophole, it was naturally not allowed. Although the Divine Armament Hall did not explicitly forbid it, they had secretly repaired the divine patterns on the dome to block the loophole.
Right now, Xie Mang had been tricked by this loophole.
Although he had exploded with the True Origin Sea in his body, the gravity had also multiplied. Although the explosion had increased his height by several feet, there was still some distance between him and the nearest red dot of light.
The most despicable thing was that the red dot of light seemed to be deliberately drifting toward his hand. The distance between the two was only one foot. However, this one foot was like a natural chasm to Xie Mang.
"Let's fight!"
At this moment, Xie Mang's eyes turned red. He bit the tip of his tongue and began burning his essence blood!
It was very difficult to make up for the loss of blood essence. Even if it was possible to make up for it, one would have to pay a considerable price.
The current Xie Mang was like a gambler who had lost everything. The more he lost, the more he had to recoup his losses. As such, he had to invest even more in order to win back all that he had lost in one go.
However, Xie Mang seemed to have forgotten that the Divine Rune of Gravity would increase according to the power Xie Mang unleashed. After he ignited his blood essence, he obtained an upward force, but the gravity became even more terrifying …
One foot, half a foot, one inch …
Despite the immense gravity, Xie Mang was only an inch away from the red dot of light!
If he remembered correctly, the treasured sword in this light spot was the Ten Directional Howl Sword!
He had studied the Ten Directional Whistling Sword in detail before. The heavy sword was not heavy for the wielder, but in reality, it contained the weight of ten thousand mountains. The wielder could control this weight as he wished, so a single slash was equivalent to the power of ten thousand mountains.
Once he obtained it, he would have earned it back!
Xie Mang clenched his teeth. He could feel that his fingertips had already touched the red dot of light. As long as he moved up half an inch, he would be able to grab the dot of light in his hand!
However, at this moment, a scene that caused him to despair occurred. The dot of light nimbly jumped and moved away from Xie Mang …
The dots of light above the dome were not fixed existences. They were like little fairies, flying all over the dome.
Seeing this scene, Xie Mang could no longer hold his breath. His strength weakened and his entire body was directly pressed down by the gravity!
While being pressed down by the gravity, Xie Mang's hands fluttered wildly in an attempt to grab another divine artifact.
"Rumble!"
A loud sound suddenly exploded in the Divine Weapon Hall. Xie Mang's body had already heavily smashed into the floor. As he fell, he did manage to grab a divine artifact. Lying on the floor, Xie Mang picked up the divine artifact and looked at it. He was so angry that he almost vomited blood. It was actually a Secondary Grade Three Divine Artifact! Even his younger brother, Xie Jun, could not compare to it.
"He still failed!"
"Actually, it's not bad. This Xie Mang is indeed not an ordinary person. He just lacked a bit of luck!"
"Don't forget that he must have burned his blood essence. Xie Mang must have suffered a great loss!"
After Xie Mang crawled up from the ground, his face was filled with depression. He threw the Secondary Grade Three Divine Artifact aside.
Seeing that Xie Mang was angry, the white-browed old man smiled. "Do you want to try again?"
"There's no need!" Xie Mang said coldly. After investing so much, he only obtained a Secondary Grade Three Divine Artifact. He felt that it was quite embarrassing. He did not want to stay here any longer and prepared to leave with Xie Jun.
At this time, Luo Zheng handed the jade pendant to the old man and asked, "If I want to take the first-grade divine artifacts, do I have to pay an extra hundred Zhen Yuanyu?"
The jade pendant in Luo Zheng's hand was the opportunity to enter the dome and obtain a divine artifact. In fact, it was equivalent to three hundred Zhen Yuanyu. Adding an extra hundred Zhen Yuanyu meant that Luo Zheng's intention was very obvious. He wanted to obtain the first-grade divine artifact.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Xie Mang, who was about to leave, suddenly stopped. A mocking smile appeared on his face. He said indifferently, "Hehe, you've just entered the Misty Clouds Heavenly Palace and you want to take the first-grade divine artifact? You're overestimating yourself!"
After saying that, he did not leave. He really wanted to see if Luo Zheng was bragging or if he really had the courage to choose a first-grade divine artifact! It was like some people who fell down in a place and hoped that others would fall down in the same place. This way, they could prove that they were not the only idiot who made a mistake here.
The old man with white eyebrows laughed and said, "That's the theory. However, according to the rules, if someone pays an extra hundred Zhen Yuanyu today, he can also take the first-grade divine artifact above!"
In other words, Luo Zheng did not need to pay the extra hundred Zhen Yuanyu and he would not take the four first-grade divine artifacts above the dome.
Luo Zheng nodded indifferently and said, "But I still want to see the drawing!"
It was not that Luo Zheng was greedy, but Luo Zheng knew that it was not easy to choose a weapon that suited him. Therefore, he wanted to take a look at it.
"Sure." The old man with white eyebrows nodded indifferently. He opened the drawing and handed it to Luo Zheng.
On the first page of the drawing, there were a total of sixteen first-grade divine artifacts. Xie Mang had chosen four swords from these sixteen artifacts.
Luo Zheng skimmed through them one by one. The four swords were the only sword-shaped weapons among the sixteen first-grade divine artifacts. The others were sabers, spears, bows, and even a bag of arrows …
With just one look, Luo Zheng knew that he did not need to pay the extra hundred Zhen Yuanyu. However, when he flipped the drawing, he found that there was a second page.
"Huh? The second page? "Luo Zheng's face revealed a curious expression.
There were only three weapons on the second page!
Xie Mang sneered when he saw Luo Zheng's expression. "You don't need to look at the second page. Those things are not for you to control!"
Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at Xie Mang and said, "I'll look at it myself. What does that have to do with you?"
Xie Mang was originally still angry. When he heard a junior who had just joined the Misty Cloud Palace speak to him in such a manner, he was immediately filled with killing intent. Beside him, Xie Jun said, "Brother, this kid's mouth is so cheap!"
Sensing Xie Mang's killing intent, Luo Zheng did not move. Instead, he looked indifferently at Xie Jun with a mocking expression and continued to look at the second page of the drawing.
The old man with white eyebrows said, "The rules of the Divine Weapon Hall cannot be broken. If you want to fight, fight outside!"

Chapter 1025
In fact, the second page wasn't a secret in the Divine Weapon Hall.
The Misty Cloud Palace had this tradition for generations.
When the previous Palace Master left his post, he would leave behind one of his treasures and place it in the Divine Weapon Hall.
These three weapons were the weapons of the previous three Palace Masters.
Among the first-grade divine weapons, there were good ones and bad ones!
Naturally, the three divine weapons of the previous Palace Master couldn't be mentioned in the same breath as ordinary first-grade divine weapons!
Therefore, there was this rule. If one wanted to place the first-grade divine weapon on the first page into the dome, one had to pay one hundred Zhen Yuanyu. Then, one could place any four of them.
If one wanted to place the first-grade divine weapon on the second page into the dome, one still had to pay one hundred Zhen Yuanyu. However, one could only place one weapon.
Luo Zheng carefully looked at the three weapons.
Two of the three weapons were sabers. They were the Pure Yang Mystic Yellow Saber and the Flaming Sun Shadow Fire Saber!
Luo Zheng didn't practice saber techniques, so saber-type weapons weren't suitable for him. After a casual glance, he skipped the illustration.
But then, Luo Zheng's eyes stopped on the last weapon.
It was a sword.
"Thunder and Wind Divine Sword!"
The sword was five feet long!
It contained the power of wind and thunder. The sword's momentum was like the wind, and the sword's edge was like thunder …
I held this sword for two hundred thousand years. When I left, I placed this sword in the Divine Weapon Hall.
Those who are fated to take it with their own strength!
It's a pleasure to meet you. I hope this sword can shine more brilliantly!
Luo Zheng carefully read the words below the sword. These words should have been left by the previous Palace Master of the Misty Cloud Palace.
After reading, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He took out one hundred Zhen Yuanyu from his Sumeru Ring and handed it to the old man with white eyebrows.
The old man with white eyebrows smiled and asked, "Kid, have you made your choice?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.

Luo Zheng pointed lightly and said, "That's it, the Dark Thunder Wind Divine Sword!"

"Could it be that this Palace Lord has already reached the Five Decays of Heaven and Man?" Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Zheng was young, but the time scale in the Upper Realm was far from what he could imagine. Since he had left his position, it was very likely that he had entered the Five Decay of Celestial Being.
Actually, Luo Zheng was asking if the Palace Master had fallen by mentioning the Five Decays of Celestial Beings.
The white-browed old man sighed and shook his head. "No, this is the sword left behind by Palace Master Xiao. Not only did he not fall into the Five Decay of the Celestial Being, he is probably trying to break through to the Empyrean Realm!"
"Attack the Celestial Venerable Realm!" Luo Zheng took a deep breath.
The white-browed old man nodded but did not say much. He just flicked his finger lightly, and a blue light spot appeared in the dome.
Now, there were many white light spots floating under the dome, and there were four red light spots at the top. However, the blue light spot was floating at the highest point of the dome!
"Blue, blue light spots … This kid actually chose the divine weapon on the second page of the illustrated handbook!"
"Why are there blue light spots? Red light spots can be seen occasionally. Although I have never seen anyone successfully take a first-grade divine weapon, someone has challenged it. However, this is the first time I've seen a blue light spot!"
"Nonsense, it's impossible to take the weapon on the second page of the illustrated handbook! There's still a slight chance of getting the first-grade divine weapon on the first page, but the second-grade divine weapon on the second page … Hehe, although everyone has a lot of Zhen Yuanyu, who would throw Zhen Yuanyu into the water? "
"This kid is too arrogant. I guess he doesn't want to take the first-grade divine weapon at all. He just wants to spend 100 Zhen Yuanyu to see the good fortune …"
Many cultivators looked at the blue light spot above their heads and began to talk about it.
As for Xie Mang and Xie Jun, the smile on their faces became even wider. Xie Mang said, "I've been in the Misty Cloud Palace for so long, but I've never seen an idiot who actually put the weapon on the second page into the dome. The ignorant are truly fearless!"
Xie Jun smiled and said, "If you have too much Zhen Yuanyu and no place to spend it, you can just give it away. Why waste it?"
Luo Zheng naturally did not have many True Essence Jades to spend. These hundred True Essence Jades were returned to him because the armored soldiers of the Spirit Martial Holy Land were especially merciful. His entire net worth was only a little more than a hundred True Essence Jades. Now that he had placed such a heavy bet, his goal was naturally very simple. He must obtain this Thunder and Wind Ghost Divine Sword!
Luo Zheng merely smiled in response to Xie Jun's words. He did not have the time to waste on this brat as he walked towards the center of the dome.
Xie Jun's strength and talent were not worth mentioning in Luo Zheng's eyes. However, his brother Xie Mang was an opponent that Luo Zheng had to pay attention to. Even if Xie Mang wanted to fly to the top of the dome, it would be very difficult for Luo Zheng to touch the blue light spot.
After adjusting his mentality, Luo Zheng took a deep breath and gently circulated the chaotic energy in his body. His figure quietly left the ground.
The moment Luo Zheng ascended, a force pressed down on his body.
"What a strong gravity." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed faintly.
This gravity was the same as the one in the space of the mineral tree in the Divine Kingdom's continent. However, it was not as tyrannical as the one in the space. The gravity of the mineral tree could not be resisted at all. As long as a leaf fell, it would be directly pulled down.
The gravity in front of Luo Zheng did not cause any trouble. He did not rush up, but slowly increased his altitude.
One zhang, two zhang …
As Luo Zheng's altitude increased, the gravity exerted on his body became stronger and stronger. It was as if there was a big hand in the dome, firmly grasping Luo Zheng and pulling him down.
However, even so, Luo Zheng still maintained a constant speed and calmly increased his speed. Apart from the fact that his speed was somewhat slow, it was impossible to see that there was such a gravity exerting on Luo Zheng's body.
"His momentum is not bad! This kid … doesn't seem to be pretending! "
"From the looks of it, it shouldn't be difficult for this kid to get a second grade divine tool!"
"Indeed, he's only at the fifth level of Life and Death Realm. I think he'll be able to challenge the Swallow Cloud Hall soon!"
These cultivators did not know Luo Zheng. Previously, when they saw that Luo Zheng was only at the fifth level of Life and Death Realm, they more or less looked down on him. However, now that they saw how Luo Zheng easily increased his altitude, they felt some admiration in their hearts.
Xie Mang coldly said, "Hmph, he's just pretending to be calm. I'm afraid he's already holding on for dear life. I've seen many people like him!"
"Brother is right. This kid can probably get a third grade divine tool at most!" Xie Jun also chimed in.
When the nearby cultivators heard this, some of them whispered, "Even a third grade divine tool is stronger than you guys …"
As for the white-browed old man, he still looked at Luo Zheng with an indifferent and amiable gaze. However, in the depths of his amiable gaze, there was a hint of surprise.
He had guarded the Divine Weapon Hall for so many years, and had seen too many cultivators who came to obtain divine tools. Among them, there were no doubt many brilliant geniuses who had strong confidence in themselves. However, the white-browed old man could feel the fluctuations in their state of mind.
Take Xie Mang for example. Although his speed was also increasing slowly, because he needed to draw out a considerable amount of power to resist the gravity, the speed of his increase was not uniform. Under careful observation, it was sometimes fast and sometimes slow.
This was similar to a person pushing a cart. Generally speaking, power was released in one breath, and it was very rhythmic.
However, there was an exception. If the cart was very light, the person pushing it could easily handle it and maintain a very uniform speed. Of course, if the cart was very heavy, the person pushing it would be incomparably strong, and could maintain a uniform speed.
Luo Zheng was like this. His speed of increase was very uniform, neither fast nor slow, neither slow nor fast. This showed that Luo Zheng's power was more than enough to deal with the gravity in front of him, or it could be said that he had completely suppressed the gravity to achieve this.

Chapter 1026
The interior of the Divine Weapon Hall was rather spacious. However, the dome above Luo Zheng was only about 200 zhang in height.
If it weren't for the strong gravitational pressure, any warrior who wanted to get close to the dome would only need an instant.
When Luo Zheng reached 50 zhang, the gravitational force was already comparable to a huge mountain pressing down on his body.
This level of gravity still didn't pose much of a problem for Luo Zheng. He still maintained a steady speed, but his expression was very calm. His eyes were fixed on the blue light in the depths of the dome. This was the target he wanted to obtain!
When Luo Zheng reached another 20 zhang in height, he was already able to touch some of the white light in the lowest layer.
When Luo Zheng passed by these white specks of light, he glanced at them indifferently. The white specks of light contained secondary grade three god artifacts, and there were all kinds of weapons. After a casual glance, Luo Zheng's target left these specks of light. He could only take one at a time. If he was itchy enough to take a single speck of light, he would probably be immediately pulled down from the sky by the old man.
At a height of 70 zhang, the gravitational force had more than doubled. Luo Zheng faintly felt some pressure.
However, this pressure was still very small. He continued to ascend slowly, neither too fast nor too slow.
At a height of 30 zhang, the pressure on Luo Zheng increased. Secondary grade three divine weapons floated around Luo Zheng.
Continue to ascend …
At a height of 120 zhang, the gravitational force doubled again. The surrounding light spots were secondary grade two divine weapons …
Continue …
At this time, the warriors who were originally clamoring in the Divine Weapon Hall had already quieted down.
Only now did they realize that Luo Zheng's effortless and calm appearance wasn't a pretense!
Even if he wanted to pretend, he had to have the ability to do so!
Xie Mang's face had already turned red at this height. However, Luo Zheng's face still didn't show much of a reaction. He didn't show any signs of weakness!
The surprise hidden in the depths of the white-browed old man's eyes was becoming more and more obvious!
"This kid, even at this height, he's still maintaining a constant speed. The gravity still doesn't have much effect on him?"
When they had just ascended, many martial artists were able to achieve a uniform speed. But now, they had already reached a height of 120 feet. Even at this height, he was still able to easily control himself. If this boy were to erupt with power and rush upwards, just how high could he reach?
In fact, when Luo Zheng took out 100 Zhen Yuanyu, the old man with white eyebrows originally wanted to dissuade him. But looking at Luo Zheng's serious eyes, he also knew that his dissuasion was useless.
What's more, young people had to have some drive. They had to have the ambition even though they knew it was impossible. Even if they failed, they could climb up again. This kid was only in his twenties. In the eyes of the white-browed old man, he was an infant. Some failures would be good for his future growth.
Therefore, the white-browed old man followed Luo Zheng's suggestion and placed the Thundergale Ghost Sword into the dome.
However, he did not even consider the possibility of Luo Zheng obtaining this first-grade divine weapon!
Of course, at this stage, the white-browed old man still did not think that Luo Zheng would be able to obtain it. However, based on Luo Zheng's momentum, it was possible for him to obtain the red dot of light, which was the first-grade divine weapon that Xie Mang had placed in the dome first.
Even so, it was still very good. To be able to obtain a first-grade divine weapon after entering the Divine Weapon Hall was extremely rare even in the other Twelve Heavenly Palaces, let alone the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
"He can still maintain a constant speed at 120 feet. I reckon he won't be able to hold on at 140 feet. After this height increases to 140 feet, the gravity will rapidly increase to a new level …" The white-browed old man was curious to what level this kid could reach.
Just as the white-browed old man was thinking about this, Luo Zheng had already crossed the height of 130 feet at a moderate pace.
When he approached the height of 140 feet, he suddenly felt that the gravity had doubled in an instant!
After several times of doubling, the gravity exerted on Luo Zheng's body had reached a terrifying level. This kind of constant doubling of gravity was extremely terrifying. After a few times of doubling, the gravity had increased by an order of magnitude.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body trembled slightly!
The originally quiet Divine Weapon Hall was suddenly filled with the sound of discussion, but the warriors kept their voices very low.
Although they could not obtain the divine weapon they wanted, they had already formed a sense of expectation for Luo Zheng. They just wanted to see what grade of divine weapon this newcomer could obtain!
Of course, not everyone had such expectations.
For example, the two brothers Xie Mang and Xie Jun, as well as the warrior who had joined the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace with Xie Jun …
How could they be happy when they saw that Luo Zheng had risen to a height of 140 feet?
Especially Xie Mang. He had entered the Divine Weapon Hall many times. Although his judgment was not as good as the white-browed old man's, Xie Mang already knew that Luo Zheng would obtain a Secondary Grade One Divine Weapon. As for a Grade One Divine Weapon, there might still be a slight chance!
Thinking that he had been in the Lingyun Hall for so long, but he was not as good as this newcomer who had just ascended, how could he not feel good?
At this time, Xie Mang even regretted staying here to see this scene. This kind of thing was a huge blow to his confidence. He had worked so hard for such a difficult thing, but it was easily done by a guy who looked much weaker than him. No one would be happy if this happened to him!
Moreover … Xie Mang had taken elixirs before, and later, he even burned his blood essence.
Burning one's blood essence was something that would only be used in a desperate situation. Compared to Luo Zheng, he wished he could find a hole to crawl into.
As for Xie Jun, he naturally had nothing to say. The gap between him and Luo Zheng was unimaginable. His heart was numb, but when he saw his brother's expression, he was a little worried. He only hoped that his brother would not be too sad, but he did not dare to open his mouth to comfort Xie Mang.
When Luo Zheng's body shook, the white-browed old man seemed to breathe a sigh of relief. This kid finally couldn't take it anymore.
If Luo Zheng could really maintain a constant speed all the way up, then he would be an indescribable monster!
Fortunately, there shouldn't be such an exaggerated kid in this world, the white-browed old man thought indifferently.
However, just as he sighed in his heart, his face suddenly stiffened, and the calm expression in his eyes was completely replaced by shock!
"This kid …"
It turned out that after experiencing the tremors, Luo Zheng had actually adapted to the gravity of a height of 140 feet in an instant. This guy was actually maintaining a constant speed and was still slowly rising …
At this time, even the white-browed old man could not sit still. Who recruited this kid into the Heavenly Palace?
Should he inform the Palace Master?
Thinking of this, he did not hesitate at all. He took out a Sound Transmitting Talisman. As the Sound Transmitting Talisman burned, the white-browed old man sent a voice into the talisman.
"Palace Master, there's a kid coming here to take the treasure. He seems to have just joined our Heavenly Palace, but I don't know who recruited him. This kid actually …"
Before the white-browed old man could finish speaking, Yun Luo's indifferent voice had already come back. "I know."
"How do you know?" The white-browed old man was stunned. He did not even finish speaking. Moreover, the Palace Master was not in the Divine Weapon Hall, so how did she know who he was talking about?
"I was the one who brought Luo Zheng to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. Whatever he can get, I'll give it to him …" Yun Luo cut off the communication talisman after she finished speaking. Since she had promised Luo Zheng to help Xun recover her soul, she would do it. She was busy at the moment.
As for the white-browed old man's words, Yun Luo did not take it to heart.
At this time, there were definitely more warriors entering the Divine Weapon Hall than Luo Zheng. However, Yun Luo was very sure that there was no one else other than Luo Zheng who could make the old man so surprised to contact her. As for the two guys brought by Xu Can, it was impossible for them to make the old man so surprised.
No matter what, she still had a lot of confidence in Luo Zheng …
After Yun Luo cut off the Sound Transmitting Talisman, the white-browed old man fixed his eyes on Luo Zheng. Only then did he realize that this kid was brought in by the Palace Master himself.
Even if he was brought in personally by the Palace Master, so what? How could he be so monstrous? The old man with white eyebrows was in a daze. The Thirteen Palaces were originally a place to discover geniuses in the Alliance. Every year, many geniuses were found by the palace masters and vice palace masters, but geniuses like Luo Zheng were too rare.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1027
The other cultivators from the Martial Arts Hall couldn't figure out what was going on. They only felt that it was too easy for Luo Zheng to increase his height.
Apart from the white-browed elder, Xie Mang also saw through the mystery.
When Xie Mang was increasing his height, he also deliberately suppressed part of his power. However, he deliberately suppressed his power because he wanted to suddenly erupt after reaching a certain height. His starting point was different from Luo Zheng's.
Moreover, even if he deliberately suppressed his power, he would still be suppressed by the gravity at this height. How could he be as calm and composed as Luo Zheng and maintain a very uniform speed?
Judging from this, this kid's power was far from the limit!
Fifty zhang, sixty zhang …
Around Luo Zheng, those tiny white specks of light were pulsating restlessly. These white specks of light were all grade two divine artifacts.
Some of the dots of light even jumped onto Luo Zheng's face. As long as he stretched out his hand to grab them, they would be within his reach.
However, Luo Zheng still raised his head. The chaotic energy in his body was circulating even more vigorously …
At sixty zhang, there were second-grade divine weapons. At one hundred and eighty zhang, there were first-grade divine weapons.
At one hundred and ninety zhang, there were only four red dots of light. They were first-grade divine weapons that Xie Mang had spent four hundred Zhen Yuanyu to place in the dome.
The only first-grade divine weapon, a blue dot of light, floated at two hundred zhang. The azure blue dot of light stuck close to the dome's ceiling and constantly fluttered …
"Rise …"
At this time, the gravity was also getting stronger and stronger!
Even Luo Zheng felt that it was extremely strenuous. However, he maintained a delicate balance from beginning to end. The chaotic energy in his body circulated faster and faster. At the same time, he used the power of the dragon scales to resist the gravity.
In his mind, the dragon scales were lit up one by one by Luo Zheng.
Five hundred …
One thousand …
Two thousand …
When Luo Zheng activated the power of four thousand dragon scales, he had already reached one hundred and ninety zhang!
If Luo Zheng moved horizontally at this time, he could definitely catch up to the four first-grade divine weapons!
"This guy's target is actually the Dark Thunder Wind Divine Sword …"
"Actually, as long as he catches a first-grade divine weapon, he'll make a fortune! This kid doesn't seem to be satisfied. "
"Nonsense. If you have the ability to chase after something better, why should you take something worse?"
Those martial artists already understood that Luo Zheng indeed had the strength!
Previously, Xie Mang had also reached a height of 190 feet. He was only a hair's breadth away from a grade one divine artifact. However, Xie Mang hadn't been at that height for long. He simply didn't have the power to chase after those red dots of light.
However, Luo Zheng had already stopped at a height of nineteen zhang, and was only one zhang away from the dome!
At this time, Luo Zheng had already ignored everything else. In his eyes, there was only the dancing blue light!
However, it was still far from enough to reach this height. These light spots were not stationary. They were like tiny fireflies, gently fluttering in the surroundings.
The fluttering speed wasn't fast. With a martial artist's reaction speed, even if these light spots fluttered ten times faster, they could still easily catch them.
However, under this gravity, even if one took a breath, the slightest bit of carelessness would cause one to be directly smashed down by the gravity. One could imagine how difficult it would be to catch those light spots.
After Luo Zheng locked onto those light spots, he let out a slight sigh of relief.
The Dark Thunder Wind Divine Sword was indeed difficult to catch. At the last three zhang, the gravity would double for every ten zhang. He had to double the power of the dragon scales to deal with this gravity.
At that height of twenty zhang, if the gravity doubled again, he was afraid that he wouldn't be able to withstand it.
This was because the limit of the power of the dragon scales Luo Zheng's body could withstand was six thousand.
He could indeed activate eight thousand dragon scales, but his body couldn't withstand it …
He could only give it a try!
It wasn't easy to reach this height. Of course, Luo Zheng didn't want to return empty-handed. If it was safe, he could indeed choose any of the four first grade divine weapons. However, since Luo Zheng had set a goal for himself, there was no reason not to complete it, even if it seemed a little difficult.
However, he had never been afraid of challenges. A martial artist could only grow through repeated challenges!
"Whoosh!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly lit up the power of the six thousand dragon scales in his body!
The surging power was like a channel that opened a gate, flowing through Luo Zheng's limbs and bones. At the same time, crackling sounds came from Luo Zheng's body again.
"It seems that some kind of secret technique has been activated!"
"This kid probably doesn't know that under this dome, the stronger he is, the greater the gravity suppressed by the dome!"
"Let's wait and see. Whether he succeeds or not depends on this moment. However, no matter whether he succeeds or not, I admire this kid's courage. He just entered our Misty Cloud Palace, and he dares to attack the weapons left by the previous Palace Masters …"
Just as these warriors had said, after the power in Luo Zheng's body erupted, the corresponding gravitational force also rapidly increased.
However, the increase in gravity wasn't as fast as Luo Zheng's!
Because at this moment, Luo Zheng used the Chaotic Qi in his body to circulate the Law of Wind, and his Vital Essence began to erupt at the same time.
"Whoosh!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng could no longer maintain the slow speed of his ascent. Under this eruption, the speed of his ascent increased by several times!
"What a powerful gravity!"
At this height, under the influence of the powerful gravity, the surrounding space began to become incomparably viscous. It was as if Luo Zheng wasn't in the air, but in a quagmire. Every breath of air was like wet mud, firmly pressed down on Luo Zheng's body by the gravity.
However, Luo Zheng was like a nimble fish. At this moment, he didn't desperately rush upwards. Instead, he changed his direction and rushed diagonally. Then, he twisted his body and increased his altitude again!
After twisting a few times, Luo Zheng finally reached the height of the dome's ceiling!
All the warriors in the Divine Weapon Hall were no longer talking. Some of them were even clenching their fists. This scene that happened in front of them was indeed inconceivable. After all, the weapons in the Divine Weapon Hall were notoriously difficult to obtain.
At the same time, they also felt lucky. After all, there were still some martial artists who had taken away first-grade divine weapons. However, taking away the weapons of the previous Palace Masters had never happened even once.
Being able to witness this scene was enough for them to boast to their friends for a year …
Luo Zheng's body stabilized at a height of 200 feet. He knew that if he relaxed even a little bit, all his efforts would be for naught. He would fall directly to the ground like Xie Mang.
Therefore, after flying to this height, he concentrated all his attention on resisting the gravity and didn't rush to chase after the blue light spot.
As for the blue light spot, it continued to float around carelessly. Occasionally, it would get closer to Luo Zheng. However, as soon as it got close to Luo Zheng, it suddenly jumped away.
One breath …
Two breaths …
Luo Zheng's breathing became heavier and heavier.
His body had already reached the limit of what it could bear. Perhaps if he increased his strength even a little bit more, it would be like the straw that broke the camel's back. Either his body would collapse or he would be directly smashed down.
"He's in a daze. He's actually in a daze at a height of 200 feet. This person is really enchanting …"
"He's probably locking onto the blue light spot. A height of 200 feet is too terrifying. Right now, it's like he's walking on a tightrope. He can slow down, but he can't make any mistakes. Otherwise, he'll fall directly!"
"But there has to be a limit to how slow he can be. He's just standing there!"
At this moment, the white-browed elder had already stood up and looked at Luo Zheng …
At this moment, the white-browed elder suddenly had a thought. He didn't want Luo Zheng to obtain the Thundergale Divine Sword so easily. He wanted to see where Luo Zheng's limit was!
These light spots were personally placed by the white-browed elder. As long as he wanted to, he could easily control these light spots.

Chapter 1028
At this height, Luo Zheng had to prepare for ten breaths of time.
During this process, he could clearly see the trajectory of the blue light.
After determining the direction of the blue light spot in the next breath, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and his body suddenly fluttered.
"Whoosh …"
Even though the power in his body was close to the limit, his speed was still not slow!
According to Luo Zheng's judgment, he should have an eighty percent chance of success!
As for the warriors below, they were all very excited.
"It's steady. He can still maintain such a speed. This guy is simply abnormal!"
"The value of any one of these three divine weapons is immeasurable. This guy has just entered the Misty Cloud Palace and he's already rich. He's really …"
Luo Zheng rushed towards the blue light, and the distance between the two was quickly shortened.
The blue light spot was drifting in a disorderly manner, but Luo Zheng was able to estimate its next possible trajectory and waited here in advance.
Therefore, Luo Zheng could be said to have reached his destination first. When the blue light hit him, the distance between the two was quickly shortened!
"It's close!"
At this time, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of a smile. He suddenly reached out his hand to grab the light!
When the light was less than a foot away from Luo Zheng's hand, the blue light seemed to have felt the danger in front of it and suddenly stopped in the air.
Luo Zheng's smile froze on his face. He gritted his teeth and his body flashed, taking the initiative to grab it!
However, when Luo Zheng took the initiative to grab it, it was fine. But when he took the initiative to grab it, the blue light actually retreated …
Not only was Luo Zheng a little stunned, but the warriors of the Divine Weapon Hall were also stunned.
"How can it be like this? I've never heard of these light spots being able to escape! "
Just like before, when Xie Mang was trying to grab the red light spot, he was just a little bit away. At this critical moment, the red light spot actually left Xie Mang.
However, everyone knew that these light spots were originally floating randomly. They could only blame Xie Mang's bad luck. If he was just a little bit luckier, he wouldn't have failed.
The problem was that the blue light spot's behavior just now was too outrageous.
The moment it was about to reach Luo Zheng, it suddenly stopped. And when Luo Zheng reached out to grab it, it actually ran away …
This was too much …
Luo Zheng didn't think much about it. He couldn't be distracted. He didn't feel discouraged when he didn't grab it. Instead, he tried his best to maintain his height! He had just pounced over, and his body had dropped four feet, but now he was slowly rising again. However, his face was getting uglier and uglier. After all, it was quite difficult for any warrior to maintain their height while resisting such a terrifying gravity.
However, the warriors below were not happy.
Everyone looked at the old man with white eyebrows in unison. Even Xie Mang looked at the old man with white eyebrows with the same expression …
Xie Mang naturally didn't want to ask for an explanation on behalf of Luo Zheng. However, he had almost let a grade one divine artifact slip from his hands. He guessed that the old man might have been behind his grab just now.
However, deep down, Xie Mang roughly understood that he might really have been unlucky. Nevertheless, by putting on such an act, he could at least save some face. Everyone thought that the old man with white eyebrows was behind it, not that he was weak.
Seeing that everyone's eyes were focused on him, the old man with white eyebrows revealed a trace of embarrassment. He knew that things weren't looking good. His control just now seemed a little too fake and had aroused suspicion.
"Cough cough." In order to hide his embarrassment, the old man with white eyebrows said, "There should be a remnant of spiritual intelligence in the former Palace Master's weapon. If we want to catch this sword, I'm afraid it will take a lot of effort …"
"Oh …"
"I see …"
"I understand. So it has spiritual intelligence. That's amazing!"
Although these warriors didn't dare to doubt the old man with white eyebrows, their expressions and the tone of their replies showed that they didn't believe him at all. Even children would find it difficult to deceive such deceitful words.
In fact, whether or not Luo Zheng could obtain the former Palace Master's weapon had nothing to do with these warriors. It was just that Luo Zheng had already reached this step. Everyone was secretly hoping that Luo Zheng would succeed. Otherwise, it would inevitably be a little disappointing.
Moreover, if Luo Zheng succeeded in his challenge, it would be a good example for them. In the future, they might be able to reach 200 feet in the dome and obtain the former Palace Master's weapon.
Naturally, they were dissatisfied with the old man with white eyebrows …
As a result, the old man with white eyebrows looked even more embarrassed. He was an upright person, so it was inevitable that he would lose face by doing such a thing. He was afraid that he would end up with a bad reputation. After all, he had repeatedly said that the weapons in the Divine Weapon Hall belonged to whoever caught them. However, he had actually controlled those light spots to run around. Who could catch them? Wasn't this a scam?
However, he didn't care about the weapons. He was only in charge of managing the Divine Weapon Hall. As for who the weapons fell into, it had nothing to do with him … He really just wanted to test Luo Zheng!
After the first lesson, Luo Zheng was a lot more cautious the second time. In fact, he had also discovered that the blue light spots could intentionally avoid him. As a result, it would be extremely difficult to catch them.
At this time, Luo Zheng was also a little apprehensive. Just flying to this height was already so difficult, but the blue light spots could still run around on their own. Wasn't it obvious that they didn't want to be caught?
Even so, Luo Zheng didn't choose to give up.
Along the way, he had encountered many more difficult encounters. Moreover, there was no danger to his life in the Divine Weapon Hall. He really mustered up his strength and rushed towards the blue light spots again.
"Whoosh …"
"Whoosh …"
As Luo Zheng's speed increased, the speed of the blue light spots also increased. At this time, Luo Zheng was like an eagle chasing an extremely agile hummingbird!
However, due to gravity, Luo Zheng's speed was greatly reduced. In addition, the blue light spots were extremely agile. Both of them circled the dome ceiling a few times. In the end, Luo Zheng had to stop to catch his breath and stare at the blue light spots.
Seeing this scene, the warriors below were even more speechless.
It was already a miracle that Luo Zheng could fly to a height of 200 feet. Now, he was actually still circling at a height of 200 feet. In addition to being surprised, they were even more annoyed with the white-browed old man.
At first, no one dared to say anything. No one was willing to offend the white-browed old man.
However, someone whispered, "This is too unfair. They might as well control all the light spots. In this way, no one can get a single weapon. I'm afraid that the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace will make a lot of money."
"That's right. 300 Zhen Yuanyu for one chance. We can't even get a fart. Let's spread the news and tell everyone not to be cheated by the Divine Weapon Hall!"
"This is too much! This is too much! "
These people's voices became louder and louder. The atmosphere became more and more intense. Among them, a warrior stood out and said to the white-browed old man, "Yue Lao, have you played enough?"
"Yes, give us the weapon or not. Just say it. Why do you have to play with people here?"
"That kid up there, come down. You can't get the weapon. Yue Lao is playing with you!"
As the crowd clamored, the white-browed old man's face turned red. If this was spread out, he was afraid that no one would come to the Divine Weapon Hall to get weapons in the future. This was not the original intention of the Divine Weapon Hall …
He had just discovered that under his control, Luo Zheng could not catch the blue light spot. However, this guy's physical strength was not ordinary. He was stubbornly holding on at a height of 200 Zhang. Although he would occasionally fall, he would rise again.
He had no way of gauging Luo Zheng's limits. Unless he modified the gravitational force of the divine imprint and increased it by a few times, Luo Zheng would most likely not be able to withstand it. However, modifying a divine imprint was a troublesome matter.
Just as he was being ridiculed by the crowd and was slightly dazed, Luo Zheng took the opportunity to flash past!
He had already noticed the movement below and roughly knew what had happened. He seized the opportunity in front of him and rushed towards the blue light spot again. He had almost squeezed out all of his potential. His speed had increased by several times! He grabbed the blue speck of light and quickly descended to the ground. He stood firmly on the ground with an astonishingly sharp sword in his hand.

Chapter 1029
Luo Zheng didn't even have time to examine the first-grade divine weapon in his hand before he heard the martial artists continuously denounce the white-browed old man.
Although the white-browed old man was an extremely senior figure, being besieged by so many young martial artists and being in the wrong himself, he didn't know what to say for a moment …
"What … are you guys talking about?" Luo Zheng walked over with a smile on his face.
The white-browed old man sighed when he realized that he would not be able to get past these juniors if he did not give them an explanation. He turned to Luo Zheng and said, "I was wrong earlier. It's not that I don't want you to obtain the sword. I was just curious about your limits. That's why I controlled the light …"
"You finally admitted it, you really were controlling it!"
"You actually have the face to admit it, the Divine Weapon Hall might as well change its name to the Blackheart Hall!"
"I won't believe it anymore …"
He admitted it, and even admitted his mistake, but these guys were still so aggressive. The white-browed old man was riding a tiger and couldn't get off.
Luo Zheng naturally understood that the blue light spot was controlled by the white-browed old man, but in the end, he still got the sword. Luo Zheng believed the old man's words, so he waved his hand and said, "It's okay, it's just a bit of effort."
In the end, if the white-browed old man had insisted on not letting him take it, Luo Zheng really wouldn't have been able to get the sword …
Since the person involved said so, these martial artists with a "sense of justice" calmed down the anger in their hearts. At this time, everyone looked at the first-grade divine weapon in Luo Zheng's hand with an envious expression.
Even if it was an ordinary first-grade divine weapon, for the majority of martial artists, it was something they could only dream of!
The standard length of an ordinary longsword was three feet and three inches, but this Thunder Wind Ghost Divine Sword was five feet long, which was nearly half the length of an ordinary sword!
Moreover, the body of the sword was slightly wider than an ordinary sword, somewhat similar to a heavy sword, but the blade was extremely thin!
At a glance, the body of the sword was divided into two layers. The inner core of the sword was light blue, and the outer edge of the sword was pure gold. The sword hilt was engraved with a complicated pattern. Just from its appearance, it was an extremely exquisite work of art!
As for power …
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that by testing here, this group of warriors could be considered to be helping him. Luo Zheng didn't mind letting them admire it for a while. After that, he kept the long sword in his bag and left the Divine Weapon Hall.
Xie Mang's gaze was gloomy and cold as he stared fixedly at Luo Zheng's departing figure. The corners of his mouth curled into a sneer. "The stronger the weapon, the stronger the strength. Although your talent is not bad, your strength is not enough to match that sword. Let's see how long this sword can last in your hands!"
Whether it was in the Lingyun Hall or the Swallow Cloud Hall, there were countless genius warriors staring at the three weapons left behind by the former Palace Master.
However, this weapon was too difficult to take away. So far, everyone could only look at it …
There were some people from the noble families who wanted to buy it from the Hall of Divine Weapons, but the Misty Cloud Palace didn't lack money at all. Why would they care about Zhen Yuanyu? He wouldn't sell it no matter how high the price was.
Just like that, these three weapons were immersed in the Hall of Divine Weapons. Now that Luo Zheng had taken them away, he was destined to be targeted by many people!
Xie Mang wanted to spread the news without restraint. He estimated that there would be an endless stream of trouble coming to find Luo Zheng until he gave up on the sword.
In fact, this matter didn't need Xie Mang to spread the news …
Two hours later, the news that a newcomer had taken away the Thundergale Ghost Sword spread like wildfire in the Misty Cloud Palace.
At the beginning, the news spread within the Lingyun Hall. After all, the warriors who witnessed Luo Zheng taking away the weapon were all warriors of the Lingyun Hall.
But soon, the news spread to the Swallow Cloud Hall …
"Is there really a newcomer who took away the Thundergale Ghost Sword?"
"Unbelievable. Hehe, I'm lacking a good sword. This Thundergale Ghost Sword should be suitable for me. I wonder if that kid is easy to talk to?"
"I can buy this sword for Zhen Yuanyu, a price that will tempt him!"
Such words appeared countless times in a day. Of course, Luo Zheng didn't know about it. After leaving the Hall of Divine Weapons, he went to find Yun Luo.
Compared to the weapon in his hand, Luo Zheng was more concerned about Xun …
After leaving the Hall of Divine Weapons, Luo Zheng wandered around a few times, but he was a little confused. He didn't seem to know where Yun Luo was.
After thinking for a while, he opened the jade slip that Yun Luo gave him. This jade slip recorded a map of the Misty Cloud Palace.
Of course, Luo Zheng had the map. The problem was that the Misty Cloud Palace was so big, but there was no indication of Yun Luo's location …
"Let's go to the center." Luo Zheng pursed his lips and walked towards the center of the palace.
The Misty Cloud Palace was built on a huge pillar, and the area it occupied was definitely not small. He shuttled through the buildings and soon appeared in front of a large hall.
"It should be here." When he arrived in front of the large hall, Luo Zheng walked up the stairs and was about to enter.
However, as soon as he stepped through the door, a strange force bounced him back ….
"Huh?"
Luo Zheng's face flashed with a touch of curiosity.
At this moment, a person walked out of the large hall. It was the hawk-nosed man he had seen before, the Deputy Palace Master of the Misty Cloud Palace, Xu Can.
Xu Can looked at Luo Zheng coldly and then scolded in a cold voice, "The Profound Hall is not a place for you to barge in!"
Luo Zheng looked indifferent and then said, "I'm looking for Yun Luo."
Xu Can was slightly stunned. He was very puzzled. Why did Luo Zheng call Yun Luo by her name?
However, because of the previous encounter on the stairs in the front courtyard, Xu Can didn't like Luo Zheng. He said coldly, "Yun Luo is not here. Leave quickly!"
Luo Zheng nodded indifferently and retreated. At this moment, a figure drifted over from not far away. It was Yun Luo, who had arrived late.
