"Oh?" Luo Zheng's eyes fell indifferently on Bi Yuanjia's body, and then he said, "And then?"
"I heard that you have a sword, the sword of the previous Palace Lord?" Bi Yuanjia asked with a sinister smile.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded slightly. He even raised his hand and took the Dark Thunderfale Divine Sword in his hand. His finger gently slid along the blade of the Dark Thunderfale Divine Sword, and then suddenly sped up. The thin sword suddenly emitted a sword cry. "Yes, this is the Dark Thunderfale Divine Sword."
Hearing this guy's words, Luo Zheng understood that this guy came for his sword. However, he was not in a hurry. Instead, he wore a faint smile on his face, waiting to hear what this guy would say.
Many warriors outside the door saw the first-grade divine weapon in Luo Zheng's hand, and their eyes suddenly lit up.
A first-grade divine weapon, and this first-grade divine weapon was much stronger than an ordinary first-grade divine weapon. If it was strengthened a little, it would be able to enter the Chaotic Myriad Spirit Tablet!
Bi Yuanjia stared at the sword in Luo Zheng's hand, and his eyes shot out rays of light. "I am lacking a good sword. Thank you for helping me take it out of the Divine Weapon Hall. Give it to me obediently! Lest I waste my strength, I will reward you with a full punch! "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face suddenly revealed an expression of not knowing whether to laugh or cry. The long sword shook slightly, and then he said, "I took this sword myself. If you have the ability, go to the Divine Weapon Hall and spend three hundred Zhen Yuanyu. There will be plenty of opportunities for you."
"It seems that you don't plan to obediently hand it over?" Bi Yuanjia certainly knew that Luo Zheng would not take out the sword so easily.
Luo Zheng nodded and said with a stupefied expression, "It seems that you don't plan to obediently leave?"
The grin on Bi Yuanjia's face deepened. "I told you from the beginning that you don't seem to understand what it means to be the fourth in the Lingyun Hall! Do you have to be taught a lesson to understand? "
Luo Zheng's face turned colder and colder. He knew that he would not be able to communicate with these idiots. He said, "I indeed don't understand, but you don't seem to understand what it means to take this sword. I will give you three breaths of time to scram. Do you have to be taught a lesson to understand?"
When Luo Zheng took the Thunder Wind Ghost Divine Sword, the news spread like the wind throughout the Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace.
The degree of jealousy in the hearts of these cultivators could be imagined.
Thinking that Luo Zheng had just entered the Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace, and his cultivation base was only at the fifth level of Life and Death Realm, many people started scheming in their hearts.
Some people wanted to spend money to buy it.
Some people thought that Luo Zheng was a newcomer and was unfamiliar with the place, so they wanted to scare him and make him obediently hand it over.
There were even some people who wanted to snatch it.
This Bi Yuanjia was one of them. He first came up to scare people, and now it seemed that he was about to make a move.
Many cultivators sighed inwardly. They had let Bi Yuanjia take the initiative. However, Bi Yuanjia was very overbearing to begin with, so no one dared to fight in front of him. They probably wouldn't have this opportunity.
As for that Luo Zheng, he would probably be beaten up badly by Bi Yuanjia. However, he deserved it. A guy who couldn't understand the situation before him couldn't blame others for courting death.
However, these cultivators didn't expect that Luo Zheng was even more arrogant than Bi Yuanjia. It was as if he didn't put Bi Yuanjia in his eyes at all!
"Three breaths of time, hahahaha … I haven't seen such an arrogant junior in a long time … hahahaha …"
"Rumble!"
Everyone saw Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flash, disappearing from where he stood. The next moment, Bi Yuanjia's entire body was like a huge rubber ball, flying backwards. His originally tall and straight chest was now like a deflated rubber ball, completely deflating. He crashed heavily into a small palace behind everyone, which was the residence of the other cultivators.
Luo Zheng held the Thunder Wind Ghost Sword in one hand, while his other hand was clenched into a fist. His eyes were full of indifference, "Three breaths of time is very fast."
"What a fast speed!"
"This is too fierce. He sent Bi Yuanjia flying with one punch?"
"Bi Yuanjia isn't this weak because he was careless. Even so, this kid's strength is very strong!"
Actually, some of the more rational cultivators were able to figure out that Luo Zheng's strength wasn't just ordinary to be able to obtain the Thunder Wind Ghost Sword.
However, many cultivators had heard that Luo Zheng was only at the fifth level of Life and Death Realm. So what if Luo Zheng's strength was outstanding? Could he fight against a Divine Sea Realm expert? This wasn't a comparison of levels!
Luo Zheng's thunder-like punch just now made everyone understand that this seemingly handsome guy was even more difficult to provoke!
"Just now, I should've tested the power of this sword …" Luo Zheng shook his head and muttered, reaching out to close the door.
The power of this sword …
Many cultivators couldn't help but swallow their saliva. That punch had already seriously injured Bi Yuanjia. If he had used his sword just now, Bi Yuanjia might not have wanted to keep his life!
Everyone heard Luo Zheng's words and understood that this was a threat. However, they didn't doubt that with this kid's decisiveness, he might really dare to take someone's life in Misty Cloud Palace.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to close the door, another voice rang out. This time, it was a short cultivator. "Wait!" The short cultivator said with a smile.
"Wait for what?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I can spend money to buy your Thunder Wind Ghost Sword! The price is absolutely fair! "The short cultivator said.
"Not for sale," Luo Zheng shook his head.

Chapter 1034
Zhen Yuanyu was worth ten thousand times more than top-grade True Origin Stones.
Three hundred thousand pieces of Zhen Yuanyu were equivalent to three billion top-grade True Origin Stones. It was an astronomical sum.
Hearing this short martial artist's bid, many of the martial artists present sucked in a breath of cold air!
The Thunder and Wind Divine Sword was indeed a first-grade divine weapon, but the price of three hundred thousand pieces of Zhen Yuanyu was still shocking!
"Jia Hao's family controls eighty percent of the mineral veins in dozens of great worlds. His offer is indeed shocking!"
"However, if it were me, I wouldn't sell it for three hundred thousand pieces of Zhen Yuanyu! Zhen Yuanyu can be earned slowly. This first-grade divine weapon … can save a life at a critical moment! "
Jia Hao stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "How about it? I'll buy this sword at a fixed price! "
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. "Sorry, it's not a matter of price. I'm not selling this sword."
Jia Hao was at his wit's end. His strength couldn't compare to Bi Yuanjia's, so he couldn't force him to sell.
Thus, Luo Zheng prepared to close the door again. He didn't want to waste time with these warriors of the Lingyun Hall.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to close the door for the third time, a round piece of Zhen Yuanyu was suddenly thrown over. It hit the ground with a clatter and rolled toward Luo Zheng.
At this time, another voice rang out. "Young Master Jing of the Swallow Cloud Hall said that he'll buy your sword for one Zhen Yuanyu. If you give the sword to him, he'll guarantee your safety. No one in the Misty Cloud Palace will dare to provoke you. If you don't agree, he can't guarantee anything."
"This …"
Luo Zheng was a little annoyed.
What was going on? He wanted to take a sword, but these guys didn't try hard to go to the Divine Weapon Hall. Why did they use all kinds of methods to compete with him? And it wasn't just one or two. It was endless. Wasn't this too much?
Now, he had sent Bi Yuanjia flying with a punch. His purpose was to intimidate these guys, but he didn't expect it to be so ineffective.
Why did the people of the Swallow Cloud Hall appear again?
Luo Zheng knew that the disciples of the Lingyun Hall couldn't compare to the Swallow Cloud Hall, but Luo Zheng didn't take them seriously.
He shook his head and stared at the piece of Zhen Yuanyu. He walked over and gently stepped on it.
"Bang!"
A rich True Essence burst out from the True Essence Jade!
The True Yuan contained in the True Yuan Jade was almost a thousand times more than the True Yuan Stone. The True Yuan contained within the True Yuan Jade had been compressed to a terrifying degree.
"I don't care who you are. Scram …" Luo Zheng closed the door as he spoke.
............................................................
The man's face turned cold. He didn't dare to push the door open since Luo Zheng had already closed the door. But he burned a Sound Transmission Talisman on the spot.
Some of the spectating martial artists wanted to disperse, but most of them stayed because they heard the words' Young Master Jing '.
Compared to the Lingyun Hall, the warriors of the Swallow Cloud Hall were a level higher in strength and talent.
Young Master Jing wouldn't let this go. There would be a good show to watch.
Luo Zheng closed the door and looked helpless. The Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace was a mess. There seemed to be no rules. Everyone was robbing each other.
When he first ascended to the Spirit Martial Holy Land, at least there were some rules. At least, people did not dare to fight in the settlement. But why were the people of Cloud Mist Palace so arrogant? Why was that? Why didn't anyone do anything?
He had to ask Yun Luo when he had the chance.
But..
. Luo Zheng looked at the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword in his hand. His eyes flashed coldly.
He hadn't tested the sword's power yet. Suddenly, the corner of his mouth twitched. The Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword was held horizontally in front of his chest.
He didn't know this Young Master Jing. If he dared to cause trouble, he could use him to test the sword.
Whoosh..
Seeing Luo Zheng close the door, Xun's figure suddenly flew out.
"Why did you come out?" Luo Zheng sat on the ground and placed the sword horizontally on his knees.
Xun also sat next to him and smiled. "Nothing. I just want to see how much your strength has grown."
"They might not come," Luo Zheng said.
"They will definitely come," Xun said with a smile. Luo Zheng sat cross-legged. She hugged her knees and sat to the side. Her two astonishingly long legs were curled up. She tilted her head and put it on her knees, looking at Luo Zheng.
"Why are you so sure?" Being stared at by Xun like this, Luo Zheng felt a little unnatural. However, he was more focused on the outside.
Xun said, "You are an outsider. You took the best thing here. It would be strange if they didn't come looking for you."
Luo Zheng curled his lips. He didn't agree with Xun's words. If the geniuses of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace didn't even have this little bit of tolerance, then they shouldn't be called the place where the three thousand geniuses gathered.
How could the real super geniuses be jealous of others?
Sure enough, as Xun said, in about the time it takes to burn an incense stick, several cultivators flew over.
As soon as the leading cultivator landed, the cultivator who threatened Luo Zheng nodded and said, "Young Master Jing, that kid is in the palace. However, he said that he won't see anyone."
Young Master Jing's skin was delicate, and his hair was meticulously combed behind his back. He looked very feminine. When he smiled, there was even a hint of flirtatious aura. Hearing that person's words, Young Master Jing took two steps forward and said indifferently, "I have passed on my message. I will give you one more chance. One Zhen Yuanyu. Sell your sword to me, and I will guarantee your safety."
Although his words were calm, the overbearingness in them was obvious. One Zhen Yuanyu was forcing a deal. If you are willing to sell, then I will guarantee your safety. If you are unwilling to sell, then you will not be safe.
Luo Zheng's eyes were tightly shut as he continuously poured the Chaotic Energy in his body into the Thundergale Divine Sword.
Luo Zheng was also looking forward to seeing the power of a grade-one divine weapon. He waited for Young Master Jing to finish speaking before he uttered a word nonchalantly.
"Scram!"
Young Master Jing was an extremely arrogant person. As one of the super geniuses in the Swallow Cloud Hall, who would dare to tell him to scram?
Hearing this word, Young Master Jing's face suddenly sank. His eyes were filled with murderous intent. However, that murderous intent disappeared in a flash. He said with a smile, "I, Jing Xuan, am a swordsman. In my life, I like to collect all kinds of famous swords. I treat swords as my friends. With your cultivation, you are not a good match for this sword. I believe that the sword will not be happy. I advise you to sell it to me and give it a good home. "
Luo Zheng almost vomited when he heard this. You like this sword, so I have to give it to you? What kind of logic was this?
Although Luo Zheng had ten thousand reasons to argue, he was too lazy to speak. If he wanted to have a foothold in the Misty Cloud Palace, his only choice was to kill the chicken to scare the dog. It was like this in the Lower Realm, and it was also the same in the Upper Realm.
Seeing that Luo Zheng did not respond for a long time, Young Master Jing shook his head. "You refuse a toast only to be forced to drink a forfeit. If I, Jing Xuan, attack you, I'm afraid you will lose an arm or a leg …"
After saying this, Young Master Jing walked towards Luo Zheng's small palace.
Everyone knew that today's show was bound to happen. With Luo Zheng's character, he would definitely not obediently hand over the sword. Moreover, Young Master Jing was an existence that could not be underestimated in the Swallow Cloud Hall. Luo Zheng was afraid that he would suffer.
As Young Master Jing came closer, Luo Zheng opened his eyes. His body did not move. He was still sitting cross-legged, but the Thunder Wind Divine Sword in his hand was pointed forward. His arm was slightly raised.
On the side, Xun looked at Luo Zheng's imposing manner with a faint smile on his face. This guy's eyes were getting sharper.
When Young Master Jing was fifty feet away from Luo Zheng's palace, Luo Zheng's arm shook slightly. The five feet long sword suddenly fluctuated. The power of wind and thunder directly merged with Luo Zheng's sword intent, forming a faint and invisible wave that suddenly pierced out.
Luo Zheng did not use his full strength. After all, he had just entered the Misty Cloud Palace and was not familiar with the rules. However, this guy could teach him a lesson, but he would not kill him!

Chapter 1035
The invisible ripples released by the Lightning-Wind Ghost Sword only left a crack on the door!
When the ripples surged out of the door, azure lightning and pitch-black wind blades burst out. The two types of Law Energies intertwined and formed a blurry figure that dashed toward Young Master Jing at an extremely fast speed!
"This is!"
Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly focused.
"The fourth level of the Law of Wind and the fourth level of the Law of Thunder …"
So far, Luo Zheng had only grasped the third level of the Law Power. In other words, whether it was the Law of Wind, the Law of Thunder, or the Law of Fire, he had only comprehended the third level.
However, when he infused the Chaotic Qi into the Lightning-Wind Ghost Sword, it directly touched the fourth level of the Law Power!
Xun hugged her knees and looked at Luo Zheng's sword through the crack. She said indifferently, "This sword has the ability to enhance the Law Power. It's not bad among first-grade divine weapons."
"Enhance the Law Power?"
"That's right, this sword can improve your power of laws …" Ye Zichen nodded.
"Doesn't that mean that when I comprehend the fourth level of the Law Power, I can use the fifth level of the Law Power?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Xun shook her head. "It's not that easy. The difference between the fourth level and the fifth level is huge. It's not difficult to control the first few levels of the Power of Law, but the difference between the later levels is like heaven and earth. This sword only increases your Power of Law, it doesn't really give you a level."
"I see." Luo Zheng nodded. Even so, it was already very helpful to Luo Zheng.
Moreover, the mysteries of this first-grade divine weapon didn't seem to be limited to this. For example, the blurry figure!
As a cultivator of the Swallow Cloud Hall, Young Master Jing was very confident in his own strength. The Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace's Lingyun Hall and Swallow Cloud Hall weren't simple newcomers and veterans. Those who could enter the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace were considered figures in the upper realm. Many top geniuses of tenth-grade Holy Lands were qualified to join.
Only those who stood out in the Lingyun Hall and joined the Swallow Cloud Hall were considered super geniuses worthy of the Alliance's resources.
That was why Young Master Jing was so arrogant. In his eyes, although this guy who had just joined the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace obtained the Lightning-Wind Ghost Sword, he was just a little lucky.
He even thought that Luo Zheng wouldn't dare to fight him.
But just as he thought so and walked over without any hesitation, an azure bolt of lightning suddenly appeared in front of him. This bolt of lightning was actually revolving around a black wind blade, and behind this wind blade was a faint silhouette …
At this moment, his pupils reflected the radiance of lightning, and all the hair on his body stood up. Before he could finish sucking in a breath of cold air, his entire body was sent flying backwards!
At the same time, a sword. Young Master Jing was Young Master Jing.
Young Master Jing wasn't lying. He loved collecting all kinds of swords. Swords of all shapes and sizes, swords with special functions, broadswords, two-handed swords, serpentine swords, piercing swords, profound artifacts, spirit artifacts, immortal artifacts, saint artifacts … He had all kinds of swords.
This Shieldsword was actually a modified version of a heavy sword. The blade was one and a half feet wide and three feet long. It looked like a shield!
As a lower first-grade divine artifact, the Shieldsword had saved his life many times in critical moments, helping him block several terrifying attacks!
Young Master Jing had never imagined that Luo Zheng's attack would be so swift and fierce. It was precisely because of this swift and fierce aura that he felt the threat of death, so he had used his strongest life-saving technique without any hesitation!
"Earthly Shieldsword, help me destroy this calamity!"
As he kept retreating, he poured a stream of True Yuan into the Shieldsword!
"Pop!"
A yellow light suddenly appeared on the Shieldsword, and a dense aura of the Law of Earth was released, making the Shieldsword seem even thicker and more solid!
At the same time, the violent lightning struck the heavy Shieldsword, leaving a pitch-black pattern on the surface of the Shieldsword. It looked like small snakes were spreading across the surface of the Shieldsword.
"Pull!"
The moment the lightning spread on the Shieldsword, Young Master Jing didn't dare to hold on to it. He pulled his hands apart, and Ling Xu stabilized the Shieldsword in front of him. He kept retreating!
However, Luo Zheng's sword didn't just contain the power of lightning. Very quickly, the black wind blade in the middle sliced the Shieldsword in the center. Under everyone's gazes, the Shieldsword was sliced into two by the wind blade!
Retreat!
Young master Jing's heart ached as he watched the shield sword being destroyed. However, his life was more important. He didn't hesitate at all as he continued to retreat. That was because there was another person besides the lightning and wind blades.
He didn't know what was going on with this figure, and neither did Luo Zheng.
However, he had lost the protection of the Shieldsword. He was bare-handed, and the figure was coming straight for him. Even a fool would run away!
However, the figure was extremely fast. After quickly closing in on Young Master Jing, it threw a punch.
This punch was the simplest punch, but it couldn't be underestimated. Young Master Jing subconsciously raised his arms to block in front of his chest.
"Boom!"
"Crack!"
Young Master Jing's arms suddenly broke, but the power of the punch didn't weaken. It landed directly on his chest, and he was sent flying backwards like a kite with a broken string. Finally, he smashed into the ground.
Luo Zheng sat in the room, and everyone stood outside. It was very quiet inside and outside.
The power of this sword had not only exceeded the expectations of many warriors, but also Luo Zheng. Only Xun looked indifferent.
There weren't many things that could shock Xun. For example, the Roaring Token that Luo Zheng had displayed before was completely beyond the scope of common sense. Naturally, she felt that it was inconceivable. However, this first-grade divine weapon was normal in her eyes.
Luo Zheng stared blankly at the gradually dissipating figure and exclaimed, "How could my sword stab a person out?"
Xun said faintly, "If I'm not wrong, that figure's moves can be modified, and it can do more than one punch …"
Hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. Indeed, when Luo Zheng had just stabbed with his sword, he had felt a telepathic connection. He had thought that it was because of the first-grade divine weapon, but now he realized that at that moment, he must have felt a telepathic connection with the figure!
However, Luo Zheng didn't know how to modify the figure's moves. He would have to spend time pondering about this in the future.
Young Master Jing lay on the ground for a long time before a figure flashed out, picked him up, and fiercely glared at Luo Zheng before leaving.
As for the other warriors, they didn't stay any longer, and no one dared to approach Luo Zheng's room.
Even Young Master Jing of the Swallow Cloud Hall was almost crippled by Luo Zheng's sword. How could these warriors of the Soaring Cloud Hall have designs on the Thunder and Wind Divine Sword? Weren't they courting death?
Soon, Luo Zheng's door was empty …
Seeing this, the corners of Luo Zheng's mouth curled up into a smile.
Finally, he could have some peace and quiet.
Luo Zheng didn't expect that taking out a sword would cause such a big reaction. The people of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace weren't easy to deal with. However, for the time being, no one would cause trouble for him.
"What do you plan to do next?" Luo Zheng asked Xun cautiously.
At present, Luo Zheng's strength was far from enough in the upper realm. Not to mention Realm Lords, he couldn't even fight against Extreme God experts.
As for the Punishment King, she should also have the cultivation of a Realm Lord. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he didn't even have the qualifications to stand in front of her.
However, Xun smiled faintly and said, "Wait!"
"Wait for what?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Wait for you to grow strong," Xun replied.
This answer was not bad, but Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry …
At present, Luo Zheng's cultivation had already reached the peak of the fifth level of Life and Death realm. He should be preparing to face the sixth minor heavenly tribulation.
However, just as he was preparing, someone came knocking on his door. What Luo Zheng didn't expect was that this person was actually the owner of the Thunder and Wind Divine Sword, the previous Palace Lord of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.

Chapter 1036
In the hall on the top floor of the Misty Cloud Palace.
An old man in a black robe sat at the side, and beside him stood a lady in green.
The lady was very young, and her bone age was only 20 years old. She was two years younger than Luo Zheng.
"I passed by the Feihong Territory this time, so I stopped by to take a look. In addition, I have one more thing to ask of you," the old man in the black robe said slowly.
Yun Luo, who was standing at the side, was very respectful to the old man. She replied with a smile, "Since the Old Palace Master has asked, I have no reason to refuse." As she spoke, she looked at the lady beside the old man and continued, "Could it be that the Old Palace Master wants to let this lady join our Misty Cloud Palace?"
The Old Palace Master that Yun Luo mentioned was the former Palace Master of the Misty Cloud Palace. He was also the former owner of the Thundergale Divine Sword, Xiao Daolai.
The current Xiao Daolai was no longer the same as before. It was rumored that he was planning to become a Heavenly Master. He was considered a popular figure in the alliance!
Unexpectedly, Xiao Daolai shook his head and smiled, "I have been in charge of the Misty Cloud Palace for 13,000 years. I have educated countless people, but I have never taken in a direct disciple. This lady is my last disciple."
After Xiao Daolai finished speaking, the lady in green beside him stood up straight, and a trace of arrogance appeared on her face.
Seeing the expression on the lady in green's face, Yun Luo smiled faintly. If Xiao Daolai could become a Heavenly Master, then this lady would be considered a Heavenly Master's successor. She was indeed qualified to be proud of being able to obtain the true inheritance of a Heavenly Master at such a young age.
"Then I must congratulate the Old Palace Master for taking in such a disciple. If I am not wrong, this disciple of yours seems to have the destiny of a Great Era Battle?" Yun Luo congratulated.
Xiao Daolai also seemed to be very happy, but after he finished laughing, his face revealed a trace of dullness. After a while, he sighed and said, "The great era has already been unveiled. We are destined to be onlookers when we attack the position of Celestial Lord. It's time to see how the darlings of this era will perform …"
At this moment, the girl in green said, "Master, I definitely won't disappoint you!"
Xiao Daolai nodded. "An Xin naturally won't disappoint me." Xiao Daolai then turned to Yun Luo and said, "If I remember correctly, the alliance is going to open the 'Torch Flame Inheritance' this time. Hence, I want An Xin to follow the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace and participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance. I will have to trouble you, Yun Luo."
Hearing Xiao Daolai's words, Yun Luo finally understood the purpose of the old Palace Master's visit. It turned out that Xiao Daolai wanted his disciple to participate in the Torch Flame inheritance.
It was not a bad thing for the Cloud Mist Palace, so Yun Luo would not reject it.
The various races in the universe all had their own methods of testing talent. The method used by the Human Race was called the Torch Flame Inheritance. It was similar to the three pagodas of the Demi-Immortal Clan. It was a very authoritative method in the universe. The only difference was that the Torch Flame Inheritance was only for Human Race cultivators, while the three pagodas of the Demi-Immortal Clan were for all geniuses in the universe.
Yun Luo nodded and said, "Old palace master, you're being too polite. If this girl can achieve a good result in the Xin Huo inheritance, our Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace will also be proud. Yun Luo can't thank you enough!"
Xiao Daolai chuckled and said, "An Xin has already opened the Nine-leaf lotus. Her result in the Torch Flame inheritance should not be too bad."
Although he said it so calmly, he was still quite proud of the fact that An Xin had opened the Nine-leaf lotus. How many super geniuses had opened the Nine-leaf lotus in the universe? With An Xin's potential, it would be strange if she could not achieve a good result in the Torch Flame inheritance.
The girl called An Xin pursed her lips. Her master had placed such high hopes on her. Her goal this time was not to achieve a good result, but to get first place!
During this conversation, Xiao Daolai seemed to have thought of something. He suddenly said, "If I remember correctly, one of my weapons is in the Divine Weapon Hall. Yun Luo, can you take me there and let An Xin retrieve it?"
Xiao Daolai did not directly ask for his weapon. Instead, he asked An Xin to retrieve it herself!
For so many years, no one had been able to retrieve the Thundergale Divine Sword. He was very confident in An Xin and was sure that she would be able to retrieve it.
These words stunned Yun Luo, and a helpless smile appeared on her face.
"What? Can't I? "Xiao Daolai's face showed a strange expression.
Yun Luo shrugged. "It's not that problem. It's because … that sword was taken away a few days ago."
"Oh?" A smile appeared on Xiao Daolai's face. With his strength and status, a first grade divine weapon was nothing in his eyes. He asked An Xin to retrieve it herself. Firstly, it was because An Xin's cultivation method was suitable for the Thundergale Divine Sword. Secondly, he wanted to prove An Xin's strength in front of Yun Luo.
However, Xiao Daolai never imagined that the sword would be taken away by someone else. As the Palace Lord of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, he had personally placed his weapon in the Divine Weapon Hall. He was very familiar with the rules of the Divine Weapon Hall. Wanting to take away his sword was not an easy matter.
"I wonder which genius took it away. What's his cultivation base?" Xiao Daolai's interest was piqued.
"His name is Luo Zheng, an ascender. His cultivation base is at the fifth layer of Life and Death realm." Yun Luo answered truthfully.
"Ascender? Fifth layer of Life and Death realm? " Xiao Daolai's eyes flashed. He was very surprised that someone with such a cultivation base could take away his sword. "I didn't expect such a cultivator to appear in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace!"
Hearing her master's words, An Xin's eyes faintly revealed an unconvinced expression. Although her master didn't compare her with Luo Zheng, the sword should have been taken away by her. But now, it was taken away by this guy called Luo Zheng. Naturally, she was somewhat unconvinced.
"I'd like to see this cultivator called Luo Zheng. I wonder if it's convenient?" Xiao Daolai said indifferently.
Yun Luo nodded and said, "There's nothing wrong with that. Luo Zheng is currently cultivating in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. I'll summon him now."
Before long, Yun Luo's Sound Transmission Talisman burned in Luo Zheng's ear. This was to tell Luo Zheng that the owner of the Thundergale Divine Sword wanted to see Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was somewhat baffled. Could it be that the former Palace Master went back on his word and wanted to ask for the sword back?
But on second thought, such a person probably wouldn't ask for a first-grade divine tool. After tidying up his clothes, he rushed over according to Yun Luo's instructions.
Soon, under the guidance of the two deacons, he entered the hall.
When Xiao Daolai saw Luo Zheng, his eyes were firmly fixed on Luo Zheng. His eyes did not leave Luo Zheng at all, and his face was filled with bewilderment. However, he quickly suppressed that expression and smiled, "I heard that you were the one who took away my Lightning Wind Serene Divine Sword?"
Luo Zheng cupped his hands towards Xiao Daolai and said, "I was the one who took away the Thundergale Divine Sword."
"Yes, Life and Death Realm cultivation base, and you've only passed five Life and Death Tribulations … It's not an injustice for this sword to fall into your hands," Xiao Daolai said indifferently.
"From what you're saying, could it be that you want to take it back?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Xiao Daolai's disciple, An Xin, was the first to speak, "Why would Master care about a first-grade divine tool? How could he take it back? That sword is naturally yours! "
The woman's words made Luo Zheng wrinkle his brows slightly, "I took this sword with my own strength, and I also used Zhen Yuanyu. How can you say that I'm giving it back?"
In fact, if the old Palace Master said that he was giving it to Luo Zheng, he wouldn't have refuted. But the woman beside the old Palace Master made it sound like she was giving alms, which made Luo Zheng feel a little uncomfortable. He, Luo Zheng, was not a beggar, and he didn't beg for the sword.

Chapter 1037
The true mystery of the Thunder Wind Dark Divine Sword lay in that human figure.
After Luo Zheng displayed his first sword move, he discovered this mystery and naturally pondered over it for a long time.
Every time Luo Zheng slashed out with the sword, it would incite lightning and wind blades, and at the same time, a human figure would accompany it!
At the same time, it would be accompanied by a feeling of telepathy.
However, Luo Zheng tried many times, but he was unable to control that human figure! In the end, he came to a conclusion. This Thunder Wind Dark Divine Sword should have a special cultivation method that matched it. As for this cultivation method, it would be difficult for Luo Zheng to obtain it.
So when he heard the Old Palace Master's words, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows and asked, "Could it be related to the cultivation method of the Thunder Wind Dark Divine Sword?"
Xiao Daolai nodded, "It seems you've already tried this sword. I'm afraid you're unable to control the divine figure in the sword …"
"Yes, I've tried many times, but I still can't grasp the trick," Luo Zheng replied.
"This isn't something you can test." Xiao Daolai took out a jade slip and handed it to his disciple An Xin.
An Xin originally wanted to step forward and hand it to Luo Zheng, but after thinking about it, she gently tossed it with her hand. The jade slip flew towards Luo Zheng at an extremely slow speed!
She didn't use true essence to rely on it, nor did she use any other method. Instead, she changed the time in the space between her and Luo Zheng!
"The Law of Time?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly in his heart.
In fact, this was only the first level of the Law of Time. Any cultivator who comprehended the first level of the Law of Time could do it. However, An Xin's use of time was extremely ingenious. She controlled the range of time very precisely, and the distance between her and Luo Zheng was neither too long nor too short, neither too short nor too short.
As for why she did this, there was only one reason, and that was to show off.
Luo Zheng was one step ahead of her and took away her sword. She refused to accept it in her heart. She wanted Luo Zheng to understand the gap between them!
She didn't care about the Thunder Wind Dark Divine Sword. She knew her destiny. As long as she didn't fall, she would be the darling of this era. As the curtains of the Great Era slowly opened, she would eventually become the main character of this era!
As for this Luo Zheng, what was he?
Luo Zheng could feel a trace of hostility from the green-robed woman, but this trace of hostility made Luo Zheng feel somewhat baffled.
Of course, with his current temperament, he wouldn't bicker with her …
However, as the jade slip flew forward, it began to slow down. Although it was still moving forward, its speed was like that of a crawling insect. If one didn't look carefully, they would think that the jade slip was frozen in the air.
As a result, Luo Zheng smiled slightly, took a step forward, and stepped into the area where she controlled time. He reached out and took the jade slip.
From beginning to end, the Law of Time had no effect on Luo Zheng!
Seeing that Luo Zheng easily dealt with her Law of Time, An Xin gently bit her lips and directly said, "You will participate in this Torch Inheritance, right?"
"Torch Inheritance?" Luo Zheng was stunned, "What's that?"
Seeing that Luo Zheng didn't know anything, An Xin whispered, "You don't even know about the Torch Inheritance. As expected of an ascender …"
On the other side, Yun Luo patted her head and said to Luo Zheng, "Sorry, I didn't have time to tell you. Luo Zheng, you must participate in this Torch Inheritance."
Luo Zheng shrugged, "I don't care, but I want to know what this Torch Inheritance is."
Yun Luo told him, "The Torch Inheritance is a way of testing one's potential in the Thirteen Palaces. It is held every once in a while." After thinking for a while, she said, "You can think of it as the tower of the Heavenly Immortal Clan!"
Yun Luo had the complete memory of the Tower of Sin!
She didn't forget that Luo Zheng had obtained the title of Golden Heavenly Immortal.
In the Great Yan Era, only a few people had obtained this title. Yun Luo was naturally looking forward to Luo Zheng's performance in the Torch Inheritance.
As for the Old Palace Master's disciple, of course, she was also good, but it seemed that she was overestimating herself if she wanted to compete with Luo Zheng in the Torch Inheritance!
If the Old Palace Master knew that Luo Zheng had the title of Golden Heavenly Immortal, he would probably immediately dispel the idea of "An Xin".
However, Yun Luo certainly wouldn't take the initiative to tell her this. This little girl was young, but because of her extraordinary talent, she was spoiled by Xiao Daolai to be so conceited. As an elder, Yun Luo couldn't stand it. This kind of girl should be defeated by Luo Zheng!
Of course, Yun Luo only subconsciously felt that An Xin was inferior to Luo Zheng, but the real situation was hard to say. The universe was so big, and there were so many strange people. Many things could inadvertently produce unexpected results. It all depended on the performance of the two people in the Torch Inheritance.
"Oh, I understand." Luo Zheng nodded. He turned to the woman in green and said, "I will participate in the Torch Inheritance. Why do you ask?"
An Xin stared at Luo Zheng and said indifferently, "I will leave you far behind!"
"And then?" Luo Zheng asked again, a stubborn smile hanging on his face.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, how could she continue to answer Luo Zheng's question? She just glared fiercely at Luo Zheng and returned to her master's side.
The smell of gunpowder between the two juniors was nothing in the eyes of Xiao Daolai and Yun Luo. The world of martial artists had always been like this. Only competition could bring out one's limits and potential.
Luo Zheng temporarily put aside the issue of the Torch Inheritance. He held the jade slip in his hand and thanked Xiao Daolai politely. No matter what, this mental cultivation method was quite useful to him. He had taken away this person's sword and received this mental cultivation method for free. Luo Zheng's gratitude was justified.
After leaving Yun Luo's place, Luo Zheng also learned about the contents of the Torch Inheritance.
The Torch Inheritance could indeed be used to test a martial artist's potential and talent, but its function was still biased towards testing …
The Tower of Sin, also known as the Exquisite Pagoda, was a place to hone a martial artist's will to fight. The Talent Stele was an important part of it, but the focus was on fighting and cultivation. Not only could one obtain good rewards from the Torch Inheritance, but there also seemed to be some very important Human Inheritance. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't refuse. He was even faintly looking forward to it. Now, Luo Zheng wouldn't give up any opportunity to enhance his strength.
A few days later, Luo Zheng was in the main hall, holding the five-foot-long Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword.
As Luo Zheng's fingers trembled slightly, the Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword shot out a sword light and shot through the air!
There were no lightning or wind blades in the sword light.
At this time, Luo Zheng had complete control of the first-grade divine tool. He temporarily filtered out the power of the two Laws and only stimulated the figure within.
The mental cultivation method that the old Palace Master passed on to Luo Zheng was called the Ghost Mental Cultivation Method, which allowed him to control the "Ghost Shadow" in the Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword.
The first time he had used this sword, the figure had only used a simple punch. After a few days of practice and using the Nether Spirit Mental Cultivation Method, the figure's movements had become much more complicated.
Whoosh!
The figure in the hall sped out. After taking a stride forward, he twisted his body in a snake-like manner. Then, with another roll, he gradually dissipated ….
The sword stabbed out again, and the other figure shot out again. The figure's fist punched out densely. In a short time, it punched out a full set of punches, and then gradually disappeared.
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng gently put away the sword, and his face fell into deep thought.
If one sword could stab out a Ghost Shadow, then wouldn't he be able to stab out several Ghost Shadows consecutively?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng extracted a lot of Primal Chaos Qi from his Dantian and poured it into the long sword. His eyes suddenly revealed a murderous aura, and the Thundergale Ghost Divine Sword nimbly waved!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
In this breath, Luo Zheng stabbed out ten times!
Ten stabs in less than a blink of an eye. For an ordinary swordsman, this wasn't a difficult task.
However, when he stabbed out ten times, ten Ghost Shadows appeared in front of him, and each of them had a different movement technique …

Chapter 1038
As long as it was a divine artifact, it was basically an item with a great origin, let alone a first-grade divine artifact.
Although the Thundergale Ghost Sword couldn't be listed in the Chaotic Myriad Spirit Stele, it could definitely be called a masterpiece.
Xiao Daolai was the owner of this sword. In the past, he could at most slash out three Serene Spirit Shadows at the same time, and according to the description in the 《Serene Spirit Mental Cultivation Method》, this Thunder Wind Serene Spirit Sword could at most slash out three times …
It wasn't that Xiao Daolai's sword wasn't fast enough. At his level, his understanding of swordsmanship wasn't something Luo Zheng could compare with. It was just that when the fourth Ghost Shadow appeared, the first Ghost Shadow would disappear. In other words, only three Ghost Shadows would appear side by side at any time.
But now, Luo Zheng could make ten Ghost Shadows appear side by side with a casual slash of his sword. In fact, he had broken the rules of the Ghost Mental Cultivation Method.
As for the reason, Luo Zheng couldn't say. He could only attribute the problem to the Chaotic Qi. It seemed that any cultivation method driven by the Chaotic Qi would be much more effective. Even the laws were the same.
However, the Ghost Shadows were really very interesting.
Using the Ghost Shadows, Luo Zheng could derive countless battle tactics. For example, several Ghost Shadows could cooperate with each other at a time, or directly use the human wave tactic to swarm up. There was a lot of room for him to display.
In addition to these Ghost Shadows, Luo Zheng originally had his own sword spirit, Xun. She could even hide among the Ghost Shadows and carry out assassinations.
Now that Xun's Soul Strength had recovered by more than half, she, who possessed a Silver Soul, could even kill some experts if she caught them off guard.
The news of Luo Zheng entering the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace quickly attracted attention because of the matter of him taking the sword.
And the news of Luo Zheng's sword was even more hotly discussed in the Heavenly Palace.
The cultivators of the Lingyun Hall didn't dare to find trouble with Luo Zheng. Although the cultivators of the Swallow Cloud Hall were very concerned about this matter, and some genius cultivators even threatened to challenge Luo Zheng, they didn't do it in the end.
On the other hand, the few talented cultivators of the Swallow Cloud Hall were very indifferent. They didn't seem to have much interest in Luo Zheng.
After all, those super geniuses were quite reserved. Although the Thundergale Ghost Sword was attractive, if they went to find trouble with a Life and Death cultivator who had just entered the Heavenly Palace, they wouldn't be so unsightly.
As for the legendary sword Ghost Shadow, they didn't seem to care about it.
Because the Torch Flame was about to be ignited, no one paid attention to Luo Zheng. All the geniuses were actively preparing. Through the Torch Flame inheritance, not only could they confirm their status, but they could also obtain some extraordinary rewards.
Therefore, Luo Zheng slowly immersed himself in what happened after he came to Misty Clouds Palace.
Luo Zheng himself did not want to stir up trouble. Now that no one was looking for trouble with him, he was naturally happy to be free.
...........
He was even considering if he should get a spear for Xun.
With Xun's current soul strength, she was no longer a good match for Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit. However, her fusion with the Sword Spirit was a kind of "attachment" to Luo Zheng. So, Luo Zheng naturally let her be.
But a Saint Tool Spear didn't match Xun's current strength!
At this stage, helping Xun get a Divine Tool Spear was also an urgent matter.
However, to enter the Divine Weapon Hall, he needed to spend three hundred Zhen Yuanyu … Getting so many Zhen Yuanyu was still not an easy thing for Luo Zheng.
In fact, borrowing money from Yun Luo was also possible, but it didn't fit Luo Zheng's character. It was better to wait until the Flame Inheritance was opened!
This peace only lasted for a few days. Soon, Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace became noisy again. It wasn't because of Luo Zheng, but because of Luo Zheng's neighbor …
The woman called "Anxin" was called Ai Anxin.
She was brought to Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace by the old Palace Master. Her goal was to participate in the Flame Inheritance!
But now, with the departure of the old Palace Master, a residence was naturally arranged in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. The area Luo Zheng lived in was the area for Cloud Mist Hall warriors. The new warriors were basically settled in this area. Ai Anxin joined Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, so she was naturally a member of Cloud Mist Hall. It wasn't surprising that Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin became neighbors.
Luo Zheng didn't have any objections to this. Anyway, under normal circumstances, he didn't step out of his house. He usually stayed in his house to cultivate.
But although Ai Anxin looked like a slender and weak woman, she was really restless!
On the first day, she announced that she wanted to challenge all the warriors in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, whether they were from Cloud Mist Hall or Swallow Cloud Hall …
She used the "Lion's Roar Command" to issue this challenge!
The "Tiger's Roar Command" was similar to the "Roar Command". Of course, the "Tiger's Roar Command" wasn't as precious as the "Roar Command", nor did it have the abnormal ability to transmit sound to the entire universe.
Below the "Roar Command", there were other treasures that could transmit sound, such as the "Dragon's Roar Command", "Lion's Roar Command", and "Tiger's Roar Command". The "Dragon's Roar Command" could transmit sound to the range of ten realms, while the "Lion's Roar Command" could transmit sound to the range of one realm.
As for the "Tiger's Roar Command", it could transmit sound to the range of hundreds of miles. In fact, some Divine Sea Realm warriors could transmit their sound to such a large range without using the "Tiger's Roar Command". Ai Anxin should be able to do it as well. But to use her True Essence to transmit her voice was a bit unreserved.
However, the "Tiger's Roar Command" was the same as the "Roar Command". Both of them could forcibly transmit sound to the mind of others …
The Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace was filled with geniuses from all walks of life who had come here to cultivate. Everyone was busy with their cultivation. Occasionally, they would be disturbed by the thunderous roar. After all, Heavenly Lords would not bother about it.
However, the moment she entered the Misty Clouds Heavenly Palace, she used the "Tiger's Roar Command" to make a ruckus. Moreover, she directly challenged the warriors of the Lingyun Hall and the Swallow Cloud Hall. Her words were full of contempt, which was somewhat inappropriate.
Her challenge was like a spark that had been thrown into kerosene. It immediately ignited the anger of the martial artists from the two Halls!
The warriors of the two halls weren't the only ones who were annoyed. Some of the deacons and some of the Deputy Palace Masters were also annoyed. However, when they heard that Ai Anxin was sent here by the Old Palace Master, they didn't say anything. Anyway, the super geniuses of the Lingyun Hall and the Swallow Cloud Hall would naturally find trouble with this girl. She had to pay the price for her arrogance.
Originally, because of the shock of Luo Zheng's sword, the area in front of his door gradually quieted down. When the warriors of the Lingyun Hall passed by his door, they even felt a sense of fear. They knew that the person inside was someone they couldn't afford to offend.
Because of Ai Anxin's ruckus, the area in front of his door suddenly became lively again. After all, he was this girl's neighbor, and his door was right next to hers …
Early in the morning, Luo Zheng cultivated in seclusion for the whole night.
He quietly lit three concentrating incense sticks. The faint fragrance could make people feel refreshed and refreshed.
At this moment, there was already a commotion outside the house.
"I, Lu Zhan Yang of the Swallow Cloud Hall, accept your challenge …"
Then, there was another round of cheering outside the house.
"Come on, Senior Brother Zhan Yang!"
"This girl is too arrogant. She should be taught a lesson!"
"Lu Zhan Yang is ranked seventy-ninth in the Swallow Cloud Hall. This girl shouldn't be a match for him …"
Hearing this noise, Luo Zheng couldn't help but smile bitterly. This was really not going to stop.
In the past few days, four or five warriors had come to challenge Ai Anxin in succession. Furthermore, they were all warriors of the Swallow Cloud Hall. From the 400th to the 200th to the 100th …
Right now, this person called Lu Zhan Yang was ranked seventy-ninth.
The previous few warriors had all suffered a crushing defeat in front of Ai Anxin.
The more the warriors of the Swallow Cloud Hall lost, the more they wanted to win back.
If the Swallow Cloud Hall didn't win a single battle, the challenges wouldn't end …
Now, Luo Zheng sincerely hoped that Lu Zhan Yang could defeat Ai Anxin. Otherwise, there would really be no end to this.
Xun glanced at the door indifferently and said, "This Lu Zhan Yang is not a match for Ai Anxin."
Her soul perception was much stronger than Luo Zheng's. Even from such a distance, she could determine the strength of the opponent.
Luo Zheng curled his lips helplessly. What happened next would be exactly the same as a few days ago.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1039
If nothing unexpected happened …
Soon, waves of True Yuan erupted outside the house.
Under the violent Qi, the door frame and windows of Luo Zheng's small palace shook unceasingly, as if a violent storm was blowing outside.
After a few breaths of time, a blood-curdling scream came from outside the house. Soon after, Luo Zheng heard a gust of wind. He frowned. In the next moment, his door was smashed by a heavy object!
It was Lu Zhan Yang's body that smashed his door.
After smashing through the door at an extremely fast speed, the momentum of his body did not decrease at all. He directly crushed a messy trench on the floor, then penetrated the wall of the palace. After smashing a large hole, he flew out of Luo Zheng's small palace.
Luo Zheng stood there alone, curling his lips helplessly. This was already the second time.
The cultivators outside the house sighed.
"Sigh, Lu Zhan Yang also lost so easily!"
"Could this Life and Death woman really be able to overturn the entire Swallow Cloud Hall by herself?"
"I'm really not convinced!"
While sighing, they faintly sized up Luo Zheng …
Ai Anxin and Luo Zheng were both newcomers to the Misty Cloud Palace. Moreover, the two had become neighbors.
Both of them were in the Life and Death realm. However, Ai Anxin's cultivation was slightly higher, at the ninth layer of Life and Death realm, while Luo Zheng was in the fifth layer.
Seeing the Swallow Cloud Hall's cultivators being crushed by Ai Anxin one after another, they couldn't help but compare Luo Zheng with Ai Anxin.
These two were both monstrous geniuses. Who was stronger?
Unfortunately, this Luo Zheng seemed to only know how to cultivate and didn't have any intention of challenging Ai Anxin.
When he first came in, he caused a disturbance by taking out his sword. However, it quickly calmed down. Everyone knew that this Luo Zheng wasn't a troublemaker.
"Is there any way for Luo Zheng to challenge Ai Anxin?"
"What can I do? Can't you see that Ai Anxin is doing this on purpose? She deliberately threw Lu Zhanyang into Luo Zheng's palace. This is a blatant provocation. Last time, Luo Zheng tolerated it. This time, he will probably tolerate it again … "
"Our Misty Clouds Palace is filled with geniuses, yet we are suppressed by a woman. How embarrassing …"
Luo Zheng followed the groove on the floor to the door. At this time, Ai Anxin, who was dressed in green, also came forward.
Before Luo Zheng could speak, Ai Anxin said, "I'll pay for the damage …"
Luo Zheng frowned. "It's not a matter of compensation. This is the second time. You did it on purpose."
Ai Anxin chuckled but refused to admit it. "How could that be? In this challenge, there will always be a mistake. I'll get someone to fix it for you!"
Before Luo Zheng could speak, the warriors on the other side had already opened their mouths.
"This woman doesn't even blush when she lies!"
"He did it on purpose!"
"Just now, she directly lifted Ai Anxin and threw her here on purpose. I saw it clearly!"
After those warriors finished speaking, Luo Zheng stared coldly at Ai Anxin. "Did you hear that?"
"I didn't hear it." Ai Anxin smiled with a carefree expression.
"Misty Clouds Palace has a dreamy space specially used for challenges. You won't get hurt in the dreamy space, and you can use your full strength. But every time you accept a challenge in front of my door and disturb my cultivation. What do you mean?" Luo Zheng stared at Ai Anxin and said.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was really angry, she curled her lips. "It's very simple. If you are willing to compete with me, I won't bother you!"
"I'm not interested." How could Luo Zheng not know what this woman was thinking? It was just that he really wasn't interested now.
Ai Anxin smiled faintly. "Then I'm afraid I'll continue to trouble you in the future. In this challenge, mistakes are inevitable …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a headache. Why did he have to get into trouble for no reason?!
Soon, some craftsmen came to help Luo Zheng repair the palace. Then, Ai Anxin's challenges continued. Every time she "made a mistake," she would bring disaster to Luo Zheng's house.
However, after enduring for a few days, Luo Zheng seemed to have finally calmed down.
In the past few days, the people who challenged Ai Anxin in the Swallow Cloud Hall were ranked higher and higher. Ranked 60, 32, 17, 9 …
The last warrior was ranked fourth in the Swallow Cloud Hall. His battle with Ai Anxin lasted for nearly two hours!
Warriors who had reached this realm could split mountains and split the earth with a raise of their hand. Previously, they hadn't caused too much damage because Ai Anxin had an overwhelming advantage. Usually, she would win in one move. However, this warrior ranked fourth in the Swallow Cloud Hall was indeed not weak. One could imagine the damage they had caused in the two hours of battle.
Luo Zheng's small palace had been completely obliterated. Not only his house, but the surrounding dozen or so palaces that were meant for martial artists to live in had all been razed to the ground. Only Ai Anxin's house remained standing. She seemed to have used some sort of secret divine runic technique to strengthen her house …
He sat on the only intact piece of floorboard and felt some regret. If he had known this would happen, he would have accepted this woman's challenge.
The fourth ranked warrior of the Swallow Cloud Hall was ultimately defeated by Ai Anxin.
After she clapped her hands, she looked at Luo Zheng, who was sitting on the floorboard with an innocent expression. She smiled faintly and lightly tapped her toes on the ground. Like a little bird, she floated in front of Luo Zheng and said apologetically, "I'm sorry … I'll compensate."
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. He was too lazy to speak.
However, Ai Anxin was very efficient. The flattened palaces were quickly repaired in a few hours.
Her current status in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace was rapidly rising because of the battle with the fourth ranked warrior of the Swallow Cloud Hall!
Everyone understood that Ai Anxin was probably a strong competitor for the Torch Flame Inheritance!
She had already challenged the fourth ranked warrior of the Swallow Cloud Hall. This cycle of challenges had come to an end.
The top three warriors of the Swallow Cloud Hall were also top geniuses of some Great Worlds. For the Torch Flame Inheritance, they had all gone into seclusion. Naturally, they couldn't come out to challenge Ai Anxin.
Luo Zheng also had a rare moment of peace and quiet. Ai Anxin had finally calmed down …
Unknowingly, another month passed. During this month, all the warriors were focused on cultivating and preparing for the upcoming Torch Flame Inheritance.
During this month, the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace was very quiet. Except for one day, when the light of six stars shone down from the sky, which attracted many warriors' guesses.
Normally, when a star produced a phenomenon, it was mostly because some martial artist practiced a cultivation method related to the stars. And, cultivation methods related to the stars were all extremely powerful cultivation methods. However, they couldn't guess who this person was …
Luo Zheng's "Starry Battle Body" didn't go to waste. During the last period of time in the Lower World, Luo Zheng still tried to communicate with the stars, hoping to attract more stars. However, attracting stars was a matter of luck. He could only try again and again!
In the Lower Realm, every time Luo Zheng's perception left his body, it would take nearly a night. A round trip would often take a day.
Now that he had ascended to the Upper World, Luo Zheng was much closer to the stars. The time he spent was greatly reduced. Therefore, whenever night fell, Luo Zheng would tirelessly try to communicate with the stars.
As he continued to experiment like this, the number of stars that Luo Zheng had attracted had increased to six.
This was the only transcendent divine might in his possession. Although it was only half of an incomplete transcendent divine might, Luo Zheng would still work hard to cultivate it. The opponents he would face in the future were far too powerful. Not to mention Luo Yan, but the Demon Night Clan's King of Punishment, Yao, was far stronger than Luo Zheng could imagine. Besides painstakingly cultivating, he had no other choice.
One month later …
A voice resounded throughout the entire Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
Yun Luo's figure was like a pure white lotus, floating alone in the skies. She lightly said, "The Torch Flame Inheritance has begun!"
After speaking, several more figures floated out. They were Xu Can and several other Vice Palace Masters.

Chapter 1040
Whether it was the Soaring Cloud Hall or the Swallow Cloud Hall, all of them walked out of their residences and looked at the crimson ring in the sky.
Some of them looked excited, while others looked gloomy.
Only by passing through the Crimson Ring would one be qualified to follow the Palace Master and participate in the Torch Inheritance. However, not everyone could pass through the Crimson Ring.
In fact, the warriors who joined the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace more or less had a certain degree of understanding of their talents. Everyone knew if they could pass through the Crimson Ring.
Those who were excited were very confident in their talents. Those who looked gloomy thought they could not pass through the Crimson Ring at all.
"I heard that only warriors with at least five leaves are qualified to pass through the Crimson Ring!"
"Not necessarily. There have been Three-leaf warriors who passed through the Crimson Ring before. However, there is some basis to the story of five leaves. Most of the warriors who passed through the Crimson Ring have opened the Five Leaves."
"I only opened the Three Leaves when I went through the Tribulation last year. Does that mean I have no hope?"
Many warriors looked at the Crimson Ring and discussed among themselves.
At this moment, a figure shot up into the sky and flew toward the Crimson Ring.
"It's Zhan Hongtang of the Swallow Cloud Hall. He must be qualified to pass through the Crimson Ring!"
Zhan Hongtang flew up at an extremely fast speed. In the blink of an eye, he was in front of the Crimson Ring. Without any hesitation, his body flashed and passed through the Crimson Ring.
Whoosh!
The moment he passed through the Crimson Ring, the entire Crimson Ring bloomed with a dazzling crimson halo.
"What a bright red light. I heard that Zhan Hongtang was a genius who opened the Five Leaves Lotus a few years ago. With such a bright red light, I guess he has opened the Seven Leaves!"
After Zhan Hongtang passed through the Crimson Ring, he stood behind a Vice Palace Master. He was introduced to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace by the Vice Palace Master, so he could be considered the Vice Palace Master's direct disciple.
The Vice Palace Master smiled slightly, looking a little proud. Zhan Hongtang's face was also arrogant as he looked down at the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
After the first person passed through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, very quickly, the second martial artist also flew up!
When the warrior was about to pass through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, the entire Scarlet Afterglow Ring suddenly emitted a black light. Then, a transparent membrane appeared in the middle of the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, blocking the warrior outside.
"Failed?"
"Well, this guy is a martial artist from Lingyun Hall. His talent is not enough to pass the Red Cloud Ring!"
"The passing rate of Lingyun Hall is not high!"
A look of disappointment appeared on the cultivator's face. Although he was not convinced, he did not dare to question the Palace Master. He could only leave unhappily.
Following that, there were martial artists flying towards the Scarlet Afterglow Ring in the sky!
But looking at the speed and momentum of their flight, it was actually possible to tell whether or not they would be able to pass through the Scarlet Dawn Ring. Those martial artists who were extremely confident were flying at full speed, almost without slowing down! Such martial artists, eight or nine out of ten, would be able to pass through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring. Of course, there were very few who were blindly confident and rushed towards the Scarlet Afterglow Ring at an extremely fast speed. As a result, they were directly bounced back by the membrane, making a fool of themselves.
And those who slowed down and hesitated, most of them could not pass through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring. They were well aware of their talent and were just giving it a try. Of course, there were also a small number of martial artists who originally thought that they would not be able to pass through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, but inexplicably passed through it. Their faces were immediately filled with ecstasy!
Luo Zheng looked at the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, the corners of his mouth slightly raised. He was not in a hurry to rush in like a swarm of bees. It was better to wait until there were fewer people.
But at this moment, he noticed the gaze of Ai Anxin, who was next to him, and he suddenly frowned.
Luo Zheng felt that this woman was very conceited and very willing to accept challenges and challenges. She had been waiting for an opportunity to compete with him.
The problem was that Luo Zheng was not very interested in this kind of thing.
After experiencing the Spiritual Dilemma, he understood his goal. What he needed to do was to achieve his goal step by step, and not to prove anything to others. Or rather, he had already passed this stage. Clearly, this Ai Anxin had not passed this stage …
"The more lotus leaves you open, the stronger the reaction to the Lotus Flower, and the brighter the Scarlet Afterglow Ring should be." Ai Anxin said with a smile, "I wonder how many leaves you have opened?"
Luo Zheng pursed his lips and replied, "I'm not sure."
"You don't want to say?" Ai Anxin smiled.
Luo Zheng shrugged. He was indeed unwilling to say.
Just as the two of them were talking, another blue figure flew up. It was a martial artist dressed in blue. As he flew up, his body exuded a fierce aura.
"It's Xue Muyang!"
"The number one monster in Swallow Cloud Hall!"
"That Xue Muyang should be a nine-leaf Lotus Flower martial artist. It's certain that he will pass through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring …"
"I heard that he is also an ascender! He ascended six years ago! "
The moment Xue Muyang passed through the Crimson Afterglow Ring, the entire Crimson Afterglow Ring bloomed with an even more blazing light. Rays of crimson afterglow lingered on the ring and fluctuated, causing the faces of everyone in the Heavenly Palace to turn red!
There were already hundreds of martial artists who had passed through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, and this Xue Muyang was the one who shone the brightest when passing through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring!
After passing through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, the corners of his mouth curled up slightly, and he flew towards Yun Luo.
Yun Luo smiled faintly, "Not bad!"
"It's far from enough," Xue Muyang said, "I will prove myself to you in the Torch Inheritance!"
The expression on Yun Luo's face was a little unnatural, but she said, "Don't say anymore!"
However, Xue Muyang did not listen at all, "I joined the Misty Cloud Palace for you! I will prove myself to you. I will prove to you that I am the main character of this great world. Just give me twenty years, twenty years, and I will definitely catch up to you! I will be a good match for you! "
Hearing this, many martial artists, including the vice palace masters, had a strange expression on their faces!
In front of so many people, he actually expressed his true feelings to the palace master, this was too ….
The key was that the difference in strength between the two was too great. Xue Muyang was only at the Divine Ocean Realm, while Yun Luo was already a half step sector lord. Although martial artists respected strength, other than the Yao Ye Clan, there were far more male martial artists than female martial artists, and they were usually stronger than female martial artists.
If Xue Muyang was really interested in Yun Luo, he had to consider his own cultivation first!
Yun Luo's face sank when she heard this. She said coldly, "Xue Muyang, what you saw and chased was only a clone created by my cultivation method. It was only an illusion. Please don't have such boring dreams!"
After being scolded by Yun Luo, Xue Muyang gritted his teeth and stood behind Yun Luo. Although his expression was not good, his eyes were still burning with passion.
"Who can tell me what's going on with this Xue Muyang?"
"Haven't you heard? Palace Master Yun Luo cultivates the Million Soul Clones Technique. She severed a million clones and scattered them in many lower realms to cultivate. Xue Muyang was originally a lower realm cultivator, but he fell in love with one of Palace Master Yun Luo's clones! "
"He's also a weirdo. It's said that the upper realm he ascended to is more than 60 great realms away from our great realm! He relied on his perseverance and luck to cross so many great realms and arrive at Misty Cloud Palace! "
"He's not really a weirdo. His talent is high, and he'll be valued in any sacred land. As long as he has a request, there's no reason for them to not send him to Misty Cloud Palace!"
"However, he fell in love with one of the palace master's clones. Now that the palace master has taken back all her clones ….. it's not the same thing!"
Hearing the discussions, Luo Zheng's face was filled with shock.
He roughly understood the reason ….
Xue Muyang, as an ascender, encountered Yun Luo's clone in the lower realms, but it was just a clone. He was trying to prove his talent and strength to pursue Yun Luo.

Chapter 1041
When Luo Zheng first came into contact with Yun Luo after he ascended, he had a very strange feeling.
In fact, this strange feeling had not disappeared until now.
In Luo Zheng's view, the Yun Luo in front of him was no different from the two Yun Luos in the mortal world in terms of appearance, but in fact, they were completely different people!
In other words, the Yun Luo Luo Zheng once knew had disappeared and was swallowed up by the Yun Luo in front of him.
Although Palace Master Yun Luo in front of him had inherited the memories of the two Yun Luos, it was only two out of a million memories after all.
The reason why Yun Luo had a deep impression of Luo Zheng was that she had extracted the memories of Luo Zheng from the memories of millions of Yun Luos …
If Yun Luo had not extracted the memories of Xue Muyang, Xue Muyang would have been equivalent to a stranger to Yun Luo.
Even though Xue Muyang had followed Yun Luo to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace and his outstanding performance had attracted Yun Luo's attention, and she had also understood Xue Muyang's intentions, this still did not change the fact that he was a stranger to her.
However, looking at Xue Muyang's posture, who seemed to be determined not to give up until he achieved his goal, Luo Zheng could only shake his head slightly. So what if he proved his strength in the future? Feelings were not won by strength. If Yun Luo was an ordinary woman, it would be fine, but she was the son of a Celestial Master, and now she was in a high position, so how could she give herself to anyone casually?
Of course, this matter had nothing to do with Luo Zheng …
The cultivators below still flew towards the Scarlet Afterglow Ring in an orderly manner. Those who passed stood behind the Palace Masters and Vice Palace Masters, while those who failed left dejectedly. Gradually, the number of cultivators flying up became fewer and fewer.
"Aren't you going?" Ai Anxin asked.
"You can go up first," Luo Zheng suggested.
Ai Anxin said, "Actually, I asked you to go first … but since you asked me to go first, I'll go first!"
After saying that, Ai Anxin tiptoed and circled in the air. Then, she nimbly moved towards the Scarlet Afterglow Ring. When she turned around, she even turned around and smiled at Luo Zheng. Then, she passed through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring with her back to him.
The moment Ai Anxin passed through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, a scarlet radiance burst out from one end of the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, spreading rapidly along the ring!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The entire Scarlet Afterglow Ring began to hum!
At the same time, dazzling rays of light surged like waves on the Scarlet Cloud Ring, refusing to disperse!
It was also because the light was too dazzling that the Scarlet Afterglow Ring no longer looked like a ring-shaped object in the eyes of the crowd. Instead, it looked like a huge fireball, like a sun.
"What a strong reaction, this woman! Her lotus flower has opened up a few leaves! "
"Xue Muyang is already at the Nine-leaf stage. Could it be that this woman has surpassed the Nine-leaf stage?"
"No wonder she's so arrogant and dares to challenge the entire Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. Her talent is truly unimaginable …"
The few Vice Palace Masters of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace looked surprised as well. They remembered this woman. Not long ago, she had used the Tiger's Roar Token to challenge all the geniuses in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. The Vice Palace Masters had asked around and found out that she was the Old Palace Master's direct disciple.
Since the Old Palace Master had placed her here, they would just let her be.
The Old Palace Master's direct disciple was, naturally, not weak in terms of strength and talent. However, they did not expect Ai Anxin to trigger such a strong reaction from the Scarlet Afterglow Ring.
Yun Luo looked surprised as well. "For the Scarlet Afterglow Ring to react like this, her lotus flower should be at the Nine-leaf stage as well!"
"Mhm." One of the Vice Palace Masters nodded. "That should be the case. If I'm not mistaken, she should be at the peak of the Nine-leaf stage. If she goes through ten minor Heavenly Tribulations, there's a chance she'll grow a Mahayana lotus flower!"
"The Mahayana Lotus Flower …" Another Vice Palace Master sighed. "The Old Palace Master's luck is so good. Not only was he able to become a Celestial Venerable and escape the Five Decay of Heaven, but he also picked up such a treasure!"
Those who possessed the Mahayana Lotus Flower were few and far between even among the various races in the entire universe!
Yun Luo nodded and smiled faintly. "This woman should have a place in the great world …"
However, Yun Luo did not dare to speak with absolute certainty. The great world had just begun, and the Battle of the Great Worlds would last for a long time. Many things were unknown and could not be judged. Even if one had a heaven-defying Luck and was the favorite of this era, one might not necessarily become the main character!
Moreover, in Yun Luo's opinion, this woman was too arrogant. Although Yun Luo did not leave her palace, she had heard some news. In particular, Ai Anxin had repeatedly provoked Luo Zheng. To put it bluntly, Ai Anxin was simply courting death.
If she kept courting death, even heaven-defying Luck would not be able to save her!
Naturally, Yun Luo did not care about this. She did not give Luo Zheng any advice, nor did she give Luo Zheng any special treatment. Since Luo Zheng had Dual Birth Charts, she would let Luo Zheng walk his own path.
"Luo Zheng, why haven't you come up yet?" Yun Luo glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently.
In fact, Luo Zheng and Xue Muyang's identities were very similar. Both of them were ascenders and both of them had encountered Yun Luo's clone in the lower realm. Both of them were very outstanding …
However, when Yun Luo recalled the memories, the shock that Luo Zheng gave her was much stronger than Xue Muyang's! Therefore, Yun Luo's impression of Luo Zheng was much deeper than Xue Muyang's.
Ai Anxin stood in the sky. She was introduced to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace by the Old Palace Master. Logically speaking, she should stand behind Yun Luo. However, she was floating alone on the side. As for the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, it was still extraordinarily gorgeous. The fiery red glow shone on her face.
She felt everyone's shocked gazes. Her expression was somewhat calm, but she was slightly proud of herself!
Being proud did not mean that she was satisfied.
Her goal was definitely not limited to the small Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. In the future, she would face the top geniuses of the entire universe. She wanted to win a place for herself in the only great battle of the era!
And this Torch Flame Inheritance was just the beginning.
As the glow slowly dimmed, the entire Scarlet Afterglow Ring returned to its calm state.
Behind her, a few more warriors flew up. At this time, she was also staring at Luo Zheng below. "Luo Zheng, it's yours. Don't you plan to participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance?"
Luo Zheng had rejected her challenge time and time again. She had no choice but to choose to defeat Luo Zheng in the Torch Flame Inheritance!
Hearing Ai Anxin's words, Luo Zheng smiled faintly. Many people also focused their gazes on Luo Zheng.
During this period of time, the two newcomers with the most outstanding performances were Ai Anxin and Luo Zheng. In fact, many warriors in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace were also curious. If these two fought, how would they determine the winner?
However, Luo Zheng had only fought twice in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. The first time, he defeated a warrior from Lingyun Hall, and the second time, he almost killed Swallow Cloud Hall's Young Master Jing Xuan!
However, Jing Xuan was only ranked around 200 in Swallow Cloud Hall … So it was difficult to determine Luo Zheng's true strength through Jing Xuan.
However, Ai Anxin had defeated more than ten people in a row. Among them, there were those ranked in the top hundreds of Swallow Cloud Hall, and there were also those ranked in the tens. Ai Anxin had also defeated the fourth-ranked warrior in Swallow Cloud Hall!
When Ai Anxin passed through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, the light she attracted was several times more than Xue Muyang's. It also made everyone understand that this woman's potential seemed to be far beyond their imagination.
Therefore, although the vast majority of martial artists did not like Ai Anxin, compared to Luo Zheng, their hearts were still biased towards her …
"Come up quickly!" Ai Anxin stretched out her hand toward Luo Zheng. Her five slender white fingers spread open as if she wanted to pull Luo Zheng up from a distance of several hundred feet.
Luo Zheng nodded. Then, he urged the Chaotic Energy to slowly float up into the air.
"Look at Luo Zheng. He doesn't seem to be confident!"
"It seems that no one knows how many lotuses he has opened. Could it be that he has only opened three? Is he afraid that he can't pass through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring? "
"That's impossible …"
"There is a slight possibility. After all, he knows how many lotuses he has opened. Look at those hesitant warriors. Most of them can't pass!"
