Chapter 1042
Hearing those words, a faint smile appeared on Yun Luo's face. She was full of confidence in Luo Zheng.
She didn't know how many lotuses Luo Zheng had opened, and she couldn't get any relevant memories from the memories of her two doppelgangers. Luo Zheng's tribulation was something that happened in the Divine Kingdom Continent. Of her two Yun Luos, one was in the Eastern Territory, and the other was in the Sea God Continent.
In front of Luo Zheng, she didn't ask. Since Luo Zheng was able to obtain a golden heavenly title in the Tower of Sin, it would be a joke if he couldn't even pass the Scarlet Cloud Ring in front of them.
Luo Zheng just maintained a steady speed, slowly flying towards the Scarlet Cloud Ring.
Those who should have passed had already passed, and those who didn't stayed below. Everyone was very curious about whether Luo Zheng could pass the Scarlet Cloud Ring, so everyone's attention was focused on him.
Although Luo Zheng's speed was slow, the Scarlet Cloud Ring was only twenty to thirty feet high. In a short while, Luo Zheng arrived in front of the Scarlet Cloud Ring.
The Scarlet Cloud Ring, which had calmed down, was like a burning iron ring, faintly emitting a dark red light.
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng quietly flew towards the Scarlet Cloud Ring …
Everyone saw very clearly that the Scarlet Cloud Ring didn't produce a transparent film to block Luo Zheng outside. The moment he passed through, the Scarlet Cloud Ring seemed to sense something, and a scarlet light began to accumulate on the ring.
"The momentum of that light … is so fierce. It seems like it's going to surpass Ai Anxin's?"
"It can't be. The light that burst out when Ai Anxin passed through the Scarlet Cloud Ring is probably the limit of the Scarlet Cloud Ring. Since it's the limit, how can it be surpassed?"
"Let's wait and see. There will always be a winner!"
Above the Scarlet Cloud Ring, Ai Anxin also bit her lips and stared at the Scarlet Cloud Ring.
Why did she consider Luo Zheng as her opponent?
It was definitely not because he took away her Master's Thunder and Wind Ghost Divine Sword.
However, that day, her master had secretly told her that Luo Zheng was the same as her. They both had the fate of the Battle of the Ages. Moreover, there seemed to be an additional uncertainty in Luo Zheng's fate. In other words, he was a fateless person.
This Luo Zheng was qualified to be her opponent.
Ai Anxin didn't care about the super geniuses of the Cloud Mist Palace at all. Xue Muyang was not bad, but she still didn't think Xue Muyang was a match for her.
Therefore, ever since she entered the Misty Cloud Palace, she could have been assigned to a very secluded residence, but she chose the small palace in the Lingyun Hall and insisted on being Luo Zheng's neighbor.
After using the Tiger Roar Command to issue a challenge, Ai Anxin hoped that Luo Zheng would challenge him. However, this guy was unmoved and ignored him …
They had provoked him many times and even destroyed his house, but he still ignored them …
In the end, Ai Anxin had no other choice. She couldn't just attack Luo Zheng without saying anything, right?
Although the most important inspection was the inheritance of the Torch Flame, she could see a thing or two from the reaction of the Crimson Cloud Ring.
Seeing the light accumulating on the Crimson Cloud Ring, which should be more intense and more dazzling than hers, Ai Anxin suddenly became nervous. With her proud personality, she believed that there were not many geniuses in the world who could stand shoulder to shoulder with her, and in the Alliance, there should be even fewer people like her!
The light on the Crimson Cloud Ring was constantly accumulating, and it was about to erupt along with the ring.
Yun Luo also watched everything indifferently, and there was a look of disbelief in the depths of her eyes. As for the Vice Palace Master, Xu Can, his face didn't look too good …
However, at this moment, the light accumulating on the Crimson Cloud Ring suddenly disappeared! It wasn't just the glow that had disappeared. Under normal circumstances, the Scarlet Afterglow Ring maintained its dark red appearance. However, at this moment, the dark red color had also disappeared. The entire Scarlet Afterglow Ring had turned into a black iron ring. The ring no longer had any luster.
Everyone was stunned when they saw this scene.
"How could this be?"
"Could it be that the Crimson Cloud Ring is broken …"
"How could it be? The Crimson Cloud Ring is a first-grade divine artifact, and it wasn't attacked. It can't be broken just like that!"
Regardless of whether it was the martial artists above or below, they were all discussing in low voices.
The Crimson Cloud Ring was the screening treasure of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, and this had never happened before!
Luo Zheng had just passed through the Crimson Cloud Ring, and when he saw this scene, he was a little stunned. What was going on?
As for Ai Anxin, she secretly breathed a sigh of relief. Perhaps she didn't think that the Crimson Cloud Ring would be able to prove anything. This was just a small screening before the Torch Flame Inheritance. Even if Luo Zheng could make the Crimson Cloud Ring shine brighter, it didn't mean that Luo Zheng's talent was better than hers.
However, Ai Anxin still didn't want Luo Zheng to surpass her in passing through the Crimson Cloud Ring. But when she saw the Crimson Cloud Ring grow brighter and brighter, she became worried. Now that the Crimson Cloud Ring seemed to have suddenly lost its effect, she was indeed relieved.
Yun Luo gently floated to the Crimson Cloud Ring, and her expression became more and more strange. She glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently, and then reached out to touch the Crimson Cloud Ring. With a gentle touch of her finger, the black Crimson Cloud Ring was activated by her, and it bloomed red again.
"What's going on?" asked Luo Zheng.
Yun Luo stared at Luo Zheng, and the corner of her mouth slightly raised. "You want to try again?"
Luo Zheng nodded, and as Yun Luo asked, he bypassed the Crimson Cloud Ring and went up from below.
The result was exactly the same as last time …
The red light on the Crimson Cloud Ring suddenly extinguished after a while, and the whole Crimson Cloud Ring became black and lost its luster.
Seeing this, Yun Luo blinked her eyes, moved her finger slightly, and said, "Okay, you've passed."
Luo Zheng nodded, and no longer wondered why the Crimson Cloud Ring was like this. He flew into the air and stood side by side with the other warriors who had passed through the Crimson Cloud Ring.
But at this time, Xu Can, the Vice Palace Master, said, "Palace Master, isn't this a bit against the rules? According to the previous rules, to participate in the Torch Inheritance, one must pass through the Crimson Cloud Ring's selection. This Luo Zheng didn't … "
Yun Luo said indifferently, "He has passed through the Crimson Cloud Ring."
"But there's a problem with the Crimson Cloud Ring!" Xu Can argued.
"There's a problem, but he still passed," answered Yun Luo.
Xu Can smiled coldly, and quibbled, "According to what Palace Master said, if the Crimson Cloud Ring is not activated, then everyone can pass through and receive the Torch Inheritance, right? Then what's the point of the Crimson Cloud Ring's selection? If this kid passes through like this, won't it be unfair for the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace? "
In fact, as long as one was not blind, one could see clearly that Luo Zheng had passed through first, and then the Crimson Cloud Ring was extinguished!
However, Xu Can's sophistry resonated with the martial artists below.
About less than ten percent of the warriors in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace had passed through the Crimson Cloud Ring, and the other ninety percent of the warriors could not participate in the Torch Inheritance. Besides being disappointed, they were naturally unhappy and felt that it was unfair.
Hearing Xu Can's sophistry, one by one, they echoed.
"Yes, if we let him pass like this, then everyone can go, right?"
"No, we must let him pass once, or it will be cheating!"
"…"
Hearing the voices of the warriors below, Yun Luo's eyes became colder and colder.
She had already guessed where the problem was, but she did not want to deliberately say it. Just now, when Luo Zheng passed through the Crimson Cloud Ring for the first time, he extinguished the Crimson Cloud Ring's glow. She knew that Luo Zheng had probably opened the Mahayana Lotus!
The Crimson Cloud Ring could only sense the limit of the Lesser Realization Lotus. Beyond this limit, the Crimson Cloud Ring could not respond normally, so it was extinguished in an instant!
According to Yun Luo's previous judgment, Luo Zheng's lotus had at least opened seven leaves, and it was very likely that he had opened the ninth leaf like Xue Muyang, or the peak of the ninth leaf like Ai Anxin. However, she did not expect Luo Zheng to open the Mahayana Lotus at the fifth level of the Life and Death Realm.
However, her judgment was based on the reaction of the Crimson Cloud Ring. As for how many leaves Luo Zheng's Mahayana Lotus had opened? Was it eleven leaves? Or thirteen leaves? Or even fifteen leaves? She still did not ask Luo Zheng.
Facing Xu Can's doubts, she smiled coldly, stared at Xu Can, and said, "In the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, am I the one who has the final say, or are you the one who has the final say?"

Chapter 1043
With Yun Luo's personality, she didn't like to compete with others.
Although she was the Palace Lord of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, she spent most of her time quietly cultivating. She didn't care much about the position of Palace Lord.
As for why Xu Can liked to aggressively find her every time, his purpose was naturally for the position of Palace Lord.
This time, Yun Luo acted out of character and questioned them. Who was the one in charge here?
In the end, Yun Luo was the Palace Lord of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace!
The other Vice Palace Lords didn't have much of an opinion. In fact, they also knew the reason why the Scarlet Glow Ring failed. It was most likely because it couldn't accurately determine Luo Zheng's level, which led to this situation.
However, Luo Zheng was indeed qualified to participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance. If Luo Zheng wasn't qualified, he might have been directly blocked when he passed through the Scarlet Glow Ring.
They were just curious how many lotus leaves Luo Zheng had unfolded.
As for Xu Can, he was just looking for trouble.
Now that Yun Luo was strong, Xu Can's eyes revealed a trace of viciousness, but he didn't dare to say anything.
As for the cultivators below who didn't pass the Scarlet Glow Ring, how could they dare to say anything in front of Yun Luo?
"The Scarlet Glow Ring's screening is complete. The 356 cultivators who passed the Scarlet Glow Ring this time, follow me!"
After speaking, Yun Luo flew in a direction without looking back. Xue Muyang's Swallow Cloud Hall cultivators looked at each other and followed closely behind Yun Luo. As for the other Vice Palace Lords, they also led some cultivators to follow.
Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin followed at the back of the group.
The two of them were considered outliers in Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. Although they were Lingyun Hall cultivators in name, they actually had strength comparable to Swallow Cloud Hall cultivators! The most important thing was that the two of them were Life and Death Realm cultivators, and Luo Zheng was the most ridiculous of them all. He was only at the fifth level of Life and Death Realm …
After following Yun Luo all the way, they left Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace and turned into a spatial passage.
In the next thirty hours, hundreds of people followed Yun Luo and hurried on their way.
Although everyone's speed wasn't slow, traveling through a Great World would take years. Even if they turned into a Great World's passageway, the distance that many martial artists traveled was still insignificant compared to a Great World.
After following Yun Luo out of the spatial tunnel, a big city appeared in front of them. This was not their destination, it was just a relay station.
Of course, this was just a relay station for warriors, but the function of this relay station was that there were tens of millions of living creatures living in the city.
When they arrived at the relay station, they boarded a large ship. Once again, they entered the great world passageway and continued on their way.
Fortunately, there was no need for them to use their Quintessential Essence to fly on this ship. Luo Zheng also found a place to close his eyes and cultivate. While cultivating, Luo Zheng also tried to use the memories of the Great Thousand Realms to check the situation in the lower realm.
Although he was very far away from the Great Thousand World, with the fusion of his soul and will, he could still continuously retrieve the memories in it at any time. However, he could not use the power transfer ability.
When he checked, he discovered that Liu Yu had already ascended.
When he returned to the Sea God's Continent for the second time, Liu Yu had already successfully passed his tribulation. It was very likely that he had been forced to ascend by the Great Thousand World just like himself.
With Liu Yu's talent, he should be able to develop smoothly in the Yao Ye Clan. Luo Zheng thought to himself.
When he looked at the Central Domain again, it seemed to be in a peaceful state while he was away. Su Lingyun's cultivation was steadily increasing, and Xi Youqin was still missing. As for Ning Yudie's figure, it seemed like she had gained some weight.
Luo Zheng suddenly thought of something. When he retrieved the memories again, he realized that Ning Yudie was pregnant!
This was the memory of the Great Thousand World, from its birth to the present moment, it had recorded almost everything that had happened in the past. Luo Zheng was constantly looking up Ning Yudie's memories during this period of time by tracing back the flow of time.
Only then did Yang Kai realize that Ning Yudie had already discovered that he was pregnant a few days before he had left.
"I'm going to be a father?" Luo Zheng was still in a daze, but his face was soon filled with joy. Unfortunately, he only managed to find out about this after he ascended. He realized that this matter was not under Luo Zheng's control. He had wanted to stay in the lower realm for a while, but the Great Thousand World did not allow him to do so.
Now that Luo Zheng had ascended, it would be impossible for him to return to the lower realm in a short period of time. He could only leave Ning Yudie to suffer. She had to face all of this alone.
A warrior's cultivation was to go against the heavens, and a woman's home was a hero's grave. Although Luo Zheng missed her, he knew that there were some things that he could not change. There were bound to be losses. He could not take all the benefits in this world.
He searched through the memories again, but there was still no trace of Xi Youqin. It seemed that she had really been staying in the Extreme Purple Realm, but he did not know what this girl was up to.
At this moment, in the Extreme Purple Realm …
There were three floors and one tower in the Extreme Purple Realm. The third floor was the Purple Jade Tower, the Soul Sweeping Tower, and the Moon Embracing Tower. The first tower was the Indefinite Tower. Among the three floors and one tower, one tower was the highest.
The Indefinite Tower was naturally related to the Indefinite Old Man. However, the Indefinite Tower was indeed an indefinite tower. It was three thousand feet wide and one million feet tall. Rather than calling it a tower, it was more appropriate to call it a huge and majestic building.
At this moment, Xi Youqin was throwing a tantrum on one of the floors of the Indefinite Tower.
"I … I don't want to cultivate anymore! Wuuu … "Xi Youqin waved her hand, and countless giant swords that were floating in the air smashed down with a loud bang. The other warriors under the giant swords quickly dodged.
"Young master, you can't do that!"
"The Tower Master has ordered you to cultivate for two hours today!"
"Young master, your progress is really fast. You're a thousand times faster than ordinary warriors. Cultivating for a while more will bring you great benefits …"
Many people came to Xi Youqin's side and comforted her.
"I don't want to cultivate anymore!" Xi Youqin felt wronged and cried.
Ever since she was born, she had never been so serious in her cultivation. When she was still in the Void Sky City, her family had always let her throw a tantrum. Because of this, even though she possessed the Extreme Purple Yin Body, she was still weaker than some Divine Reflection Realm warriors when she was still in the Divine Core realm, and her foundation was weak.
Luo Zheng had ascended to the Immortal Realm. She wanted to meet Luo Zheng in the Immortal Realm and give him a big surprise. That was why she agreed to enter the Indefinite Tower and become his disciple.
In the beginning, Xi Youqin could still bear it, but as time passed, she couldn't bear it anymore …
A warrior's cultivation was all about cultivation. After reincarnation, it was still reincarnation. How could Xi Youqin, who loved to play the zither and admire the flowers, get used to this?
Therefore, she became more and more resistant to the idea, which made all the other disciples in the Indefinite Tower feel depressed.
Xi Youqin had only been in the Indefinite Tower for a short period of time. Compared to the thousands of years she had spent in the Indefinite Tower, these few months were just a blink of an eye. In these few months, Xi Youqin had continuously broken all kinds of records. Her talent was so outstanding that it made all the other disciples jealous.
The key reason was the Extreme Purple Yin Body. The Extreme Purple Yin Body possessed by ordinary warriors couldn't be compared with hers. Almost all the cultivation techniques in the Extreme Purple Realm were related to the Extreme Purple Yin Body.
What made everyone speechless was that Xi Youqin had only endured for a short period of time, but she couldn't bear it anymore. She wanted to waste her great talent!
Of course, Indefinite Old Man wouldn't allow such a waste. Although Indefinite Old Man had gone out for a few days, he had ordered a few elders to take care of Xi Youqin. He was treating Xi Youqin like a treasure. No wonder she attracted so many jealous gazes.
"Youqin, the path of martial dao is about perseverance and perseverance. If you don't have perseverance, how are you going to achieve great things?" One of the elders said.
"What great things do I want? I have my own husband. I'm just a little girl!" Xi Youqin whimpered.
Another Elder shook his head, "I know that your husband is not a fish in a pond. The world of cultivators is also very cruel. Even if they are husband and wife, there is a reason why they are compatible. In the future, if you want to be together with your husband, the effort you put in today is the most important!"
"Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng is not that kind of person!" Xi Youqin retorted. Anyway, she really couldn't stand this kind of boring cultivation.

Chapter 1044
Upon hearing Xi Youqin's words, another elder sneered and said, "I admit that you're not wrong! However, it's normal for a man to have multiple wives. Since he has ascended to the Upper Realm, it's hard to guarantee that he won't have a new lover. Your strength is too weak, how can you compete with him? "
In fact, this elder was only guessing. She didn't know Luo Zheng, so how could she know what Luo Zheng's character was like? However, she had heard the Pagoda Master mention that Luo Zheng was the little fellow that her old man favored …
However, in order to make Xi Youqin focus on cultivation, she could only say such nonsense. At least, it would make Xi Youqin alert or motivate her to focus on cultivation.
"I don't care …"
It was a pity that this method had been used too many times. No matter how good the effect was, it would be obliterated by the boredom of cultivation!
Xi Youqin sat in a corner and refused to cultivate with her sword.
Seeing Xi Youqin so willful, the elders couldn't do anything about it. They all felt a headache coming on. This little fellow was really difficult to train!
In fact, Xi Youqin's talent was not bad since she was young. At least, it wasn't worse than Xi Xiaojie's. However, she had been stubborn since she was young. Coupled with the fact that she was pampered by everyone, no one dared to force her to cultivate.
Everything had its vanquisher. If there was anyone in this world who could make her listen to him, it would probably be Luo Zheng.
These people weren't nobodies in the Extreme Purple Realm, but they really couldn't do anything to Xi Youqin …
At this moment, a figure quietly appeared not far away.
When Xi Youqin saw the figure, she said with an aggrieved expression, "Master …"
When the others saw that person, they respectfully called out, "Pagoda Master!"
Indefinite Old Man slowly walked back. When he saw Xi Youqin's reaction, he smiled faintly and said, "In a few years, the Illusory Battlefield will open …"
Xi Youqin's face revealed a strange expression. She asked, "Master, what is the Illusory Battlefield?"
Indefinite Old Man smiled and said, "Youqin, you've been to the Illusory Battlefield before, right?"
In the Upper Realm, building the Illusory Battlefield was a very common method. Many Holy Lands had such a battlefield for warriors to compete with each other.
Xi Youqin nodded. Of course, she had been there.
Indefinite Old Man continued, "When the Illusory Battlefield opens, all the younger warriors in the world will enter it to fight and compete with each other! I believe that at that time, Luo Zheng will also enter it! "
Xi Youqin thought for a moment before saying, "Then …"
"If you can enter the Divine Sea Realm, I'll let you enter the Illusory Battlefield. If you can rely on your own strength to fight for a place in the Illusory Battlefield, meeting Luo Zheng will be easy! "Indefinite Old Man said with a smile.
"Ah!" Xi Youqin was stunned for a moment, then a look of surprise appeared on her face.
"However, you'll encounter all the races in the world in the Illusory Battlefield. There are countless geniuses from all kinds of races, and the strength of those super geniuses is not to be underestimated. It won't be easy to meet Luo Zheng …" Indefinite Old Man continued smiling.
"Is that so?" Hearing this, Xi Youqin's expression slowly changed.
In fact, after entering the Indefinite Tower, Xi Youqin understood many things. She naturally understood the advantages of her Extreme Violet Yin Body and the heights she could reach in the future.
However, she was very confident in her talent, but not in her willpower …
As for her lack of willpower, the biggest reason was that she did not know when she would be able to meet Luo Zheng again if she continued to cultivate like this. Ten years? A hundred years? A thousand years?
Just thinking about it made her scalp tingle!
If it was really like what her master said, she would break through to the Divine Sea Realm and wait for the Illusory Battlefield to open. Naturally, she would not give up this opportunity!
"Yes, I can guarantee that if you cultivate diligently, with your talent, the probability of meeting Luo Zheng in the Illusory Battlefield is very high!" Indefinite Old Man said seriously.
Xi Youqin pursed her lips, and a trace of unswerving determination slowly emerged in her eyes. All her perseverance was for Luo Zheng.
"I understand, Master …"
…
…
Luo Zheng's ship entered the trans-boundary passage and began its trans-boundary voyage.
In contrast, Luo Zheng was not as lucky as Xi Youqin. Since she entered the Indefinite Tower, no matter what she wanted, Indefinite Old Man would satisfy her to the best of his ability. Moreover, he had a respected status in the Indefinite Tower and even called her Young Master. As for cultivation, he spent a lot of effort coaxing Xi Youqin to do it …
Now, Luo Zheng had to rely on himself to fight for everything.
It was said that there would be good rewards in the Torch Flame Inheritance. Of course, Luo Zheng would not let it go.
A few days later, the ship finally left the trans-boundary passage, and what appeared in front of everyone was a grotesque and variegated world.
Yun Luo led the people of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace away from the ship and continued to fly forward.
Under everyone's feet was an orange sea. This vast and boundless orange sea indeed gave people a strange feeling.
"This world …" Feeling the strange atmosphere of this world, Luo Zheng could not help but reveal a puzzled look on his face.
On the other hand, Ai Anxin looked indifferent and then said, "This is a Broken World."
"A Broken World? Just like the Purple Extreme Realm? "Luo Zheng asked.
Ai Anxin nodded and then said, "That's why the Alliance set the Torch Flame Inheritance here. This Broken World used to be called the Orange Sea Realm because the sea water in this realm is orange!"
In the Orange Sea Realm's sea water, there were extremely small creatures. There were many of these creatures densely distributed in the sea water, dyeing the entire Orange Sea Realm's sea water orange.
Millions of years ago, the Orange Sea Realm was also a complete great realm. But because several human Empyreans were at odds with each other, they fought in the Orange Sea Realm. The entire great realm could not withstand such a huge amount of power. In the end, the great realm collapsed. Now, the Orange Sea Realm was only a tenth of its former size. It could no longer be considered a complete great realm.
This time, their destination was a huge island in the Orange Sea Realm called Changsu Island. Everyone would participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance on Changsu Island.
In fact, Changsu Island was called an island by Ai Anxin, but that was just the view of the Upper World warriors. According to the standards of the Lower World, Changsu Island was ten times the size of the Central Domain and could be called a huge continent.
After listening to Ai Anxin's introduction, Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Although Ai Anxin came to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace to stir up trouble, he had done his homework.
However, Luo Zheng came from the Lower Realm, and Yun Luo was the only person he knew in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. He wouldn't ask Yun Luo about everything he didn't know, so he didn't know what the Torch Flame inheritance was for.
Two hours later, the people of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace finally entered Changsu Island.
After another hour of walking along Changsu Island, a huge rock appeared in front of everyone's eyes!
The huge rock was more than ten thousand feet high and tens of thousands of feet wide. Someone had carved a poem on the surface of the rock!
The strokes of this poem were as sharp as knives, and it seemed to contain a kind of magical power. With just a glance, many cultivators revealed uncomfortable expressions. It was as if these strokes contained a sword that could cut their hearts at any time.
However, when they saw that the people around them were suppressing this uncomfortable feeling and trying to keep calm, the other warriors also suppressed their discomfort. As warriors, they were not willing to fall behind others. If others could bear it, they could also bear it. Otherwise, they would lose face.
However, at this moment, Ai Anxin smiled and turned to Luo Zheng, asking, "How do you feel?"
Luo Zheng looked at the big words and said indifferently, "A little uncomfortable."
Ai Anxin curled her lips, "You are honest. Look at those guys in front of us. They are all uncomfortable, but they have to pretend as if nothing happened. They are willing to suffer because of their pride!"
Her voice was not soft, and many warriors in front of her heard it. Naturally, they were enraged. Ai Anxin had hit the nail on the head. The brush strokes on the boulder were indeed a form of torture for many warriors.
"This poem was carved by a Celestial Master with a sword. The Sword Essence contained in it can point directly to one's heart. His Sword Essence can be said to be perfect, without any flaws!" Ai Anxin felt the anger of the warriors in front of her, but did not care at all. She continued to explain to Luo Zheng as if nothing had happened.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1045
Upon hearing Ai Anxin's words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. "No more flaws?"
Although Luo Zheng had comprehended the Perfect Sword Intent and even created his own Formless Sword Intent, he couldn't guarantee that his Sword Intent was flawless.
However, Formless Sword Intent didn't have any fixed moves. It was very difficult to break through the moves.
Although it was difficult, it didn't mean that others couldn't break it. However, Luo Zheng hadn't met anyone who could break his Formless Sword Intent!
"Of course. How could the Sword Intent of the owner of the Massacre Sword Mountain have any flaws?" Ai Anxin said with a matter-of-fact expression.
"The owner of the Massacre Sword Mountain?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a sharp light.
Ai Anxin curled her lips. This Luo Zheng really came from the Lower Realm and didn't seem to know anything …
At this time, a cold voice floated into Luo Zheng's mind. It was Xun. "It's Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword. Among the many Heavenly Venerables in the universe, Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword is an extremely powerful Heavenly Venerable. His sword is inserted at the top of the Massacre Sword Mountain. It's in the highest position! If you have any questions, ask me directly. Don't ask this woman! "
Xun didn't like this woman from the bottom of her heart …
However, Xun didn't know much about the affairs of the Human Race. For example, the broken Orange Sea Realm and the Torch Flame Inheritance. After all, these were internal affairs of the Alliance.
However, how could she not know about Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword and the Massacre Sword Mountain?
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, but his eyes were still fixed on the poem on the huge rock!
Every word on the poem was dozens of feet wide and tall. The closer one got to the words, the more one could feel the sharp Sword Intent in the words!
The warriors in front had already turned their heads away, unwilling to look directly at the words.
However, Luo Zheng's gaze became more and more serious!
Under Luo Zheng's gaze, the words seemed to come alive, and a Sword Will shot toward Luo Zheng!
The shadow of the Sword Intent was very faint. Luo Zheng stared at it for a long time, but he could only see a shadow.
Luo Zheng didn't try to defend himself. Instead, he allowed the sword intent to shoot towards him. The moment the sword intent shot into his body, he felt very uncomfortable. It was as if someone had really stabbed him with a sword. It was a little uncomfortable.
So that was the reason why everyone felt uncomfortable when they saw these words. It was because of these invisible sword intents.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed with a bright light!
Could this formless sword intent be used to refine his sword intent?
Although he had this conjecture, Luo Zheng was still uncertain … Even if he was uncertain, Luo Zheng would still test it!
Yun Luo, who was in front of them, was about to lead them around the boulder. At the same time, she turned her head and told everyone, "Our destination is behind this boulder." After saying that, she saw Luo Zheng stop.
Ai Anxin also looked at Luo Zheng curiously and asked, "Hey, what are you doing? Are you stupid? "
Yun Luo also circled over. Although she did not care much about Luo Zheng, the inheritance of the Torch Flame was very important. She did not know what Luo Zheng was doing here.
"What's wrong? Luo Zheng? "Yun Luo followed Luo Zheng's gaze and asked," Is there a problem with these words? "
Luo Zheng said, "I want to try …"
"Try what?" Yun Luo asked curiously.
Ai Anxin was also curious and looked at Luo Zheng strangely.
At this time, Luo Zheng had already taken out the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword. Holding the long sword, he pointed at the words and said, "I see some faint Sword Intent. I want to try and see if I can use these Sword Intents to refine my Sword Intent."
After hearing Luo Zheng's words, Yun Luo and Ai Anxin's eyes suddenly widened.
"Impossible! Is the legend true? "Ai Anxin cried out softly.
Yun Luo also looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression. To be honest, when she brought Luo Zheng to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, Yun Luo once told herself that anything could happen to this kid and that there was no need to be too surprised! However, she did not expect that Luo Zheng had only been in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace for such a short time. She already found it hard to accept …
Because of their overreaction, Luo Zheng's face showed a strange expression. Because his mind was not focused enough, the faint Sword Intent shadows disappeared. It seemed that he could only see these Sword Intents if he concentrated very hard!
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Yun Luo did not answer Luo Zheng's question. Instead, she continued to ask, "Can you really see those Sword Intents? What do they look like? "
Ai Anxin also stared at Luo Zheng. She did not speak. Yun Luo had already asked the question she wanted to ask.
"They're pale white swords …" Luo Zheng described. "They're about two feet long. They're quite short."
The Thunder Wind Divine Sword in Luo Zheng's hand was five feet long. It was almost twice the length of those white sword shadows.
Yun Luo nodded and exhaled. "It is indeed the Bright Sword Heavenly Venerable's two-foot Bright God Sword. Bright God Sword … two and a half feet long, white. It is said to be the Sword Heavenly Venerable that can play chess with God …"
Ai Anxin curled her lips. She believed Luo Zheng's words more than Yun Luo. Yun Luo had just asked Luo Zheng because she was somewhat suspicious. However, Ai Anxin had just come here. She already knew that Luo Zheng knew nothing about the Orange Sea Realm. He did not even know who the Bright Sword Heavenly Venerable was, so it was even more impossible for him to know about that legend.
Naturally, it was impossible for him to make up a story to fool them …
Seeing Yun Luo's stunned expression, Luo Zheng asked, "What's wrong with you two?"
"Although this matter occurred within the Human Race, that legend is still very famous." At this time, Xun told Luo Zheng.
This Heavenly Venerable was named Bright Sword because his sword technique was said to be able to play chess with True Gods!
However, the Bright Sword Heavenly Venerable did not have a successor. His Bright Sword Technique also disappeared with him …
Legend had it that the Bright Sword Heavenly Venerable had carved his sword technique into a poem. This poem should be the words on the boulder in front of them.
If he could see his sword intent in this poem, then he would be able to comprehend his Graceful Sword Technique, which could be considered as inheriting his legacy …
This legend had once been very famous.
Countless swordsmen had spent all their savings, and some had even become poor and destitute, just to read this poem and comprehend the Bright Sword Heavenly Venerable's lifelong secret skill. That was a sword technique that could play chess with True Gods. It was the master of Slaughter Sword Mountain's lifetime secret skill. It was a sword technique that could play chess with True Gods!
It was enough to drive all swordsmen crazy.
Xun spoke the first half of the poem in Luo Zheng's mind. As for the rest, it was Yun Luo who spoke. Yun Luo pointed at the island below them.
Below this boulder was a desert. "Look at the numerous small pits in the desert."
Luo Zheng looked in the direction Yun Luo was pointing at. Only then did he notice that below them was a desert. But, this desert was covered with numerous small pits. Just looking at them would cause one's scalp to tingle. There were probably millions of these small pits …
"What is this?" Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
"Those are the swordsmen that sat in front of this boulder, meditating and comprehending …"
In this world, there was never a lack of martial artists with great perseverance. In order to comprehend an Empyrean's secret skill, what did it matter how hard they worked?
Some Life and Death realm and Divine Sea realm martial artists had spent their entire lives in front of this boulder. For thousands of years, tens of thousands of years, they would meditate, meditate, and comprehend, hoping that one day they would be able to see through the mysteries of this poem.
Thousands of years, tens of thousands of years of sitting here would cause the ground to sink, creating countless small pits.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng sucked in a breath of cold air. He asked with concern, "Did anyone … succeed?"
Yun Luo faintly smiled, shook her head, and replied, "No, not a single one."
"This … if they spent their entire life here and still couldn't succeed, wouldn't it be too much of a loss for them?" Luo Zheng sighed.
Yun Luo faintly smiled, and said, "They are like gamblers. The more they lose, the more persistent they become. In the end, they can't leave, so they spend their entire life on the gambling table. If they succeed, they will cultivate the sword art. If they fail, they will accept it. At least they have paid the price!"
"Worthy of admiration," Luo Zheng sighed.
Ai Anxin curled her lips, "Not everyone will accept it. There are still some who feel it is unfair. I heard that many people cursed Empyrean Empyrean after Empyrean Decay! It is said that he also carved some words, but I don't know where … "

Chapter 1046
Looking in the direction that Yun Luo was pointing, Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin saw some densely packed words.
These words were of varying sizes. The larger ones were as tall as a person while the smaller ones were only the size of a palm …
"I burned my lifespan and gave up my bright future. In the end, I gained nothing. I will tell the world that this is nothing but a scam set up by Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword!"
"Liar! Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword is a big liar. Unfortunately, he wasted ten thousand years of my lifespan and entered the Five Decay of Heaven! If I can reincarnate in the future and have the fortune to come here again, I will break this stone … "
"Hey, latecomers, don't dream. You should peacefully cultivate your own martial arts. Wanting to comprehend Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword's sword intent from these words is nothing more than the ravings of an idiot! Life is short, wasting time here is really sad and lamentable. "
These densely packed words were filled with pessimism and advice for latecomers to give up on their futile observation.
Seeing these words, Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. He could understand the feelings of these people. They had thrown away so much of their lifespan here only to gain nothing in the end. When their lifespan was nearing its end, it was inevitable that they would feel resentment. If it were Luo Zheng, he would also find it hard to accept.
"Why is there no one observing now?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
From the potholes on the ground, Luo Zheng could imagine that many martial artists had once come here to observe. But now, not a single one could be seen.
"Ever since this Orange Sea Realm became the Eternal Flame Inheritance of the Alliance, ordinary martial artists have been unable to enter. Moreover, for millions of years, no one has been able to comprehend the mysteries within. Many martial artists have begun to doubt the words on these boulders … but occasionally, there will still be martial artists who come here to comprehend them." As Yun Luo spoke, she looked around and shook her head, "I haven't seen any today."
Ai Anxin stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Luo Zheng, are you sure you saw that white sword shadow?"
Although Yun Luo and Ai An both believed that Luo Zheng was not lying, they still could not believe that Luo Zheng could see the white sword shadow …
After all, in the past several million years, it had attracted countless warriors to comprehend it, but no one had been able to comprehend it. Luo Zheng had just arrived here, and he saw the white sword shadow. It was really incredible!
The other vice palace masters of Misty Cloud Palace led the other geniuses to wait on the side of the giant rock, avoiding the sharp light of the words.
"What is the Palace Lord doing?"
"It seems like that brat said that he saw through the mystery of this huge rock!"
"This is simply impossible. Empyrean Bright Sword's poem should have already become a joke in the universe, a complete scam!"
"You seem very excited?"
"Nonsense! One of my uncles originally had a high chance of entering the Ultimate Divine Realm. Back then, he spent all his time here, watching him enter the Five Decays of Heaven and Man, and watch him die … "
When Vice Palace Master Xu Can saw this scene, his face was full of disdain as he smiled and said, "Hmph, this kid is most likely bragging that he has seen something. However, this kind of thing is too common on Changsu Island. He obviously didn't see anything, yet he still wants to deceive others. This kind of person is a scourge. Back then, many people swore that they had seen the Heaven and Earth Chessboard, the Snake Shadow, and the 10,000 Swords Formation!"
Xu Can's words weren't without reason. Trying to comprehend the mysteries of this giant rock had almost evolved into a real scam in the end. Some swindlers would fabricate strange and bizarre things and tell other martial artists that they could see them. In this way, they would be able to swindle a lot of martial artists out of their possessions …
In short, there were too many disputes around this huge rock, and it was difficult to describe all of them.
"Let's go! They can't wait any longer. There's no need to wait for the Palace Lord. Let's go first! " Xu Can said coldly. He had brought his people to participate in the Torch Inheritance, not to accompany Yun Luo to observe the huge rock.
The other warriors also left one after another …
In their opinion, Luo Zheng wanted to solve a puzzle that even tens of thousands of people could not solve. It was undoubtedly a fool's dream.
Ai Anxin asked Luo Zheng. After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng shook his head cautiously. "I'm not sure …" He did see the faint white sword shadows, and he also saw those sword shadows piercing his body, which produced a kind of "heart-piercing" feeling!
But Luo Zheng could not conclude that this was the mystery left behind by Heavenly Master Bright Sword. In addition, Yun Luo and Ai Anxin had exaggerated it. So many people had not been able to comprehend it, so how could Luo Zheng understand it? Even if Luo Zheng was confident in his own luck, he could not be sure.
Ai Anxin curled her lips, and a trace of disappointment appeared on her face. She had really believed Luo Zheng just now, so she looked at the team on the side of the huge rock and said, "Then let's not waste time here. Let's go first!"
Yun Luo stared at Luo Zheng suspiciously, not knowing what she was thinking. Then she nodded. "Let's go first. The other warriors of the Twelve Palaces should have already arrived."
"Can I stay here for a while?" Luo Zheng asked.
Yun Luo smiled. In fact, she had already guessed that Luo Zheng must have seen something, so she nodded. "After the Torch Inheritance begins, I will inform you." After saying this, she rushed to the back of the huge rock with Ai Anxin.
After they had all left, Luo Zheng's figure quietly descended.
The ground was a hard desert. Luo Zheng could not imagine the amount of time it would take to create so many small pits by meditating. In any case, it was not something that could be done in a few decades.
He casually found a place to sit down and looked at the big words on the huge rock again. Just now, when he was concentrating, he could see the white sword shadows. However, as long as he was distracted, the sword shadows would disappear from his sight.
"Can I see them just by staring?"
Luo Zheng's eyes were fixed on the big words, and his expression became more and more focused.
However, after staring for a long time, Luo Zheng did not see the white sword shadows again. However, he could feel the "piercing" feeling, which meant that the sword shadows were still there. It was just that he could not see them.
Was his mind not focused enough?
Perhaps because he heard Yun Luo and Ai Anxin exaggerate, Luo Zheng's state of mind was slightly impetuous.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng silently chanted the word "calm" in his heart. After dozens of times, his breathing gradually became even, and his whole person was immersed in a quiet state.
After dozens of breaths of time, a very faint thing seemed to come out from the big words!
This was indeed the shadow of a sword …
Although the shadow was faint and inaudible, Luo Zheng was very sure that he had seen the sword shadows again.
Just when Luo Zheng planned to concentrate even more, hoping to see more clearly, an ear-piercing laughter suddenly came from the side. "Haha, another friend has come! These days, young people who are interested in the mysteries of the Heavenly Sovereign Bright Sword are really rare! "
Along with the ear-piercing laughter, the white sword shadow that Luo Zheng had been struggling to see suddenly disappeared. He furrowed his brows.
He was so focused on comprehending that he hated being disturbed the most!
The most taboo thing between martial artists was to disturb others when they were cultivating. That was why there was the saying of closed-door cultivation.
After all, when one's cultivation reached a critical juncture, even a light cough could cause one's cultivation to go berserk, causing irreversible consequences …
Luo Zheng looked up with a gloomy face and saw a middle-aged man in ragged clothes. This middle-aged man was slightly fat and his whole body was pitch-black. It was unknown how many years he had not bathed. As for the beard on his face, it was also unknown how many years he had not taken care of it. It was scattered all over his face, making him look quite sloppy.
This middle-aged fat man was very excited when he saw Luo Zheng. He hurriedly called out, "Old Chen, Old Chen, another person has joined us! Come quickly! "
"Whoosh!"
On the other side, another person shot over. That person was thin and weak, but like this middle-aged fat man, his clothes were ragged and he looked like a beggar …
"These two people's cultivations are not low!" Luo Zheng frowned and sized them up. These two should be peak Divine Realm experts.
The martial artist called Old Chen flew over and sized up Luo Zheng with a smile. He then said, "Little brother, you also want to comprehend the mysteries of this huge rock?"
Luo Zheng nodded. He was indeed a little interested, so he temporarily stayed here. However, when Luo Zheng saw the appearance of these two people, he asked, "How long have you been comprehending here?"

Chapter 1047
Luo Zheng had heard from Yun Luo that many people had come here to observe and comprehend for tens of thousands of years.
But when he heard that Old Xu had actually observed for 20,000 years, he couldn't help but sigh.
The scenery on Changsu Island wasn't bad, but to stay in this environment for 20,000 years while facing this barren boulder, one could imagine how boring it was!
Luo Zheng turned to look at Old Chen and asked, "What about you?"
Old Chen chuckled and stretched out a finger. "60,000 years …"
60,000 years …
Luo Zheng took a deep breath.
Luo Zheng had only lived for about 20 years since he was born, but this person had stayed here for 60,000 years just to observe and comprehend the mysteries left behind by Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword?
Old Chen seemed to be used to other people's surprised expressions. He chuckled and said, "60,000 years is nothing. There was an old guy who lived for 130,000 years, but … he went back after entering the Five Decay of Heaven and Man. He should have died by now …"
These two people didn't have any bad intentions and were even very enthusiastic about Luo Zheng. After chatting for a while, they realized that Luo Zheng had come to participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance.
"Since you were able to participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance, you must be a martial artist who is highly valued by the Alliance. You still have a long way to go, so I suggest that you don't try to comprehend it. This stone has a kind of fascinating magic. Once you fall in, it's hard to extricate yourself. I'm afraid that once you're truly captivated, you'll waste your time like us." Old Chen actually warned Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng asked curiously, "Since you know you've wasted your time, why don't you leave?"
As soon as he asked this question, Old Xu revealed a bitter smile. "Hahaha, we've spent so many years here, and now you want us to leave? Wouldn't all our previous efforts be wasted? "
"Actually, we also understand that it's impossible for us to obtain anything in this lifetime, but so what if we leave? We can't take another step forward, so we might as well persevere. If we die, we die here. "Old Chen smiled helplessly.
Previously, Luo Zheng still couldn't figure it out. Since these guys couldn't comprehend it, they could just give up. But now, he somewhat understood the mentality of these martial artists …
On the path of martial arts, "perseverance" was the most important thing. Warriors who could persevere were all people with great perseverance. Their achievements would certainly not be low. For example, these two were Ultimate God Realm warriors. In fact, in any Holy Land in the Upper Realm, they would be like fish in water. In a small place, they would be powerful characters who could call the wind and summon the rain.
The willpower of these two people was definitely not weak.
However, this "perseverance" also required him to find the right direction. If he persevered in the wrong direction, he would be further and further away from his goal …
They already knew that they were wrong, but they had persisted for too long. They had walked too far down this wrong path, and there was no turning back.
Luo Zheng nodded with a smile. "I understand. I was just passing by and got curious, so I wanted to give it a try!"
"That's great!" Old Xu nodded. Then, with a flip of his body, he sat down in a large pit. The entire pit had sunk ten feet deep, and the walls of the pit had been rubbed until they were extremely smooth. It was like a mirror, perfectly fitting Old Xu's entire body into it!
This big pit was the result of Old Xu's 20,000 years of "meditation"!
Old Chen's figure flashed, and he sat down in another depression. He stared at the huge rock with his eyes wide open, as if he was fighting with someone else. In an instant, the two of them had entered a meditative state!
Luo Zheng smiled slightly, and raised his head to look at the huge rock again …
After dozens of breaths, in Luo Zheng's vision, faint white sword shadows appeared again!
As Luo Zheng became more and more focused, the shapes of these sword shadows became more and more real!
At first, they were just white sword shadows, and the swords were not very clear. But after the time it took to burn three sticks of incense, those sword shadows had become like real swords.
As these sword shadows became more and more real, when they pierced Luo Zheng's body, the feeling of "heart piercing" became more and more clear, and it made Luo Zheng's heart more and more painful!
This was just an illusion. His body was not injured! Luo Zheng warned himself.
But this warning seemed to be of no use. The pain seemed to be real. Later, every time Luo Zheng's body was pierced, his heart would twitch, as if a sword was really piercing him. It was just that he was not injured or bleeding.
"No …"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He could not let these white sword shadows bring him pain!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng turned over, and took out his Thunder Wind Dark Divine Sword. Facing the white sword shadows flying towards him, Luo Zheng suddenly slashed out!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's sword intent came from the Thunder Wind Dark Divine Sword!
At this moment, a strange scene appeared. The white sword shadow directly swallowed Luo Zheng's sword intent!
After swallowing Luo Zheng's sword intent, the white sword shadow continued to rush towards Luo Zheng. Unable to dodge, it entered Luo Zheng's body!
"Pu!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body emitted a muffled sound. Immediately after, Luo Zheng felt a sweet taste in his throat, and he spat out a mouthful of blood.
This time, Luo Zheng was really injured!
Although Old Chen and Old Xu had already entered a meditative state, when they saw Luo Zheng's actions, strange expressions flickered on their faces ….
In front of this huge rock, many things had happened that made people not know whether to laugh or cry.
Some people swore that they saw the soul of Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword, and even pretended to kneel down to acknowledge him as their master, and talked to him while facing the air. Some people said that they were surrounded by a sword array, and as long as they could break this sword array, they would be able to comprehend Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword's sword technique …
In any case, all sorts of melodramatic things had happened before.
It could be said that Luo Zheng's crazed actions were the most common, as he constantly brandished his sword towards the words on the huge rock!
Many people used their own sword techniques in front of this huge rock, as if they wanted to use their own sword techniques to break through Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword's mysteries. But in the end, this was nothing more than wishful thinking.
However, what Old Chen and Old Xu were curious about was that Luo Zheng had been injured? How had he been injured?
The reflection of the brush strokes on the poem on the boulder was indeed very uncomfortable, but it was only uncomfortable. Those who were not used to it would even feel uneasy when facing those words, but after getting used to it, like Old Xu and Old Chen, they did not feel anything at all.
How could it possibly injure someone?
But Luo Zheng vomiting blood was a matter that was set in stone …
"This boy, what kind of trick is he playing?"
"He might be deliberately injuring himself, but what's the point of playing this trick in front of us?"
The two of them quietly used true essence sound transmissions to converse. What had they not encountered in this place? They could be considered shrewd people amongst shrewd people. With a casual inference, they felt that Luo Zheng was putting on a show for them, and that the blood was deliberately shaken out by Luo Zheng himself.
However, the two of them couldn't figure it out. Luo Zheng had no reason to put on a show in front of them. The two of them had spent their entire lives here, and they had no value to speak of. Although Luo Zheng's cultivation wasn't high, he still had the qualifications to participate in the Eternal Flame Inheritance. He was a seed of the Thirteen Palaces of the Alliance, and his future prospects were limitless. There was no reason for him to deceive them.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know what the two of them were thinking, but his face revealed an unconvinced expression!
He had just released his sword intent, but it had been directly swallowed by the white sword shadow. Moreover, when it struck back, it had actually injured him. This was equal to Luo Zheng injuring himself with his own sword intent!
"Again!"
"Whoosh …"
This time, Luo Zheng didn't slash out with his sword intent. Instead, he wrapped his sword intent around the Thunder Wind Ghost Divine Sword. When the white sword shadow approached, he blocked it with his sword!
The two swords collided. The white sword shadow was an illusory object, and there was no sound from the collision. But this time, Luo Zheng's sword directly deflected the white sword shadow to one side. However, the white sword shadow circled around and shot into Luo Zheng's body again!
"I can't block it. It seems useless." Luo Zheng frowned and thought. It seemed that even if he saw the white sword shadow, he couldn't do anything about it!
But this time, Luo Zheng wasn't injured.
