Chapter 1048
So many people, so many years, yet none of them were able to comprehend the mysteries within.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't think that he would be able to comprehend anything in such a short period of time.
The reason he stayed here was purely because he was curious about those white sword shadows …
These sword shadows should have been left behind by Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword. When he carved this poem, he imbued his comprehension of the Dao of the Sword into these large characters!
Therefore, these large characters, from sunset to sunrise, ten thousand years, a hundred thousand years, a million years, had been constantly releasing those white sword shadows outside …
However, from the looks of it, while others were unable to see these sword shadows, Luo Zheng was able to see them when he concentrated.
Through his own sword intent, Luo Zheng was able to resist these white sword shadows. However, after Luo Zheng blocked the sword, the white sword shadow changed direction and continued to stab towards Luo Zheng!
Couldn't block it?
A trace of stubbornness appeared on Luo Zheng's face!
Again!
He still didn't release his sword intent. If he released his sword intent and it was swallowed by the white sword shadow, it would cause him to be injured!
Facing the white sword shadow flying towards him, Luo Zheng was especially serious this time!
His five foot long sword floated over and lightly blocked the white sword shadow. In the instant it bounced off, the white sword shadow spun a few times in the air and once again shot towards Luo Zheng!
This time, Luo Zheng was already prepared. The Thundergale Dark Divine Sword gently twisted, and with a light push, it once again bounced off the white sword shadow …
Once, twice, thrice, thrice …
The Thundergale Dark Divine Sword in Luo Zheng's hand danced with a blue sword flower, and the white sword shadow was bounced off time and time again!
In a breath's time, Luo Zheng directly bounced off the sword flower seven times!
After bouncing off seven times in a row, the white sword shadow loudly shattered!
"Shattered?"
Luo Zheng's eyebrows slightly raised.
After the white sword shadow shattered, it turned into faint light spots and once again shot towards Luo Zheng's body!
"Is this a sword spirit?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Inside Luo Zheng's body, Xun asked, "What sword spirit? Why can't I see it? "
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I'm not sure either …"
Whether it was the white sword shadow or the white light spot, it seemed that only he could see them. Xun couldn't see them, and neither could the two Ultimate God Realm warriors behind him.
And this white dot of light didn't seem to be a sword spirit …
However, just as Luo Zheng was pondering, he suddenly felt that something was wrong.
The characters on the boulder had been releasing thousands of white sword shadows in all directions, and one of them was aimed at Luo Zheng.
However, when Luo Zheng shattered the first white sword shadow, all the other sword shadows disappeared...
Luo Zheng looked at the several dozen words left behind by Empyrean Bright Sword in a daze. He didn't know why, but he had an ominous feeling in his heart.
This feeling was also felt by Old Xu and Old Chen …
Of course, their feelings were different from Luo Zheng's. Because at this time, the two of them suddenly discovered that the "piercing heart" feeling that had always existed had actually disappeared.
Old Chen had meditated here for 60,000 years, and Old Xu had meditated here for 20,000 years!
In such a long period of time, the world of mortals had probably changed dynasties hundreds of times already …
However, this "piercing heart" feeling had never disappeared. It had lasted for a day, two hours, an incense stick's worth of time, and even a breath's worth of time! As long as one stood here, they could feel the fierceness of those large characters, and their hearts would constantly feel a stabbing pain!
Old Chen and Old Xu looked at each other in dismay.
The two of them had already gotten used to this feeling and didn't feel any discomfort. However, after it suddenly disappeared, they actually felt unaccustomed.
More importantly, this feeling had suddenly disappeared. This was too strange. Could it be that the characters left behind by Empyrean Bright Sword had already lost their imposing manner?
"What's going on! That's impossible … "
"Could it be that brat?"
"Impossible, impossible …"
"But that feeling has really disappeared." The two of them revealed disbelief in their eyes.
This Luo Zheng was pretending to be mysterious and performing sword dances here. In the eyes of the two of them, it was just a little baffling. The two of them had the mentality of watching a play, wanting to see how long Luo Zheng would keep up this act.
However, this kind of situation just had to happen at this time. The two of them couldn't help but associate this situation with Luo Zheng.
And at this time, the two of them saw Luo Zheng suddenly turn around, and then madly run away at an extremely fast speed. The speed at which he flew away caused the two Divine Realm experts to raise their eyebrows …
"What's this guy doing?"
"I don't know …"
Luo Zheng had no choice but to run.
After the large characters on the huge rock stopped for a while, countless white sword shadows suddenly shot out, and all of the white sword shadows shot towards one direction — the direction where Luo Zheng was!
Did he provoke a hornet's nest? Didn't he just shatter one of your white sword shadows? Did he have to go so far?
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of helplessness. He also didn't know what was going on, but every time a white sword shadow pierced his body, he would feel a piercing pain in his heart. With so many white sword shadows shooting towards him at the same time, wouldn't he die of heartache?
Of course, he wouldn't stupidly stand there …
Seeing that the white sword shadows were like a swarm of hornets, Luo Zheng could only turn around and run.
"Swoosh …"
Luo Zheng's speed was extremely fast, but the white sword shadows were even faster.
He only ran a few hundred feet before his entire body was submerged by the white sword shadows …
Then, Luo Zheng felt a stabbing pain in his heart, and then he felt as if his heart was about to explode. He no longer had the strength to escape, and let the white sword shadows shoot into his body.
Luo Zheng's eyes rolled back, and he directly fainted, falling down from the sky.
But the moment he was about to hit the ground, Xun shot out from Luo Zheng's body, held Luo Zheng in her arms, and stood gracefully on the ground.
With the strength of Luo Zheng's body, he wouldn't be injured even if he fell from this height, but Xun still took action.
Xun then put Luo Zheng on the ground, stretched out her finger, looked at it, and murmured, "Just now, it seems that this Sword Spirit Body has become more powerful?"
This Sword Spirit belonged to Luo Zheng, and Xun's soul was just attached to it.
However, Luo Zheng gave full control to Xun, so this Sword Spirit Body could be regarded as Xun's temporary shell!
Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to his Sword Spirit, but Xun clearly felt that the Sword Spirit Body seemed to have strengthened a lot just now.
With the strength of Xun's soul now, she didn't need to attach herself to the Sword Spirit Body. But since her soul was released, it had been hiding there, so she was used to it.
She was also very curious. How did Luo Zheng strengthen this Sword Spirit? What happened to him when he was wielding his sword in the air just now?
Luo Zheng didn't faint for long. This kind of pain was actually a kind of illusory pain, and it didn't really exist, so it wouldn't bring any harm to Luo Zheng …
"Xun?" He slowly woke up, and saw Xun standing next to him.
Xun stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Just now, you seemed to have honed your Sword Spirit again?"
Luo Zheng touched his head, and said with a puzzled face, "I'm not sure." Thinking of the huge amount of white sword shadows, Luo Zheng still had a lingering fear!
"I've become more powerful!" Xun said to Luo Zheng with a smile.
In fact, the Sword Spirit Body had only strengthened a little, and it didn't match Xun's soul at all. Rather than saying that she had become more powerful, it should be Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit that had become more powerful.
"Ah …" Only then did Luo Zheng feel something strange.
He felt that the Sword Spirit's aura seemed to have become sharper. That aura didn't come from Xun's soul, but from the Sword Spirit itself.
"That's true." Luo Zheng was slightly happy. "It seems that I can use those white sword shadows to hone my Sword Spirit!"
Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit had indeed reached perfection, but perfection wasn't the limit. When Luo Zheng entered the Killing Sword Mountain, he just stuck his sword in the mountainside.
It was indeed a good thing to be able to hone his Sword Spirit again. But thinking of that swarm of white sword shadows like hornets, Luo Zheng's mouth was full of bitterness.

Chapter 1049
Luo Zheng didn't believe that he could unravel the mystery that Empyrean Bright Sword had left behind here.
But if he could use these white sword shadows to increase the strength of the Sword Spirit, that was enough.
Those white sword shadows truly caused Luo Zheng great pain. Every time a white sword shadow shuttled back and forth, he would feel as if his heart was being pierced.
And when thousands upon thousands of white sword shadows shuttled back and forth, he felt as if his heart was about to explode!
However, this sort of pain paled in comparison to the pain that Luo Zheng had experienced when he replaced the primal chaos energy … Luo Zheng had forcefully endured the pain of replacing the true essence in his dantian. Of course, he could also endure the pain in front of him!
But when Luo Zheng returned to the front of the boulder, the two Ultimate Divine realm martial artists glared at Luo Zheng!
These two martial artists were originally very kind, but now they revealed ferocious expressions, as if they were wild beasts that had gone berserk!
"You, you … what did you just do!?" Old Chen asked with a trembling voice.
"How did you do it? How did you make those words … that feeling disappear! "Old Xu also asked.
No matter how much they didn't believe it, this matter had happened right in front of their eyes …
They had meditated here for tens of thousands of years and had dreamed countless times that they could have some sort of inconceivable reaction with the words on the boulder. But in the end, the miracle didn't favor them.
This boulder and those words had experienced countless years of wind and rain, sun and dew, but they still persisted here, still emitting a sharp aura, without any change.
Not long after Luo Zheng had arrived here, a strange change had occurred. How could they remain calm?
In truth, because these two martial artists had been meditating for a long time, they had already lost the edge of a martial artist. Their strength was also inferior to an ordinary Ultimate Divine realm martial artist. Their moods were also relatively calm, but at this time, they couldn't control their emotions!
After all, Empyrean Bright Sword was an Empyrean that could play chess with a god! The sword skill he left behind was also a sword skill that could play chess with a god. As long as they could comprehend it, they would be able to earn back everything they had invested in it. Their life's work would be worth it. In this sort of situation, who could control their own emotions?
Seeing them so excited, Luo Zheng's face was filled with surprise. "I didn't do anything!"
"Impossible! You better tell us the truth! "
"Do you think we're three years old? Do you think you can fool me so easily? "
Previously, they didn't believe that Luo Zheng could comprehend anything, so they just watched indifferently from the side.
This time, their faces were filled with murderous intent …
In fact, Luo Zheng could understand their feelings. These people had spent their entire lives here. It was as if they were guarding a huge treasure trove, but they couldn't enter it. They had been guarding it for tens of thousands of years.
After coming here, he had indeed caused some sort of phenomenon. Although he might not have solved the mysteries left behind by Heavenly Exalt Bright Sword, he had still let them see a sliver of hope.
It would be strange if they didn't go crazy!
Luo Zheng was not worried. Yun Luo was not far from here. These two people were basically courting death if they attacked him. Moreover, although the two of them were ferocious, they could not control their emotions. Luo Zheng did not feel any killing intent from them.
At this moment, Luo Zheng faintly felt pity for them …
"I really didn't do anything. I just saw some faint sword shadows. After I shattered the sword shadows, it became like this." Luo Zheng shrugged. He had no need to hide it. Moreover, he had told Yun Luo and Ai Anxin before.
It was just that no one believed him.
"Sword shadows!"
"What did the sword shadows look like …"
Luo Zheng casually made a few gestures and explained. At this moment, he felt the "piercing" feeling again. He smiled faintly. "Those sword shadows appeared again."
If Luo Zheng could feel it, so could the two of them. It seemed that the words on the boulder had returned to normal.
"So that feeling all these years was caused by the sword shadows?"
"Why can't I see it? Why can't I see it …"
The two of them murmured with a dazed expression on their faces.
Luo Zheng curled his lips. He could not be bothered to pay attention to these two lunatics. Even if they saw the sword shadows, it was not a big deal. There were many ways to sharpen the sword intent. Why would these two be bothered by this?
As the two of them murmured, their eyes widened, as if they also wanted to see the sword shadows.
Luo Zheng raised the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword again. Seeing a white sword shadow flying towards him, his sword intent once again circled around the sword and he slashed seven times in a row!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
After seven slashes, the white sword shadow shattered without any suspense!
Therefore, all the white sword shadows once again rushed towards Luo Zheng!
This time, Luo Zheng did not choose to retreat. In any case, he could not outrun these white sword shadows. Since he wanted to sharpen himself, he would do it thoroughly!
He held the long sword straight in front of him and pressed two fingers on the sword with his other hand. His eyes were full of seriousness!
The white sword shadows were like a tide, rushing towards Luo Zheng.
At this moment, Luo Zheng once again attacked with his sword.
This was not a battle, but a tempering of the sword intent. Luo Zheng did not display the power of law within the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword. He just crazily urged the sword intent and waved the sword in his hand. His feet were firmly planted on the ground, not moving a single step.
If a mortal saw Luo Zheng, that mortal would not be able to see Luo Zheng's right hand or the sword in Luo Zheng's hand. They would only see the flickering sleeves and the blue sword flowers …
The speed at which he waved his sword had already exceeded the limits of the eye.
"Whoosh —"
As he brandished the sword at high speed, gusts of wind swept out from his sleeves, and the white sword shadows that were rushing towards Luo Zheng were continuously blocked by the Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword that was wrapped in sword intent. The white sword shadows that were blocked circled around before flying back towards Luo Zheng.
After repeating this seven times, they shattered with a loud bang …
Therefore, this time, the white sword shadows shattered one after another, and the white specks of light quickly rushed into Luo Zheng's body and fused with Luo Zheng's sword spirit.
There were too many white sword shadows, and the speed at which Luo Zheng waved his sword had already reached a limit.
At present, Luo Zheng was like a giant standing in a valley, resisting the torrent by himself. At first, he was able to block the torrent formed by the white sword shadows, but after a few breaths, he was unable to block the torrent. The torrent once again submerged Luo Zheng.
"My heart … is going to explode!"
Very helpless, Luo Zheng's eyes rolled back and he fainted again. This was what he thought before he fainted.
After Luo Zheng fainted, Xun once again left Luo Zheng's body and coldly stared at the two people in front of her. While Luo Zheng fainted, she silently protected Luo Zheng.
With Xun's current strength of the Silver Soul Stage, even if she was only left with her soul, she could still fight against these two warriors of the Divine Realm. However, if these two did not harm Luo Zheng, she would not take the initiative to attack.
Old Chen and Old Xu stared blankly at Luo Zheng, who had fainted.
No matter what, they were certain that Luo Zheng must have discovered some mysteries, but they could not see these mysteries.
Although these two were on the verge of losing their minds, they still knew the identity of the warriors of the Thirteen Palaces.
Ever since Changsu Island was designated as the territory of the Thirteen Palaces for the Torch Flame Inheritance, it was not easy to enter and observe the huge rock. Even the two of them had to spend a lot of effort to not be chased away after bribing everyone …
Now that the people of the Thirteen Palaces had come to Changsu Island, if they really dared to touch Luo Zheng, they would be courting death.
The two of them also had some other ideas. Perhaps, when Luo Zheng comprehended the mysteries of Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword, they could also get a share of the profits?
Therefore, although they were excited, they forcibly suppressed some of the impulses in their hearts, such as kidnapping Luo Zheng and forcing him to reveal the truth …
After a while, Luo Zheng woke up again.
At this time, he was becoming more and more courageous. At least, after resisting just now, the Sword Spirit had become stronger, and he found that it was not impossible for him to block these white sword shadows!
"Again!"
As Luo Zheng slashed out seven times in the air, he once again triggered the torrent of sword shadows and was drowned again …
This time, Luo Zheng lasted a little longer, at least six or seven breaths of time. Then, he once again experienced the feeling of his heart exploding before he fainted.
In this way, he was drowned by the hundred sword shadows again and again, and woke up again and again.
Xun's Sword Spirit Body began to become stronger at a visible speed. At the same time, Luo Zheng also felt that his Sword Essence was changing again.

Chapter 1050
Originally, Luo Zheng's Perfect Sword Essence was already close to perfection.
The cyclones of Sword Essence seemed to be extremely full. Luo Zheng had seen many Perfect Sword Essences during his travels. His Perfect Sword Essence was close to perfection, and there seemed to be no possibility for it to be improved.
But now, Luo Zheng found that his Perfect Sword Essence began to become extremely unstable!
Previously, Luo Zheng could easily release the Sword Essence with a single thought. However, the faint Sword Essence lingering on Luo Zheng's long sword was becoming fiercer and fiercer, but Luo Zheng found that it was very difficult for him to control it!
The cyclones formed by the Sword Essence were no longer full. Instead, they were like snakes, constantly jumping on Luo Zheng's long sword!
In other words, the Sword Essence didn't seem to be getting stronger. Instead, it became extreme!
"Why did the Sword Essence's characteristics become like this?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Using the white sword shadows to temper the Sword Essence did make the Sword Spirit stronger than ever, but it was difficult for Luo Zheng to control the Sword Essence.
For a moment, Luo Zheng hesitated whether he should continue or not …
Looking at the white sword shadows scattering around, Luo Zheng's face flashed with a touch of persistence again.
There were some things that one either didn't do, but once it was done, one must do one's best. Giving up halfway was not a habit for Luo Zheng. He wanted to see how far his Sword Essence would go if it continued to develop like this!
At the present stage, although Luo Zheng's Sword Essence was extremely unstable, he could still forcibly suppress it.
Moreover, as the Sword Essence became fiercer and fiercer, the speed of shattering the white sword shadows also increased a lot.
At the beginning, Luo Zheng needed seven strokes to shatter the white sword shadows. Now, Luo Zheng only needed four strokes.
Of course, when facing the torrent of white sword shadows, each stroke of his sword could sweep away hundreds of white sword shadows. Therefore, after four consecutive strokes, he was able to shatter hundreds of white sword shadows!
Unknowingly, he had already stayed in front of this huge rock for four hours.
Just as he was trying his best to kill the white sword shadows, Yun Luo's voice rang out.
"Luo Zheng, are you still trying to decipher the words on the boulder? The Torch Flame Inheritance is about to begin … "Yun Luo said faintly.
Luo Zheng thought for a while and said, "Can we wait?"
"I can, but after the Torch Flame inheritance begins, there will be a first round of assessment. You should hurry …"
The disciples of the Thirteen Palaces had to go through another assessment. If Luo Zheng was delayed, he might be ranked last. Moreover, after the assessment, the Origin Source Law stone inscriptions would be distributed. Luo Zheng being the last to pick would definitely have some influence.
Luo Zheng couldn't care less about that now!
The Lightning Wind Dark Divine Sword in his hand danced faster and faster, and the sword intent also became sharper and sharper.
"Sou!"
In a moment of carelessness, the sword intent lingering on the sword suddenly shot out. After shattering dozens of white sword shadows, it directly entered the desert nearby, leaving a small crack!
After losing the sword intent, Luo Zheng naturally couldn't resist the white sword shadows. Before he could condense a new sword intent, the white sword shadows once again drowned Luo Zheng …
After a short period of fainting, Luo Zheng turned over and stood up. This time, he became braver and braver as he continued …
After two hours, Luo Zheng only needed three strokes to shatter the white sword shadows!
After another two hours, Luo Zheng only needed two strokes to shatter the white sword shadows!
After six hours, Luo Zheng didn't faint again. No matter how many white sword shadows there were, they couldn't break through the sword flowers he formed. No matter how many came, Luo Zheng could shatter them all!
However, at this time, Luo Zheng was completely unable to control his sword intent. Although he tried his best to control the sword intent on the sword, every time he condensed a new sword intent, it wouldn't take more than three breaths of time for the sword intent to explode!
However, at this time, the white sword shadows were no longer able to devour his sword intent. If the white sword shadows were still able to devour his sword shadows, then Luo Zheng would have been seriously injured, if not dead.
However, he couldn't control his own sword intent. This was something that gave Luo Zheng a headache. In three breaths of time, he could slash out hundreds of times, but after three breaths of time, he had to quickly condense a new sword intent. Under this repeated process, the Primal Chaos Qi in his body was consumed at an extremely fast rate!
If his dantian was still filled with True Essence, then he might have already replenished it with over a thousand top-grade True Essence Stones. Fortunately, it was Primal Chaos Qi. He only needed to take out a little bit each time and it was just enough!
To the side, Old Chen and Old Xu were already staring blankly at Luo Zheng's sword practice. They remained motionless for several hours.
Although they couldn't understand what was happening in front of them, they could faintly feel that something was about to happen ….
No matter what it was, they didn't want to miss anything. If this boy were to reveal the secret of Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword, then they could act according to the circumstances!
About an hour after Luo Zheng continued to shatter the white sword shadows, Luo Zheng stood still. The sword intent in his hand suddenly shot out, but he didn't condense a new sword intent. This was because the large characters on the boulder no longer shot out sword shadows ….
"What's going on? Are there no more?" Luo Zheng curiously stared at the large characters. "Could it be that there aren't enough? I don't even know how many white sword shadows I've shattered! "
In these several hours, one sword shadow after another had been shattered in front of Luo Zheng. The number was naturally difficult to calculate.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that in these several hours, Luo Zheng had already shattered 9.96 million white sword shadows.
In the past, Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword had used a sword to carve these characters, leaving behind a sharp sword intent that could pierce the heart. And what pierced the heart of a martial artist were these white sword shadows!
Originally, there were nine million nine hundred and ninety thousand white sword phantoms, but in the past several million years, countless cultivators had come here to meditate. Although they weren't able to see the white sword phantoms like Luo Zheng, there were still some people who had accidentally broken them!
Thus, there were some people who had inadvertently shattered a white sword shadow, causing the other white sword shadows to attack them, causing them to faint! It was just that there were too few of them. Even if there were people who noticed, they would only think that they couldn't withstand the 'piercing' pain. Instead, they would be ridiculed by others …
Now, Luo Zheng had shattered all of the white sword shadows, and Luo Zheng's sword intent had already reached the pinnacle! This was a sword intent that he couldn't control, but the power contained within this sword intent was also extremely sharp!
"It seems that Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword made such an arrangement in order to temper his sword intent. The question is, what is the purpose of tempering such a strange sword intent?" Luo Zheng thought to himself.
And at this time, Xun also left Luo Zheng's body and stood beside him.
Xun was also sizing up her own body …
Besides her sword intent becoming so sharp that she couldn't control it, her sword spirit body had also undergone earth-shaking changes.
Originally, a sword spirit was an illusion. Although Xun looked exactly the same as an ordinary person, if one looked carefully, they would be able to see some differences. Like a reflection in water, it still had a somewhat fleeting feeling. If there was a strong light, it could even penetrate Xun's body and shine through.
But now, if Xun didn't tell the other party that she was a sword spirit body, one wouldn't be able to see the difference at all. The body that the sword spirit had transformed into was exactly the same as a real body!
Elegant black hair, snow-white skin, everything was vivid and lifelike. It was no different from an ordinary body, appearing incomparably solid.
Xun slightly raised her wrist, her slender fingers lightly brushing against her arm. She tilted her head and smiled faintly, "The feeling of this sword spirit body seems to have become real! When I control this Sword Spirit Body, my strength will also increase greatly. "
Before she found a new body, she still liked to rely on Luo Zheng's body. Or it could be said that her motivation to find a new body wasn't that strong!
In the depths of Xun's heart, perhaps she thought that accompanying Luo Zheng's side was also a kind of good home!
Luo Zheng indifferently sized up Xun. Under the contrast of her pitch-black hair and snow-white face, his heart palpitated. However, he once again turned his face to the huge rock.
It seemed that there was no way to solve the mystery left behind by the Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword.
After spending a few hours, being able to strengthen the sword spirit to this extent could be considered a good harvest. As for solving the mystery of the Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword, it was something that could only be encountered and not sought. He wouldn't be obsessed with it.
Since the Torch Flame Inheritance had already begun, Luo Zheng didn't delay any longer. He directly flew to the side of the huge rock.
Not long after Luo Zheng left, the two Divine Realm warriors, Old Chen and Old Xu, were still staring blankly at the big words on the rock. At this moment, a slight cracking sound suddenly came from one of the big words. A crack appeared on that big word!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1051
The two of them had mixed feelings about this.
They found that the sharp sword intent on the words had completely disappeared.
The root of the problem must be Luo Zheng …
However, they didn't dare to stop Luo Zheng and force him to tell the truth. Besides, Luo Zheng probably didn't know what the truth was.
Therefore, they could only watch as Luo Zheng left, while they continued to stare at the words on the boulder in a daze ….
After losing the sharp sword intent, although these words were exactly the same as before, they had already lost their charm. Even the strokes that were carved were no longer sharp.
If someone had said that this poem was carved by a Heavenly Venerate, everyone would have nodded! Only a Heavenly Venerate could carve such sharp words.
If others saw it now, they would probably think that it was carved by an ordinary craftsman.
"That kid took away the spirituality of these words … What's going on?" Old Chen said with a stupefied face.
Old Xu shook his head, his face full of disinterest.
Under such circumstances, a crack suddenly appeared on one of the words. How could the two of them not be surprised?
This boulder had been here for countless years and had never been damaged by anyone. Now that a crack had appeared, wasn't this a huge anomaly?
The two of them looked at each other, their faces full of excitement …
Compared to the past million years, they were the last of the countless martial artists that had persisted until the end. Perhaps what was happening in front of them was a turning point in their destiny!
…

Yun Luo and dozens of others were waiting here.

Yun Luo said indifferently, "Wait a little longer."
After she finished speaking, a tall woman dressed in green sneered and said, "Hehe, Yun Luo is really influential. If it were any other palace master, I'm afraid the Clear Growth Pool would have been closed long ago!"
The Thirteen Palaces of the Alliance had rushed over from the Thirteen Great Worlds to participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance. The green-clothed woman was the Palace Master of the "Sorrow Heavenly Palace".

Normally, not to mention being late by so many hours, even if it was only 30 minutes, the old man in linen clothes would not show any mercy and would directly close the Clear Growth Pool. How could he give this latecomer a chance to participate in the Torch Flame inheritance?
However, Yun Luo was the daughter of a Celestial Venerable and had a high status, so she patiently left a back door for the late guy …
At this time, another cultivator also said to Yun Luo, "Actually, it doesn't matter if one person is missing. Why do you doubt this cultivator, Yun Luo? Could it be that he really has something special? "
In one Heavenly Palace, there were hundreds of people participating in the Torch Flame inheritance. When these Palace Masters saw that Yun Luo insisted on waiting for Luo Zheng, they were very interested in Luo Zheng's identity. They wanted to see what this little guy who could make so many half-step Realm Lords wait for him looked like!
Yun Luo curled her lips. From the bottom of her heart, she had high hopes for Luo Zheng. She just did not know if he had any results from comprehending the words of Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword.

According to the rules, everyone who entered the Torch Flame inheritance land would be sealed off. Now that Luo Zheng had not come, it could not be sealed off. How could the Palace Masters of Tang Tang Heavenly Palace not be annoyed when they were delayed by a little guy?
"Hehe, that little guy should be comprehending the poem of Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword! He might even need to comprehend it for a few thousand years! "said Xu Can, the Vice Palace Master of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.

"Haha, I didn't expect Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace to have such a stupid little guy. He actually believed in Heavenly Venerable Bright Sword's mystery? "laughed a Palace Master.

His tone was very unfriendly. However, Luo Zheng was indeed late, so he didn't say anything. Speaking of which, the old man's words weren't wrong. He was just a little guy in Life and Death realm, so he wasn't qualified to make these big shots wait for so long.

In fact, what Luo Zheng didn't know was that the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces had a round of competition after passing through the Clear Growth Pool!
The buoyancy of the Clear Growth Pool was great. The Clear Growth Pool in front of him was only twenty feet wide and twenty feet deep. If the Clear Growth Pool was expanded countless times, even a mountain range wouldn't be able to sink!
Many martial artists of the Heavenly Palaces took pride in sinking to the bottom in order to show their strength!
However, it was not easy to sink to the bottom of the Clear Growth Pool. Most of the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces couldn't sink to the bottom, and most of them only stopped in the middle …
Of course, the Thirteen Palaces never lacked powerful martial artists. For example, Ai Anxin, Xue Muyang, and other martial artists naturally sank to the bottom. In addition, more than ten martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces also sank to the bottom.
In fact, it didn't matter whether they sank to the bottom or not. This was just a way for the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces to compete with each other.
"Little guy, you can try and see how deep you can sink," a Palace Master said to Luo Zheng with a smile.
Luo Zheng glanced at Yun Luo. Was it important to know how deep he could sink?
Seeing that Yun Luo didn't speak, Luo Zheng didn't bother to pay attention to her. He just turned his body and dived into the Clear Growth Pool. However, he just soaked himself in it and didn't dive to the bottom of the pool.

Chapter 1052
The Clear Sage Pool was a small measure to measure the strength of the Thirteen Palaces.
In the past few hours, thousands of cultivators had jumped into the Clear Sage Pool one after another, each displaying their Divine Abilities.

Chapter 1053
Ai Anxin's words finally aroused Luo Zheng's curiosity. Instead, a triumphant smile appeared on her face.

The One Thought Barrier Tower was an enchantment left behind by an Empyrean. It looked like a tower, but in fact, it was an enchantment laid down by an Empyrean.
In the past, several Empyreans took action and caused the entire Orange Sea Realm to fall apart. Because the turbulent flow of Laws couldn't be restrained, the Orange Sea Realm was still a dangerous place. If one wasn't careful, they would be sucked into the turbulent flow of Laws and die without a burial site.
It wasn't until a human Empyrean took action and gathered all the turbulent flow of Laws together and created the One Thought Barrier Tower that the shattered Orange Sea Realm gradually calmed down.
The turbulent flow of Laws slowly evolved in the One Thought Barrier Tower. As it continued to weaken, it formed a storm. The storm's power had weakened by a great deal. In the past several hundred years, it had evolved into a method used to assess the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces.
Hearing Ai Anxin's explanation, Luo Zheng finally understood. No wonder the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces were asked to comprehend these stone inscriptions. There were six Laws on the six sides of these stone inscriptions, and the storm of Laws was originally a composite of many Source Laws. If one wanted to resist the storm of Laws, they had to comprehend a sufficient number of different Laws.

She was indeed thinking about how Luo Zheng would react if she still left him behind despite his serious comprehension. What kind of expression would Luo Zheng have at that time?
Luo Zheng didn't know what Ai An was thinking. He stared at the stone inscription and fell into deep thought.
What would happen if the power of six Laws were completely fused together at the same time?

However …
It was very difficult to completely fuse Origin Source Laws with each other.
Some Origin Source Laws were existences that reinforced and restrained each other. For example, the Laws of Fire and the Laws of Water and the Laws of Five Elements were even more powerful …
However, was there really no one in this world who could fuse all the Laws together?
The six Laws on the stone inscription were only fused in pairs, and the fusion wasn't complete!
However, Luo Zheng didn't know how powerful the storm of Laws would be. He couldn't imagine the scene of multiple Laws fusing together, but he could obtain the method of fusing two Laws from the stone inscription.
Soon, Luo Zheng fell into a state of immersion.
Most of the thousands of warriors in the Thirteen Palaces were immersed in the world of Laws like Luo Zheng.
Ai Anxin was bored to death. She even stretched her body. Looking at Luo Zheng's serious expression, she curled her lips and finally picked up the stone inscription in her hand.

When Luo Zheng crossed the minor Heavenly Tribulation for the first time, he was almost annihilated by the black light.
However, crossing the Heavenly Tribulation was also greatly beneficial to the warrior. The warriors of the Thirteen Palaces were all geniuses of tenth grade Holy Lands. Crossing the Heavenly Tribulation wasn't a bad thing, but a good thing. For example, Ai An, a ninth level Life and Death Realm warrior, was looking forward to the tenth minor Heavenly Tribulation!
If the Calamity Annihilation Pill wasn't a good thing for them, it would be a great loss instead.
Ai An smiled faintly and said, "Of course, the Calamity Annihilation Pill can't completely neutralize the Heavenly Tribulation! If so, I don't want it! This Calamity Annihilation Pill can only delay the arrival of the Heavenly Tribulation! In case a warrior is in danger or is seriously injured and can't cross the Heavenly Tribulation, the Calamity Annihilation Pill will naturally come in handy! "
Luo Zheng nodded. This was also true. Back in the Sea God Continent, the Twin Demon Holy Land launched a surprise attack while Liu Yu was out crossing the Heavenly Tribulation. Although Liu Yu succeeded in crossing the Heavenly Tribulation, it was still extremely dangerous.
This Calamity Annihilation Pill could indeed come in handy at a critical moment.
"One Calamity Annihilation Pill for a thousand miles …"
"The reward for this Torch Inheritance seems to be too stingy!"

Luo Zheng was somewhat interested in the Calamity Annihilation Pill, but Luo Zheng was somewhat uninterested in the Minor First Grade Divine Artifact …
As for the warriors of the Thirteen Palaces, they were somewhat dissatisfied.

"Indeed, the reward this time seems to have been downgraded."
The old man ignored everyone's dissatisfaction and continued, "As for the reward for crossing ten thousand miles, hehe, I'll announce it when someone can cross this boundary!"
Clearly, this guy was keeping them in suspense.
Naturally, everyone was dissatisfied …
The difficulty of advancing through the storm of laws was definitely not low. Not many people could cross a thousand miles!
As for the difficulty of crossing three thousand miles, perhaps only a few dozen people could complete it.
As for the difficulty of crossing ten thousand miles, it was even more unimaginable. The warriors of the Thirteen Palaces doubted whether anyone could complete it. There was no point in announcing the unfairness …
However, although everyone was dissatisfied, no one chose to give up. They knew that the benefits of the Torch Flame inheritance were not limited to this. If they could achieve a good result, they would be valued in the Heavenly Palace, and the resources they would receive would be several times more than now.

Chapter 1054
Under the linen-robed old man's arrangement, everyone moved forward and entered the One Thought Barrier Tower.
This year, although the Boundary Tower was called a tower, it was actually a huge space that firmly imprisoned the Law Storm within.
The Law Storm continued to wreak havoc, making it seem as if this space was boundless, but in fact, it had a boundary. However, in the deepest part of the One Thought Barrier Tower, there were remnants of the battle between several Heavenly Venerables. The only consequence of being sucked in was dying without a complete corpse.
Of course, that was the depth of a hundred thousand li. It was impossible for the younger generation of the thirteen palaces to reach such a depth.
Several thousand cultivators entered the One Thought Barrier Tower in a single file …
Just as they entered the One Thought Barrier Tower, everyone immediately felt an intense power of law rushing towards them!
"This power of law contains many different kinds of laws!"
Luo Zheng raised his head and gazed ahead. The Law Power before his eyes formed a violent storm that covered almost every corner of the One Thought Barrier Tower.
Even though they had just entered the tower, everyone could feel a powerful pressure. Many of them operated their Protective Zhen Yuan to protect themselves.
Only a few people didn't activate their Protective Zhen Yuan, such as Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace's Xue Muyang and Ai Anxin, as well as the other super geniuses of the twelve Heavenly Palaces.
As for Luo Zheng … he didn't activate his Protective Zhen Yuan either. It wasn't that he didn't want to, but that he didn't have Zhen Yuan. Although the Primal Chaos Qi had similar characteristics to Zhen Yuan and could be regarded as a strengthened version of Zhen Yuan to a certain extent, it wasn't evenly spread out like Zhen Yuan.
In other words, Luo Zheng hadn't grasped this method.
The martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces were recruited from many Great Worlds. They were core existences in any Rank Ten Holy Land. Naturally, they had the intention to compete with Ye Xiwen.
Seeing Xue Muyang, Ai Anxin, and the other cultivators easily adapt to the Law Storm without activating their Protective Zhen Yuan, everyone was naturally convinced.
However, seeing that Luo Zheng, a fifth level Life and Death expert, didn't activate his Protective Zhen Yuan, disdain appeared in everyone's eyes.
"Hold on for now. If you can't hold on for a hundred li, then you'll lose face!"
"Hehe, there's no shortage of people who puff themselves up at their own cost …"
This year, the Spirit Barrier Tower wasn't a competition of speed. After everyone entered the tower, they began to approach the depths of the tower.
In the beginning, the people were still very concentrated, but gradually, the thousands of people formed a long line, and the whole group became scattered.
The storm of laws continued to roar. Occasionally, a few strands of the Law of Fire would condense together and form a fist-sized flame, which would suddenly hit Luo Zheng's body and give him a burning sensation.
Or when several strands of the Laws of Wind collided with each other and formed a few tiny wind blades that swept over.
Luo Zheng's speed was not fast. His gaze was indifferent, but he was quietly sizing up this world filled with all kinds of laws.
The power of these Laws didn't fuse with each other. They only intertwined with each other after being shattered!
At this moment, Luo Zheng noticed from the side that several Time Laws had condensed together and turned into a small ball that flew towards him.
He stretched out his hand and gently poked the small ball. The moment his finger drilled into the small ball, its speed suddenly slowed down. However, after Luo Zheng silently operated the Law of Time, he offset the time limit of the Law of Time, and the small ball burst like a soap bubble.
"These Laws are intertwined. There's a very familiar feeling." After Luo Zheng entered the Spirit Barrier Tower, he was somewhat absent-minded. This feeling originated from this familiarity.
But after thinking for a long time, he still couldn't figure out where this sense of familiarity came from …
Ai Anxin had already rushed to the front of the group, and Luo Zheng was now at the tail of the group. There were still some cultivators behind him who sped up and overtook Luo Zheng.
When they passed by Luo Zheng, these cultivators' faces showed surprise.
"This guy didn't use True Essence to protect his body. It's very difficult for him to move forward in this storm of laws. What is he trying to do?" Many people thought so in their hearts.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't care about the gazes of others. He still maintained his speed and moved forward at a constant speed.
At this cultivation level, the flying speed of cultivators was already extremely fast. If there were no Law Storms, this distance of thousands of miles would probably only take them three hours.
But the further they went, the more terrifying the Law Storms were. Naturally, their speed would be greatly reduced.
Outside the One Thought Spirit Barrier Tower, on the rooftop of an ancient building, many Palace Masters and Vice Palace Masters of the Heavenly Palaces were sitting around a large round table, and the linen-clothed old man was sitting in the center of the round table. They were indifferently watching every movement in the tower.
"There are a few little guys who have begun to speed up, hehe." The linen-clothed old man smiled faintly.
A Palace Master smiled faintly and said, "The one rushing at the front seems to be Fang Xiaobing of our Cambrian Heavenly Palace! Hehe, it seems that Fang Xiaobing will be the first in the competition of a thousand miles! "
"What first? It's not a competition of speed. The one who can last the longest will have the last laugh!" Another Palace Master retorted.
The linen-clothed old man smiled faintly and said, "In this time's Torch Flame Inheritance, I think the most highly of that girl!"
After saying that, he pointed into the distance, and the gazes of the Palace Masters fell on Ai Anxin …
"Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace?"
"This girl seems to be called Ai Anxin, right?"
"She does have potential, but it's a little too early to draw this conclusion …"
Ai Anxin was indeed very outstanding, but there were also some super geniuses in the other twelve Heavenly Palaces. They wouldn't be defeated so easily.
The linen-clothed old man shook his head firmly. "You all know that this old man has always been very good at judging people! This Ai Anxin is too outstanding. The degree of her excellence far exceeds that of a genius in the ordinary sense! "
Hearing this, the faces of the other Palace Masters turned a little ugly. After all, the thirteen Heavenly Palaces had always been on par with each other. In each competition of the Torch Flame Inheritance, there might be winners and losers, but the gap between them was not that big.
"This Ai Anxin is Xiao Dao's disciple, and she has the fate of a Battle of the Worlds. It's not surprising that she's so outstanding. In fact, strictly speaking, she's not a cultivator of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace! It must be Xiao Dao's idea to let her participate in the Torch Flame Inheritance! "A Palace Master said faintly.
"I see …"
"The fate of a Battle of the Worlds is monstrous. It's indeed difficult for ordinary geniuses to compete …"
When the other Palace Masters heard this, they heaved a sigh of relief in their hearts. After hearing this, they felt a little better in their hearts.
Yun Luo also nodded faintly. "Yes, Ai Anxin is not a cultivator of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace."
She didn't pin her hopes on Ai Anxin, so her answer was very calm.
At this moment, the linen-clothed old man pointed again and said with a smile, "This kid is actually the last one again!"
Everyone's eyes swept over and found Luo Zheng at the tail.
At present, thousands of cultivators formed a long line, advancing in the storm of Laws. Only Luo Zheng was at the back, and he was the last one to finish. Naturally, he attracted attention.
Several Palace Masters also smiled. This kid was the last cultivator to arrive late, and now he was also the last person in the One Thought Seal Tower. It was indeed strange.
Some of the Palace Masters even glanced at Yun Luo with a hint of mockery in their eyes.
"This kid doesn't use True Essence to protect his body?"
"It's really interesting, but I don't know why he's advancing so slowly!"
Yun Luo's eyes were still indifferent. She believed in Luo Zheng's strength, but she was a little curious. What was Luo Zheng thinking about?
Luo Zheng was still orderly, maintaining his speed. These Law Fragments were extremely messy, and he couldn't comprehend them at all. Of course, he wouldn't comprehend the Origin Source Law here.
And at this moment …
The dozens of cultivators at the front had already advanced a distance of eight hundred miles, and they were beginning to sprint to a thousand miles!
The first to reach the top would not be rewarded, but the linen-clothed old man would announce the names of the top ten cultivators who had rushed over a thousand miles, which was also to win glory for his Heavenly Palace.
Ai Anxin naturally wouldn't fight for any glory for Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, but she was proud, so how could she fall behind others?

Chapter 1055
The long dragon formed by more than four thousand people could be divided into three echelons.
The first echelon only had more than twenty people. At this time, they had already entered the eight hundred kilometer area.
Although the Law Storm in this area was much stronger, it did not pose too much pressure to these super geniuses.
Therefore, they were now scrambling to compete in speed. It was also because of this that after these people entered the One Thought Sealed Tower, they rushed all the way to the one thousand kilometer area …
Right now, the one in first place was a cultivator surnamed Li from Twilight Heavenly Palace. This cultivator surnamed Li was surrounded by a dark Domain. Any Law Fragment that approached him was directly swallowed by the dark Domain. He used this method to rapidly advance. The Law Fragments in the Space Storm could not obstruct him at all. Instead, they became his driving force after being swallowed.
Xue Muyang was ranked second. He was indeed considered a peak existence among the ascenders. He didn't use any tricks to advance in the storm of laws. Instead, he relied on his own strength to advance while staring at the storm of laws. His speed was extremely fast.
"It's already nine hundred kilometers …" She muttered to herself. "One hundred more kilometers." At this point, a smile suddenly appeared on her face. Her figure, which had been rapidly advancing, suddenly stopped in place.
The twenty or so cultivators in the first echelon were not far apart from each other. There was only a one or two kilometer gap between them. Everyone was also pushing their speed to the limit!
Therefore, when Ai Anxin suddenly stopped, the cultivators in the first echelon overtook her in an instant!
In the blink of an eye, Ai Anxin fell from third place to the end of the first echelon.
The cultivators who overtook Ai Anxin also had strange expressions on their faces. They did not know why this woman suddenly stopped.
However, Ai Anxin only paused for a few dozen breaths of time. With a light wave of her wrist, rays of green light blossomed from her back. Under the constant refraction of these rays of light, they finally formed a pair of wings that emitted a green radiance!
Then, with a light flap of her wings, she shot forward agilely.
Sou …
At this speed, Ai Anxin's body and wings left a long trail behind her!
The cultivator surnamed Li's face was filled with excitement. He had already traveled 950 kilometers, and only 50 kilometers was left before he could break through the 1000 kilometer mark.
He did not care about the reward of the Thousand Mile Competition. It was just a Calamity Annihilation Pill. This reward could be obtained by anyone. However, the Dimmed Heavenly Palace that he belonged to had privately promised that if he could obtain the first place in the Thousand Mile Competition, he would be rewarded with an additional first-grade divine artifact!
He wanted to help Sorrow Heavenly Palace reap this glory!
And this glory was right in front of him. It seemed to have come a little too easily …
However, just as he was rushing forward, he suddenly heard a whooshing sound beside his ear. A shadow flashed past his eyes, and his eyes were suddenly dazzled.
"Eh? What was that just now … "
This martial artist surnamed Li hadn't been able to clearly see Ai Anxin's figure. After all, under the devastation of this storm of Laws, everyone's field of vision had been compressed to an extremely small range. He thought that the movement just now had been caused by a storm.
After a few breaths, the crowd heard the old man's voice.
"The first cultivator to break through the 1000 kilometer mark is Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace's Ai Anxin!"
Hearing this, the cultivator surnamed Li froze, his face filled with disbelief. "This, this … what's going on?"
Outside the Sealed Tower, the old man and the many Palace Masters stared at the wings on Ai Anxin's back. Their eyes were also filled with shock.
"That's the Nimble Azure Wind Wings!"
"This girl can control this pair of wings in the Life and Death realm …"
"Nimble Azure Wind Wings … Doesn't she need to cultivate the Azure Wind Spirit's Dark Embryo in her Dantian? She's only in the Life and Death realm and hasn't opened her Divine Sea. How can she cultivate it in her body? "
The Nimble Azure Wind Wings were not something that humans could possess. It was the ability of a special creature in the storm, the Azure Wind Spirit!
Those who possessed these wings could Ao Xiang a thousand kilometers in the blink of an eye …
However, the Azure Wind Spirit was not an ordinary creature. Cultivators could not inherit or obtain their bloodline!
However, in order to become stronger and faster, every cultivator would rack their brains. In the end, someone came up with a method, which was to capture the Azure Wind Spirit's Dark Embryo and find a way to place it in her Dantian. Through Zhen Yuan cultivation, hatching, and gradually controlling it …
This method was extremely dangerous, and the cost was also extremely high. Moreover, if there was even the slightest mistake, the Dantian would be implicated by the Dark Embryo, turning the cultivator into a cripple!
In the universe, there were very few cultivators who could not capture the Azure Wind Spirit's Dark Embryo, and those who could successfully cultivate it were even fewer. Even if they succeeded, they would at least be Extreme God experts, or even higher.
They just could not figure out how Ai Anxin could obtain the Nimble Azure Wind Wings at the ninth level of Life and Death realm.
After Ai Anxin rushed over a thousand miles, she stopped and turned around to look. She had already left everyone far behind, and her face revealed a faint look of indifference.
"There's no challenge at all. I wonder what's Luo Zheng's rank now? That guy definitely won't fight for first place … "Even if she obtained first place in the 1000 kilometer mark, Ai Anxin did not let down her guard. Her premonition was always easy to verify. The competition between her and Luo Zheng was still to come!
Four hundred kilometers away, Luo Zheng was still flying forward at a neither fast nor slow pace. When he heard the old man's voice, a smile appeared on his face.
The first echelon had already crossed the 1000 kilometer mark. The second echelon's cultivators were concentrated between seven and eight hundred kilometers, while the third echelon's cultivators were between five hundred and six hundred kilometers.
Luo Zheng could not even catch up with the third echelon's tail.
"If I can't figure it out, then I won't think about it …" Luo Zheng suddenly shook his head, emptying his mind of thoughts, and increased his speed.
The intensity of the storm of laws in the first 1000 kilometer mark did not fluctuate too much.
After the time it took for two incense sticks to burn, Luo Zheng finally crossed the 1000 kilometer mark.
At this time, all the cultivators in the third echelon had also crossed this mark. He was still the last one.
When Luo Zheng broke through the 1000 kilometer mark, he felt that the storm of laws seemed to have become stronger …
"This storm of laws should also be circular, and now we should be approaching the center." This reminded Luo Zheng of the storm in the Chaotic Astral Sea.
However, the storm in the Chaotic Astral Sea was purely a storm formed by the law of wind, not the chaotic power of laws in front of him. However, although this storm of laws was a little stronger, it still did not put too much pressure on Luo Zheng!
Outside the Sealed Tower, the plain-clothed old man said indifferently, "Three thousand miles is a threshold. At this distance, many little fellows should be eliminated!"
A Palace Master next to him nodded. "After crossing the 1000 kilometer mark, some of the power of laws will condense. The strength of the Law Spirit formed will not be weak. Let's see how these little guys deal with it."
"What about the last little guy? Is he still the last one? "A Palace Master laughed.
"Yes, this little guy is really a little strange. He's already crossed the 1000 kilometer mark but he still hasn't used his true essence to protect his body. Does he plan on not using his true essence to protect his body?" Another Palace Master smiled.
In fact, these Palace Masters also discovered that Luo Zheng was a little special.
Of course, this wasn't a compliment. Rather, they felt that Luo Zheng was a little strange …
Their thoughts were still focused on the martial artists in their own palaces. The first place in the 1000 kilometer mark had already been taken away by Ai Anxin. Now, it was time to compete in endurance. Who would be able to travel the furthest in this storm of laws?
In the vast and boundless storm of laws, many genius martial artists were struggling to move forward. Besides the martial artists in the first echelon, many of the martial artists in the second echelon had slowed down by a great deal.
From time to time, an extremely powerful Fire Spirit would drill out from the storm and try to swallow a martial artist.
It might also shoot out a black wind blade …

Chapter 1056
Everyone's speed slowed down, but Luo Zheng maintained a steady speed. He slowly caught up with the main group.
Not long after, he finally overtook a martial artist, followed by a second, a third, and a fourth …
The martial artists at the back were probably not strong enough, so their speed could not keep up. Some of them even had the intention of giving up when faced with the fierce storm of laws.
After all, everyone had different goals. Some martial artists knew that their strength was limited, so they were satisfied with just one Calamity Annihilation Pill.
However, there were also some martial artists who gritted their teeth and persevered even though they were not strong enough. They knew that they could not surpass the threshold of 3,000 li, but they wanted to challenge themselves and not others! The mentality of these martial artists was rare and commendable!
Amidst the storm of Laws, a strange space suddenly formed. A cultivator inadvertently entered this space but was bound by this space. It was too late for that cultivator to escape. But at this time, a sharp black wind blade appeared in front of him and shot straight towards him, cutting off his arm from his shoulder …
A martial artist inadvertently triggered a fluctuation of laws and was surrounded by scarlet flames. At the same time, beams of fierce golden light shrouded him, and a few holes appeared on his body …
After crossing 1,000 li, this situation kept happening. Martial artists kept getting injured and could only give up midway.
The speed of the martial artists in the third and second echelon also dropped sharply. Every martial artist advanced cautiously.
However, the speed of the martial artists in the first echelon was still not to be underestimated!
The martial artists in the first echelon were the elites of the Thirteen Palaces. They were also the elites of the younger generation in the 3,000 Great Realms.
Moreover, they were basically martial artists who had unleashed the Nine-leaf Lotus Flower!
Whether it was their mentality, perseverance, talent, or luck, they were far from what ordinary martial artists could compare to!
Therefore, the distance between the martial artists in the first echelon and the second echelon was widening …
When the martial artists in the second echelon reached 1,700 li, Ai Anxin, who was the leader of the first echelon, had already broken through 2,000 li.

Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!
Spears of ice suddenly appeared in the storm of Laws, shrouding down on Luo Zheng not too far away!
And in front of Luo Zheng, there was a warrior, who happened to be in the middle. That warrior's face revealed a trace of fear. Just as he wanted to escape, he found that his speed had become extremely slow! He was shrouded in a cage formed by the Laws of Time! Although his speed was not slow, under the hundred times time law, his speed was reduced to 1% of his original speed. It was impossible for him to dodge the ice spears above his head!
If one was pierced by these ice spears, the result was obvious. Even if one didn't die on the spot, they would be seriously injured!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed, and he traversed a distance of several tens of meters and arrived by the side of the cultivator!
The laws of time …
With a thought, Luo Zheng operated the Law of Time.
The Laws of Time and Space were rather special. When comprehended together, they could cancel each other out.
After breaking the cage of the Laws of Time, Luo Zheng pulled that warrior away, and those sharp ice spears nailed to the ground …
That warrior was scared out of his wits by the scene just now, but after being rescued by Luo Zheng, his face naturally revealed a look of gratitude. "Thank you for saving me!"
Luo Zheng nodded faintly. Without stopping, he maintained his speed and continued to move forward.
The number of people eliminated by the storm of Laws continued to increase …
Especially the warriors in the third echelon. Just like that warrior, if it weren't for Luo Zheng, he would have been eliminated.
There were about three thousand warriors in the third echelon. After crossing a thousand miles, more than a thousand of them had been eliminated.
As for the second echelon, there were more than a thousand warriors, and more than three hundred of them had been eliminated!
As for the super geniuses in the first echelon, none of them had been eliminated!

Chapter 1057

The nine Heavenly Tribulations were also a standard that the super geniuses of the Upper Realm had to face!
If a genius entered the Divine Sea Realm at the eighth level of the Life and Death Realm, he wouldn't be qualified to be called a genius.
However, the Ten Direction Calamity Guide Pill was different. Although it was also a pill, the two couldn't be mentioned in the same breath …
The Calamity Avoid Pill was only a seventh grade pill, while the Ten Direction Calamity Guide Pill was a ninth grade pill!
Most importantly, this pill could help a martial artist trigger the tenth Heavenly Tribulation. The tenth Heavenly Tribulation was also known as the perfect Heavenly Tribulation, because it was said that after the tenth Heavenly Tribulation, a martial artist's foundation would be in a perfect state. The impact of such a foundation on a martial artist's future was almost immeasurable!
Ai Anxin faintly curled her lips and continued forward …

Chapter 1058
Not only was Xue Muyang injured, but all the cultivators in the first echelon, except for Ai Anxin, were injured to varying degrees.
Although all the martial artists who entered the One Thought Sealed Tower were equipped with many healing panaceas, the tower was filled with space storms everywhere. There was nowhere to hide, and there was no chance for one to stay in a certain place to heal their wounds.
Therefore, at this time, everyone was holding their breath. Now was the time to test their endurance.
After Xue Muyang rushed past ten thousand miles, the other cultivators in the first echelon also rushed past ten thousand miles one after another!
In the previous Torch Flame Inheritance, there were usually only two or three cultivators who could rush past ten thousand miles. Sometimes, there was even only one person. This time, the results were obviously better than in the past.
When Luo Zheng rushed past ten thousand miles, he was already in the thirteenth place.
Except for occasionally dodging some powerful power of law in the storm, he could still maintain a constant speed!
His speed was not considered fast. After all, some warriors had already begun to use special methods in order to strive for a good result. When they were at the last moment, they would make a dash for it.

And now, among the people in the One Thought Sealed Tower, only Ai Anxin and Luo Zheng were not injured at all!
"I found a problem! This little guy called Luo Zheng seems to have maintained his speed from the beginning to the end! "
"Yes, he is not fast nor slow. In this level of law storm, he is actually unscathed. It is really incredible!"
Hearing this, Yun Luo's eyes flashed faintly. These guys, only now did they realize …
The current Luo Zheng was already in the limelight.
Although he still did not rush into the top ten, his calm appearance made people feel that he was more and more unfathomable.
After Luo Zheng maintained the thirteenth place for half an hour, two super geniuses in front of him were injured and eliminated from the One Thought Sealed Tower.
With their elimination, Luo Zheng's ranking also rose by two places, reaching the eleventh place!
As for the cultivator in the tenth place, he was not far away from Luo Zheng. It seemed that he was also ravaged by the law storm. Now, it was difficult for him to move forward. Surpassing that person and entering the top ten was also right in front of Luo Zheng's eyes.
However, just as Luo Zheng approached the cultivator's back and wanted to surpass him, the cultivator turned his head and glared fiercely at Luo Zheng. After a cold snort, he bit the tip of his tongue and directly burned his blood essence. Then, a wild aura was released from the cultivator's body!
"Whoosh —"
The cultivator's speed suddenly increased by more than three times, leaving Luo Zheng far behind!
Obviously, he did not want Luo Zheng to surpass him!
Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's, Luo, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, Luo, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng.
This cultivator should possess the bloodline inheritance of some ancient species. His blood essence was also very precious. Burning his blood essence just to keep his position in the top ten was a bit wasteful …
What's more, at the stage of ten thousand miles, speed was no longer important. What was more important was the competition of endurance.
The cultivator quickly flew away in the vast law storm. His figure soon became blurry and gradually disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
Luo Zheng still maintained his speed, flying in the air unhurriedly!
After another incense stick's worth of time …
As Luo Zheng continued to move forward, he saw a huge black hole appear not far away. This black hole firmly sealed the cultivator in the tenth place.
The power of law at this stage was already very terrifying. Moreover, some very rare laws were born in the law storm. Although these laws were also derived from the Origin Laws, they had become another law system.
Just like this huge black hole, it was the legendary Law of Darkness. It was said that when the Law of Darkness was cultivated to the extreme, it could swallow everything, including the entire universe …
Although the black hole in front of him could not achieve this effect, it would not be easy to get out after swallowing this cultivator. At the very least, Luo Zheng himself would not be able to escape!
When he passed by the black hole, Luo Zheng also felt the powerful suction force produced by the black hole. At the same time, he saw the cultivator in the black hole shouting at him.
Luo Zheng could not hear any sound. This black hole could suck everything in. Even the sound made by the black hole could not escape the suction force.
Relying on the shape of his lips, Luo Zheng was able to read his words. "Help him!"
Luo Zheng shook his head faintly.
It was not that Luo Zheng wanted to watch this cultivator be eliminated. Even if he released him, it would not be a big deal for Luo Zheng.
It was just that Luo Zheng himself was not familiar with the Law of Darkness. If he was sucked in, it would be bad luck. If he was trapped, he believed that the Palace Masters would get this guy out. However, at that time, this cultivator would face elimination. Or rather, he was already eliminated.
Replacing this martial artist's position, Luo Zheng was now finally ranked tenth.
However, just as Luo Zheng entered the tenth place, a figure in front of him rapidly flew back. In this vast law storm, Luo Zheng could not see very clearly. He only felt that the faint shadow seemed to be a big bird.

After Ai Anxin flew closer, Luo Zheng asked with a strange expression, "Why did you turn back?"
Ai Anxin glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and said, "I thought you were already eliminated!"
"How could it be that easy?" Luo Zheng shook his head.
"Too slow!" Ai Anxin said again.
"It's not a competition of speed." Luo Zheng would not increase his speed to catch up with someone, nor would he deliberately slow down for someone.
"But it's fine as long as you're still here. I'll wait for you in front!"
After speaking, Ai Anxin spread her wings and flew out directly. But at this time, a "blizzard" suddenly appeared in front of her. Giant fireballs condensed from the Law of Fire and ice spears dozens of feet long directly smashed down.
But Ai Anxin's flying speed was extremely fast. In a flash, she drilled through this "blizzard". Occasionally, there would be fireballs blocking her way, but she would wrap her body with her wings and directly rush through them.
"She turned back just to say a few words …"

At this moment, there were not many cultivators left in the Sealed Tower. Every cultivator was paid attention to, and Ai Anxin, who was previously ranked first, was naturally the last to receive attention.

Other cultivators were struggling, but this woman did not seem to know what difficulty was. In such a fierce storm of Law, she was still able to move around with ease. They could not help but have a whole new level of respect for her!
Luo Zheng's ranking was still rising in an orderly manner!
Ninth place, eighth place, seventh place …

At 13,000 miles, he was already in the top three.
Because among the top ten martial artists, seven of them had already been eliminated!

The first place was still Ai Anxin, the second was Xue Muyang, and the third was naturally Luo Zheng …
At this time, the Palace Masters and Vice Palace Masters of the other Twelve Palaces looked a little embarrassed.

Chapter 1059

In One Thought Barrier Tower …

When he achieved his goal and wanted to find Yun Luo, he realized that she was just a clone that was destined to return to the Upper Realm …

But now, the storm of laws was getting stronger and stronger, and the injuries on his body were getting worse and worse. His legs and arms were riddled with wounds that were so deep that his bones could be seen.
To be able to hold on until now, he had relied on his incomparably strong willpower and belief …
As for Luo Zheng, he was still ranked third. His speed still didn't increase or decrease!

Another example was a terraced pool with purple liquid flowing in it. But if one looked carefully, the liquid was actually a lightning pool formed by lightning.

Just as Luo Zheng continued to move forward, a violent roar suddenly came from the front!
"Ah ah ah …"

Xue Muyang?

Xue Muyang was an exception. He knew Yun Luo in the lower realm. Because of this, he always felt that he was a special existence in Yun Luo's eyes. Moreover, Yun Luo herself didn't care about this kind of address!

Luo Zheng was not an emotional expert. It was just that Xue Muyang was also an ascender, so he said a few words to persuade him.

Upon hearing Luo Zheng's words, Xue Muyang remained silent. On the other hand, Luo Zheng shook his head. He didn't wish to enlighten Xue Muyang. He only wanted to tell Xue Muyang his feelings. Chapter 1060

In the Upper Realm, there were all kinds of special physiques and special talents. For example, the Ancient Giant Demon's Indestructible Physique. After cultivating to a certain level, the body was almost indestructible. Even if it was reduced to powder, it could still be restored …
Another example was the Extreme Purple Yin Body, the bloodline of the True Phoenix and True Dragon, and the bloodline of various divine beasts. But the Jade Gate Immortal Battle was different.
Up until now, martial artists who possessed the Jade Gate Immortal Battle were extremely rare in the entire universe, and they were also extremely mysterious!
It was said that each Jade Door Celestial Combat Scroll had different abilities, but it definitely recorded a Karmic Law! What was even more frightening was that this Karma Law would grow along with the growth of the martial artist.
A weapon that possessed the Karma Law was a supreme divine artifact. But, supreme divine artifacts only numbered around 3000 in the entire universe. A normal martial artist would only see the names of these weapons on the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele; they would never see one in their entire life!
And once a supreme divine artifact was forged, the Karma Law within could not be changed, and it was even more impossible for it to grow …
Thus, it was understandable why the Palace Masters were so shocked when the cyan stone appeared on Ai Anxin's forehead.

Chapter 1061
Within the One Thought Sealed Tower, Ai Anxin wanted to observe where Luo Zheng was.

How could he have thought that there would be a fighter breaking through 20,000 miles?

He alone controlled the Thirteen Great Worlds of the Thirteen Heavenly Palaces. Together with a few other powerful Realm Lords, his status was second only to the Heavenly Venerables.
