The Sunset Realm Lord nodded and looked at Ai Anxin with a kind gaze, "Yes, little girl."
"I'm not a little girl. I'm Ai Anxin!" Ai Anxin corrected him.
"Good, good." The Sunset Realm Lord didn't know whether to laugh or cry. He then said, "I came in a hurry and didn't prepare any rewards. However, I have three treasures here. They're quite extraordinary. You can choose from them."
After speaking, the Sunset Realm Lord gently waved his hand and three rays of light flashed in front of him.
The three rays of light wrapped around three treasures …
Two of the treasures were First Grade Divine Artifacts. They were a snow-white sword and a pale yellow saber.
The last item was a feather robe. Although the feather robe was only a Secondary Grade One Sacred Artifact, defensive treasures were extremely rare. The value of this Secondary Grade One Sacred Artifact wasn't any lower than the value of the other two Secondary Grade One Sacred Artifacts.
"The first two Divine Artifacts are famous on the Universal Spirit Stele. Although they're ranked very low, they're still much stronger than ordinary First Grade Divine Artifacts. As for this Rainbow Feather Robe, I obtained it by accident. It's also a very special defensive treasure."
Ai Anxin didn't hesitate and chose the Rainbow Feather Robe.
"Are you sure you want to choose this Rainbow Feather Robe?" The Sunset Realm Lord asked.
"Of course!" Ai Anxin smiled and said, "This looks better!"
When these words came out, many of the Palace Lords revealed speechless expressions. Treasures that could be on the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele were indeed very precious existences, but this little girl only chose based on appearance. Wasn't she taking it too lightly?
The rainbow-colored feather robe was naturally very beautiful. Although the feather robe looked extremely thin, the rainbow-colored patterns on the surface of the feather robe gave off a dreamy feeling. However, when a cultivator chose a treasure, they would consider it from a practical point of view. How could they judge it based on its appearance?
The Sunset Realm Lord nodded. With a flick of her hand, the ray of light drifted towards Ai Anxin. Ai Anxin reached out and grabbed the rainbow-colored feather dress from the ball of light. With a flip of her hand, the feather dress draped over her body.
She even tiptoed and spun in a circle. A slightly proud expression appeared on her face. She never knew how to hide her pride.
The Sunset Realm Lord continued, "To be able to reach such a distance, it won't be long before Spring Fragrance Pavilion comes to ask for her. I wonder what Xiao Daolai's intentions are?"
Yun Luo looked at the lively little girl and said, "Since Xiao Daolai is willing to send her here to take the test of the Torch Flame Inheritance, he naturally has plans. I think he won't reject Spring Fragrance Pavilion."
"That's good." The Sunset Realm Master nodded. Her gaze turned towards the One Thought Boundary Tower, and a curious expression appeared on her face. "There seems to be another person in this One Thought Boundary Tower?"
Yun Luo nodded, "Yes, that's a cultivator from my Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace."
"Oh?" When the Sunset Realm Lord looked over, he was slightly surprised. "He's actually at 18,000 miles. This result is not bad."
18,000 miles. How could it be just not bad? This was already the same as Huan Hai Celestial's record. Luo Zheng's 18,000 miles was equivalent to surpassing Huan Hai Celestial's record.
If it was in the past, how could they use the word "not bad" to describe it?
Unfortunately, Ai Anxin had just broken the 28,000 miles record and was 10,000 miles ahead of Luo Zheng. Therefore, Luo Zheng's terrifying record could only be described as "not bad".
Yun Luo curled her lips and said, "Luo Zheng, he seems to be still in the process of comprehending. Maybe he will try to break through …"
Yun Luo was not confident now. Previously, she felt that Luo Zheng could break Huan Hai Celestial's record. This premonition was correct!
However, Yun Luo could not say how far Luo Zheng could go. She could only say that he would try again.
Ai Anxin also looked at the One Thought Seal Tower and said with a smile, "Yes! Luo Zheng's result is already very good! He should be able to break through 20,000 miles! "
Yun Luo glanced at Ai Anxin indifferently. She didn't like this woman being so proud. From her words, it was impossible for Luo Zheng to break her record?
She hoped that Luo Zheng could work harder. However, breaking Ai Anxin's record was still too difficult. For some reason, Yun Luo was slightly depressed.
The Sunset Realm Lord said, "Then let's see if this kid can work hard. If he can break through 20,000 miles, my reward will naturally be great. However, I don't know how long this kid will be comprehending? It's difficult to comprehend in the storm of laws. "
Everyone looked at the last person in the One Thought Seal Tower. From the beginning until now, Luo Zheng had not moved at all. Who knew how long he would be comprehending?

Chapter 1062
Actually, there wasn't much suspense about the results of the One Thought Sealed Pagoda this time.
If the 18,000-kilometer record left behind by Heavenly Venerate Yun Hai was an insurmountable mountain, then it was now an insurmountable mountain.
Now, Ai Anxin had successfully flipped over it and even stepped on it firmly.
The record that Ai Anxin had created was like a layer of heaven. A layer of heaven that couldn't be surpassed!
Unless you had the ability to overturn the heavens, it was impossible to overturn this layer of heaven …
Luo Zheng had indeed climbed over the mountain set by Heavenly Venerate Yun Hai, but no one believed that Luo Zheng could surpass Ai Anxin's terrifying record!
18,000-kilometer was already an insurmountable record, let alone 28,000-kilometer?
However, Luo Zheng was the last person in the One Thought Sealed Pagoda, so everyone's attention was naturally focused on him.
Time continued to pass …
Luo Zheng remained motionless in the One Thought Sealed Pagoda like an old monk meditating, as if he was comprehending something.
Ai Anxin pursed her lips as her eyes sparkled.
18,000-kilometer was also a threshold in the One Thought Sealed Pagoda. After 18,000-kilometer, the nomological laws would be compressed to the extreme!
All the nomological law fragments would be condensed together, forming a black nomological law storm.
Back then, Heavenly Venerate Yun Hai had stopped here probably because of this black nomological law storm …
However, Ai Anxin didn't stop here at all. After she opened the jade door, she didn't think much and just focused on charging forward.
Although the black nomological law storm was fierce, it couldn't do much to her.
However, she didn't know what Luo Zheng was trying to do by comprehending it like this.
Time passed by, one breath after another …
In the time it takes for an incense stick to burn …
Half an hour had passed …
Two hours of time …
Four hours of time ….
The Palace Masters and the Sunset Realmlord were still fine.
They were all martialists who had experienced countless years. This bit of time was really like a white steed flashing across a crevice. It was just an instant.
However, the martialists in the square seemed a little anxious.
It wasn't that they didn't have the patience.
To be able to cultivate to this realm, all of the martial artists had gone through numerous crises and boring cultivation.
Sometimes, a single seclusion could last for years. What did a few hours count for?
However, the Xin Huo inheritance didn't only contain the One Thought Sealed Tower. They were all looking forward to the next trial. Under such anticipation, time passed slowly.
"What is this Luo Zheng doing? Aren't you wasting everyone's time by dilly-dallying? "

With thousands of people complaining, it was a hubbub of voices …
Yun Luo's slightly white eyes were filled with curiosity.
What was Luo Zheng thinking?
"Huff huff huff …"
The violent storm of laws continued to sweep in front of Luo Zheng.
These Law Storms were divided into different levels.
At a thousand miles, the fragments of laws were more scattered and difficult to condense together. Even if they did condense, they would only form some small fragments of laws, such as short wind blades, small fireballs, and so on.
However, after three thousand miles, the fragments in the storm of laws condensed together on a large scale.
The further one went, the higher the degree of condensation. After ten thousand miles, the laws formed on a large scale and the attacks produced were even more terrifying and powerful.
From ten thousand miles to eighteen thousand miles, the power of the storm of laws only continued to increase, but there was no fundamental change.
However, the storm of laws in front of Luo Zheng had already condensed to the extreme. The colorful laws gathered together and looked like a gray fog.
At first glance, the speed of the storm of laws was much slower and didn't seem as fierce as before. However, due to the compression of the various laws, it was more difficult to advance in it.
However, Luo Zheng stopped here not because he couldn't pass through the storm of laws, but because he found that the highly condensed storm of laws was very similar to the chaotic aura in his body …
"When all the source Laws are condensed to the extreme, it seems to become the elementary form of chaotic aura. It's very similar, it's very similar …"
Staring at the space storm in front of him, Luo Zheng was lost in thought.
The source Laws of this space storm were the result of several Empyreans fighting. They had transformed heaven and earth origin energy into the power of Laws, intertwining and compressing it, ultimately turning it into this.
All things were originally evolved from chaos, and the source Laws were the foundation of all things in the universe …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Heaven and earth origin energy could be seized by martial artists who cultivated the true essence system …
According to this logic, the highly condensed power of Laws already had the nature of chaotic aura. And now, he was the first person to cultivate the Primal Chaos Secret Arts!
Could he control this storm of laws?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. He began to recite the formula of the Primal Chaos Secret Arts and tried to stir up the concentrated storm of Laws.
The chaotic aura within Luo Zheng's dantian was something that his master had planted and then slowly refined.
But now, Luo Zheng wasn't stimulating the chaotic aura within his dantian, but was using the Primal Chaos Secret Arts to operate in reverse. Like a true essence system martial artist, he was using the storm of Laws as heaven and earth origin energy to control it!
This situation was something that even Luo Zheng's master hadn't expected. After all, in the universe, there was almost no chaotic aura.
But Luo Zheng wasn't someone who stuck to formalities. One of the greatest pleasures of stepping onto the road of martial artists was exploration. Moreover, his master didn't cultivate the Primal Chaos Secret Arts. Since he had chosen this road, he could only comprehend it himself, ponder it himself, create it himself …
After a breath of time, an invisible fluctuation spread out from his body.
Although Luo Zheng closed his eyes, the corners of his mouth curled up slightly. The storm of laws in front of him seemed to be affected by an invisible force, rolling to both sides.
This gray storm of laws was originally rolling constantly, but its rolling speed was relatively slow. Although it was affected by Luo Zheng's Primal Chaos Secret Arts, the Sunset Realm Master, Yun Luo, and many other Palace Masters outside the One Thought Sealed Tower didn't notice anything wrong.
Soon, someone shouted, "This guy finally stood up!"
"Is he planning to leave the One Thought Sealed Tower? Or … continue to move forward? "
"He's starting to move forward …"
"He's actually walking with his legs …"
"He's finally flying …"
"His speed is still very slow …"
"He's speeding up, he's so fast!"
"This … he still hasn't used his protective true essence! He doesn't seem to be using any secret techniques, so why is he moving forward so fast? "
"Oh my god …"
The warriors on the side of the square, who had been complaining before, were now in an uproar again. Of course, this time, they weren't complaining about Luo Zheng's delay, but marveling at Luo Zheng's speed.
His current speed was even faster than Ai Anxin's!
The Palace Masters' expressions were also very interesting, especially the Sunset Realm Master, who looked at Luo Zheng with a thoughtful expression.
He could find a reasonable explanation for Ai Anxin's speed to reach 28,000 miles, and that was because of the Jade Gate Immortal Battle.
But now, Luo Zheng was forcefully charging forward. Even though the Sunset Realm Master was very knowledgeable, he couldn't guess the reason …
"Luo Zheng's momentum seems to be even fiercer than Ai Anxin's!" A Palace Master opened his mouth wide in astonishment.
"Fierce indeed, but he hasn't entered the 20,000 mile range, so he shouldn't be able to surpass Ai Anxin." Another Palace Master's expression was very calm.
As for Yun Luo, her expression was still indifferent, but a faint smile appeared at the corner of her mouth.
Ai Anxin, who was standing on the top of the building, had a look of surprise in her eyes, but after gently pursing her lips, she said indifferently in her heart, I know you won't stop at 18,000 miles, or you wouldn't be qualified to be my competitor. But it's impossible to surpass 28,000 miles!

Chapter 1063
Luo Zheng didn't really absorb these nomological storms into his body.

After all, under his control, these nomological storms couldn't pose any obstruction to Luo Zheng! It was as if he was flying in the outside world. It was extremely easy!

"There's no 'what if'. It's impossible," another Palace Lord said firmly.

On the side, Ai Anxin naturally heard their conversation. Although there was no expression on her face, she was very unwilling in her heart.
30,000 miles?

"How can this be …"

Ai Anxin's heart was very unbalanced.
She was originally a proud daughter of the heavens. Since she entered the Misty Cloud Palace, who had she ever thought highly of?
No one in the Thirteen Palaces could compare to her!
On the first day she entered the Misty Cloud Palace, she faintly felt that Luo Zheng was a threat to her. Although she tried her best to ignore this feeling, it grew stronger and stronger.
Now it seemed that this feeling was very accurate. As for the noise from the side of the square, she was already somewhat numb to it.

"This Luo Zheng will definitely surpass Ai Anxin!"

"27,000 …"

At this time, Ai Anxin's originally pink and tender face was now pale. Her neatly trimmed fingernails had sunk into her flesh.
200 miles …
With Luo Zheng's speed, it would only take a few breaths of time.
There was no doubt that he would surpass her …
If she had known, if she had known, she would have tried harder. Even if she had to suffer some injuries, she would have been able to extend the distance.
A trace of regret appeared in Ai Anxin's heart.
Because she was very confident that Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to catch up to this distance, she safely left the One Thought Sealed Tower. Now Ai Anxin realized that she was still too naive.
Just as she was regretting in her heart, Luo Zheng had already crossed 28,200 miles and broken her own record.
Originally, many martial artists believed that Ai Anxin's record would be an unprecedented record that would never be broken in this era. Her record might not be able to be broken in this era.
But the result made many people's jaws drop.
The record that Ai Anxin had created was so terrifying that it made people feel suffocated. It was like a heavenly record, but Luo Zheng had easily broken it.
This record only lasted for a few hours before it was broken …
"Will Luo Zheng continue to advance?"
"Will he be able to break through 29,000 miles?"

In their view, Luo Zheng was already the last person in the One Thought Sealed Tower. There was no one else who could compete with him. Since he had obtained first place, he could take the initiative to leave the One Thought Sealed Tower. There was not much meaning in continuing to advance.
However, they were wrong.
Because from the beginning, Luo Zheng had never thought of surpassing someone. He was only advancing at his own pace.
The one he wanted to challenge was not others, but himself!
So he still didn't stop and continued to advance …
"In the inner circle, we can no longer use our eyes to observe his movements." A Palace Master stared at the dark grey nomological storm.

Chapter 1064
After entering the inner circle of the nomological storm, no one could see Luo Zheng's situation. However, they could still sense Luo Zheng's location and estimate his distance.
These Palace Masters were also very familiar with the One Thought Sealed Tower.
When the Palace Master mentioned the Fire Pool, everyone understood what he meant.
This fire pool was located 29,800 miles away. It was the Inextinguishable Mystic Flame left behind by Heavenly Venerate Xuanhuo!
During the chaotic battle between several Heavenly Venerates, the nomological power used almost tore the entire Orange Ocean Realm apart. The remnant power reverberated throughout the entire realm, turning the Orange Ocean Realm into a dead realm.
In the end, the alliance took a fancy to the Orange Ocean Realm and used the remnant power to create a training ground. It was renamed the Torch Flame Inheritance and made the best use of it.
The remnant power slowly evolved and disintegrated into all sorts of nomological fragments. Finally, they intertwined together and formed a huge nomological storm!
However, not all the remnant power left behind by Heavenly Venerates was disintegrated.
Some Heavenly Venerates had methods that could transcend time and space.
For example, the Inextinguishable Mystic Flame …
Even after countless years and being swept by the storm of laws, the Undying Mysterious Flame still maintained its exuberant state and burned fiercely in the One Thought Seal Tower!
It wasn't that the Inextinguishable Mystic Flame didn't disintegrate. The Inextinguishable Mystic Flame also disintegrated a considerable amount of fire nomological fragments. These fire nomological fragments were also integrated into the nomological storm.
However, even after so many years, when some Palace Masters entered to investigate, they discovered that the fire pool still had a certain amount of remnant power.
The remnant power of the fire pool might not be worth mentioning to those Palace Masters, but it was a fire seed left behind by a Heavenly Venerate. For Life and Death Realm and Divine Sea Realm warriors, it was a hellish existence.
Those Palace Masters, Yun Luo, and the Sunset Realm Lord were all aware of this.
Therefore, some Palace Masters had resolutely told Yun Luo that there was no such thing as a 'what if' when Luo Zheng crossed 30,000 miles.
This was equivalent to asking a newborn child to walk in a circle in a blazing brazier. Yet, some people were saying that there was a 'what if' when the child could cross? This was a very laughable assumption!
Twenty-nine thousand and six hundred miles …
Twenty-nine thousand and seven hundred kilometers …
Twenty-nine thousand and eight hundred kilometers …
The crowd couldn't see Luo Zheng's figure, but they could feel that he had stopped at a distance of 29,800 li!
"Like I said, Luo Zheng can't get through. Even if this Immortal Flame is less than one-hundred-thousandth of its original power, Luo Zheng still can't get through!"
"I'm guessing that this brat is worrying beside the fire pool!"
"Actually, it's enough. 29,800 miles. I can guarantee that no one in the Xin Huo inheritance will be able to reach this height, not a single one!"
These Palace Masters were not targeting Luo Zheng. They just relied on their own common sense to make a correct estimation and evaluation of Luo Zheng.
Yun Luo did not speak. It was meaningless to argue. The key was that she was not confident and was even a little worried.
Yun Luo had been to the fire pool formed by the Immortal Flame before. Naturally, it was nothing to her. However, Luo Zheng's cultivation was still there. No matter how hard he tried, it would be difficult for him to cross.
She was afraid that this guy was too stubborn and insisted on entering the fire pool despite knowing that he was not strong enough. It would be bad if he was seriously injured or even died in the fire pool!
Luo Zheng was a fateless person, but it didn't mean that this person's fate was very powerful or that he would not die. It was just that no one could predict his fate …
As for the warriors on the side of the square, although they could not see Luo Zheng's movements, the discussion became even more intense now!
"Luo Zheng must break through 30,000!"
"He's already at 29,800 miles. If he can't break through, it'll be a pity!"
"What do you know? There's a fire pool here. Have you heard of it? Someone investigated the residual power left behind by Celestial Venerable Xuanhuo a few years ago. It's said that even if a Divine Realm warrior touched it, he would be burned to ashes. What do you think Luo Zheng was made of? Do you intend to let him swim through this fire pool? "
Their debate was so intense that it was as if the warrior in the One Thought Sealed Tower was not Luo Zheng, but themselves!
After all, at this stage, it was difficult for them to even look up to Luo Zheng. They would not compare themselves with Luo Zheng. Therefore, during the discussion, some points of view were more objective …
Ai Anxin sat in a corner and pouted like a little girl who had been wronged. No one knew what she was thinking.
At this time, Luo Zheng was indeed standing in front of a huge fire pool.
Although it was called a "pool," the fire pool was actually 200 miles in diameter. It was already comparable to some of the large lakes in the Vast Thousand Domains.
In this fire pool, purple flames soared into the sky, directly cutting through the surrounding nomological storms and entering the dome of the One Thought Sealed Tower, blocking Luo Zheng's path forward.
"I can't understand the nomological power in this flame at all. It's at least a fire nomological law of the fifth level or higher," Luo Zheng murmured.
Luo Zheng understood that this fire nomological law of the fifth level or higher. After all, these nomological storms were the residual power left behind by a Celestial Venerable. The level of nomological laws that a Celestial Venerable could comprehend was not something that Luo Zheng could imagine.
"Moreover, this flame contains the power of the Law of Endless Vitality, and it seems to have fused with the Law of Life …" The Law of Fire represented destruction, and it was contrary to the Law of Life. It was easy to imagine how difficult it was to fuse these two Laws together to create such a flame.
Luo Zheng could control the nomological storms that had been compressed to the extreme to a certain extent, but he could not control the flames in this fire pool!
However, he already had a plan in his heart. The warriors outside the One Thought Sealed Tower were right. Luo Zheng planned to swim over!
In the Vast Thousand Domains, after Luo Zheng's body had been forged into a sacred artifact body, he had not found a suitable flame to calcine it. For Luo Zheng, this fire pool in front of him was not a crisis. Instead, it was an opportunity!
Perhaps he could use this fire pool to improve his physical body again and achieve a divine artifact body!
If his physical body became stronger, the power of the dragon scales that he could bear would increase by another level. This would also be of obvious benefit to the growth of his strength.
However, as he stopped in front of this fire pool, Luo Zheng was a little hesitant.
After all, this fire pool was something left behind by a Celestial Venerable. Perhaps its residual power had weakened countless times over the years, but this purple flame still made Luo Zheng feel afraid.
Could his sacred artifact body withstand the calcination of this purple flame?
Previously, Luo Zheng also understood that his sacred artifact body did not seem to be afraid of any flames. However, this time, the flames he encountered were completely different.
If he did not succeed and was directly burned down, that would be a tragedy …
As he thought of this, Luo Zheng slowly walked to the edge of the fire pool. The closer he got, the more he could feel the blazing heat assaulting his face!
When he was about 30 meters away, Luo Zheng's clothes had already turned into flames. They turned into a pile of white ash and drifted away from his body.
"This flame is the most powerful flame I've ever come into contact with …"
In addition to vigilance, Luo Zheng's eyes also had a trace of expectation. He knew that he was taking a risk, but some dangers had to be taken.

Chapter 1065
Luo Zheng only stretched out one hand, just to be on the safe side.
If this flame could really burn his body to ashes, he could retreat in time. Losing an arm was better than having his entire body burned to ashes.
However, Luo Zheng's idea was somewhat naive.
The moment he touched the purple flame, the flame spread along Luo Zheng's arm to his entire body. The speed at which it spread was far beyond his imagination. There was simply no room for him to react.
The scorching aura of the flame poured directly into Luo Zheng's body, spreading to every part of Luo Zheng's bones and organs.
"Oh, no …" Luo Zheng's face darkened. He didn't expect the purple flame to be so strange.
At this time, Luo Zheng could only clench his teeth and bear the scorching heat of the purple flame. As for whether he could succeed or not, he could only resign himself to fate.
Compared to the previous time when he was burned by the flame, Luo Zheng's body's reaction this time was many times more intense, and the speed was also many times faster!
Under the scorching heat of the flame, Luo Zheng's body turned red again, like a sculpture that had just been poured out of molten steel!
At the same time, golden whirlpools began to rapidly appear on the surface of his body, and they began to rotate rapidly.
Only when those golden whirlpools began to absorb the purple flame did Luo Zheng's heart finally relax.
His previous worry wasn't because Luo Zheng wasn't confident. It was just that the flame left behind by this Heavenly Venerable was too powerful. Even if his body was as strong as a Sacred Tool, Luo Zheng was afraid that if he casually took out a Sacred Tool and threw it into this pool of flame, it would be instantly melted into molten iron …
Luo Zheng himself didn't expect that the flame would be so fierce, but the reaction speed of his body was also extremely fast!
Since he had already drawn the purple flame to his body, Luo Zheng didn't rush to cross the pool of flame. He wanted to wait for his body to absorb the purple flame first.
At this time, the densely packed golden vortexes were also madly absorbing the purple flames. But soon, Luo Zheng discovered a problem.
The speed at which the golden vortexes absorbed the flames was quite fast. It was not an exaggeration to describe it as' swallowing '. Every time the golden vortexes were activated, they would be able to completely absorb the flames within a short period of time. They were like ferocious beasts that had been starving for a thousand years, and were quite greedy for the flames.
But this time, his physical body had been absorbing for a long time, and not only did the purple flames on his body not disappear, they burned even more fiercely.
From a distance, Luo Zheng looked like a burning pillar of fire. The long tongue of fire rose from Luo Zheng's head and rushed to the top of the One Thought Sealed Tower!
"These purple flames seem to be endless. I can't even absorb them!"
Luo Zheng put his hands in front of his chest and looked at the purple flames that were constantly circling around him. His expression was very serious.
Unexpectedly, Xun's voice rang out faintly. "How could Celestial Venerate Xuanhuo's Indestructible Mysterious Flame be extinguished so easily?"
"Indestructible Mysterious Flame?" Luo Zheng asked in reply.
"Yes, this Indestructible Mysterious Flame is also an item of great origin. Moreover, it was not created by Celestial Venerate Xuanhuo himself. It was taken from the Divine Fire World!" Xun continued.
"Divine Fire World?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. He had never heard of this big world. However, he had not ascended for long, so he did not know much about it. It was normal that he did not know much about it.
The Divine Fire World was also an independent big world in the universe.
However, the special thing about this big world was that the whole big world was burning with raging flames. Moreover, there were all kinds of flames in this big world!
This kind of environment was not suitable for living creatures to live in. Only very few creatures that could withstand fire or creatures with special forms could survive here. It could also be said that flames were the original inhabitants of the Divine Fire World!
In fact, whether it was flames or thunderbolts, they would gradually condense into spirits and slowly give birth to intelligence.
Just like the golden wind blades in the storm that Luo Zheng encountered in the Chaotic Astral Ocean, they also had their own intelligence.
The Indestructible Mysterious Flame was very rare in the huge Divine Fire World.
In order to obtain this flame, Celestial Venerate Xuanhuo had personally entered the Divine Fire World and spent almost ten years to capture a wisp of this flame. After that, he had carefully cultivated it to its current scale!
The biggest characteristic of the Indestructible Mysterious Flame was that it could not be extinguished.
Of course, this was relatively speaking. For example, most of the remnant power left behind by the Celestial Venerates in the nomological storm gradually disintegrated into nomological fragments. However, the Indestructible Mysterious Flame was still burning fiercely. Although its power was many times weaker than before, it still could not be extinguished.
Hearing that the Mysterious Flame could not be extinguished, Luo Zheng immediately became anxious.
"This … the Indestructible Mysterious Flame cannot be extinguished. Doesn't that mean it will always burn on my body? How long can it burn for? "Luo Zheng asked.
"This Indestructible Mysterious Flame can burn on your body for at least tens of thousands of years. Hee hee, running around with this flame for tens of thousands of years is also very cool!" Xun laughed.
Looking at the current situation, Luo Zheng's Sacred Artifact body could withstand the burning of the Indestructible Mysterious Flame. After absorbing this Indestructible Mysterious Flame, Luo Zheng's physical body was very likely to take another step forward and complete a new round of transformation.
The problem was that if the flame could not be extinguished, Luo Zheng could not walk around with the flame all over his body, right? He could not even wear clothes.
Moreover, Luo Zheng's Spirit Beast Pouch, Sumeru Ring, and other belongings were still placed not far away …
If he could not extinguish the Indestructible Mysterious Flame, he could not take those things away. The Sumeru Ring that Ning Yudie gave him was not of a high grade. If it was burned by the Indestructible Mysterious Flame, it would probably melt in an instant.
Now, Luo Zheng was completely at a loss.
However, he suddenly felt that something was wrong. He found that Xun's tone was very relaxed …
Xun now supported Luo Zheng almost unconditionally. Since she already knew the characteristics of the Indestructible Mysterious Flame, she should have stopped him when he tried to use the Indestructible Mysterious Flame to refine his body.
However, not only did Xun not stop him, but her tone was also very relaxed. It seemed that Xun had a way to break it?
"Well … Don't tease Luo Zheng anymore. Luo Zheng, you forgot about the Ancestral Dragon. Although it is now in its infancy and has no fighting power, it still has some abilities that can be used." Qinglong's voice sounded faintly.
Ancestral Dragon? Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
The Ancestral Dragon that had just been born was still in Luo Zheng's Spirit Beast Pouch. However, since he ascended, this guy had been sleeping for a long time. When he was in the Cloud Mist Palace, Luo Zheng let the Ancestral Dragon out for a few rounds occasionally. However, this little guy did not seem to like him very much. Every time he let it out, it seemed to be looking for Xi Youqin's figure.
In fact, the Ancestral Dragon had no interest in eating Xi Youqin's dishes. It only liked the delicious food made by Xi Youqin.
When Qinglong mentioned the Ancestral Dragon, he quickly reacted. This Ancestral Dragon seemed to be able to ignore all the Power of Law!
When Luo Zheng was in the Tian Chen Secret Territory, he was unable to take out the Ancestral Dragon's egg. He had relied on this ability of the Ancestral Dragon to easily break through the restrictions of the spatial refraction. However, Luo Zheng and many Divine Realm experts of the Demi-Immortal Clan were unable to enter the spatial refraction.
"The Ancestral Dragon …"
Looking at the Spirit Beast Pouch, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with light.
However, he did not reach out to take the Spirit Beast Pouch. Instead, he gently flicked the Spirit Beast Pouch from a distance. With a "Gu Gu" sound, a figure dashed towards Luo Zheng and directly jumped into Luo Zheng's arms.
This little guy had been kept in the Spirit Beast Pouch by Luo Zheng most of the time. Although the space inside the Spirit Beast Pouch was not small, it would still feel suffocated. Therefore, it ran out to get some fresh air.
This time, it jumped up, just like how it ignored the spatial refraction. It did not put Luo Zheng's Indestructible Flame in its eyes. Moreover, this Indestructible Flame did not cause any harm to the Ancestral Dragon!
This guy was immune to all the Power of Law …
At this time, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand in front of the Ancestral Dragon. The raging purple flames on his hands were still burning, showing no signs of weakening.
When the Ancestral Dragon saw this, it understood tacitly. It directly opened its small dragon mouth and swallowed the flames on Luo Zheng's hands …
Not long after, Luo Zheng began to fly across the fire pool. When he flew across the fire pool, he would still be contaminated by the Indestructible Flame. He did not let the Ancestral Dragon clean up the Indestructible Flame on his body. If he wanted to clean up the Indestructible Flame, he would have to cross the two hundred miles first.
At this time, the warriors outside the One Thought Sealed Tower found that Luo Zheng was moving forward again!

Chapter 1066
"This kid … actually dares to force his way into the fire pool!"

The judgement of these palace lords was reasonable, but Luo Zheng wasn't a reasonable person to begin with!
After Yun Luo spoke, the Sunset sector lord stopped his steps. He actually also wanted to see if Luo Zheng could cross the fire pool …

Luo Zheng's speed wasn't slow, so it wouldn't take him too much time to pass through it.

"30,500 li!"

Master said that she would be the main character of this great era, the darling of this era.

She lowered her head and took off a blue butterfly hair ornament from her head. She kept rubbing it in her palm.

When she was nervous, Ai Anxin liked to clench her fists. When she was depressed or frustrated, she liked to rub some of the small ornaments on her body.
This second-grade divine tool, under Ai Anxin's pair of jade-like hands, cracks began to appear on it.
As she continued to rub it, she heard a small cracking sound. The miniature divine patterns on the butterfly's wings began to shatter one by one. The entire butterfly hair ornament started to break into two pieces from the middle. Then, under her fingers, it turned into countless pieces.
The price of a second-grade divine tool wasn't cheap. It wasn't easy to destroy it. She obviously used her true energy to destroy this butterfly hair ornament.
When the pieces in Ai Anxin's hand turned into powder, she suddenly raised her head. Her beautiful eyes were filled with a strong aura!
"I, Ai Anxin, have indeed lost for a while. But in the next segment, I won't lose to you, Luo Zheng!" She whispered.
The Torch Flame Inheritance wasn't only the segment of One Thought Sealed Tower. This time, Ai Anxin had lost to Luo Zheng. But after adjusting her state of mind, Ai Anxin wasn't discouraged in the slightest!
It was just that the price she paid to adjust her state of mind was a bit high. She had destroyed a second-grade divine tool and crushed the butterfly hair ornament into dust. But thinking about those geniuses who were unable to recover from the setback, Ai Anxin's heart of martial arts was already extremely valiant!

31,000 li ….

33,000 li ….

This thunder and lightning was wrapped in the power of the Law of Water. This thunder and lightning was like a huge waterfall flowing down from above.
As it flowed down, the violent power of thunder and lightning intertwined and collided with each other, blossoming with dazzling light. The power it emitted was even more intense than the fire pool from before. This should be the "masterpiece" of an Empyrean.
After reaching this point, Luo Zheng finally stopped.
Maybe Luo Zheng still had a chance to pass through this waterfall of thunder and lightning, but Luo Zheng didn't need to take this risk.
Challenging one's limits might be important, but if one didn't know what was right or wrong and rushed forward without any assurance, such a martial artist was doomed to not live for long.
Previously, he dared to pass through the fire pool because he had absolute confidence in the characteristics of his body. He could easily resist the attack of the Law of Fire. With this assurance, Luo Zheng only needed to grit his teeth and rush forward.
But Luo Zheng didn't have much resistance to the power of thunder and lightning.
Therefore, Luo Zheng summoned the Ancestral Dragon and began to let it completely devour the Law of Fire on his body!
After passing through the fire pool, Luo Zheng didn't put the Ancestral Dragon into the Spirit Beast Pouch. Instead, he let it accompany him as he flew.
In any case, this little guy seemed to completely ignore the Law of Fire and could freely soar in this storm.
This time, he borrowed the Inextinguishable Profound Flame to burn his body. Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to extinguish the Inextinguishable Profound Flame. Since those golden whirlpools were constantly absorbing the Inextinguishable Profound Flame, then he would absorb it to his heart's content.
At present, those golden whirlpools gradually began to close. Luo Zheng would naturally get rid of the Inextinguishable Profound Flame. Otherwise, as Xun said, if he really walked out with the Inextinguishable Profound Flame, it would indeed look very cool, but it would be too shocking.
After the Ancestral Dragon completely devoured the Inextinguishable Profound Flame, this guy seemed to be full. He rolled in the air, even burped, and protruded a ball of flame …
Then, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand toward the Ancestral Dragon.
Accompanied by a "gu gu" sound, Luo Zheng stuffed it back into the Spirit Beast Pouch. The Ancestral Dragon seemed to be very dissatisfied. It even bumped around in the Spirit Beast Pouch for a while before it calmed down.
As the Inextinguishable Profound Flame gradually extinguished, Luo Zheng's originally red body, which looked like it was made of molten steel, began to gradually cool down.
After this round of calcining, Luo Zheng's entire body increased by another level. Luo Zheng looked at his chest. It seemed that no divine patterns had appeared. He couldn't determine what level his body was at, or what grade of divine tool it was. He could only slowly verify it in the future.
However, the strength of Luo Zheng's body had indeed increased by a large margin!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly began to condense the power of the dragon scales!
The surging power of the dragon scales rapidly poured into his body.
One thousand …
Three thousand …
Five thousand …
Ten thousand …
Fifteen thousand …
Luo Zheng lit up all the power of the dragon scales. However, Luo Zheng didn't feel anything strange in his body. The only feeling he had was that his body was filled with surging power!
He clenched his fist. Under the slight vibration of the enormous power, the surrounding space was distorted!

Chapter 1067
Collecting the Sky Evolution Essence was not a particularly difficult task for Luo Zheng now.
Previously, he had been restricted by his body as a Divine Artifact. Now, he no longer had this concern.
His body had already become a Divine Artifact. Although he did not know what grade of Divine Artifact it was, it could definitely hold more than 10,000 dragon scales.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly patted his head.
If his body continued to strengthen, would it step into the ranks of Divine Artifacts?
It was said that the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele was very special. Although the Karma Law it contained was not helpful in battle, it could sniff out all the divine weapons in the universe.
If his fleshly body was strengthened to that extent, would his name appear on the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele?
For Luo Zheng, this was not a small problem. He did not want to expose his special constitution in front of others.
Thus, Luo Zhengcai voiced out his worries …
As a result, Xun and Qinglong made the same judgment at the same time. This was very likely!
Although Luo Zheng's body looked like a body of flesh and blood, its essence had been changed by the Divine Fire Cauldron. To put it bluntly, in many aspects, it was a body of a Magic Treasure.
It could be said that he was half human and half Magic Treasure.
However, this situation had never happened in the universe. Xun and Qinglong only thought it was possible, but they could not be sure whether his name would appear on the Chaotic Myriad Spirit Stele.
…
Luo Zheng stopped in front of the Lightning Waterfall. The Palace Masters, including the Sunset Realm Lord and Yun Luo, almost thought that he was going to walk through the Lightning Waterfall.
At this time, even Yun Luo began to feel nervous. If Luo Zheng was really going to do this, she might go in personally to bring Luo Zheng out.
However, after pausing for a while at the 36,000-mile mark, Luo Zheng voluntarily gave up. After choosing to give up, he appeared at the entrance of the One Thought Sealed Tower.
At this time, Luo Zheng had experienced the burning of the Mysterious Fire, and his whole body had improved to another level. Because his clothes were burned, he took out a spare set of clothes. It was a set of white clothes, and he looked very energetic.
Many martial artists returned from One Thought Sealed Pagoda. If they were not injured, they were covered in dirt. This Luo Zheng had entered 36,000 miles, which was twice as deep as Empyrean Mirage Sea and 8000 miles further than Ai Anxin. The dangers within were not hard to imagine for the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces.
But this guy looked perfectly fine, as if he had just returned from a vacation.
Many martial artists were speechless, and even more martial artists were indignant. The disparity between martial artists was too great. Could this Luo Zheng have used some method to cheat in the One Thought Sealed Tower?

Previously, he only heard that there were rewards for reaching 1,000 miles, 3,000 miles, and 10,000 miles. Naturally, he did not know about the latter. Moreover, Luo Zheng did his best to reach 36,000 miles, not for any reward.
But if there was a reward, and it was the Great Sector Lord's reward, Luo Zheng would certainly not refuse …

What do you want?"

Originally, Luo Zheng wanted to enter the Divine Weapon Hall to help Xun choose a spear, but he was restricted by the fact that he did not have enough Zhen Yuanyu …
Yun Luo naturally understood. The Yao Ye Clan's King of Massacre's weapon was a spear. Luo Zheng's weapon was to help Xun. He had found the right person this time!

But if Luo Zheng chooses that spear, I'm afraid I won't be able to give you the thirty thousand mile reward …"
A grade one divine weapon that could be listed in the top 4000 was a high divine weapon. The top 3000 on the Universe Thousand Spirit Stele were all supreme divine weapons.
Yun Luo asked Luo Zheng, "If you choose that spear, the Sunset Sector Lord will have to use two rewards to exchange for it!"
Luo Zheng had to make the decision himself.

However, the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele would be refreshed every month. The words on the stele would change, and the ranking would also constantly change. The lower the ranking of the treasures, the greater the fluctuations in the ranking. However, the Cold Plum Ancient Blood Spear was much more outstanding than the other three spears.
Luo Zheng saw this spear and thought that Yun Luo was teaching Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1068
This was the truth. Although others couldn't hear Zhen Yuan transmissions, they could feel it.
How sensitive was the Sunset Realm Lord to Zhen Yuan?

In the next moment, the plum blossoms on the spear shaft gradually revealed themselves, turning into blood-colored plum blossoms. Under the blossoming of the blood-colored light, the spear was bright and glistening. At the same time, it revealed a boundless and ancient aura. This Cold Plum Ancient Blood Spear had the words' Ancient Blood 'on it.

As a Realm Lord, the Zhen Yuan he condensed was many times more condensed than the Zhen Yuan of a Life and Death Realm kid. However, he still couldn't activate the Ancient Blood to this extent. How did Luo Zheng do it?
Unless Luo Zheng had a strand of Ancient Blood in his body and could interact with the Ancient Blood in the spear, it would be impossible.
However, the Sunset Realm Lord couldn't sense any Ancient Blood from Luo Zheng's body. This made the Sunset Realm Lord very puzzled …
However, every genius more or less had their own secrets. For example, Luo Zheng was able to fly thirty-six thousand miles with his physical body. It was already difficult to guess what kind of ability he used to travel such a long distance, so he wouldn't ask about it.

Unlike the Exquisite Pagoda of the Demi-Immortal Tribe, the Torch Flame Inheritance was a pure trial. This trial was divided into two parts. The first part was naturally the One Thought Seal Tower, and the second part was the Dream Challenge.
After Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin descended from the roof, they joined the group of warriors.

Many warriors of the Thirteen Palaces suddenly remembered why Ai Anxin only allowed Luo Zheng to sit beside her when they were comprehending the Law Stone Inscriptions.
After understanding this, they sighed in their hearts. Indeed, there were not many people in the younger generation who were qualified to sit beside Ai Anxin. Luo Zheng must be one of them …
At this time, the linen-clothed old man floated in the air, and an old voice leisurely sounded.

After saying this, the linen-clothed old man walked in front to lead the way, and the warriors of the Thirteen Palaces got up and followed behind him.
Ai Anxin's face was as calm as water, but there was an uncontrollable excitement in her heart!
She had already lost the One Thought Seal Tower.
However, she must win the next trial in the Dream Space!
It was an extremely difficult thing for Ai Xin to compromise and admit defeat wholeheartedly. After confirming her fate, the effort she put in was ten times that of other martial artists!
In this great battle, she was not willing to only be a supporting role. Perhaps, Luo Zheng was an obstacle arranged by God for her. Otherwise, how could there be such a coincidence that Luo Zheng appeared, and he was even more monstrous than her!
I will definitely surpass you!
Looking at Luo Zheng's back, Ai Anxin's eyes flashed. Her strong determination made her unconsciously exude a decisive aura, causing the other warriors to look at her.
Only Luo Zheng did not look back. He walked in front of Ai Anxin with his head held high, but the corners of his mouth were slightly curved.
The Dream Space on Changsu Island was built in an ancient palace, and it was not far from the One Thought Seal Tower. Everyone quickly arrived at their destination.

She already knew that Luo Zheng was an Ascender, and he had only ascended for a short time. The possibility of him not entering the Dream Space was relatively high.

The successors of various Realm Lords, Heavenly Venerables, and some hidden peerless geniuses will all participate." Ai Anxin smiled and said, "Actually, I haven't seen it either. Or rather, not a single living being in this Evolution Era has seen it!"
"Not even a single living being in this Evolution Era has seen it?" Luo Zheng was slightly shocked.
An Evolution Era was 3.6 billion years. It was said that the end of an Evolution Era was often accompanied by a great extinction.
Countless living beings in the universe would be annihilated and reshuffled. After the next Evolution Era, they would slowly blossom with vitality …
This universe was an Evolution Era, and the cycle of an Evolution Era was endless. No one knew how many Evolution Eras it had gone through.
When an Evolution Era ended, even the Heavenly Dao would collapse. For example, Luo Zheng's Heavenly Dao fragment was a fragment of the Heavenly Dao from the previous Evolution Era.
Only an extremely small number of peerless powerhouses could survive from the previous Evolution Era to the next Evolution Era, breaking through the great extinction limit.
"What's so strange about it? It is said that the Dream Realization Battlefield was originally opened with the Battle of the Great Eras. Or perhaps, the Dream Realization Battlefield is the prelude to the Battle of the Great Eras!" Ai Anxin felt that all of this was common knowledge, but he still needed to explain it to Luo Zheng.
"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded.
Now, everyone's attention was on the trial in the ancient palace.
Under the arrangement of the linen-clothed old man, there were people who hung up rows of letter tablets, allowing others to investigate the situation in the ancient palace.
200 people entered the Dream Realization Space at the same time. It was definitely not for everyone to compete.
When the letter tablets were hung up, Luo Zheng could clearly see that after these martial artists entered the Dream Realization Space, they were constantly accepting challenge after challenge!
Soon, some martial artists were eliminated. Their projections died in the Dream Realization Space, and their true bodies opened their eyes, dejectedly walking out of the ancient palace.
As time went on, the difficulty of the challenge would increase, and more and more people would leave.
The last person persisted for 11 rounds of challenges before his projection died in the Dream Realization Space. When he opened his eyes, he discovered that he was the only one left in the ancient palace, and his face naturally revealed a proud expression.
After all 200 people left the ancient palace, the linen-clothed old man shook his head. "The best result in this round was only 11 rounds of challenges. Too bad, too bad …"
Obviously, the linen-clothed old man's evaluation of these 200 people was not high. He did not even mention the name of the first place.

Chapter 1069

Chapter 1070

. Ai Anxin liked to collect all kinds of hair ornaments. Therefore, she often wore all kinds of beautiful hair ornaments on her head.

After three consecutive waves, the first round of the Illusory Space trial was passed.
This speed wasn't slow. From the beginning of the first round to the end, it only took ten breaths of time. However, when Luo Zheng passed the first round, he looked to the right and saw Ai Anxin's long hair dragging on the ground. She tilted her head and looked at him. It seemed that her speed was faster.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly and stepped on the "two" word to start the second round.
The first few rounds of the Illusory Space trial were relatively simple. The weakest warriors of the Thirteen Palaces could pass the sixth round. Very few people would be eliminated in the seventh round.

After ten breaths of time, Luo Zheng passed the second round.
However, when Luo Zheng passed the second round, he found that Ai Anxin was still standing in the same place. Her long hair was spread on the ground, and she was staring at him with a faint smile.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise. He originally thought that passing the second round in ten breaths of time was already very fast. He didn't expect Ai Anxin to be faster than him.

In the eighth round … two breaths of time.

As for Ai Anxin and Luo Zheng, one was at the fifth layer of Life and Death realm and the other was at the ninth layer of Life and Death realm. They might not be able to walk further!

Therefore, on the other side of Changsu Island, there were more than thirty thousand warriors on standby. Every time a warrior opened the eleventh round, they would enter the Illusory Space and attack the warriors in it!
Not all warriors had the opportunity to enter the Illusory Space. Even if they were training partners, they had to rely on their own strength to fight for it. Therefore, every warrior training partner worked extra hard. In addition, if they died in the Illusory Space, they would only die in projection. Therefore, they were basically fighting with their lives on the line!
What's more, these warriors were also some of the geniuses of the Holy Lands. It was just that their talent wasn't enough to enter the Thirteen Palaces. In their hearts, they yearned for the Thirteen Palaces, but at the same time, they were full of jealousy towards the warriors of the Thirteen Palaces!
Since they had the opportunity to fight against the warriors of the Thirteen Palaces, even if they weren't their opponents, even if they were killed, they would bite off a piece of meat from their opponents' hands …
Because of this, they were especially fierce!
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng nodded slightly. If it was this kind of arrangement, it might be a little troublesome, but for Luo Zheng, it wasn't too big of a problem!

The dozen warriors were like a pack of hungry wolves who found a lamb strolling on the grassland. They swarmed up and attacked Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1072

As for the several tens of thousands of martial artists on the other side of Changsu Island, they were all dumbstruck.
Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin had participated in the Illusory Space's trial alone. They all understood that these two were definitely special.
However, Luo Zheng was only at the fifth level of Life and Death realm. How special could he be?
A single sword strike killed thirteen martial artists who were two or three levels higher than him … The several tens of thousands of martial artists in the huge plaza were extremely quiet at this moment. They were all silent.
"I finally understand the strength of the top geniuses of the Alliance …"
"Moreover, the Thirteen Palaces are only the reserves of the younger generation. They still need to grow before they can become true super geniuses. However, I don't know that the super geniuses of Spring Fragrance Pavilion and Lingyan Pavilion are even more terrifying …"
"Humph! We'll definitely take down this kid!"
Everyone had their own pride. Moreover, these martial artists were only inferior to the geniuses of the Thirteen Palaces. They were also the core existences of their respective Holy Lands. How could they tolerate being killed so easily by Luo Zheng?
This kid had passed the eleventh trial. There was still the twelfth trial. This time, they were going to select the strongest martial artist of the second group to take down Luo Zheng!
These martial artists saw Luo Zheng's battle strength. However, the Palace Lords, including the Sunset Realm Lord, didn't see the same thing. They saw the Spirit Shadows …
"How did this Luo Zheng do it?" A Palace Lord asked with a puzzled expression.
In the past, Xiao Daolai's Thunder and Wind Spirit Sword was quite famous. The Sunset Realm Lord also said with a puzzled expression, "Isn't this Spirit Shadow only able to form three at a time?"
"This guy can form thirteen Spirit Shadows at once. Is there a problem with the sword or with the person?" Another Palace Lord was also puzzled.
Yun Luo giggled. "Of course there's a problem with the person. The Spirit Heart Sutra was personally taught to Luo Zheng by Xiao Daolai. In the past, Xiao Daolai could only form three Spirit Shadows at once. Who knows how this kid managed to form so many Spirit Shadows …"
The Palace Lords' expressions were messy. For a moment, they didn't know how to evaluate Luo Zheng.
As for Ai Anxin …
When the martial artists on the other side of Changsu Island entered the Illusory Space and saw that their opponent was actually a woman, a woman as beautiful as a flower, they were all a little stunned.
However, most of the martial artists' performance was normal. However, there were some who began to talk dirty.
The genius woman of the Thirteen Palaces was a lofty existence to them. In any case, if they died in the Illusory Space, they would just be kicked out. It wasn't a bad idea to take advantage of them with their words.
However, they didn't know that although they wouldn't die in the Illusory Space, Ai Anxin could make them wish they were dead!
Ai Anxin raised the azure longsword slightly and began to vibrate at a different frequency … …
Just as Ai Anxin raised the sword and started to vibrate, an invisible ripple spread out along the longsword! Ai Anxin had always used this method to deal with the first ten rounds. However, this time, the longsword vibrated at a higher frequency and the method was more special.
Under this vibration, the dozen or so martial artists also rushed toward Ai Anxin. Some of them had evil intentions and didn't even plan to fight properly. Instead, they wanted to take advantage of Ai Anxin …
However, they weren't able to get close to Ai Anxin. The fastest martial artist was still two feet away from Ai Anxin.
Next, they felt their hearts beating at a strange frequency. Then, their breathing became disordered and they couldn't control their bodies.
Then, an intense pain was transmitted from their dantian!
Generally speaking, when the pain exceeded the limits of the body, they would faint. The body had a self-protection mechanism. However, under the vibration of Ai Anxin's longsword, they were able to maintain their consciousness!
It was the same as continuously torturing a prisoner and continuously pouring cold water on them to wake them up!
At this time, they really wanted to "die" quickly. If they died, they could leave the Illusory Space. If they died, they wouldn't have to suffer anymore …
Ai Anxin seemed to be a heartless person. However, martial artists who offended her didn't have a good ending.
Now, she didn't compete in speed with Luo Zheng. Instead, she used a very subtle state to control the pain of these martial artists. She let them clearly taste this kind of punishment that was worse than death.
Ten breaths, twenty breaths, thirty breaths …
Although it was merely thirty breaths, to these dozen or so martial artists, it was like hundreds of years had passed.
At this time, the voice of the linen-clothed old man leisurely came from outside the ancient palace, "Ai Anxin, stop …"
The linen-clothed old man had no choice but to dissuade her. After all, these guys were invited by the Thirteen Palaces to be sparring partners. It wasn't good to torture them like this.
"Hmph!"
The tip of Ai Anxin's sword lightly drew a circle. Her long black hair flowed down her chest, waist, and legs. The ends of her long hair spread out in a spiral shape. Then, she forcefully inserted the sword into the platform. Her other finger lightly flicked the blade of the sword. The blade of the sword hummed softly.
"Pop …"

Chapter 1073
Luo Zheng had already completed the eleventh round and was watching Ai Anxin's battle from his own platform.

After the clouds of blood mist exploded, these martial artists had a quick death and were kicked out of the Illusory Space one after another.

Therefore, with their current cultivation, they can face cultivators several levels higher than them without being defeated, and even easily kill them! "

Although some cultivators were talented, their cultivation and strength were unable to increase. However, when it came to the world's Cultivation Methods, legends, treasures, and structures, they were able to analyze them with great precision. This kind of cultivator was a think-tank type of cultivator, and Rao An was one of them.

There were some powerful cultivators in this huge square. They were all at the Divine Sea Realm, and some were even at the advanced stage of the Divine Sea Realm …

Before this, Rao An had observed the characteristics of Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin for a long time. He had analyzed them several times in his mind.
"That cultivator called Luo Zheng relied on the sharpness of the long sword in his hand. However, he only revealed his weapon. His strength is still unknown." Rao An said, "In the second group, those who are confident in their Body Movements can sign up …"

In the absence of a better method, Rao An had to use his Body Movements to create obstacles for Luo Zheng. This was the next step of Rao An's strategy …

The twelfth round …
Luo Zheng didn't know how many rounds of trials there were in this illusory space. However, at this stage, Luo Zheng planned to use the Serene Spirit Shadow to crush his opponents.
It had to be said that the Serene Spirit Shadow was very effective in dealing with many people. Every Serene Spirit Shadow was under the control of Luo Zheng. They could perform different moves. Controlling so many Serene Spirit Shadows at the same time consumed a lot of soul power. However, Luo Zheng's soul had already entered the Blue Soul Realm, so it wasn't a big problem for him.
However, after the twelfth round began, Luo Zheng discovered some abnormalities.
In the eleventh round, the martial artists he encountered all had different characteristics. However, the martial artists in the twelfth round all seemed to be proficient in Body Movements!
In the instant that Luo Zheng released the Serene Spirit Shadow, these martial artists relied on the power of various Laws to constantly dodge the Serene Spirit Shadow's attacks …
Using the Law of Space to use the "Dreamlike Illusion", using the Law of Earth to display the Earth Escape Secret Technique, using the Law of Wind to display the Wind Escape Technique …
Unexpectedly, not a single martial artist was willing to fight with him!
Was this an obstacle deliberately set up by the linen-clothed old man? Or was someone plotting against him?
Luo Zheng slightly guessed in his heart. However, his face quickly revealed a faint smile. No matter what the goal was, he only needed to follow his own plan!
Since these people's Body Movements were so powerful, Luo Zheng couldn't stay in place …
However, just as Luo Zheng's figure flashed, he suddenly discovered that a brown vine had appeared under his feet, firmly binding him to the ground.
You want to bind me with a vine?
Luo Zheng didn't even look at it. He took a step forward, and along with the eruption of his power, he wanted to tear the vine apart. However, to Luo Zheng's surprise, the vine was extremely tenacious. Not only did it not break, but it also extended as he moved.
Whoosh!
With a backhand slash, the vine broke. However, immediately after, two more vines appeared from the ground, once again binding Luo Zheng. At the same time, the martial artists who had been dodging his attacks earlier surrounded him from all directions.
This scene made the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces puzzled. When they passed the twelfth round, they didn't think that these guys could cooperate so well!

Chapter 1074
Under the suppression of absolute strength, petty tricks were of little use. At most, they could only create some obstacles for the opponent. Most people had this view.

Even though he was surrounded by more than ten cultivators and Luo Zheng's movements were restricted by a vine, Luo Zheng's face didn't show any signs of panic!
Seeing that the cultivators had already surrounded him, a crackling sound suddenly came from the Thundergale Dark God Sword!
The difference between these cultivators and the Thirteen Palaces wasn't just the difference in talent and strength, but also the difference in the weapons in their hands. This was equivalent to using a layer of lightning net to sift through the entire platform. There was no space to dodge at all ….

Could it be that this guy only relied on this sword?

.....................................

But the cultivators that Ai Anxin encountered were all cultivators of the Water Law!

All liquids were viscous. Although her "Sword Chime Technique" was very powerful, it could not "chime" in the water. In other words, its effect would be greatly reduced. It could not cause any substantial harm to the cultivators.
At the beginning, she was caught off guard and forgot to use her True Essence to protect her body. Her entire body was wet from head to toe! Her clothes were wet and stuck to her body ….
How could Ai Anxin not know? Someone must have deliberately designed a countermeasure to her "Sword Chime Technique".
Those cultivators didn't answer Ai Anxin's question. They didn't approach Ai Anxin, nor did they try to hide. Instead, each of them performed their own roles. Some of them continued to unleash huge waves, crashing down on Ai Anxin. Some of them condensed water spears, shooting them at Ai Anxin …
Ai Anxin did not care about this level of attack. She gently waved the cyan longsword in her hand and easily resolved the oncoming attacks.

After speaking, the cyan longsword was raised above her head again. Her body trembled slightly, and the cyan longsword dragged out sword shadows. She was still unwilling to kill them one by one. It seemed that she used some kind of powerful sword technique, wanting to kill all the cultivators on the platform in one move.

Empty Hand ….
This was one of Xiao Daolai's famous consummate skills. It required a deep understanding of the Law of Space to use. With a stretch of hand, one could directly touch a space.
The space that Xiao Daolai wiped out was naturally quite large, but Ai Anxin only wiped out a circle of space around her sword shadows. It was a circular space that was about a hundred feet wide ….
Those cultivators were all distributed on the edge of the platform, but the moment this space was wiped out, their bodies involuntarily moved!
This was also a characteristic of space. Ordinary cultivators of the Law of Space might not be able to use the Empty Hand, but they understood the principle behind it.
If the center of a section of space was missing, the two sections of space would instantly fit together!
Those cultivators were in the outer space of the platform, and Ai Anxin wiped out the circle of space in the middle. So at this moment, all the cultivators were involuntarily "pulled" to the center of the space!
In the center of the platform, sword shadows were still spinning rapidly. Almost in the blink of an eye, the cultivators who were pulled in were cut into pieces.

Chapter 1075

I, Rao An, of course cannot be compared to you … However, you are now only a death row prisoner. A death row prisoner who can only live for eight hundred years …"
Execution in eight hundred years … For mortals, eight hundred years was more than a dozen lifetimes. However, for a warrior of the Divine Sea Realm, eight hundred years was still too short.
"What do you want to do? Just say it," the burly man said coldly.
Rao An still did not answer the burly man's question directly. Instead, he continued, "You are all death row prisoners carefully selected from the Wicked World. If you successfully ambush the geniuses of the Thirteen Palaces, you can pardon your crimes."
"Nonsense," the burly man said coldly.
For ordinary warriors, the rewards might be various. However, if these death row prisoners successfully ambushed the geniuses of the Thirteen Palaces, they would also be rewarded. The reward was freedom.
It was also because this reward could stimulate the most powerful combat strength of these death row prisoners, so the Torch Inheritance made such an arrangement.
However, these death row prisoners rarely had the chance to go on stage. Moreover, once they went on stage, they would face very strong geniuses. Therefore, these death row prisoners did not have many opportunities to obtain freedom.
"I can help you go on stage," Rao An continued.
"And then?" the burly man asked.
"I will teach you how to defeat that opponent and obtain freedom," Rao An said again.
At this point, the burly man suddenly laughed. His laughter was filled with contempt for Rao An. "Who do you think you are? I need you to teach me … "
Whether it was cultivation, strength, or talent, this burly man was much, much stronger than Rao An. If he was Rao An's opponent, even if there were a hundred Rao Ans, they would all be annihilated in an instant by him!
Being despised like this, Rao An was not annoyed. He smiled calmly and said, "You may not know, but there are only two people left in the Torch Inheritance. These two people are the most powerful people in this Torch Inheritance. And I can decide who will go on stage."
Rao An said these words to the burly man with a True Essence sound transmission. The death row prisoners beside him naturally could not hear it.
Hearing this, the burly man finally frowned, "So … this is why you dare to look for me."
"Yes." Rao An's smile brightened. He even calmly took out a pill and stuffed it into his mouth. Just now, the burly man's soul pressure had caused some damage to his soul.
"What do you want?" the burly man asked again.
