"30%?" The burly man grinned, "Do you know what the Sifting Yin World's secret treasure means?"
"I know, so I only want 30%," Rao An said again.
Rao An was a person who understood people's hearts and knew how to act appropriately. Therefore, he did not ask for 50% or even 70% or 30% …
The burly man closed his eyes and let out a deep sigh. A tiger that had fallen to the plains would be bullied by a dog. In the past, he had been a glorious figure, but now that he had been exiled to the Evil Men World, his fate was actually being controlled by such a nobody.
Rao An guessed the burly man's thoughts and lightly said, "Everyone is useful. In the world of martial artists, strength is respected, but this is also very important. Don't look down on me. With me here, it will be much easier to obtain the Sifting Yin World's secret treasure."
When he said these words, his face revealed an incomparable confidence. Perhaps many people thought that he, Rao An, was a person who only knew how to play petty tricks. However, he wanted to tell the world that petty tricks could often bring about fatal threats and could even reverse many situations.
The burly man opened his eyes once more and stared at Rao An with a complex expression. Then, he faintly said, "Good, I agree …"
Rao An returned to the giant square with satisfaction. In the square, many martial artists were watching the scene unfold in the dreamland space. There was a constant clamor as some people greeted him.
"Rao An, we can't hold on any longer. How do we break that woman's move!?"
"The people in the third group can't hold on for long. Rao An, think of a way!"
A faint smile appeared on Rao An's face as he seriously analyzed, "It's okay. The next group should be …"
In fact, he had no interest in sniping Ai Anxin and Luo Zheng. However, in order to deal with this group of hot-blooded fellows, he naturally had to pay a price. After a series of logical and logical analysis, it attracted a wave of cheers. The cultivators in the fourth group were selected by Rao An to enter the Illusory Space.
Fourteenth round …
Fifteenth round …
Sixteenth round …
On the side of Gu Palace, the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces could clearly see that the strength of these martial artists was far beyond their imagination when they worked together.
They knew that those martial artists who had passed the eighteenth or nineteenth round would not even be able to pass the sixteenth round if they were here!
However, Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin were not ordinary martial artists. Although they found it tricky, under the suppression of absolute strength, they still passed through the trials in an orderly manner.
However, there was still a slight gap between the two …
Luo Zheng was still relying on the Thunder and Wind Divine Sword.
On the other hand, Ai Anxin had already used several trump cards. Every time she used a trump card, her opponent would always be able to think of a way to restrain it. In the next round, he would be able to tie her hands and feet, making her unable to use her full strength.

Chapter 1076
Of course, Ai Anxin could deal with these damn guys. However, being targeted by these bastards, she couldn't help but feel irritable.
Under such a fierce battle, she and Luo Zheng entered the twentieth round not long after.
The twentieth round was the best result achieved by some martial artists.
No one had been able to cross the threshold of the twentieth round because the opponents in the twentieth round were all at the middle stage of the Spirit Sea realm, who had experienced at least four major heavenly tribulations.
For these geniuses of the Thirteen Palaces, challenging those with higher realms wasn't difficult. However, facing several Spirit Sea realm martial artists at the same time was still quite difficult.
After this round, Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin finally felt the pressure.
The Spirit Sea realm was a qualitative leap from the Life and Death realm. The most troublesome part was their Domains. Under the suppression of each person's Domains, Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin were greatly troubled.
"Finally, there's some trouble. However, these two shouldn't stop at the twentieth round, right?" A Palace Lord said.
Sunset Realm Lord nodded indifferently, "It's hard to say. After all, this is a one-versus-many trial. It's completely different from One Thought Sealed Tower. The opponents they will face are also different."
"This Luo Zheng is only at the fifth stage of Life and Death realm, but he can already face the siege of several Spirit Sea realm martial artists. I wonder what will happen after he breaks through to the Spirit Sea realm." Because of the results achieved in One Thought Sealed Tower, they were more concerned about Luo Zheng.
Another Palace Lord wasn't thinking about this issue. He looked at Yun Luo and said, "I seem to have overlooked a problem. I want to know, how many leaves has Luo Zheng opened?"
Hearing the Palace Master's question, everyone looked at Yun Luo curiously.
Before coming to the Torch Flame Inheritance, they would all screen the martial artists in the Heavenly Palace. The Scarlet Afterglow Ring could sense this and make a rough estimate.
Yun Luo shook her head, "I don't know."
"You don't know? Could it be that this Luo Zheng didn't pass the test? "A Palace Lord asked.
"No," Yun Luo continued, "When Luo Zheng passed through the Scarlet Afterglow Ring, the Scarlet Afterglow Ring's sensing power seemed to shut down due to saturation. Therefore, we can't measure how many leaves has Luo Zheng opened."
Hearing Yun Luo's words, many of the Palace Masters were stunned.
Sunset Realm Lord's eyes also flashed, "So, this Luo Zheng is likely to open a Mahayana Lotus?"
In the universe, there was a threshold between Lesser Lotus Flower and Mahayana Lotus Flower. Martial artists who had opened the Nine-leaf Lotus Flower were already top existences. However, there were too few martial artists who had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower. Even among the trillions of living beings, it was difficult to find many martial artists who had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower.
..................... ".............................................................................
"This … isn't a good idea?" Yun Luo looked hesitant.
. "What's wrong with that? After the Torch Flame Inheritance, isn't it a tradition to attract tribulations? "Another Palace Lord said.
Many martial artists couldn't wait to swallow the Ten Direction Tribulation Pill in order to improve their cultivation as soon as possible. After experiencing such a high-intensity trial in the Torch Flame Inheritance, they often gained a lot of insights.
Therefore, every time after the Torch Flame Inheritance, about a hundred people would choose to undergo tribulations!
Hearing this suggestion, Yun Luo finally nodded, "Let's see what Luo Zheng wants to do."
At this time, the thousands of martial artists outside the ancient palace stared at the battle in the letter tablet and burst into exclamations again.
Although everyone knew that no one would die in the illusory space, the battle inside was no different from the outside world. Seeing the thrilling scenes, they were naturally amazed.
After the 20th round of trials, Luo Zheng felt that it was more troublesome to deal with...
. Although the number of his opponents had been reduced to six, these six Divine Sea Realm martial artists were in a pincer formation. It was very difficult to break through such a temporary formation.
As for these six people, they were all Divine Sea Realm martial artists. In front of them, Luo Zheng's cultivation could only be regarded as a junior.
To be honest, the fact that Luo Zheng was able to remain undefeated under the pincer attack of six people was already a very humiliating thing for them. Six Divine Sea Realm warriors besieging a Life and Death Realm warrior. If this news spread out, it would be a very unpleasant thing to hear!
However, as Luo Zheng and the six people attacked each other back and forth, they had already forgotten this concern in their hearts.
Because the six of them found that as time passed, Luo Zheng seemed to be getting more and more powerful … They were faintly being suppressed by Luo Zheng, a Life and Death Realm little guy!
Thinking of this, the six people became more and more anxious.
However, before the battle, Rao An told them that the most important thing to do when facing Luo Zheng was to maintain their mentality. No matter what, they had to force Luo Zheng to use all of his strength to win the battle.
In the end, they did it … They witnessed Luo Zheng's strength.
As martial artists from the upper realm, these Divine Sea Realm martial artists had seen a lot of things. However, when Luo Zheng's strength was revealed, they were still frightened.
In order to resist Luo Zheng's terrifying strength, one of the Divine Sea Realm martial artists launched a "Xu Earth Realm"!
This was the Xu Earth Realm comprehended from the Law of Earth. It was the most solid realm. That Divine Sea Realm martial artist directly blocked in front of the other five people.
However, such a realm was still easily shattered by Luo Zheng. Moreover, Luo Zheng, a Life and Death Realm martial artist, rushed into a group of Divine Sea Realm martial artists like a tiger in a flock of sheep. This scene was quite shocking …
Most of the geniuses from the Thirteen Palaces could easily win against these Divine Sea Realm martial artists. However, not many people could do what Luo Zheng did. Apart from Luo Zheng, perhaps only Ai Anxin could do it.
After Luo Zheng's battle ended, Ai Anxin's battle also ended at the same time. Ai Anxin seemed to have used some kind of powerful trump card. The cyan stone between her eyebrows had already appeared. She had used the Jade Gate Immortal Combat Technique.
Twenty rounds passed …
The two obtained the best results in the Illusory Space Trial at the same time. However, it was not over yet.
Twenty-one rounds quickly continued …
Twenty-two rounds …
Twenty-three rounds …
Twenty-four rounds …
Because of the previous example, no one was willing to guess where their limits were.
Just like in the One Thought Seal Tower, at first, everyone guessed Ai Anxin's limit. In the end, she rushed to twenty-eight thousand li. Later, they predicted that Luo Zheng would not be able to pass thirty thousand li, but he rushed to thirty-six thousand li.
Now, everyone had learned to be smart. Otherwise, after Luo Zheng or Ai Anxin broke through their estimated range, they would be slapped in the face with actual actions …
However, the Illusory Space was different from the One Thought Seal Tower. These rounds of tests were arranged by people.
Since they were arranged by people, there must be an upper limit.
On the other side of Changsu Island, there were only fifteen groups of martial artists, from the lowest to the highest, corresponding to the eleventh to twenty-fifth rounds of the Illusory Space.
The martial artists in the fifteenth group were the three cages on the high platform!
Therefore, the twenty-fifth round was the ultimate trial …
From the beginning to the end, Rao An had been giving out orders. Although he did not succeed in sniping Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin.
However, everyone also saw the effect of Rao An's suggestion. Naturally, they were willing to let Rao An give out orders. Now, Rao An once again walked onto the high platform of the huge square.
"It seems that to fight against that woman, we can only use the death row prisoners …"
"I heard that these death row prisoners were brought here from the World of Villains by some extraordinary people!"
"If they can defeat those two people in the ultimate trial and obtain their freedom, they will surely fight with their lives on the line, right?"
Under everyone's gazes, Rao An walked towards the tall and sturdy man and asked, "Two people have entered the ultimate trial, a man and a woman. Who will you choose?"
The tall and sturdy man's face revealed an arrogant expression, "How can I choose a woman?"
"I suggest you choose a woman. That man's strength is very strong, unfathomable. At least, I can't see his limit," Rao An said in a low voice. After passing through round after round of tests, Rao An actually did not reach his goal. Especially Luo Zheng, he simply could not see where Luo Zheng's limit was.
"What's his cultivation base?" The tall and sturdy man asked.
"Fifth level of Life and Death realm," Rao An reported truthfully.
The tall and sturdy man coldly glanced at Rao An, "You're insulting my strength!"
In the tall and sturdy man's opinion, fifth level Life and Death realm could be considered a top grade genius of the Thirteen Palaces. However, no matter how talented he was, he was still only at the fifth level of Life and Death realm!

Chapter 1077
This tall and sturdy man didn't expect that his opponent was only at the fifth level of Life and Death realm.
He naturally knew that the martial artists of the thirteen Heavenly Palaces had innate talent and strength that couldn't be measured with ordinary eyes.
And to be able to challenge the twenty-fifth round, the martial artist worthy of him taking action must be one of the top existences in the thirteen Heavenly Palaces.
However, he didn't expect that this guy was only at the fifth level of Life and Death realm.
Rao An shook his head, "Don't look down on that guy. I've carefully observed the first twenty-five rounds. According to my estimation, he's only used thirty percent of his strength."
"Thirty percent of his strength is enough to resist the encirclement of several Divine Sea realm martial artists?" Hearing these words, a trace of surprise appeared on the tall and sturdy man's face.
"I suggest you pick that woman." Rao An's suggestion was very pertinent.
In fact, after twenty-four rounds of battles, Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin were both unimpeded. Perhaps because of Rao An's strategy, the two were troubled to varying degrees, but it still couldn't stop them from advancing.
Similarly, Luo Zheng was constantly advancing. From beginning to end, Luo Zheng only used the longsword in his hand to face the enemy, while Ai Anxin used all kinds of martial skills and cultivation methods. The variety of martial skills and cultivation methods left people dumbfounded.
However, because of this, Luo Zheng's performance was even more unfathomable. Therefore, Rao An didn't have much confidence in Luo Zheng. As for Ai Anxin, although he was powerful, he still had a way to deal with him. Therefore, Rao An had a higher chance of defeating him.
His point of view was very similar to the views of the Palace Masters outside the ancient palace. The Palace Masters of the thirteen Heavenly Palaces, Sunset Realm Lord, and the others also thought the same.
However, the strength of these Palace Masters and Realm Lords was much higher than Rao An's. Moreover, Rao An only relied on his keen sense of smell to make such a judgment. This was already very commendable!
However, the tall and sturdy man didn't take Rao An's advice.
"I choose that man, hehe … a Life and Death realm junior. With such strength, I'm interested." The tall and sturdy man sneered.
Hearing the burly man's judgment, Rao An instinctively frowned. His goal was the secret treasures of the Sifting Yin World. He had no interest in fighting with these geniuses. He would defeat Ai Anxin and free the burly man. Then, he would excavate the secret treasures of the Sifting Yin World and complete his goal step by step.
The most important part was that the burly man had to defeat his opponent. Rao An didn't want to create more problems and provoke the unfathomable Luo Zheng.
Rao An originally wanted to persuade him, but the burly man's expression sunk. "I will naturally honor my promise to you. As for my opponent, I will naturally choose! Back then, even my Lingyan Pavilion almost entered. Could it be that even a junior from the Thirteen Palaces has to cower in fear? Wouldn't that be a joke? "
Hearing this, Rao An sighed in his heart. This fellow was still too arrogant. If he hadn't been too cocky back then, he wouldn't have been imprisoned in the Wicked World and become a prisoner on death row …
But after all, the other party was a key figure in his plan. Rao An couldn't influence this guy's thinking. In the end, he could only follow his wishes and let this tall and sturdy man challenge Luo Zheng.
As for Ai Anxin's opponent …
Rao An looked at the several cages next to him, and a faint smile appeared on his face again.
For these prisoners on death row, this was an opportunity to leave the Wicked World. However, the power to distribute this opportunity was in Rao An's hands. He naturally wouldn't waste this power. If they wanted to challenge Ai Anxin, these prisoners on death row would have to show some sincerity. This "sincerity" might not be as attractive as the Gathering Yin World's secret treasures, but Rao An understood the principle of many little drops make a mickle.
"The ultimate trial … how many opponents will appear?"
Luo Zheng, who was standing on the platform, had a calm light in his eyes. The trial in the dreamland had reached its final stage.
Not far away, Ai Anxin was also quietly waiting for the final round of opponents.
The trial in the Fantasy Space made Ai Anxin a little unhappy. Although she had left the martialists of the Thirteen Palaces in the dust, Luo Zheng was still stubbornly following behind her and entering the final round of the trial with her.
Moreover, after the twentieth round, Luo Zheng's speed of passing the trial became faster and faster. Sometimes, he was even twice as fast as her, and it became more and more difficult for her to deal with him. Naturally, she was unhappy.
What's more, now that everyone had entered the final round at the same time, even if they both passed, it would only be a draw …
She hoped that the opponent in the final round would be stronger. Ai Anxin had this kind of expectation in her heart.
After a short while …
As a faint light condensed on the platform, a person appeared opposite Ai Anxin.
This person's clothes were ragged, and there was a shocking scar on his forehead. Layers of gloomy and cold aura were emitted from his body. When this person stared at him, it was as if he was being stared at by an evil spirit.
"A guy who cultivates the Ghost Dao?" Even if Ai Anxin was extremely confident, when she noticed the aura emitted by this person, her beautiful face changed slightly. This kind of cultivator who cultivates the Ghost Dao was strange, and their attacks were very insidious.
"Jie Jie, good luck, my opponent is a little girl," the cultivator grinded his teeth and grinned.
This time, a total of seven death row prisoners were escorted from the Evil World, but there were only two chances. The burly man chose Luo Zheng, so the cultivator could only choose Ai Anxin. In order to get this opportunity, he had paid a great price. If he obtained his freedom, he would repay Rao An heavily.
Ai Anxin's expression quickly returned to normal. This was the final round of the trial. Her opponent was strong, so Luo Zheng's opponent would certainly not be weak. This was what she wanted.
This Ghost Cultivator was indeed powerful, but it was not impossible for her to defeat him.
What about Luo Zheng's side? Thinking of this, she slightly tilted her head …
At this moment, she saw a burly man come out of the light. The moment she saw the burly man, her eyes suddenly opened wide!
"Brother!" Ai Anxin suddenly cried out in alarm.
The burly man stepped onto the platform. He was originally sizing up his opponent. Rao An was right, it was indeed a little guy at the fifth stage of the Life and Death realm.
The burly man did not underestimate him. To be able to pass the trial round after round and fight until the twenty-fifth round, the strength of their opponents could not be measured by cultivation base. However, the burly man was once a top genius in the Alliance. At that time, he was almost able to ascend to the Lingyan Pavilion. In the past, he had always been the one to challenge people across realms. It was very rare for him to encounter an opponent weaker than him who challenged him!
Even if he was in the Evil World, a great world that was lacking in resources, and his cultivation base had stagnated, he would never fear a Life and Death realm cultivator!
He was sizing up Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng was naturally sizing him up …
The first impression that the burly man gave Luo Zheng was that his foundation was very stable. Moreover, there was a faint arrogance that stood proudly in the world. Generally, people who were full of arrogance had the strength to be proud! 'This guy is very strong,' Luo Zheng faintly gave this evaluation in his heart.
Unexpectedly, at this time, Ai Anxin, who was on the platform next door, suddenly screamed, "Brother!"
Luo Zheng was stunned, and so was the burly man. As for everyone outside the ancient palace, their expressions were different …
The burly man looked at Ai Anxin, and a trace of doubt flashed in his eyes. He indeed had a little sister, but when he was imprisoned in the Evil World more than ten years ago, his little sister was only seven or eight years old. For a moment, he was unable to recognize his little sister.
"Brother, I am Anxin!" Ai Anxin anxiously called out.
The doubt on the burly man's face gradually disappeared. His originally calm eyes gradually blossomed with a light that had not been seen for a long time.
He did not expect that the woman that Rao An had mentioned before was actually his little sister …
He also did not expect that his little sister would grow into a peak existence in the Thirteen Palaces. For a moment, he found it hard to accept.
Outside the ancient palace, the Sunset Realm Lord's face also revealed a trace of doubt. He did not expect such a coincidental thing to happen in this ultimate trial.

Chapter 1078
"This person, who is he?" The Sunset Realm Lord asked.
Hearing the Sunset Realm Lord's question, Yun Luo blinked but didn't speak.
At this time, a Palace Lord said, "I remember. This is the little guy who had a conflict with Empyrean Feng Nu's son during the Dao Fruit Conference!"
That matter hadn't happened for long, only a dozen years. Once this Palace Lord mentioned it, the other Palace Lords also remembered.
"This guy's name should be 'Ai Hu', that peerless genius of the Great Cultivation World. Back then, the Lingyan Pavilion made an exception to accept him, but because of his conflict with Empyrean Feng Nu, he was sent to the Wicked World!" Another Palace Lord also remembered this matter.
"Ai Anxin, Ai Hu … are they really brother and sister?"
Compared to these Palace Lords, the reaction of the Thirteen Palaces martial artists outside the ancient palace seemed to be more intense.
To these palace lords and world overlords, Ai Hu might only be a genius of the younger generation. They didn't have a strong impression of him. It was only a small matter in the past. For characters like the Sunset World Overlord, it was normal that they didn't recognize Ai Hu.
However, this Ai Hu was quite famous among the younger generation. Therefore, the moment this burly man appeared, some martial artists had already recognized him!
"It's Ai Hu! He was actually released from the Wicked World! "
"It's actually Ai Hu who is Luo Zheng's opponent! If he wasn't suppressed by Empyrean Feng Nu back then, Ai Hu might have already reached the Divine Realm! "
"A mere Divine Realm martial artist actually has the nerve to talk about him? At that time, he was only at the Life and Death Realm. Even if he was accepted by the Lingyan Pavilion, his future would be limitless … what a pity! "
In the eyes of many martial artists, Ai Hu was bullied by Empyrean Feng Nu. Therefore, it was easy for him to gain the recognition of lower level martial artists. All kinds of legends about Ai Hu were widely spread. There were rumors that in order to save one of his brothers, he had entered hell and fought with all kinds of evil spirits …
All kinds of legends might be unfamiliar to the core figures of the alliance, but to the lower level martial artists, they were all amazing stories!
"I also didn't expect Ai Anxin to be Ai Hu's sister!"
"These two siblings are indeed monsters. Back then, Ai Hu was so fierce. During the Dao Fruit Conference, he sparred with the arrogant Empyrean's son and humiliated him to the point that he wished he could find a hole to hide in. His sister's talent is no weaker than her brother's …"
"…"
In the dreamland …
Ai Anxin had never thought that she would be able to see her brother here!
She knew that her brother had offended a powerful person and was banished to the Evil World. Moreover, he only had 800 years of life left …
However, eight hundred years was enough for her. She would grow up in this period of time and rescue her brother from the Evil World.
This was a secret in her heart that she had never shared with anyone since she stepped onto the path of martial arts.
Looking at her brother's ragged clothes, Ai Anxin recalled the tall and mighty image of him in her mind when she was young. Tears welled up in her eyes …
"Anxin, you really are Anxin!" . "The smile on Ai Hu's face grew wider and wider. He could already vaguely recognize Ai Anxin's appearance when she was young. To be able to see his sister here, Ai Hu was naturally happy!
Back then, when he was banished to the Evil World, he suffered all kinds of pain and humiliation. There were no rules in the Evil World, and the resources were extremely poor. Surviving there was not an easy task. This time, because of Rao An, he had a chance to obtain freedom. Now, he had met his sister, and Ai Anxin had already grown to such an extent …
Ai Anxin's eyes filled with tears as she nodded. For a moment, she was so excited that she didn't know what to say …
Ai Hu smiled and said, "Good, very good. I didn't expect that the day we siblings reunite would be the day I, Ai Hu, obtain my freedom. Once I defeat this boy, I'll be able to obtain my freedom …"
Hearing her brother's words, Ai Anxin's expression suddenly changed. "You can obtain freedom by defeating Luo Zheng?" She didn't know that there would be such a reward.
Ai Hu nodded and said, "All the death row prisoners participating in the Torch Flame Inheritance have this reward."
Ai Anxin's eyes lit up. She turned to Luo Zheng and said, "Luo Zheng, can you admit defeat?"
… …
This sudden scene also made Luo Zheng somewhat confused. How could an opponent appear out of nowhere and be Ai Anxin's brother? And this woman, who wanted to challenge him in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, wanted him to directly admit defeat. This …
"Don't worry, I'll also admit defeat!" Ai Anxin said seriously, "I can give up the ultimate test. This way, our results will still be the same!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was somewhat speechless. All along, he had never regarded Ai Anxin as his opponent. Participating in the Torch Flame Inheritance was just a test for him.
However, Luo Zheng also had a sister. Ai Anxin could reunite with her brother here, but so far, there was still no news about her sister …
It wasn't that Luo Zheng didn't want to fulfill her wish.
"I beg you." Ai Anxin was so proud before, but now she urgently hoped that Luo Zheng would give her a positive response. "I'll try my best to satisfy any request you have! Any request is fine! I beg you, Luo Zheng! "
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to agree, Ai Hu laughed boldly and said, "Anxin, how can you beg me?"
"But …" Ai Anxin was about to say something.
However, Ai Hu interrupted her again and said proudly, "I, Ai Hu, have always relied on myself. How can I let my little sister ask for my freedom?! What's more, Anxin, don't you have confidence in your brother? Do you think I'm not a match for this guy? "
Ai Hu and Ai Anxin weren't from any aristocratic families. The Ai Clan they belonged to was only a fourth-grade aristocratic family in a large border world. Compared to the seventh-grade, eighth-grade, ninth-grade, and tenth-grade aristocratic families in the universe, it was really too ordinary.
It was under such a foundation that Ai Hu was able to swiftly become famous and even be chosen by the Lingyan Pavilion. He had always relied on himself. As for the charity of others, he had always been disdainful of it. How could he tolerate his own sister pleading for him?
Luo Zheng really intended to directly admit defeat …
However, since Ai Hu had said so, he couldn't say anything. The brother and sister seemed to have the same temperament and were as proud as anything. He couldn't help but shake his head. Encountering such a dramatic scene, he also felt somewhat helpless.
After Ai Hu finished speaking, he turned to Luo Zheng and smiled faintly. "You're Luo Zheng, right?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
"With the cultivation of fifth level Life and Death realm, you were able to reach this round and become my opponent. You're indeed not bad. So, don't listen to my sister's words. Bring out your full strength!" Ai Hu said sternly. "If I can't defeat an opponent whose cultivation is far below mine, then I'll give up my freedom!"
Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes in his heart. He couldn't help but mutter to himself about the brother and sister's characters.
Opposite him, Ai Anxin felt very conflicted. She also knew her brother's character. The possibility of persuading her brother in this kind of situation was too small. Therefore, this battle was very worrying for her.
"Make your move." Ai Hu's expression also became more and more serious. Although Luo Zheng was only at fifth level Life and Death realm, he still couldn't defeat Ai Hu.
Since his opponent had said so, Luo Zheng lightly held his Thunder Wind Divine Sword horizontally and made a "please" gesture.
Soon after, a sword light burst out from the tip of the sword. The lightning and wind blades wrapped around a figure and rushed towards Ai Hu.
Outside the ancient palace, the discussions of many cultivators about Ai Hu didn't stop. Instead, it became even more intense …
"Why didn't Luo Zheng admit defeat?!"
"I saw that Luo Zheng had the intention to admit defeat, but Ai Hu didn't want to!"
"Hmph! What kind of person is Ai Hu? Does he need Luo Zheng to admit defeat? What a joke! "

Chapter 1079
The discussions in the outside world couldn't reach the Illusory Space.
Ai Anxin originally planned to watch the battle between her brother and Luo Zheng. However, her opponent didn't give her the time to do so …
"Little girl, don't watch anymore. Hehe …" While the cultivator was laughing maliciously, streams of gloomy ghost energy suddenly surged out from his body. The ghost energy transformed into fist-sized skulls and shot toward Ai Anxin.
This battle concerned her brother's freedom. How could Ai Anxin be in the mood to fight with her opponent?
However, she didn't take the attack head-on. Her body fluttered and her long hair swirled in the air. Her beautiful eyes were still fixed on Luo Zheng …
Facing Luo Zheng's sword move, Ai Hu laughed boldly. His body flickered with golden light as he grabbed the wind blade with his bare hands!
"Cough …"
The sharp wind blade was instantly torn apart by Ai Hu. As for the lightning that struck Ai Hu, it didn't affect him in the slightest. Then, facing Luo Zheng's Serene Spirit Shadow, Ai Hu smashed it with a punch.
After receiving Luo Zheng's first move, Ai Hu took a step forward and dashed toward Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. No matter what happened before, once Luo Zheng entered battle mode, he would focus on the battle in front of him. He wouldn't think too much about other things.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
In the blink of an eye, more than ten Serene Spirit Shadows shot out from Luo Zheng's hands!
Seeing the surging Serene Spirit Shadows, Ai Hu roared. The golden light on his body became even brighter. His entire person was like a golden man. The light was dazzling!
Luo Zheng's Serene Spirit Shadows were fierce, but as soon as they got close to Ai Hu, they were instantly shattered. They couldn't hurt him at all …
"Ai Hu's mastery of the Law of Metal has already reached a decent level. I'm afraid it will be very troublesome for Luo Zheng …" a Palace Master commented.
But another Palace Lord shook his head and said, "Not necessarily. Since this Luo Zheng was able to cross the fire pool, he must have some unknown secrets. In the first 24 rounds of the trial, he only relied on the Thunderstorm Divine Sword. Ai Hu might be able to force him to use his trump card, but the result is hard to say."
The evaluations of these Palace Masters were naturally much more pertinent compared to the warriors of the Thirteen Palaces.
As for the warriors from the Thirteen Palaces, they kept flattering Ai Hu …
After tearing apart Luo Zheng's Nether God Shadows one by one, Ai Hu strode forward, rapidly closing in on Luo Zheng. At the same time, the Law of Time suddenly erupted from Ai Hu's body. In the blink of an eye, time on the entire platform slowed down!
However, Luo Zheng only needed a thought to undo the bindings of Ai Hu's Law of Time.
The Law of Time was the opposite of the Law of Time. It was only that they were not affected by each other. Ai Hu's move was just a test, but it was almost inevitable that a warrior who could stand in this position would cultivate the Law of Time.
"Nether God Shadows …"
After undoing the Law of Time, Luo Zheng's figure retreated once again. The Nether God Shadows continued to entangle him. Each of the Nether God Shadows used a different fist technique and surrounded him from different directions.
"It's useless," Ai Hu said with a faint smile. This time, he did not even put up any defense. His body was like a raging bull, directly crashing into the Nether God Shadows. The Nether God Shadows that came close to him were directly smashed by him …
With the force of his impact, even a mountain range would be easily destroyed by him. "I know your strength is definitely more than this. If you want to defeat me, then let's see what your trump card is like!"
Facing Ai Hu, who was getting closer and closer, Luo Zheng's legs were like the wind, constantly moving horizontally along the platform, releasing the Nether God Shadows again!
Seeing that Luo Zheng was still the same as before, Ai Hu shook his head. "I already told you that this move is useless. Since you insist on using the sword in your hand to defeat me, then I'll … remove your weapon!"
After saying that, Ai Hu's hands suddenly crossed together, and a round golden ring suddenly appeared in front of him!
The moment the golden ring appeared, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a strong suction force coming from his Thunder and Wind Nether God Sword, almost making it fly out of his hand!
How strong was Luo Zheng's strength? That golden ring almost made him unable to wield his sword. This suction force also made Luo Zheng's heart jump.
Ai Hu's face showed a trace of surprise when he found that his golden ring did not pull Luo Zheng's sword over. However, he shouted again, and the golden ring suddenly expanded three times in size, and the suction force became several times stronger!
This time, Luo Zheng's face was expressionless. The suction force was indeed strong, but was it purely a competition of strength? Who could be his opponent?
With the influx of the power of the dragon scale, the longsword in his hand became even more stable. Not to mention the sword hilt in Luo Zheng's hand, even the sword itself did not sway in the slightest!
"Huh?"
Ai Hu's face showed a stunned expression …
He mainly cultivated the Metal Elemental Law, and using the Metal Elemental Law to display the "Everlasting Golden Ring" could almost disarm some magical weapons. Under such a suction force, Luo Zheng's sword was not pulled away, which made him very surprised! This guy's strength seemed to be extraordinary?
Was strength his trump card?
Thinking of this, Ai Hu crossed his hands again, and the golden ring expanded again. When the Metal Elemental Law was cultivated to an extremely high level, it was also an extremely domineering existence!

Who was strong and who was weak depended on the person who used these Laws and these Cultivation Methods!
Feeling the inexplicable suction force, Luo Zheng smiled slightly, and the power of the dragon scale quickly flowed out. How could he allow his sword to be taken away by his opponent?
The two of them confronted each other like this for a while, and they were almost evenly matched. While Ai Hu was puzzled, he grabbed the golden ring in his hand, and with a light turn, rays of golden light shot toward Luo Zheng. This battle was also in a stalemate …
As for Ai Anxin's side, she had already activated her movement technique to the extreme. While dodging her opponent's attacks, she was also paying attention to her brother's battle.
"Little girl, be more serious. Otherwise, you might lose if you're not careful," the ghost cultivator laughed cruelly.

This Ai Hu was forcing Luo Zheng to fight him in close combat. Seeing this scene, a faint smile appeared on his face. What he was most proud of was his body!
Nourished by the Metal Attribute Law, he cultivated the Metal Spirit Body, which was comparable to a Divine Artifact. Coupled with the explosive power of his Body Refining Technique, who could be his opponent in close combat?
"Finally not wasting any more time?" Ai Hu smiled faintly.
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. He didn't speak, but the strength in his body had been accumulating for a long time. He faced Ai Hu, who was shining with golden light!
"Boom!"
Ai Hu, who was originally extremely confident in his physical body, didn't retreat at all. He also returned a punch. There was no technique or fanciness. At this moment, the two of them were competing with the strength of their physical bodies. They were both using the strength they were proud of!

Chapter 1080
If an ordinary Divine Sea Realm expert was so close to Ai Hu, they would definitely be smashed to pieces by this punch.

For this brat to be able to display such power, his physical body must be even stronger than mine! " Ai Hu's face revealed a look of surprise and uncertainty.

Chapter 1081
In the higher realms, a transcendent divine might was an extremely rare existence.
Only an Empyrean could create a transcendent divine might!
In this world, the compilation of cultivation methods all relied on the Laws of the universe to create …
For instance, visualization techniques were created by visualizing all things in the world. Or, one could rely on one's deep comprehension of the Laws to create a new cultivation method.
But, normal martial arts only involved the lower Laws of the Laws. As for transcendent divine mights, they involved the higher Laws, in other words, the Laws of Cause and Effect.
Once something involved the Laws of Cause and Effect, it was inevitably an extraordinary treasure. Chapter 1083

On the contrary, Luo Zheng looked calm. After all, he was about to face his sixth minor tribulation, so he wasn't as anxious as Ai Anxin.
However, at this moment …
Ai Anxin suddenly realized that a faint shadow had appeared above her head. The shadow became more and more solid, and her Lotus Flower had appeared!
"They're here!" Ai Anxin was pleasantly surprised.
At the same time, a faint black dot began to appear in the sky. The black dot grew larger and larger, forming a black hole that was several feet wide. Purple lightning circled around the black hole, and mysterious divine runes faintly floated around the lightning. These divine runes weren't drawn by humans, and they seemed to be naturally formed!
"Heavenly Firmament Holy Lightning Tribulation!" A Palace Master's eyes suddenly shot out a beam of light.
"Heh heh, this girl … is no longer surprising. With such a cultivation base matching her strength, the heavens won't be blind!"
The heavens wouldn't be blind, and it wasn't a curse on Ai Anxin. After all, a cultivator's cultivation was originally against the heavens. The stronger the cultivator's strength, the more powerful the Heavenly Tribulation would be. The heavens naturally wouldn't mismatch the Heavenly Tribulation!
As for the other cultivators of the Thirteen Palaces, when they saw the Heavenly Firmament Holy Lightning Tribulation descending, they were all frightened. Some of them couldn't help but worry for Ai Anxin. If she was caught in such a powerful Heavenly Tribulation, wouldn't there be nothing left of her?
"Rumble …"
Although the tenth minor Heavenly Tribulation was powerful, Ai Anxin's strength was also extraordinary!
This woman's methods seemed to be limitless. From sealing the tower with a single thought to entering the dreamlike space, there was almost no repetition in the methods she used. Moreover, every method she used would be a trump card for any other martial artist!
After Ai Anxin passed the Heavenly Firmament Holy Lightning Tribulation, what came next was the "Invisible Fire", and then the "Cleansing Nightmare" …
These minor Heavenly Tribulations were all peak existences of the minor Heavenly Tribulations. Even the Palace Masters who were accustomed to the might of Heavenly Tribulations felt their hearts palpitate when they saw these Heavenly Tribulations.
In a minor heavenly tribulation, there would be several kinds of tribulations. The difficulty and power of these tribulations would also increase gradually. For example, the heavenly tribulations that Ai Anxin experienced would be the strongest wave of heavenly tribulations if placed on any other martial artist. But, she had to face them one by one, each one more terrifying than the last!
At this time, Ai Anxin had already erupted with all her strength. Her entire body danced in the sky as she resisted the unceasing Heavenly Tribulations with all her strength!
After a long time, the Heavenly Tribulations finally dissipated.
Ai Anxin's face was also filled with exhaustion. She slowly returned to the ground and sat down cross-legged. Although she was exhausted, her face still revealed a smile. After all, she had successfully passed the tenth minor Heavenly Tribulation. This was also the strongest Heavenly Tribulation she had ever faced.

Generally speaking, after passing a Heavenly Tribulation, the lotus flower phantom would become fainter and fainter. Eventually, the entire lotus flower would completely disappear …
However, not only did the lotus flower above Ai Anxin's head not disappear, but it was also becoming more and more solid!
Seeing this, even the Sunset Realm Lord's expression became very serious. "This is …"
Yun Luo glanced indifferently and said, "She might be opening the Mahayana Lotus Flower."

Even the experienced and knowledgeable Palace Masters found it difficult to remain calm in the face of such a scene.
People who could open the Mahayana Lotus Flower were already extremely rare. Moreover, Ai Anxin had just broken through to the Divine Ocean Realm, but she had already opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower. The significance of this was self-evident. When such a person matured, she would probably be an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao. How could they not be shocked?
The cultivators of the Thirteen Palaces had yet to recover from their shock …
To be able to participate in the Torch Inheritance, they were undoubtedly the elites of the Thirteen Palaces. The Thirteen Palaces gathered the elites of countless holy lands in the entire Alliance. Every one of them was not lacking in talent! At the very least, they were all cultivators who had opened the Five Leaf Lotus Flower.
However, the Mahayana Lotus Flower was at a level that they did not dare to imagine! However, the monstrous Ai Anxin was about to open the Mahayana Lotus Flower in front of everyone …
As the Mahayana Lotus Flower spun continuously, an even larger lotus flower appeared above it. This lotus flower was even larger than the one below it, and it had more lotus leaves.
There were nine leaves in the Hinayana Lotus Flower, and 29 leaves in the Mahayana Lotus Flower!
When the Mahayana Lotus Flower appeared, the first lotus leaf began to unfurl, followed by the second, third, fourth, and fifth leaves …
Ai Anxin raised her head and looked at the lotus leaves that kept opening. A faint smile appeared on her tired face.
With five leaves and the nine leaves of the Hinayana Lotus Flower, she had opened a total of fourteen leaves!
In the end, the Mahayana Lotus Flower stopped at five leaves. Not long after, it disappeared faintly.
The Palace Masters looked at Ai Anxin with complicated expressions. Although the Xin Huo inheritance was to inspect the juniors of the thirteen palaces, the things that Ai Anxin displayed were something that even the Palace Masters were envious of!
After her Mahayana Lotus Flower disappeared, Ai Anxin glanced at Luo Zheng and smiled faintly.
In the One Thought Sealed Tower, because Ai Anxin came out early, she was left far behind by Luo Zheng.
In the Illusory Space's trial, Luo Zheng lost to her brother. Although she won a city and had one more trial than Luo Zheng, her brother was too strong. Strictly speaking, she did not leave Luo Zheng too far behind!
This time, how could Luo Zheng compete with her?
Luo Zheng was not a fool. He easily understood the smile in Ai Anxin's eyes. However, his expression was still very calm, and his eyes were full of calmness.
He was concerned about his sixth minor heavenly tribulation. Why was it not here yet?

Chapter 1084
The Ten Direction Calamity Bringer Pill couldn't have lost its effect, right?

After all, Luo Zheng was only a sixth-stage Heavenly Tribulation cultivator. There was still a huge gap between him and a tenth-stage minor Heavenly Tribulation cultivator!

What Ai Anxin didn't know was that Luo Zheng hadn't bloomed twenty-six leaves during the fifth minor heavenly tribulation. He had only broken through the Life and Death Realm for the first time. "This is too much …" Ai Anxin inexplicably muttered such a sentence in a low voice.

Chapter 1085
The various Palace Lords looked at the Sunset Realm Lord in confusion. One of them asked, "What's not good about it?"

"Bamboo Snake? How could such a thing appear? "
"Now that you mention it, it does seem somewhat similar to the legends!"
"The Bamboo Snake of Annihilation … How could a Life and Death realm martial artist encounter such a legendary tribulation …"
These Palace Lords didn't recognize the origins of the Bamboo Snake, but after hearing the Sunset Realm Lord's words, they instantly understood.
The Hundred Thousand Great Realms had a clearer understanding of providence than the Lower Realms.
When martial artists cultivated in defiance of the heavens and ascended to the Divine Path, there was the saying of defying the heavens and changing one's fate.
Defying the heavens was naturally cultivating in defiance of the heavens. Starting from the Life and Death Realm, every step forward was defying the heavens, leaving a footprint and welcoming a heavenly tribulation!
Changing one's fate was changing one's own destiny! Although there was a predestined fate, every martial artist exhausted all means to jump out of this predestined fate!
In fact, everyone was constantly trying to escape from the predestined fate …
Mortals might face a bucket of water they couldn't lift, an unbreakable city, or a cup of poisoned wine bestowed by a monarch that they couldn't refuse.
On the other hand, martial artists might face a mountain they couldn't push down, an opponent they couldn't defeat, a heavenly tribulation that they couldn't resist even if they exhausted all their strength …
This was also the reason why the martial artists in the Upper Realm were so interested in the shadow of the lotus flower. It was because all the lives in the universe were included in the Heavenly Axiom, from the stars in the sky to the insects and ants. The number of lotus flowers represented the possibility of jumping out of the Heavenly Axiom!
Jumping out of the Heavenly Dao meant that the fate set by the Heavenly Dao was the end. Only then could one be considered true freedom. If one couldn't jump out of the Heavenly Dao, then one would be continuously reincarnated in one evolution cycle after another. After the next evolution cycle, one might be a speck of dust or a Celestial Venerate, but in the end, one wouldn't be able to jump out of the rut.
Therefore, the more lotuses one bloomed, the stronger one's luck would be. This was also a publicly recognized deduction in the Upper Realm. It was not a symbol of talent, but in many cases, it was more important than talent!
The Sunset Realm Lord looked at Luo Zheng, the Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snake, and the thirty-five leaf Mahayana Lotus above Luo Zheng's head. In the end, he murmured, "What kind of fate does Luo Zheng possess for the heavens to send down the Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snake?"
Upon hearing the Sunset Realm Lord's puzzlement, Yun Luo took the initiative to say, "Luo Zheng has two Life Providences. Strictly speaking, his War of the Era Life Providence is actually a fake Life Providence. His fate is not controlled by others …"

Yun Luo pursed her lips and smiled. "Let's wait until he transcends his tribulation!" The Lingyan Pavilion was indeed a good place for Luo Zheng to go, but Luo Zheng had inherited too many important things.
"But can Luo Zheng really transcend this Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snake tribulation?" The Sunset Realm Lord had a worried look on his face.
The Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snake was different from ordinary Heavenly Tribulations. These snakes could devour providence, and besides talent, the most important thing for a martialist was providence!
Those with strong Karmic Luck were able to make the best of the situation amidst boundless danger, and they were even able to transform danger into fortune. Everywhere they went, they would be able to move smoothly. On the other hand, those with weak Karmic Luck would face killing intent with every step they took, and it could be said that every step they took was difficult to take!
If Luo Zheng's providence was devoured by the Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snake, it would probably be troublesome for Luo Zheng.
At this moment, Luo Zheng looked at the slowly crawling golden snakes in the sky and a bad premonition arose in his heart.
These golden snakes did not have much power and were far inferior to the various lightning tribulations, fire tribulations, and nightmare tribulations. However, after seeing these golden snakes, Luo Zheng felt a sense of danger rise in his heart. It was as if these golden snakes could snatch away the most important thing to him!
When these golden snakes crawled slowly in the air to a certain point, they actually opened their mouths and seemed to be devouring something in the air?
What were they eating?
Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of confusion.
"It's providence." Xun's voice transmitted over. "These are Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snakes. They can directly devour a martialist's providence. These Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snakes are a very special kind of creature that can feed on providence!"
"Devour my providence?" Luo Zheng was shocked. The most important thing for Luo Zheng to be able to walk step by step to where he was now was not his cultivation method or treasures. It was his willpower, talent, and providence. These three were the most important and could not be missing! Not only for Luo Zheng, but any martialist who wanted to climb to the peak had to be like this!
Ever since he left the Eastern Territory, Luo Zheng's providence had always been very strong. His providence could not only affect himself, but also the people around him. Therefore, anyone who was close to Luo Zheng had always had good providence. For example, once Xi Youqin entered the Extreme Purple Realm, she became a disciple of the Indefinite Tower. This was also influenced by providence!
After that, Ning Yudie, Su Lingyun, and even Hua Tianming and the others all had their own fortuitous encounters. They were more or less influenced by Luo Zheng's powerful providence!
It was not easy to change a person's providence.
Luo Zheng had once participated in the test of the Wheel of Providence in the Heaven Mist Immortal Tomb. He had been cursed by the Saintess of the Divine Kingdom, and his providence had been affected. When a martialist who had been cursed shook the die, he would get the worst result no matter what. It looked as if the curse had really changed Luo Zheng's providence.
In fact, Daoist Heaven Mist had changed the Wheel of Providence, not Luo Zheng. According to the rules set by Daoist Heaven Mist, as long as one was cursed, no matter how strong one's providence was, it would be impossible to get a good result from shaking the die …
Right now, these Fate-Destroying Yellow Snakes were devouring Luo Zheng's providence bite by bite. Every time a strand of providence was devoured, it could not be recovered!
"Why would such a strange Heavenly Tribulation descend? That providence can't be seen or touched. It's actually being devoured by these golden snakes! "
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. He suddenly jumped up and went straight for the Fate-Destroying Yellow Snakes.
No matter who it was, once they discovered that something was devouring their providence, they would not sit idly by!
"Whoosh!"
Unexpectedly, after Luo Zheng rushed up, he directly passed through the golden snakes. These golden snakes were illusory existences!
The other martialists, including Ai Anxin, did not understand the scene before them. From their point of view, these golden snakes that descended from the sky were not very powerful. They were naturally far inferior to the Heavenly Tribulation that Ai Anxin had encountered!
The Sunset Realmlord and the others had worried expressions on their faces.
When a martialist underwent a Heavenly Tribulation, they could not rely on the help of others. They could only rely on their own strength to deal with it.
Originally, the Sunset Realm Lord thought that Luo Zheng had a way to deal with it. However, when he saw this scene, he understood that Luo Zheng did not seem to have any way to deal with these Fate-Destroying Yellow Snakes …
The Fate-Destroying Yellow Snakes did not pay any attention to Luo Zheng. However, after Luo Zheng moved, these Fate-Destroying Yellow Snakes kept twisting in the air. They seemed to have changed directions and continued to devour Luo Zheng's providence.

However, after the Fate-Destroying Yellow Snakes' tribulation descended, they could only watch these snakes devour their providence without any means to resist!"

Although Luo Zheng did not come from the Heavenly Palace that he belonged to, everyone cherished talents. Seeing such a person's providence end, he could not help but sigh in his heart.

Chapter 1086
These Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes were still circling in the air, constantly opening their mouths and gnawing in the void. There were even soft sounds coming from them.
They did not cause any harm to Luo Zheng's body, but Luo Zheng's expression grew darker and darker …
"Crunch, crunch …"
The soft gnawing sounds were like silkworms gnawing on mulberry leaves, constantly wearing down Luo Zheng's luck.
The importance of luck to him was self-evident, but these Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes were completely illusory existences.
It was as if they did not exist in this world, and Luo Zheng could do nothing about them now!
Even Qinglong, who was in Luo Zheng's mind, faintly sighed. Today's Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes tribulation was because Luo Zheng's luck was too strong that it attracted such a tribulation.

Qinglong could not think of a solution, and neither could Xun. This was a heavenly tribulation that could not be resisted …

It was not that Luo Zheng had chosen to give up. Since he had embarked on this path, Luo Zheng had never thought of giving up!
No matter how powerful the heavenly tribulation, no matter how strong the opponent, Luo Zheng had never yielded.
The heavenly tribulation was not a death tribulation, otherwise, all the warriors in the universe would have died long ago. Since the Heavenly Dao had given such a difficult tribulation, there must be a deeper meaning. Rather than being anxious in vain, it was better to maintain his state of mind and wait for changes.
No matter how bad his luck was, it would not tell him to go and die, right? He comforted himself in his heart.

Those Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes were still gnawing slowly in the air, slowly twisting their bodies, as if they were eating Luo Zheng's luck as exquisite food, swallowing it bite by bite …
"This Luo Zheng is actually able to remain calm and collected. That's really commendable," one of the Palace Masters commented.
"Perhaps, he already has a way to break it?" Another Palace Master asked.
"Haha … Let's not talk about Luo Zheng. Even the Celestial Sovereigns of the various Great Realms would not be able to think of a way to break it. I'm afraid this thing shouldn't exist in the universe! "The Palace Master continued.
Luo Zheng's state of mind was like an ancient well. There were no ups and downs, only calmness!
This Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity of the Calamity to him.
So what if he lost the protection of providence?
In many cases, luck could indeed create many miracles. However, Luo Zheng did not rely solely on luck to get to where he was today.
Without luck, his future path might be even more difficult. This meant that he would encounter even more difficult challenges, but so what?
"So what!"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng opened his eyes again. There was a deep look in his eyes. In the face of this irresistible calamity, Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts sublimated once again.
This was a contest between gains and losses. This was also the realization of knowing that it was impossible to do something!
Ai Anxin stood directly across from Luo Zheng. When she met Luo Zheng's eyes and saw Luo Zheng's eyes, her heart suddenly contracted and began to beat wildly!
She did not understand the look in Luo Zheng's eyes, but in those deep eyes, there was an extremely complicated emotion!
What was going on with Luo Zheng?
A huge mystery appeared in Ai Anxin's heart. She did not know what Luo Zheng had experienced in this short period of time.
It wasn't just Ai Anxin. When the martialists of the 13 Palaces inadvertently met Luo Zheng's eyes, their hearts began to beat wildly …
Clap, clap, clap … At this moment, the Sunset Realmlord actually clapped his hands. "This kid is amazing. He clearly knows that he can't fight against the Fate-Destroying Bamboo Snake, yet he actually used it to train his state of mind. This kid's heart of martial arts has probably improved greatly!"
Few martialists could remain calm under such circumstances. Not only that, but they could also use this upheaval to train themselves. The Sunset Realmlord also applauded Luo Zheng's actions …
Every martialist had a different personality and state of mind. For example, the Sunset Realmlord's nature was straightforward. When he met someone he admired, he would be full of praise. Yun Luo's personality was more introverted. When he saw Luo Zheng's performance, he only smiled slightly. However, no matter who it was, he still admired Luo Zheng's performance very much.

Whoosh …
A gentle breeze suddenly swept up!

When this cool breeze swept through Ai Anxin's hair, a puzzled look appeared on her face.
"The wind is blowing. But where is the wind coming from?" She thought to herself.
