The other Palace Masters also nodded. "Indeed, there's nothing strange behind Luo Zheng …"
However, this wind was real. It was not the power of Laws that had been transformed into true essence. It was as if there was something extremely powerful that was about to be born, causing the airflow to become chaotic.
This breeze became more and more fierce. The strength of the wind became stronger and stronger. Gradually, it turned from a breeze into a strong gale!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
This gust of strong wind circled around everyone, making a whistling sound.
In fact, the martialists present could use their own comprehension of the Power of Laws to create a hurricane that was ten thousand times stronger than this gale!
However, no one knew where this wind came from or how it was created. This was very strange!
Just as everyone was puzzled, a martialist suddenly pointed at Luo Zheng's back and said, "Look! What is that! "
Luo Zheng's back was still empty. There was nothing behind him. However, it was precisely because of this that it appeared even more strange.
This strong gale that constantly whistled and blew at every corner. It was inevitable that it would bring up traces of dust. Everyone's vision was slightly blurred. However, this blurriness did not affect the vision of many martialists.
However, when the strong gale blew at Luo Zheng's back, everyone discovered something strange.
This gale seemed to be unable to pass through Luo Zheng's back. This was because under the sweeping of the wind, which carried traces of dust, a faint shadow was outlined on Luo Zheng's back. It was the shadow of a giant!
To be exact, it could not be said to be a shadow. It was just that the wind was unable to pass through this colossus. It was a vague outline of nothingness!
Coincidentally, the Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes were gnawing along this "outline". It looked as if the Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes were gnawing on a non-existent "giant"!
Hearing everyone's voices, Luo Zheng's expression was calm. However, when he looked back, a ray of light shot out from his eyes.
The outline of a giant? What was this?
The Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes … Since they were gnawing on his providence, then this giant's outline was his providence?
Why was his providence like this?
Although Luo Zheng could maintain a calm heart, it was inevitable that doubts would arise when he saw this strange scene.
More importantly, when did this thing follow behind him?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1087
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The wind was still howling.
The crowd stared at this strange scene. No one knew what it meant.
'Is this my luck?'
'It's said that luck is innate. Does this mean that this giant's silhouette has been with me since I was born?'
'It accompanied me through the Eastern Territory, the Central Territory, the Sea God Continent, the Kingdom of God, and ascended …'
'Why does my luck have such a shape?'
Luo Zheng's heart was filled with doubts. Meanwhile, those Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were still conscientiously gnawing on the giant's body in the 'air', or rather, surrounding it!
Even the Sunset Realm Lord had a puzzled look on his face.
In the Upper Realm, the research on fate was quite thorough. Although this thing could not be seen or touched, everyone truly understood that fate was something that truly existed. If one were to use a certain method, it could also affect one's own fate to a certain extent …
However, luck was just a description. How could it have a shape?
That being said, the Luck Destroying Yellow Serpent was a legendary existence. It sounded ridiculous to say that it could devour luck.
Since this happened to Luo Zheng, everything that was happening now was not surprising.
Just as everyone was feeling strange, they saw the giant's silhouette start to move …
The wind could not blow through this area, so it could only outline the giant's silhouette. In everyone's eyes, it was not really a giant that started to move. They only saw a silhouette changing shape and changing the path of the wind, causing everyone to have the illusion that the giant was moving.
They saw the giant's silhouette suddenly raise a hand and pat itself lightly!
A golden Late-luck Bamboo Snake was crushed into pieces!
"This …"
The Sunset Realmlord was speechless when he saw this scene.
The other Palace Lords had the same expression. To them, this scene was beyond the scope of understanding. They could not understand what was happening.
After the first Late-luck Bamboo Snake was crushed, the giant's silhouette was still "waving" its hand. Its other hand patted itself again. On the second day, the Late-luck Bamboo Snake was crushed into pieces again, turning into golden powder …
Then, the third, the fourth, the fifth …
The Palace Lords watched with their eyes wide open. They held their breaths and no one spoke.
No one expected this to happen. Everyone should wait until they finished watching before speaking …
Just like that, the Luck Destroyer Bamboo Snakes were smashed to pieces with a single slap.
There were not many Late-luck Bamboo Snakes in the first place. There were only eight of them. After a few slaps, seven of them had been crushed. As for the last one, based on its silhouette, it seemed to have burrowed into the body of the 'giant'. The giant reached out his hand and plucked the Late-luck Bamboo Snake out of its body. The giant's hand lightly and crushed the Late-luck Bamboo Snake!
"…"
Luo Zheng's face was also filled with astonishment.
In comparison, as the person, he was more than anyone else …
Moreover, the giant's, the giant's silhouette, the giant's silhouette, Luo Zheng's, Luo, Luo
He seemed to have seen this giant's figure before! But as for who it was, Luo Zheng could not remember.
After the eight Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were crushed, they turned into golden powder and scattered down. Finally, they surrounded Luo Zheng and disappeared …
The Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were also things that could not be touched. The giant's silhouette was an existence that did not exist.
After the Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were destroyed, the wind gradually began to stop. As the wind began to fade, the giant's silhouette could no longer be seen. It gradually disappeared before everyone's eyes.
Even after it disappeared, the scene was still very quiet.
No one knew what had happened. They only confirmed one thing. Luo Zheng seemed to have survived the Late-luck Bamboo Snakes.
No one knew why Luo Zheng was able to survive. Luo Zheng himself could not give a reasonable explanation …
"After those Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were crushed, they seemed to have been absorbed by Luo Zheng. I wonder if there were any benefits?" someone asked.
The more powerful something was, the more good things it had on it. This was common knowledge in the world of martial artists.
The Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were such a strange existence. Would absorbing them strengthen one's luck?
In fact, no one could answer the Palace Master's question. The Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were originally legendary creatures. Most of the people present had never heard of them. How would they know what the Late-luck Bamboo Snakes were used for?
"This … Does this count as surviving the first Heavenly Tribulation?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, and his expression became calmer.
However, what happened next left everyone speechless ….
"Crackle …"
After the Late-luck Bamboo Snakes disappeared, Luo Zheng immediately faced a Heavenly Tribulation.
This Heavenly Tribulation was the most common type of Heavenly Tribulation. However, the Heavenly Tribulation Luo Zheng faced was not an existence that ordinary Heavenly Tribulation could compare to.
As soon as the purple-black Heavenly Tribulation Lightning appeared, it directly struck down from the sky!
"Boom!"
This Heavenly Tribulation Lightning, which extended a great distance, turned into a huge dragon and rushed down towards Luo Zheng.
In the end, it landed right in front of Luo Zheng, smashing a huge hole in the ground in front of him.
In other words, this Heavenly Tribulation Lightning missed …
Seeing this scene, everyone's face revealed a strange expression.
This Heavenly Tribulation Lightning could miss? They didn't seem to have heard of this before.
"Boom …"
Suddenly, another purple-black Heavenly Tribulation Lightning came down from the sky. This Heavenly Tribulation Lightning was not far from Luo Zheng's right side, but it still did not hit Luo Zheng.
"Boom, boom …"
Bolts of lightning came down from the sky. The power contained in each bolt of lightning could probably destroy an ordinary Divine Sea Realm warrior.
However, it did not cause the slightest harm to Luo Zheng. The reason was very simple. These bolts of lightning did not hit Luo Zheng. Those bolts of lightning all fell outside the range of Luo Zheng's body.
Not long after, a huge circular hole formed in front of Luo Zheng. Those bolts of lightning still fell down relentlessly, but they still did not hit Luo Zheng.
"Fate! Luo Zheng's fate should have been strengthened! "
"It should be because of that Bamboo Snake of Exterminating Fate. But I don't know if this heaven-defying fate is permanent or will it last for a period of time …"
"How can this kind of providence be permanent? If it can last forever, then it's too terrifying! "
The Palace Masters continued to discuss among themselves …
In their view, the Heavenly Tribulation was the most accurate thing in the entire universe. How could the Heavenly Dao tolerate its own mistakes?
However, Luo Zheng's current fate was so powerful that it made people's hair stand on end. Even the Heavenly Tribulation formed by those bolts of lightning could not hit Luo Zheng. This was just too strange!
At this time, Luo Zheng's face was also filled with surprise. He also did not know what had happened to him. It seemed that his luck had suddenly become extremely good?
Thinking of this, Yun Luo suddenly floated gently, closing the distance between her and Luo Zheng by several feet. Then, she stretched out her hand toward Luo Zheng, who was in the middle of the Heavenly Tribulation. An invisible wave of energy went straight toward Luo Zheng.
Yun Luo used one-thousandth of her strength in this palm. If Luo Zheng sat where he was, he would not be able to avoid it!
But at this time, a strange scene occurred. Before the palm floated in front of Luo Zheng, a complicated wave of energy suddenly appeared in the air. An extremely powerful spatial storm suddenly broke out, directly annihilating Yun Luo's palm.
When the other Palace Masters saw this, their expressions became more and more strange …
That spatial storm was definitely not released by Luo Zheng, nor was it released by anyone else. It was a naturally formed spatial storm!
Although the Orange Sea Realm was a broken realm, after so many years of operation by the Alliance, it was very stable. The possibility of a spatial storm appearing in such a stable space was very small!

Chapter 1088
The spatial storm 'coincidentally' appeared in the path of Yun Luo's palm strike at Luo Zheng at this moment, and coincidentally cancelled out Yun Luo's palm strike … …
This coincidence was so coincidental that it made one's hair stand on end!
The warriors of the Thirteen Palaces didn't see this scene in front of them. They were still puzzled. Why couldn't the Heavenly Tribulation blast Luo Zheng? Why did the Palace Lord of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace strike towards Luo Zheng? Why would a spatial storm appear out of nowhere in this stable space?
They couldn't understand it at all …
Ai Anxin, on the other hand, seemed to have understood something …
She lightly tapped the ground with the tip of her foot, and cracks appeared on the ground beneath her feet. She lightly raised her finger, and a small stone was pulled up from the ground by her.
Then she grabbed the stone and threw it towards Luo Zheng.
The stone wasn't big, but it flew at an extremely fast speed. In the blink of an eye, it was in front of Luo Zheng.
"Boom!"
At the same time, a bolt of lightning 'coincidentally' struck beside Luo Zheng, and directly shattered the stone into pieces …
Yun Luo smiled and said, "Luo Zheng's luck seems to be a bit heaven-defying. It's probably wishful thinking to want to cause any harm to Luo Zheng now!"
Seeing this incomprehensible coincidence, the Sunset Realm Lord smiled helplessly. Luo Zheng's absorption of the Luck Destroying Yellow Snakes did seem to have increased his luck …
In the Upper Realm, there were some great methods that could temporarily change one's luck, but the degree of increase was very small. For example, the 'Luck Prayer' of the Esoteric Sect, and some divine weapons that could increase one's luck, and so on. However, in the eyes of the Sunset Realm Lord and Yun Luo, these things didn't increase one's luck so much as they gave one a psychological hint. The real effect was still to be discussed.
But the scene in front of them was different …
After Luo Zheng absorbed the Luck Destroying Yellow Snakes, the change in his luck was a fact that was set in stone!
In this state, no matter what Luo Zheng did, it would be as if God was helping him.
Take the space storm that resisted Yun Luo's palm strike as an example, the probability of it "suddenly" appearing on Changsu Island was very low, perhaps only one in a trillion. However, it happened to appear in front of Luo Zheng and when Luo Zheng launched his palm strike. The probability was so small that it was hard to calculate. It was not a problem that could be ignored …
But it just so happened that it appeared "coincidentally"!
"It's a bit of a waste." The Sunset Realm Master shook his head. Unfortunately, he couldn't touch those Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes and didn't know how to store them. If Luo Zheng could keep one or two Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes, he could probably do a lot of things with them!
Luo Zheng naturally understood what had happened, but he was still transcending the tribulation …
Bolts of lightning struck down, and all of them were crooked. What kind of tribulation was this?
Depressed, Luo Zheng did not move too much. It was better to wait and see. But was his luck really that strong now?
Luo Zheng's Tribulation Transcendence lasted for an entire hour …
An hour later, the sixth minor heavenly tribulation finally dissipated. The Mahayana Lotus Flower spun a few times, and the shadow slowly faded away.
To Luo Zheng's surprise, the Mahayana Lotus Flower did not seem to open its last leaf.
Just now, he was thinking that since his luck was so strong now, would he be able to make the Mahayana Lotus Flower bloom perfectly? Now, Luo Zheng realized that he was thinking too much. Perhaps his luck was not enough to affect the Mahayana Lotus Flower at all.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to get up, the Mahayana Lotus Flower that had disappeared appeared again above Luo Zheng's head, still spinning slowly.
Everyone, including Luo Zheng, was stunned. What was going on now?
As the Heavenly Might in the sky became denser and denser, everyone immediately understood that Luo Zheng's tribulation was not over. The seventh minor tribulation had arrived …
"Luo Zheng is transcending tribulations consecutively?"
"Actually, there's nothing strange about it. When one's cultivation reaches a certain level, the Heavenly Dao will sense it, and there will be no shortage of tribulations!"
"I've seen a guy transcending tribulations consecutively before. That guy deliberately suppressed his cultivation for too long. Therefore, as soon as he reached the Life and Death realm, he was hit by two minor tribulations consecutively. However, that guy wasn't prepared enough and was struck by lightning, dying from serious injuries …"
"This Luo Zheng won't die, right?"
After Luo Zheng transcended the sixth minor tribulation, the linen-clothed old man was ready to remove the seal. He never expected that Luo Zheng would continue to transcend the tribulation.
It was just a matter of a few more hours, but for them, it was like a mortal's breath. Everyone was very patient.
In the seventh minor tribulation, the Luck Destroying Luo Snake tribulation didn't appear …
However, all kinds of powerful tribulations weren't rare. The might of the seventh minor tribulation was already comparable to many people's ninth or even tenth minor tribulation.
However, the key point was that because Luo Zheng's heaven-defying luck was temporarily on his body, those tribulations seemed to have lost their aim and didn't cause too much trouble for Luo Zheng!
However, at this time, Luo Zheng was somewhat unhappy. Heavenly tribulation was a calamity for martial artists, but at the same time, it was also a test. Martial artists accepted the punishment of heavenly tribulation, and at the same time, underwent the baptism of heavenly tribulation. Only in this way could one complete layer after layer of transformation in the Life and Death realm.
The problem was that those tribulations had lost their aim and smashed around Luo Zheng. What kind of situation was this? Could this still be called a heavenly tribulation?
Just as Luo Zheng's thoughts were slightly stirred …
"Rumble …"
Terrifying purple lightning, like long whips from hell, ruthlessly lashed at Luo Zheng's body …
Under this terrifying luck, Luo Zheng seemed to be able to command the heavens and earth. This was something that Luo Zheng had never imagined. Therefore, he was caught off guard and directly struck down by these lightning bolts.
Luo Zheng flipped over from the ground and immediately jumped up. Since these heavenly tribulations appeared one after another, he would naturally endure them. Moreover, with his luck, he could completely control the effects of these heavenly tribulations on himself.
Even if it wasn't because of the sudden increase in his luck, Luo Zheng was very confident that he could pass through the minor tribulation.
After all, with his divine tool body, resisting the heavenly tribulation wasn't difficult at all.
As Luo Zheng continued to receive the baptism of heavenly tribulations, the seventh minor tribulation finally passed after an hour. However, the Mahayana Lotus Flower still hadn't fully bloomed, faintly disappearing from everyone's sight.
"It can't be … there's more, right?" Luo Zheng thought in his heart.
Perhaps it was because the eighth minor heavenly tribulation would definitely appear, or perhaps it was because of Luo Zheng's extraordinary luck that he could get whatever he wanted.
As the lotus flower's phantom emerged, the eighth minor tribulation descended …
Luo Zheng sighed, revealing a bitter smile on his face. Today was really strange. He couldn't tell whether his luck was super good or super bad.
"Three consecutive heavenly tribulations …"
"From the fifth layer of Life and Death realm to the eighth layer of Life and Death realm in less than two hours. This guy …"
Many warriors were speechless at this time.
Although it was rare for a warrior to cross two heavenly tribulations in a row, it wasn't that rare. However, a warrior crossing three heavenly tribulations in a row was extremely rare.
However, after an hour …
"Four consecutive minor heavenly tribulations …"
"After passing through them, his cultivation base will rise to the ninth layer of Life and Death realm!"
"He can't be planning to break through to the Divine Sea realm like this, right?"
"Don't forget, this Luo Zheng swallowed the Ten Direction Calamity Guide Pill. Could it be that these heavenly tribulations were attracted by the Ten Direction Calamity Guide Pill?"
"There's no end to this!"
Luo Zheng did not know whether to laugh or cry when he saw the power of the Heavenly Tribulation gathering in the sky again. There was really no end to it. Although he was eager to improve his cultivation level and even wanted to see how his Chaotic Qi would look like after he opened his Deity Sea, he did not know whether to laugh or cry.
But it shouldn't be this fast, right?

Chapter 1089
Consecutive tribulations were rare to begin with.
Even Luo Zheng hadn't expected that he would trigger five minor heavenly tribulations in a row this time.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's body had advanced once more. Adding to the fact that his luck had reached an unimaginable level, the tribulations weren't that difficult to deal with.
Under the baptism of the heavenly tribulations, Luo Zheng's cultivation base rapidly rose.
Luo Zheng could now be considered a tenth layer Life and Death realm martial artist.
Strictly speaking, Life and Death realm didn't have such a level, because starting from the sixth layer of Life and Death realm, True Yuan would continuously liquefy. And the later the liquefy, the more perfect the Deity Sea would be.
After reaching the tenth layer of Life and Death realm, True Yuan would rapidly liquefy and form a flawless Deity Sea, making one a Deity Sea martial artist.
However, Luo Zheng's current situation was somewhat special.
For example, Ai Anxin, Xue Muyang, and the others' Dantians were naturally evolving into Deity Seas.
After reaching the tenth layer of Life and Death realm, Luo Zheng also tried to investigate his Dantian. He wanted to see if the Primal Chaos Qi had successfully evolved into Deity Sea.
In the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, his master had deduced that the Primal Chaos Qi was the essence of the universe. Logically speaking, it could also evolve into a Deity Sea. Moreover, unlike the True Essence Sea, the Deity Sea created by the Primal Chaos Qi would be a real world!
Deity Sea martial artists could open Deity Seas, and when they reached the Extreme God Realm, they could gradually nurture a world within their body. From there, they could nurture all kinds of creatures, and even give birth to countries, societies, and brand new cultures.
However, this world and the real world had an iron-clad rule. Any creature nurtured in the inner world couldn't leave that world. The moment it left, it would be suppressed by the rules of the universe and disintegrate into True Yuan!
This iron-clad rule was also the strongest law of causality in the universe! So far, no one had been able to break this rule.
There were many legends and stories circulating in the great world. One of them was about an Empyrean who fell in love with a woman nurtured in his inner world!
In the end, the Empyrean racked his brains to find a way to take that woman out of his inner world. For this reason, he spent tens of thousands of years traveling the universe in search of a solution. Finally, the Empyrean seemed to have found a way!
The Empyrean paid an enormous price to allow the woman to live outside for a day, just a single day!
After a day, the woman's that day, under the influence of the law................ com.............
For the time being, this could be considered a beautiful and tragic story. In the upper realms, it was widely spread, especially amongst the human race. Many martial artists understood that even an Empyrean couldn't defy these rules, so it was naturally impossible for ordinary martial artists to defy them!
However, in the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, according to his master's deduction, the world cultivated with the Primal Chaos Aura was no different from the living beings and materials in this universe!
In other words, if Luo Zheng successfully opened the Divine Sea and cultivated all sorts of living beings and materials, he could bring them out of his inner world …
The reason was very simple. All the living beings and matter in the entire universe were formed from Chaos, and the living beings that Luo Zheng had formed within the Chaotic Ocean were naturally no different from the living beings in the universe. So, they wouldn't be affected by the supreme Karma Law.
However, this was only his master's inference in the Primal Chaos Secret Technique. After all, up until now, no one had cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique. Although his master was an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao, it was inevitable that there would be a large error in his inference.
After passing through the tenth heavenly tribulation, Luo Zheng began to use his inner vision to observe the movements in his inner world. Under this observation, he discovered that the chaos energy was becoming increasingly viscous, but there were no signs of it liquefying.
When Luo Zheng first stepped into the Life and Death boundary, his true essence had already begun to liquefy. He didn't expect that even after he stepped into the Divine Sea boundary, the chaos energy still showed no signs of liquefying.
Unfortunately, in this universe, Luo Zheng was the only one who cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique. No one could answer the question he encountered. Even his master, who created the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, couldn't give him a precise answer. He could only take one step at a time.
After Luo Zheng passed the tribulation, the Torch Flame inheritance came to an end.
The linen-clothed old man waved his hand and removed the seal that enveloped the area.
However, the moment the old man removed the seal, everyone suddenly discovered that the surrounding scenery seemed a little strange.
Ai Anxin raised his head and indifferently looked at the sky. He said, "So many … clouds."
Above everyone's heads, there were white clouds shrouding them.
"What's going on?"
"There's no rain in the Orange Ocean Realm all year round. How can there be clouds and mist?"
"There's something wrong with these clouds and mist …"
Soon, someone discovered something strange. Within the clouds and mist, there were faint sword shadows constantly evolving.
The Sunset Realm Lord stared at the clouds and his gaze suddenly focused. He took a step forward and disappeared on the spot.
Aside from the Sunset Realm Lord, the other Palace Masters seemed to have thought of something and instantly disappeared.
In the next instant, the Sunset Realm Lord arrived in front of the huge rock at the front of Changsu Island.
In front of the huge rock, the two ragged Divine Realm warriors were staring blankly at the clouds and mist in the sky. Their eyes flickered with an indescribable joy. They were actually constantly comprehending something from the clouds and mist.
This huge cloud and mist enveloped the entire Changsu Island.
However, as it continued to float, numerous faint figures appeared within the clouds. All of the figures only had one action, and that was a simple stab.
However, it was this sword that contained boundless power, giving people an unfathomable feeling.
"The Bright God's Sword … This is the sword that the Bright Sword Heavenly Sovereign spent his entire life comprehending. Although it's only one sword, only such a sword has the qualifications to hurt a True God …"
"It's a pity that I'm not a sword cultivator …"
"However, such a rare opportunity naturally can't be missed. I can't comprehend the sword intent within, but I can transform the concept into my own and integrate it into my spear …"
The Sunset Realm Lord murmured. When he thought of this, he sat down cross-legged like the other two Ultimate Divine Stage warriors and floated in the air. He raised his head and looked at the thick layer of clouds, trying to comprehend the meaning within!
The Sunset Realm Lord wouldn't let go of this opportunity, and the others were naturally the same.
The Palace Masters, including Yun Luo herself and the cloth-clothed old man, all began to comprehend the ideas within the clouds.
As for Luo Zheng and the others, they couldn't compare to the Palace Masters. The Realm Lords usually teleported here, but their speed wasn't slow either. After rushing over and seeing this scene, how could they not understand what was happening?
Someone must have touched upon the profound mysteries left behind by the Bright Sword Heavenly Sovereign!
At this moment, no one cared about who exactly it was and how exactly it was that had touched this mystery. In the face of such a great opportunity, no matter who it was, no one was willing to let it go. They all raised their heads and began to comprehend the Sword Intent contained within the figures. As for how much Sword Intent they could comprehend, it would depend on their luck.
When Luo Zheng saw those figures, his mouth opened wide and his eyes were slightly dazed.
This sword was very similar to the "Formless" that Luo Zheng had comprehended. It could be said that it was very similar …
However, in terms of essence, there was still a huge difference!
It was the same sword, and it also walked the path of "the Great Dao is the simplest". However, the Bright Sword Heavenly Sovereign's sword was even sharper than Luo Zheng's Formless.
Luo Zheng's Formless was unbreakable because it didn't have a fixed pattern. However, the Bright Sword Heavenly Sovereign's sword was unbreakable because it had a fixed pattern …
It was the same path, but a completely different method. The results were as different as heaven and earth!
It had to be said that the Bright Sword Heavenly Sovereign was superior. This Heavenly Sovereign with the title of "Sword", in terms of comprehension of sword moves, he was an ancestor level figure!
However, this didn't mean that Luo Zheng's comprehension was useless. On the contrary, because Luo Zheng had comprehended Formless, he could easily understand the wonders of the Bright Sword Heavenly Sovereign's sword …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1090
On Changsu Island, all the martial artists were seizing every moment to comprehend the sword intent in the clouds.
At this time, sword cultivators had a natural advantage. This was because they had been accompanied by swords their entire lives. Naturally, their comprehension speed was much faster than other martial artists'.
However, Intent Realm was originally a path that led to the same destination. Other saber and spear cultivators certainly wouldn't let go of this opportunity. They concentrated on comprehending every sword in the sword cloud.
Luo Zheng stood in place, looking up at the sky. After being stunned for a while, a faint smile suddenly appeared on his face.
"I understand why it's so difficult to control my sword intent after it's been tempered …"
"Because this sword … is a sword that can break a cauldron and sink a ship. This sword is a sure-kill sword. When I use this sword, I don't need to care about anything, including my own life!"
"Only such an extreme sword move requires such an extreme sword intent …"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly felt enlightened!
Normally, when a martial artist comprehended sword intent or cultivated a cultivation technique, the first stage was to control it. Only by controlling one's own strength could they release this strength. Therefore, martial artists cultivated step by step. They constantly controlled their own cultivation technique step by step. The more proficient they were at controlling it, the stronger the strength they could release.
However, Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword had directly skipped the first step. Therefore, when this sword intent was displayed, there was no need to control it. This was because this sword had already abandoned everything …
"This Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword is known as a Heavenly Venerate who can fight with a True God. In other words, if one wants to injure a True God, they can only do so by abandoning everything?" Luo Zheng muttered.
If Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword stood beside Luo Zhen and heard Luo Zheng say such words, he might feel gratified. A young man who had just stepped into the Divine Sea Realm was able to infer this idea by relying on this move. This was enough to make Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword have a whole new level of respect for him.
Luo Zheng had already been baptized by Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword's sword intent. Facing this thick sword cloud, his comprehension speed was much faster than other martial artists.
Changsu Island was in complete silence …
During this period of time when the sword cloud was floating, no one was willing to waste even a breath of time.
Two hours …
Twenty hours …
Two days …
Three days passed, and the sword clouds gradually thinned, revealing a light purple sky. This was the magnificent color of the Orange Sea Realm's blue sky reflected by the sea water.
It wasn't until the sword cloud had completely dissipated that some of the cultivators revealed an expression of longing for more.
Any martial artist who carefully comprehended under this sword cloud would gain a lot!
"Sigh, it's a pity that I'm not a sword cultivator. Just by sensing this Sword Intent, I feel like I've gained a lot. You sword cultivators are really lucky this time!" A warrior sighed.
As for the other warrior, he still stood there quietly with a longsword in his hand. His mind was still unable to calm down. Just as that warrior had said, his own comprehension was indeed quite deep!
"Ten percent. I have only comprehended ten percent of the Yi Shen Sword. However, even if it is only ten percent, it will be of great benefit to my swordsmanship!" The warrior said with a smile.
The amount of comprehension depended on one's perception, as well as the depth of one's understanding of swordsmanship and Sword Intent. The deeper one's understanding, the more one would naturally comprehend!
The Palace Masters all revealed faint smiles. Their cultivation was far stronger than the 13 Palaces' martial artists, so they had naturally gained a lot from encountering this sword cloud by chance.
"However, who was the one who created this sword cloud? Why were the words on the stone wall all destroyed? "a Palace Master asked in bewilderment.
One had to know that the words of Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword had been left here for countless years. Countless sword cultivators had prostrated themselves here to comprehend the mysteries within. However, for millions of years, no one had been able to decipher the mysteries within. Why did the mysteries naturally reveal themselves during the Torch Flame Inheritance?
While many Palace Masters were puzzled, Yun Luo's gaze quietly fell on Luo Zheng. Before participating in the Torch Flame Inheritance, Luo Zheng had said that he seemed to have seen some white sword shadows. Then, he had been delayed for several hours before entering the Torch Flame Inheritance. Was this sword cloud because of Luo Zheng?
At this moment, the linen-clothed old man's gaze also fell on Luo Zheng. "Luo Zheng, before the Torch Flame Inheritance, were you meditating here?"
Originally, these Palace Masters had forgotten this small detail. After the Torch Flame Inheritance opened, many martial artists had passed through the Clear Growth Pool and begun meditating on the Law Stone Inscriptions. However, Luo Zheng had been late by several hours?
When the linen-clothed old man said this, Ai Anxin also remembered …
Seeing everyone looking at him suspiciously, Luo Zheng revealed a helpless expression. Although he had absorbed those white sword shadows, this sword cloud really had nothing to do with him!
While everyone was puzzled, the two ragged Divine Reaching Realm martial artists not too far away suddenly emitted two extremely powerful auras. Two swords appeared in their hands!
Although these two martial artists were at the Divine Reaching Realm, their talent was still considered average. They couldn't be mentioned in the same breath as the martial artists of the Thirteen Palaces. Therefore, the weapons in their hands were very ordinary. They were two third-grade swords. The weapons of the vast majority of martial artists in the Thirteen Palaces were far superior to these two.
They had stayed here for tens of thousands of years, especially the martial artist surnamed Chen. The long sword in his hand had experienced tens of thousands of years, and its blade was already covered in cracks.
Those who practiced the sword often cherished their swords …
After tens of thousands of years, even a Divine Artifact would inevitably show signs of rust. This martial artist surnamed Chen would polish his sword every once in a while, but as he did so, cracks began to appear on it. This was a broken sword that had already decayed!
But such a broken sword attracted everyone's attention. They could feel that this martial artist surnamed Chen's sword was emitting an extremely powerful aura!
Then, the martial artist surnamed Chen thrust his sword towards the boulder …
"Bright God's Single Sword!"
Everyone had been comprehending for a long time. This action and aura were incomparably familiar to them. Someone immediately shouted out.
"This old man … he comprehended at least 30% of Bright God's Single Sword!" A Palace Master shouted.
These Palace Masters relied on their own cultivation and talent to comprehend a lot, but most of them only comprehended 10% to 20%.
How could this Ultimate God Realm warrior comprehend thirty percent of it?
Some Palace Masters were unconvinced. This Divine Reaching Realm martial artist was far inferior to them in terms of talent and cultivation. How could he comprehend more spiritual objects than them?
They didn't consider that this old man surnamed Chen had been meditating here for sixty thousand years. He had thrown away sixty thousand years of his lifespan here. He had used his entire life to comprehend the hidden concepts within Heavenly Sovereign Bright Sword's words!
After spending so many years comprehending, the martial artist surnamed Chen and the martial artist surnamed Xu had comprehended far more than those Palace Masters and even the Sunset Realm Master!
Sou –
A sharp sound burst out from the tip of the martial artist surnamed Chen's sword!
Although this sword only had 30% of the charm of Heavenly Sovereign Bright Sword's sword, compared to the might of a Divine Reaching Realm martial artist, it was already formidable to a terrifying degree.
The boulder was directly pierced through by this sword, leaving behind only a finger-thin crack …
After a while, this boulder that was even larger than a mountain range suddenly collapsed. The entire boulder turned into palm-sized rubble that began to tumble and collapse around the boulder.
Seeing this, the martial artist surnamed Chen's eyes became moist. Tears flowed down his face …
In fact, after staying here for several thousand years, he already knew that there was no hope for him in this lifetime.
However, like the vast majority of martial artists who stayed behind to comprehend, even though they knew they were in despair, they could only despair to the end. This was because there was no turning back!
The vast majority of people didn't persist. A very small number of people were driven away because the Orange Sea Realm was requisitioned. In the end, only the martial artists surnamed Chen and Xu were left. The two of them begged to stay here.

Chapter 1091
When they first saw those sword clouds, they didn't believe that a miracle could really happen.
It wasn't that they didn't believe in miracles. In fact, they believed in miracles more than anyone else, which was why they had stayed here for tens of thousands of years. It could also be said that they were stupider than most people … but time had eroded their determination, making them unable to believe that a miracle was happening in front of them!
Even if they had comprehended 30% of the Graceful Soul Sword, it would be difficult for these two martial artists surnamed Chen and Xu to achieve much in the future.
After all, they had stayed here for too many years and wasted too much time. They had forgotten to cultivate their inner worlds, didn't consolidate their cultivation, and gave up too many things.
However, by relying on their own comprehension, these two were enough to become powerful characters in some tenth grade Holy Lands …
In fact, several years later, the slightly younger martial artist surnamed Xu would become an Elder of some large Sect, and the martial artist surnamed Chen would become a Sword Grandmaster. In the Holy Lands of several Great Worlds, he would preach and teach the younger generation of martial artists about his own comprehension of the Graceful Soul Sword.
Ai Anxin curled her lips. She tilted her head slightly and looked at Luo Zheng, asking, "How much have you comprehended?"
Hearing this question, Luo Zheng shook his head, "I haven't comprehended much …"
Ai Anxin said, "I should have comprehended about 15%. This Graceful Soul Sword is at odds with my state of mind, so comprehending it is a bit difficult for me …"
Her words sounded modest, but in fact, she was more or less proud. After all, besides a few Palace Masters, most people had only comprehended 10%, or even 5%. Ai Anxin was able to comprehend 15%. Amongst the younger generation of martial artists, she was likely the one who had comprehended the most.
Of course, this was on the premise that Luo Zheng was eliminated.
Luo Zheng had almost completely comprehended this sword!
Before this, Luo Zheng had swallowed those white sword shadows and tempered his own Sword Intent. This Graceful Soul Sword was almost tailor-made for Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng might not be able to completely comprehend this Graceful Soul Sword. The most important thing was that several years ago, when Luo Zheng was still a Xiantian life form, he had already comprehended the Great Dao to its simplest form! The Graceful Soul Sword in front of them was extremely compatible with the Sword Intent that Luo Zheng had comprehended!
It was because of all these reasons added together that Luo Zheng was so proud of comprehending this sword. Only by combining it with the uncontrollable Sword Intent could he truly display this sword!
Although Luo Zheng had comprehended the Graceful Soul Sword, Luo Zheng's Luo Zheng't's power.
After all, a figure like Heavenly Venerate Bright Sword had probably cultivated the Bright God Sword for millions of years. Furthermore, the gap between Luo Zheng's current cultivation and strength was simply indescribable. Although he could use this sword, it did not mean that he could use it perfectly.
Even so, Luo Zheng was satisfied. While traveling in the Upper Realm, the more trump cards Luo Zheng kept, the better …

After passing the Torch Flame Inheritance, the warriors began to choose to go out and train, or return to their families to deal with some important matters.
Ai Anxin still lived across from Luo Zheng, waiting for her brother to come back.
Her brother had defeated Luo Zheng and regained his freedom, but he couldn't go back to the Misty Cloud Palace with her. It would take some time for Ai Hu to cross worlds and find her in the Misty Cloud Palace.
During this time, she was naturally immersed in joy. It wouldn't be long before she could reunite with her brother.
As for Luo Zheng ….
The Torch Flame Inheritance had indeed brought Ai Anxin a strong sense of frustration.
But this guy had lost to her brother in the end. This was the only thing that Ai Anxin could comfort herself with.
During these days in Cloud Mist Palace, Luo Zheng had been constantly observing the movements in his Inner World. The flow of the Chaotic Qi was originally very slow, but now it was getting slower and slower. However, it still hadn't completely liquefied. He observed it several times every day, but couldn't see any signs of the Chaotic Qi turning into a sea.
In addition, Luo Zheng recalled the Sword of Graceful God in the sword cloud ….
To be honest, Luo Zheng really wanted to use it once. It was said that the warriors who came back from the Torch Flame Inheritance had experienced an astonishing increase in strength because they had comprehended the concept of the Sword of Graceful God!
The revelation of the Heavenly Venerate Graceful Sword's secret was rapidly spreading in the various Great Realms of the Alliance.
Some warriors who had persisted in front of the boulder for a long time later gave up and left the Orange Sea Realm. Some of these warriors left on their own accord, while others were driven away.
Now that they heard about this, they all cried bitterly. There were many warriors who persisted in front of the boulder, but only two of them really persevered until the end.
After learning this sword, Luo Zheng couldn't help but feel an itch in his heart because he had nowhere to use it ….
On this day, after inquiring about the whereabouts of Cloud Mist Palace, he came to a large hall in the depths of Cloud Mist Palace.
There was also a dreamlike space here. This dreamlike space wasn't a place for warriors to fight, but a place for training.
Because it was late at night, there weren't any warriors using this dreamlike space ….
Luo Zheng quietly entered and saw a plaque hanging on the side of the hall. On the plaque were the words "Sword Testing Hall".
Although it was called the Sword Testing Hall, this place wasn't just used to test swords. It was also used to test sabers, spears, and fists.
With his previous experience, Luo Zheng quickly opened this dreamlike space. In the blink of an eye, the surrounding scenery changed. He stood in a boundless space, and in front of him, a massive mountain range appeared!
This was a mountain range whose peak couldn't be seen, whose bottom couldn't be seen ….
The entire mountain range flashed with a gold and silver light. It was unknown how large it was.
Luo Zheng had never seen such a massive mountain range before. This unimaginable existence could only exist in dreamlike spaces. After all, the things in this space were all created by divine runes. They weren't real existences.
And on this massive mountain range, there were all sorts of marks.
Sword marks, saber marks, arrow marks, spear marks, fist marks ….
It was hard to tell how many of them there were.
Generations of warriors in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace had come here to test their swords. These marks were accumulated over countless years.
"Looks like I came to the right place!"
Luo Zheng revealed a smile. He was originally itching to test his swords, but in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace, he couldn't just randomly pick out a target to test his swords.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng pulled out the Thunder Wind Divine Sword. With a light flick of the sword, he pointed the tip of the sword at the massive mountain range!
At this moment, Luo Zheng's aura suddenly changed. His eyes bloomed with a sharp light, as if he could cut through mountains and chisel stones with just his gaze.
"Sword of the Bright Divine Sword, unable to accumulate power …."
"Raising the hand is the sword. Once the sword is unleashed, there is no turning back …."
"Without accumulating power, without hesitation …. Just like the Taishang Unruffled, this mentality is faintly tainted with a trace of divinity …."
Legend had it that only by unruffled could one become a True God. When the Bright Sword Heavenly Master unleashed this sword, he had also unruffled, but only for a moment.
Luo Zheng felt that he had grasped something wonderful, but this thing was like a slippery fish, quietly slipping away from his thoughts.
Luo Zheng, who was in front of him, only wanted to test his swords, so he didn't care too much. Many of the epiphanies of warriors were completed in an instant, but some epiphanies often bore fruit before they bloomed. Every warrior would experience this situation many times in their life. Even if the thought of slipping away was very important to them, they might still have a chance to appear again in the future.
Without any foreshadowing, without any hesitation, the Sword Essence, which had just flashed a faint light, burst out along with Luo Zheng's long sword ….
The constantly swallowing Sword Essence was still not under Luo Zheng's control, but Luo Zheng didn't need to control this Sword Essence anymore. He just followed his own comprehension and thrust his sword toward the boundless mountain range.

Chapter 1092
That day, Old Chen had comprehended 30% of the Yi Shen Sword. The power that erupted from it even shocked many Palace Masters.
The palace lords who were close to the sector lord level wouldn't be afraid of Old Chen's sword. However, Old Chen was an extreme god realm with no talent, yet he was able to explode with such power. The palace lords couldn't help but feel jealous.
Luo Zheng's sword was almost the complete version of God Yi's sword.
In terms of sword techniques, Luo Zheng didn't dare to call himself a master. After all, it was a sword technique created by an Empyrean level character. He didn't have the qualifications to comment too much.
However, based on his understanding of the Yi Shen Sword Empyrean, he was able to unleash 90% of the sword technique …
Of course, it wasn't 90% of the power. If it was 90% of the power, not to mention the Misty Cloud Palace, even the great world would be cut in two!
That faint sword intent shot out at an extremely fast speed. At first, it didn't seem that shocking. However, after this sword intent entered the boundless gold and silver mountain, it disappeared.
Luo Zheng, who was floating in the air, had a calm gaze. However, there was a hint of confusion in his eyes.
This sword, just from its power, didn't seem to be very powerful?
Or could it be that he hadn't completely comprehended the Yi Shen Sword?
He didn't expect this sword to be able to shake the heavens and earth. However, if it just entered the mountain and disappeared, it would be letting down Luo Zheng's expectations. After all, it was a legendary sword technique that could injure a true god …
His understanding of the Yi Shen Sword wasn't deep enough? Or could it be that this Yi Shen Sword needed to be emotionless to be unleashed?
Thinking about it, Luo Zheng still couldn't figure out the crux of the matter …
Perhaps, his state of mind hadn't reached that level yet!
When Luo Zheng had comprehended the state of Emotionless, it was only a technique of the state of mind.
As for the state of Emotionless, it required another state of mind. However, Luo Zheng hadn't found such a mental cultivation method …
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that he had underestimated the state of mind.
The Realm of Unruffled Emotions wasn't something that Luo Zheng could grasp at his current stage. Even if he reached the Sector Lord level, he would probably only be able to touch the threshold.
Why did Yun Luo sever her own soul and create a million clones?
Clones created through this method would have incomplete emotions. They would reincarnate into the mortal world with incomplete emotions, experience life and death, and see life. Then, these clones would be recalled to comprehend the memories within.
In this way, she could achieve the goal of "severing her emotions". This was related to the cultivation method that Yun Luo was going to cultivate in the future. In the eyes of others, Yun Luo might be a beautiful girl who had the backing of a Celestial Master. However, with the comprehension of severing her emotions, she would embark on a different path.
But how could it be so easy for Luo Zheng to comprehend the Realm of Indifference so easily?
However, Luo Zheng wasn't too disappointed. The power of Yi God's Sword was lower than what he had expected, but it didn't mean that it was useless. The most important thing was that this sword didn't need to be stored up, so it was quite powerful when used against enemies.
After exiting the Illusory Space, Luo Zheng faintly glanced at the three words "Sword Trial Hall" and quietly left.
However, what Luo Zheng didn't know was that the moment he left, the entire Illusory Space suddenly reverberated with a loud rumbling sound!
This gold and silver giant mountain was transformed from an eighth-grade divine rune. Then, it was fixed by 60,000 auxiliary divine runes. It was an imitation of the legendary Gold and Silver Demon Spring Mountain. Whether it was the height or width of this giant mountain, it was beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
If Luo Zheng was placed at the foot of the mountain and allowed to climb up at his fastest speed, it would probably take him a year and a half to climb it. If he was placed at the foot of the mountain, he would probably be able to fill several large worlds …
It was also because the Illusory Space was boundless that it could use divine runes to create such an exaggerated giant mountain. Therefore, when Luo Zheng entered the Illusory Space, he couldn't see the peak or the bottom of the mountain. It was also because it was unimaginably large.
The loud rumbling sound lasted for a few hours.
Luo Zheng entered the Illusory Space in the middle of the night, and the loud rumbling sound continued until the sun rose and the sky was just beginning to brighten.
The warriors of Misty Cloud Palace were quite diligent.
For any warrior, entering the Heavenly Palace was enough to be proud of among the younger generation.
However, after entering the Heavenly Palace, the warriors who gathered the geniuses of various large worlds found that their former pride was simply a fart.
Early in the morning, the warrior named Gao Han carried his maple leaf spear and entered the Sword Trial Hall. He used to be the absolute pride of his family. Ever since he stepped onto the path of martial arts, he was surrounded by envious gazes.
He had smoothly joined an eighth-rank holy land, but because of an unexpected competition, he had been chosen by another tenth-rank holy land. He had been 'kidnapped' to that tenth-rank holy land and had become the disciple of the holy land's lord. After that, his master had spent a lot of effort to send him to the Misty Cloud Palace.
Gao Han thought that with his talent, he was destined to call the wind and summon the rain in Misty Cloud Palace.
After arriving, he felt like he was directly kicked off a cliff. He used to be proud that he had a lotus flower and a lotus leaf, which was a symbol of transcending the Heavenly Dao. However, in the Misty Cloud Palace, there were many who had a lotus flower, and he was simply an insignificant existence. He didn't even meet the minimum requirements of the Torch Flame Inheritance …
However, geniuses weren't just arrogant. After adjusting his mentality, Gao Han forced himself to face the difficulties. He believed that one day, he would be able to reclaim the glory that originally belonged to him here.
After a long period of seclusion, Gao Han rushed to the Sword Trial Hall. He wanted to test his newly comprehended thirteenth move of the "Tyrant Blood Spear", "Tyrant Blood of the Heavens". The power of this move was extremely fierce and domineering, so the effect should be good …
But when Gao Han entered the Illusory Space, he heard a continuous rumbling sound in his ears.
"Thunder?" Gao Han was stunned. How could there be thunder in the Illusory Space?
This made Gao Han very puzzled …
But while he was puzzled, Gao Han suddenly found that the Gold Silver Demonic Spring Mountain, which was so huge that he couldn't see the top and bottom of it, seemed to be a little strange.
In the middle of the Gold Silver Demonic Spring Mountain, there was a slender crack, and this crack extended from top to bottom, all the way to the limit of Gao Han's vision!
Seeing this scene, Gao Han's face revealed a look of surprise.
What was going on?
Was there someone testing the sword before? Since the rumbling sound was still echoing, the person who tested the sword should not have gone far. But when he came in, it was empty, and he didn't meet any other cultivators along the way …
Gao Han naturally didn't know that there was someone who tested the sword before, but the person who tested the sword had been gone for several hours.
Out of curiosity, he flew all the way down the crack, but after flying down for a long time, he still couldn't find the end of the crack. In the end, he could only choose to fly up. This time, it took twice as long to fly down, but he still couldn't see the end.
During this process, Gao Han's heart became more and more shocked. Who in the world caused this injury?
At their cultivation level, it wasn't difficult for them to destroy a mountain range with a wave of their hand. Gao Han could do it himself.
The problem was that the Gold Silver Demonic Spring Mountain in the dreamland wasn't an ordinary mountain. If Gao Han used his full strength, he could leave a mark of a hundred miles on the mountain. If he used his full strength, he could leave a mark of two or three hundred miles on the mountain.
In the Misty Cloud Palace, where geniuses came in large numbers, this wasn't even worth mentioning.
A year ago, he happened to meet Xue Muyang, the number one of the Swallow Cloud Hall. Although Xue Muyang was only at the ninth level of the Life and Death Realm, he left a mark of a thousand miles on the Gold Silver Demonic Spring Mountain with a casual swing of his sword.
But today, he didn't know how many miles the mark was. What was going on?
Thinking of this, Gao Han left the Illusory Space and walked to a corner of the Sword Trial Hall. There was a tall curtain hanging in the corner of the Sword Trial Hall.
This screen recorded the attack that the Gold Silver Demonic Spring Mountain bore.
Gao Han didn't know the exact calculation method. It should be based on a martial artist who had just entered the Divine Reflection Realm as a basis to measure the strength of other martial artists. Perhaps this method wasn't very accurate, but it could be used as a reference.
For example, a martial artist like Gao Han could achieve three times the result with a single attack. In other words, one attack of his was three times the strength of the basic martial artist who was used as a reference.
However, this kind of result couldn't make the list. He just wanted to see how many times the strength of the person who left that terrifying mark was.

Chapter 1093
On the screen, there was a simple record of the rankings in the Sword Trial Hall.
If he remembered correctly, the previous record was set by Xue Muyang, thirty-two times!
Just from this point alone, Xue Muyang's strength should be more than ten times that of Gao Han. However, there were many variables in a cultivator's battle. Some cultivation methods were extremely powerful, but they might not be able to erupt with particularly powerful power.
Therefore, it was not certain that Xue Muyang was ten times stronger than Gao Han, but it was certain that Xue Muyang was much stronger than Gao Han.
