Just now, she had used all her strength to achieve a result of 82 times.
If it weren't for the fact that the result on the screen was firmly above her head, her result would have locked her in the first place for a long time.
"Looks like it's not Ai Anxin …"
"Then it can only be Luo Zheng?"
"I still can't believe it. Maybe it's someone else!"
Hearing the discussions, Ai Anxin pouted slightly. Her face showed that she was not convinced. She turned around and entered the Dream Space again.
Not long after, another result that was ranked second appeared on the screen. Eighty-four times. This result pushed Ai Anxin's previous result to third place. As for Xue Muyang's previous result, it was ranked fourth.
Ai Anxin entered the Dream Space again. This time, it was only 81 times.
86 times …
85 times …
80 times …
Seeing Ai Anxin's actions, many warriors were speechless at this time.
This way of flooding the rankings was simply too much!
Previously, Xue Muyang's result had been at the top of the list for a long time. All the results after that were brushed off under the screen.
Now, Ai Anxin was constantly brushing off the list. She had directly pushed all the results out. Except for the first place result, all the other results were left by Ai Anxin!
A result of more than 80 times was unattainable for most warriors in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. In the future, it would be almost impossible for other warriors to be on the list.
Many warriors were already extremely dissatisfied. However, thinking of Ai Anxin's monstrous strength, they could only grit their teeth and bear with it.
Ai Anxin brushed off the list again and again. When she exited the Dream Space for the last time, her face was pale. It seemed that she had used a secret method that exhausted her mind. This time, her result was only 96 times.
In the end, Ai Anxin could only choose to give up …
The Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace was abuzz with the news. However, Luo Zheng himself seemed to be uninvolved. He still maintained a very regular life.
Cultivating and observing the movement of the chaotic aura in his dantian.
The entire universe was as lively as ever. Occasionally, there would be creatures using the Roar Command to spread their voices throughout the universe.
In the past two days, there seemed to be a few more Celestial Masters fighting a war of words. In addition to the Celestial Masters of the Human Clan, there were also two or three Celestial Masters of the Demon Clan.
Luo Zheng naturally would not participate in the war of words between these Celestial Masters. However, after listening, Luo Zheng understood the reason for the war of words. It seemed to be because of a lost treasure. They were wrangling with each other …
After a period of time, the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace once again welcomed two groups of guests. As a result, new topics spread throughout the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
"The Spring Fragrance Pavilion and the Lingyan Pavilion came together …"
"It seems that they are going to take Luo Zheng and Ai Anxin in!"
"Sigh, these two people's lives are extraordinarily good!"
Envy was envy. If they could achieve the same results as Ai Anxin and Luo Zheng, they would naturally be valued by the two pavilions.
After joining the Lingyan Pavilion, they would be given a Lingyan Token. With this token, they could travel freely in the big worlds of the Alliance. No one could stop them. Moreover, as long as they were in the territory of the Alliance, they could get a lot of help by showing the Lingyan Token.
It was not easy for many warriors to travel through the big worlds of the Upper World. Traveling across several big worlds often meant expensive expenses. The passage between big worlds and the cross-realm passage could almost consume the life savings of an ordinary warrior. Luo Zheng still had many things to do. Since this token could solve so many problems, Luo Zheng naturally would not refuse.
After a period of time, Ai Hu, who had fought with Luo Zheng in the Torch Inheritance, finally arrived at the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
After he regained his freedom, he did not stop and went straight to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
It was also because the distance between the Great Worlds was unimaginably long that it took him almost two months to cross the Great Worlds to come here.
After reuniting with Ai Anxin and his sister, Ai Hu asked his sister if Luo Zheng was still in the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace.
Ai Anxin was curious why her brother was so concerned about Luo Zheng's whereabouts.
In her opinion, Luo Zheng was just her brother's defeated opponent.
However, Ai Hu only told her that she was only able to regain her freedom because Luo Zheng had shown mercy.
After hearing this, Ai Anxin was stunned. At this time, she recalled Luo Zheng's faint smile and understood a little. In her heart, she was completely convinced by Luo Zheng. Whether it was talent or strength, that guy really surpassed her in all aspects.

Chapter 1094
Ai Hu's visit surprised Luo Zheng.
The reason why he lost was because he felt that Ai Anxin's experience was similar to his.
However, the problem Luo Zheng was facing was even more troublesome. So far, he hadn't even seen Luo Yan's shadow. Even if he used the Roar Command, it didn't have any effect in the entire universe.
Ai Hu was also a forthright person. After visiting, his burly body bowed deeply to Luo Zheng.
Perhaps for Luo Zheng, it was an insignificant matter. However, for Ai Hu, the meaning of gaining freedom was extraordinary.
Back then, he offended the son of the Heavenly Sovereign and was sent to the Evil World. It was equivalent to cutting off his future. A lifespan of eight hundred years was too short for a cultivator. Now that he could gain freedom, it was an extremely fortunate thing for him.
Ai Anxin sat quietly to the side. Her mood was naturally very complicated.
From the moment she stepped into the Misty Cloud Palace, she regarded Luo Zheng as her opponent. She had provoked Luo Zheng time and time again, but this man didn't care. Instead, he helped her brother gain freedom.
"I came this time to reunite with Anxin. I also have an important matter to invite you!" Ai Hu said with cupped hands.
"Important matter?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of surprise. He originally thought that Ai Hu came to visit just to express his gratitude.
Ai Hu's eyes slightly flashed, and a trace of vigilance appeared on his face. He stretched out his hand and took out a light yellow divine inscription. After pouring his True Yuan into it, an invisible barrier spread out and enveloped the three of them.
Seeing Ai Hu so cautious, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of curiosity. What was so important that he needed to be so cautious?
Then Ai Hu cupped his hands and said, "If this matter was in the past, even if Ai Hu lost his life, he wouldn't easily reveal it. However, my benefactor is an exception."
"Please speak." Hearing this, Luo Zheng's curiosity grew even stronger.
Then he listened to Ai Hu's tireless explanation …
After Ai Hu was sent to the Evil World, he was in a very dangerous situation.
The Evil World was originally a very prosperous Great World, and it was one of the more established Great Worlds in the universe.
However, after hundreds of millions of years of development, the resources in the Wicked World were in a barren state. Some of the sects in the Wicked World had also moved away from this place. In the end, the entire Great World became a land of exile. Some of the martial artists who had committed crimes were banished to the Wicked World.
The entire Evil World was in extreme chaos. After all, most of the cultivators who were banished there were evil. There were no rules there. It was common for cultivators to kill each other in order to survive.
When he had just entered the Evil World, Ai Hu himself had also suffered a lot and had even almost lost his life inside.
It had to be said that Ai Hu's luck was not bad. While being chased again, he accidentally stumbled into a cave mansion and found clues to the secret treasures of the Yin Sifting World.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a curious look. "A secret treasure of the Gathering Yin World?"
"Yes!" Ai Hu's eyes revealed a glimmer of light. "So I want to invite my benefactor to explore it together!"
Faced with Ai Hu's invitation, Luo Zheng showed a hesitant look on his face. He didn't know what this artifact was, but seeing Ai Hu so nervous, it was clear this artifact was no trivial matter, so he couldn't make up his mind for a while.
At this moment, Xun's voice sounded. "Luo Zheng, agree to him."
"En? Why … "
"Agree to him first."
Since Xun said so, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. Xun rarely gave him advice, but since she had spoken, she must have her reasons.
There were not many people who could obtain Luo Zheng's complete trust, and Xun was naturally one of them.
Ai Hu naturally did not know what Luo Zheng was thinking. In Ai Hu's understanding, the secret treasure of the Yin Sifting World was of great importance. If it was revealed to anyone, even the Realm Lord would not have a reason to refuse.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng did not know much about the Upper Realm. When he heard about the secret treasure of the Yin Sifting World, he did not seem to be moved. Instead, because of Xun's words, he agreed to this matter.
Then, Ai Hu handed a map that he had drawn in advance to Luo Zheng. At the same time, he told Luo Zheng, "After half a year, we will meet in the Yin Sifting World at the location marked on the map."
Luo Zheng took the map and looked at it for a while before asking, "But where is this Yin Sifting World?"
Hearing this question that involved common sense, Ai Hu was slightly stunned. How could there be a warrior who did not know where the Yin Sifting World was? This was a little …
Ai Anxin, who was beside him, laughed out loud. "Brother, Luo Zheng is an ascender, and he ascended not long ago …"
However, after learning the location of the Yin Sifting World, a smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. He did not expect that the Yin Sifting World was actually adjacent to the World of Spirits, which was the great realm that Luo Zheng ascended from.
The World of Spirits was originally the border of the Three Thousand Great Realms of the Alliance, and the other side of the Yin Sifting World was no longer the territory of the Alliance.
With such a long distance, even if they continuously traveled across realms, it would probably take several months. Only now did Luo Zheng understand why Ai Hu suggested that they meet in the Yin Sifting World after half a year.
Since he had already agreed to it, Luo Zheng would not change his mind.
After Ai Hu and Ai Anxin left, Luo Zheng asked Xun, "Xun, what is this secret treasure of the Yin Sifting World? Why did you ask me to agree? "
At this time, the Sword Spirit gradually condensed in front of Luo Zheng. She smiled and replied, "This secret treasure of the Yin Sifting World … is an old story that has been covered in dust for hundreds of thousands of years. However, as the person involved, I happen to know some of its secrets, and some of them are quite important to you."
"I don't know if this cultivator called Ai Hu really knows how to open the secret treasure, but it's completely worth a try."
Hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng nodded silently.
He could not always stay in the Misty Cloud Palace to cultivate, not to mention that he promised Xun that he would help her return to the throne …
Half a year was too short for cultivators of the Upper Realms. Traveling through great realms basically took months. After some simple preparations, Luo Zheng bid farewell to Yun Luo. Yun Luo was not surprised by Luo Zheng's departure. Even the cultivators of the Thirteen Palaces would seek opportunities to go out and gain experience, not to mention that she had promised Luo Zheng that she would not restrict him too much.
Therefore, not long after, Luo Zheng quietly left the Misty Cloud Palace and entered the great realm passage.

Chapter 1095
Luo Zheng left on a Heavenly Luan Horse.
This Mythological Beast was a rare item even in the Upper Realm. Naturally, it was given to Luo Zheng by Yun Luo.
After Luo Zheng left, Yun Luo turned around and returned to the depths of her hall.
There was an obscure and complicated divine pattern engraved on a flight of stairs in the depths of the hall. After Yun Luo walked up the stairs and activated the divine pattern, a black figure gradually emerged.
"Father," Yun Luo called out faintly.
Yun Luo's father was one of the three Celestial Venerables in charge of the Alliance, Celestial Venerable Cloud Fox!
A Celestial Venerate was a supreme existence in the universe. Even Yun Luo would not disturb her father unless she had something important to do.
Celestial Venerable Cloud Fox looked indifferent. Even though he was talking to his daughter, there was no emotion in his voice. "That boy … left?"
Yun Luo nodded.
"I will do my best to help you with this earth-shattering plan of yours. However, there are no secrets under heaven. If you can think of it, so can they," Celestial Venerable Cloud Fox said.
However, Yun Luo smiled sweetly. "Of course, I know about this. Although not many people know about the Fateless, there are still quite a few. But even if they can think of it, so what? The fate of the Fateless cannot be predicted or figured out. This is a path that no one can predict. I'm just giving Luo Zheng a little push behind his back … Who can say for sure what will happen in the future? "
"That's right. This is the most difficult thing for schemers in the past to jump out of. Yun Luo, you're not very scheming, but you've done well this time," Celestial Venerable Cloud Fox said faintly.
"Thank you for your praise, Father." Yun Luo smiled sweetly.
"However, you must pay attention to this. Luo Zheng's heaven defying Karmic Luck is extremely rare, but the things he carries on his back are no small matter. For example, it's extremely difficult for him to help the Demon Night Clan's King of Slaughter reclaim his throne. It would be a pity if he were to perish just like that …."
"Yes, I understand!"
Father had always been a man of few words. Empyrean powerhouses were at the stage of severing their emotions, vainly attempting to comprehend a trace of divinity beneath the Heavenly Dao!
It was also because Yun Luo had severed his emotions that her father had not shown any emotion since she was born. Today, her father was still as cold as ever, but to be able to say so much to her and even show a trace of concern, Yun Luo was very happy.
Ever since he reported the news about Luo Zheng to his father, he had attracted the attention of Celestial Venerable Cloud Fox. His father was also very interested in Luo Zheng. This was the first time she had received her father's praise since she embarked on the path of martial arts …
Riding on a divine beast and traveling through the great realm passageways was quite eye-catching.
Countless warriors were hurrying along in the spacious world passageway. Every time they saw Luo Zheng's Heavenly Luan Horse, their faces would reveal a trace of envy. At the same time, they would silently speculate about Luo Zheng's identity.
A martial artist who was qualified to ride a Heavenly Luan Horse was not an ordinary person.
Along the way, Luo Zheng had a preliminary plan for his next step.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, helping Xun regain the throne was not something he could do alone.
Fortunately, Xun was not an isolated existence in the Upper World. Celestial Venerable Divine Arrow, for example, had stood firmly behind her. In addition to Celestial Venerable Divine Arrow, there were also some old subordinates who were hiding, waiting for their king to return.
In addition, Xun had hidden a large number of resources. Her current goal was to use these resources to help Luo Zheng rapidly improve his strength!
After reaching the Divine Sea Realm, Luo Zheng's cultivation would improve very slowly, and the resources he would consume would be a hundred times, or even a thousand times more than before!
This was not a matter of talent.
In the world of warriors, below the Divine Sea Realm, one could only cultivate one's foundation, which was not suitable to be accelerated by various kinds of elixirs and resources. Otherwise, the foundation would be unstable.
Therefore, whether it was Luo Zheng or other super geniuses, they all relied on their own talent to make breakthroughs in their cultivation, instead of accumulating resources.
However, after reaching the Divine Sea Realm, the improvement of a martial artist's cultivation was related to the Inner World!
From opening the Spirit Sea, expanding the Inner World, guiding the Divine Thunder into the body, cultivating a small world in the Spirit Sea …
This was not a problem that could be solved by talent.
In fact, this point began to manifest at the end of the Life and Death Realm. For example, if an ordinary martial artist did not have the Ten Direction Calamity Inducing Elixir, it was almost impossible to attract the tenth minor tribulation. Therefore, elixirs of that level were very precious in many Holy Lands. Even the martial artists of the 13 Heavenly Palaces could only rely on their own strength to fight for them in the inheritance of the Eternal Flame.
A world that competed with resources was even crueler for martial artists. This was also one of the reasons why it was difficult for ascenders to succeed. Although talent was important here, resources were also more and more important. Therefore, when Celestial Venerable Fantasy Sea ascended to the Celestial Venerable position, it was considered a miracle for ascenders!
Xun also knew some of the secrets of the Gathering Yin World's secret treasures. It could even be said that the origin of the Yin World's secret treasures was closely related to Xun. Therefore, when Luo Zheng was hesitant and vigilant, Xun immediately made Luo Zheng agree. This secret treasure was the first batch of resources she gave to Luo Zheng …
If Luo Zheng simply flew at his own speed, it would probably take him dozens of years or even longer to cross the realms.
However, this Luan Horse was originally a rare creature in the world. At such a high speed, it had already arrived at the edge of the realm occupied by the Alliance in a month …
"Zi Yi Realm …"
"Vast Realm …"
"Star Rain Realm …"
"Psychic Realm."
After crossing the Psychic Realm, it was the Gathering Yin World.
He did not expect that after traveling through so many realms in the Upper Realm, he would circle back to the Psychic Realm.
In the universe, many martial artists, even Divine Sea Realm martial artists, were not qualified to travel through a realm in their lifetime. However, Luo Zheng had traveled through more than a thousand realms and then circled back …
However, he still had to thank Yun Luo for this.
For a martial artist, the greatest difficulty in crossing realms lay in time and money. Crossing a great boundary could exhaust a martial artist's life savings, and the time it would take was calculated in decades. For an ordinary martial artist, this kind of consumption was simply unbearable.
However, Luo Zheng had the Lingyan Jade Token and the Luan Horse, so naturally, he was unimpeded.
As for Ai Hu, he promised to meet in the Gathering Yin World in half a year and to rush to the Gathering Yin World within half a year. He was also a very resourceful person.
Luo Zheng did not intend to stay in the Psychic Realm. This was just a place for him to ascend.
However, when he was flying in the cross-realm passageway, a voice suddenly sounded in his ears. "Luo Zheng, where are you? Can you help me …"
Hearing that voice, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. It was actually Mu Mingxue's voice …
Before leaving the lode, Mu Mingxue and Luo Zheng had exchanged voice-transmitting talismans.
Thinking about it later, it was indeed a very funny thing …
The universe was too big. It was just a realm, but its vastness was far beyond their imagination. The voice-transmitting talismans used in the Lower Realm could reach up to twenty to thirty thousand miles. The quality of the voice-transmitting talisman that Mu Mingxue exchanged with Luo Zheng was even higher, reaching up to sixty thousand miles.
In the Great Thousand World, sixty thousand miles was enough to cover a continent, and it could even transmit sound between two continents.
However, in the Upper Realm, this distance could only be regarded as a joke. When Luo Zheng was in the One Thought Seal Tower of the Torch Flame Inheritance, he had rushed over thirty thousand miles against the storm of laws …
Since the voice-transmitting talisman rang in his ear, it meant that the distance between him and Mu Mingxue was within sixty thousand miles. In the Upper Realm, this distance was almost equivalent to the distance between his home and the market.
He was not familiar with Mu Mingxue. In order to leave the lode, the two of them had a relationship of using each other. She was able to leave the lode one day earlier because of Luo Zheng. What happened later proved that even if Luo Zheng did not mine, someone would naturally take him away.
Mu Mingxue had burned a voice-transmitting talisman. It seemed that there was trouble?
He did not know when she had burned the talisman. Perhaps it had been several months, and the talisman had been echoing here.
At that time, she had said that one of her uncles was the City Lord of the Spirit Martial Holy Land. Presumably, her fate should be better than that of ordinary ascenders.
There was a slight hesitation on Luo Zheng's face, but he still gently patted the neck of the Heavenly Phoenix Horse. According to Professor Yun Luo's method, the Heavenly Phoenix Horse stopped, turned around, and left the Upper Realm passageway.

Chapter 1096
Riding the Heavenly Luan Horse greatly increased Luo Zheng's speed.
The agreement with Ai Hu was half a year later, and Luo Zheng had only spent a month, so Luo Zheng still had plenty of time.
Mu Mingxue was his acquaintance, and she had helped him a lot when he collected Zhen Yuanyu from the mine.
He wondered what kind of trouble she had encountered.
As they were leaving the cross-realm passageway, the soldiers guarding the passageway glanced at Luo Zheng's horse with a respectful look on their faces.
Using the cross-realm passage required a lot of True Origin Stones. Luo Zheng had planned to take out the Lingyan Jade Token, but he didn't expect that the guards would let him go without saying anything.
These guards were not stupid. A person who could ride a divine beast was not someone they could afford to offend. If the guy on the Heavenly Luan Horse had a bad temper, it would be a big problem for them. Therefore, no one dared to stop Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng left the Great Realm Passageway and entered the ordinary Teleportation Passageway, the armored soldiers looked at Luo Zheng's back and discussed in low voices.
"A Heavenly Luan Horse. Wow, it's very rare in our Border Great World."
"Hehe, even our Spirit Martial Holy Land's Saint Lord doesn't have the ability to get such a horse. I wonder who this person is?"
"Could he be the son of a Realm Lord? Most likely! But this person doesn't seem to have any evil aura, and he looks very amiable. "
What they didn't know was that Luo Zheng had ascended from here half a year ago. At that time, he was still an ascender from the lower dimensions, and he was even imprisoned in a mine by the Spirit Martial Holy Land. He had to dig out over a hundred True Origin Stones before he could regain his freedom …
After leaving the passage, Luo Zheng returned to the Spirit Martial Holy Land's territory.
Although the Spirit Martial Holy Land only occupied ten to twenty percent of the Spiritual World, it was still very large.
Outside the Transfer Passage was a large city. After he leaped down from the Heavenly Luan Horse, he sized up the surrounding environment. Trying to find a person in a city in the Higher Realm was undoubtedly like looking for a needle in a haystack.
Fortunately, he had exchanged voice transmission talismans with Mu Mingxue. However, the voice transmission talisman he gave to Mu Mingxue was only 20,000 miles away.
Without hesitation, he ignited the voice transmission talisman. "I've received your message. Mu Mingxue, where are you?"
There was no response from the other side …
"Is the distance not enough?"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng once again mounted the Heavenly Luan Horse. He patted the horse's neck and once again soared into the sky. This time, he was above the Lingwu Holy Land, where Ao Xiang was.
He tried to circle within a range of hundreds of thousands of kilometers, constantly changing his position and burning his voice transmission talisman several times …
On the sixth burn, Luo Zheng finally got a response!
Mu Mingxue's voice once again sounded in Luo Zheng's ears.
"Luo Zheng! You left the mine? "Mu Mingxue's voice carried a hint of surprise.
In fact, Mu Mingxue sent a voice transmission talisman to Luo Zheng out of helplessness. At that time, when Luo Zheng had accumulated 100 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, he did not choose to leave. Instead, he continued to stay in the mine to mine. Mu Mingxue wanted to seek refuge with her biological uncle. Naturally, she had no intention of staying in the mine to accumulate Zhen Yuanyu.
However, the mine was more than 60,000 miles away from Mu Mingxue. She did not expect Luo Zheng to receive her message!
She did not know that Luo Zheng had crossed more than 1,000 big worlds … It was a distance that she could not imagine. Many warriors in the Spirit Sea Realm would not be able to cross such a long distance in their lifetime!
Coincidentally, Luo Zheng's destination this time was the Yin Luo World. He traveled along the big world passage and unexpectedly received the message from her voice transmission talisman.
Since Luo Zheng had received Mu Mingxue's response, he could conclude that Mu Mingxue was less than 20,000 miles away from him. Within this 20,000 miles, there was only one big city called "Huatian City". Luo Zheng turned his horse around and went straight to Huatian City.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, it was a big city, but that was only relative to the big cities in the Central Domain.
In fact, there were 36,000 cities like Huatian City in the Spirit Martial Holy Land. Hall Master Shen and Hall Master He, who had taken Luo Zheng away from the mine, were in charge of thousands of cities like this …
After arriving at Huatian City, Luo Zheng rode the Heavenly Luan Horse and slowly descended. There was a no-fly restriction in Huatian City, so it was not suitable for him to ride the Heavenly Luan Horse directly into the city.
After landing on the ground, Luo Zheng took out his Spirit Beast Pouch and wanted to put the Heavenly Luan Horse into it …
The space in the Spirit Beast Pouch was not small, but what Luo Zheng did not expect was that the Heavenly Luan Horse seemed to be very resistant to the Spirit Beast Pouch. It raised its hooves and kept retreating. It was very resistant to being put into the Spirit Beast Pouch by Luo Zheng.
"Eh?"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression. The Heavenly Luan Horse had been very docile and obedient along the way. Why would it resist the Spirit Beast Pouch?
"Is it because of the Ancestor Dragon?" Xun's voice floated over.
After all, the Ancestor Dragon was a very special existence. Although it was very close to Luo Zheng, one could not ignore this little guy's momentum. Even though the Heavenly Luan Horse was also a divine beast, it was still instinctively afraid of the Ancestor Dragon. Understanding this, Luo Zheng could only choose to give up. He could not force this guy into the Spirit Beast Pouch, so he could only lead the Heavenly Luan Horse into the city.
Along the way, Mu Mingxue's voice was still constantly transmitted.
Although Mu Mingxue told Luo Zheng that she had encountered some trouble, she was very hesitant and did not want to explain clearly.
Therefore, Luo Zheng could only follow the direction that Mu Mingxue pointed out. Not long after, he saw a residence with a good environment. However, there were four Divine Sea Realm warriors guarding around this residence. Luo Zheng could feel that there were even several divine runes arranged around this residence. There was a faint power coming from that residence.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng understood almost instantly. Mu Mingxue was locked up here?
"What are you doing?" One of the warriors saw Luo Zheng approaching and scolded loudly.
These warriors' eyesight was not as good as that of the armored warriors in the passageway. Although they could feel that the Heavenly Luan Horse behind Luo Zheng was not an ordinary horse, the armored warriors guarding the passageway had a clear understanding of the vehicles used by the warriors coming and going every day. Therefore, they recognized the origin of the Heavenly Luan Horse.
These cultivators were guarding this place under the orders of the City Lord. With the City Lord's orders here, no matter who approached, they would naturally be reprimanded.
Hearing this voice, Luo Zheng slightly frowned and said, "I'm looking for Mu Mingxue."
The warriors looked at each other. Mu Mingxue was just an ascender from the Mortal World. Moreover, it had not been long since she ascended. How did this guy know her?
"There is no such person here. Leave quickly," the warrior said impatiently.
Luo Zheng pointed to the residence and said, "She is inside. She just sent me a True Essence Sound Transmission."
After their lies were exposed by Luo Zheng, the faces of the warriors became increasingly gloomy and cold. "Boy, there are some things that you should not meddle in. Otherwise, you will inevitably get yourself into trouble!"
Hearing this, the corners of Luo Zheng's mouth gently raised, revealing a smile. "I wonder how I can get myself into trouble?"
"The woman inside is a woman chosen by the City Lord of Hua Tian City. She is to be offered to the big shots of the Spirit Martial Holy Land. However, this woman seems to be somewhat disobedient, so she has been imprisoned here! Boy, did you hear that? Is the City Lord someone you can afford to offend? "Another warrior slanted his eyes and said arrogantly.
It was probably because Luo Zheng was leading the Heavenly Luan Horse. In addition, although Luo Zheng had just stepped into the Divine Sea Realm, his aura was extraordinary. Although the warriors could not recognize the Heavenly Luan Horse, they were not completely blind. Therefore, they gave Luo Zheng such an explanation.
If not for this, if it were another martial artist, the four of them would have already made a move!
"I see..."
Luo Zheng gently nodded. No wonder this Mu Mingxue asked for his help, but from the beginning to the end, she was so secretive. It seemed that it was not easy to ask.
Wasn't the City Lord of Hua Tian City Mu Mingxue's uncle? Why did his own niece have to be offered to someone else after she ascended?
"Boy, it's good that you know. Leave quickly. If you are late, I'm afraid you won't be able to leave!" Seeing Luo Zheng's thoughtful expression, the warrior thought that his words had worked, and a trace of pride appeared on his face.
At this moment, Mu Mingxue's figure appeared in the residence. After ascending, Mu Mingxue's cultivation did not seem to have made any progress. Instead, there was a trace of fatigue on her face.
"Luo Zheng... you've already broken through to the Divine Sea Realm?" After Mu Mingxue saw Luo Zheng, her eyes flashed a trace of a bitter smile.
In fact, Mu Mingxue did not have any expectations for Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng's performance in the mine was extraordinary, his strength was nothing in the Upper Realm.
However, after crossing countless distances and ascending to the Upper Realm to seek refuge with her uncle, she was finally offered by her uncle and became a cultivation vessel for a big shot. She had finally ascended to the Upper Realm. How could she be willing to become someone's cultivation vessel?
However, with her cultivation at the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm, she did not have the strength to go against her uncle!
In the end, she was imprisoned here, unable to go out...
What was more abominable was that the divine patterns in this residence even isolated the Yuan Qi of the world from the outside world. She could not even cultivate!
Thinking of this, Mu Mingxue's heart was filled with regret. If she had known, she would have continued to mine with Luo Zheng in the mine. After accumulating 200 pieces of Zhen Yuanyu, she would have been able to exchange them for the identity of an independent warrior. Although she did not have her uncle to rely on, at least she would be able to fly freely in this big world. She would not be imprisoned here and become a cultivation vessel for a big shot...
Therefore, when she saw Luo Zheng, her heart was filled with misery.
She never thought that the two of them would be in such a different situation.
She used the Sound Transmitting Talisman to ask for help from Luo Zheng because she had no other choice. In fact, she also knew that with Luo Zheng's strength, it was impossible for him to take her away.
Her uncle was the City Lord of Huatian City and his cultivation was at the late stage of the Divine Realm. Moreover, they were in the Lingwu Holy Place. Luo Zheng was just an insignificant ascender.
After seeing Mu Mingxue, Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "No wonder you wanted to ask me for help. I didn't expect that you would be imprisoned by your own uncle..."
"Don't talk about it. I used the Sound Transmitting Talisman because I had no other choice. You should leave." Mu Mingxue shook her head again. After seeing Luo Zheng, she calmed down and accepted her fate. This path was her own choice. Luo Zheng was not her relative. In the mine, the two of them were simply cooperating.
Moreover, even if Luo Zheng really wanted to save her, she was afraid that he would not have the strength to do so...
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng suddenly reached out to Mu Mingxue and said, "Come with me. It's okay."

Chapter 1097
No one dared to cause trouble here.
Hua Tian City was just an insignificant city in the Upper World, but it was backed by the entire Spiritual Martial Holy Land.
Therefore, not to mention Luo Zheng, who was only at the Divine Sea Realm, even Ultimate God Realm warriors, or those who were even stronger than Ultimate God Realm warriors, didn't dare to cause trouble here!
Because offending Hua Tian City meant offending the 36 Palaces, and offending the 36 Palaces meant offending the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, whose Holy Lord was as strong as a Realm Lord.
These warriors showed some mercy to Luo Zheng. Firstly, Luo Zheng's bearing was extraordinary. Secondly, the horse behind him was extraordinary. Presumably, this person in front of them had some background.
But now, Luo Zheng ignored their warning and dared to take Mu Mingxue away. This was wishful thinking.
"What do I mean? Don't you understand? "Luo Zheng replied with a smile. Then, he said to Mu Mingxue again," Come with me. "
Mu Mingxue was also stunned. She was already very touched that Luo Zheng was willing to come and see her.
Although Luo Zheng's cultivation had broken through to the Divine Sea Realm, there was no way he could take her away. This was Hua Tian City!
"Luo Zheng, go. I, I can't leave …" Mu Mingxue shook her head. She regretted burning that Sound Transmitting Charm to Luo Zheng. She didn't have any hope in the first place, but Luo Zheng was the only person she knew after she ascended. She had no choice but to burn that Sound Transmitting Charm. In her heart, she knew very well that it was better than nothing.
It was very likely that Luo Zheng couldn't receive her burning Voice-transmitting Talisman. However, coincidentally, Luo Zheng received her request for help when he left the lode … But what was the use of that?
The four warriors had already moved and surrounded Luo Zheng from four directions. Their faces were very unfriendly. A cold light flashed in the hands of the warrior in the lead. It was a bright silver short sword, and on the short sword, there were rays of black light. "I gave you a chance just now. Now that you want to leave, I'm afraid it won't be so easy!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the warrior in the lead suddenly attacked Luo Zheng.
These warriors weren't easy to deal with. They had already overestimated Luo Zheng by being able to endure for so long. They thought that Luo Zheng didn't know what was good for him. Under such circumstances, he still wanted to take away Mu Mingxue. There was no other explanation other than seeking death!
They were, after all, four warriors of the Divine Sea Realm. Once they attacked, they were as fast as lightning. The warrior in the lead was very close to Luo Zheng. The short sword shot out from such a distance, carrying rays of black light, towards Luo Zheng. There was almost no room for him to dodge!
Luo Zheng was still standing in the same place. In their eyes, Luo Zheng's figure just swayed slightly and then returned to the same place. That short sword directly pierced through Luo Zheng's body, but didn't cause any harm to him.
He still had a smile on his face, but a faint killing intent had spread out in his eyes.
The four warriors instantly understood that this guy who had just entered the Divine Sea Realm couldn't be underestimated!
"Kill him!"
The four of them surrounded Luo Zheng from all directions, leaving no room for him to maneuver.
Seeing this, Mu Mingxue's face was full of despair. However, it was useless for her to regret now. With Mu Mingxue's insight, she naturally knew how excellent Luo Zheng was. When he was in the Life and Death Realm, his strength was comparable to a warrior who had just entered the Divine Sea Realm like her. In the future, he would definitely achieve something.
At the very least, he would be qualified to become a Castellan of the Lingwu Holy Place in the future. However, she burned a Transmitting Sound Amulet and got Luo Zheng into trouble …
However, she only saw Luo Zheng slightly raise his hand and a long sword appeared in his hand. That long sword only swayed slightly. Suddenly, several gray figures appeared around Luo Zheng!
"Bang, bang, bang, bang …"
As soon as those crisp sounds entered Mu Mingxue's ears, the four warriors of the Divine Sea Realm flew out in four different directions. One of them even smashed into Mu Mingxue's residence!
The scene in front of her eyes happened in a flash. After a moment, the four warriors of the Divine Sea Realm were lying on the ground. It was unknown whether they were dead or alive.
Mu Mingxue's pupils suddenly widened. She stared at Luo Zheng who was not far away in a daze. That astonishment was frozen on her face.
Defeating four warriors of the Divine Sea Realm with one move?
Among the many ascenders, Mu Mingxue's strength could be considered above average. However, she didn't have the ability to fight against warriors from the Upper World. She didn't have the confidence to win against any of the four warriors of the Divine Sea Realm in front of her, not to mention that Luo Zheng fought against all four of them in an instant!
Luo Zheng's strength improved so much after breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm?
She couldn't believe the scene in front of her eyes …
However, when she came back to her senses, her face became even uglier. "Luo Zheng, you're in big trouble. Hurry up and leave. This Huatian City is the territory of the Spirit Martial Holy Land. No matter how strong you are, you can't be their opponent …"
However, not only did Luo Zheng not listen to Mu Mingxue's words, the long sword in his hand flashed out a few rays of sword light. Then, the restrictions in front of the residence suddenly exploded, emitting black smoke.
These restrictions were enough to restrain warriors of the Divine Sea Realm, but they couldn't stop Luo Zheng at all. He didn't even want to waste time to break the divine patterns behind the restrictions. He completely relied on brute force to destroy these restrictions …
After breaking these restrictions, Luo Zheng said indifferently, "Okay, let's leave this place together!
Mu Mingxue looked at Luo Zheng and then looked at the extraordinary horse behind Luo Zheng. Suddenly, a sense of trust rose in Luo Zheng …
The worst result for her to escape with Luo Zheng was that she would still be caught and become someone's cultivation vessel. However, if she escaped with Luo Zheng, she would have a chance to escape from the Spirit Martial Holy Land!
Although this chance was very slim, it was still a chance in the end!
She had already fallen into despair. In her heart, the Huatian City was a colossus. It wasn't something that a small ascender like her could fight against. Not to mention, behind the Huatian City was the Spirit Martial Holy Land. An ascender like her was just like an ant in there!
But now, Mu Mingxue's desperate state of mind was like a drop of sweet dew. A trace of confidence quietly recovered. She no longer had any hesitation or worry.
Then, Luo Zheng pulled her onto the Heavenly Luan Horse. As Luo Zheng gently patted the horse's neck, the Heavenly Luan Horse's hooves gently stepped on the ground. As if riding on the clouds, the Heavenly Luan Horse shot directly into the sky!
The Heavenly Luan Horse's speed wasn't particularly fast at first, but it only took two or three breaths of time to rush into the sky. When it reached a height of three thousand feet, a huge net suddenly appeared in the sky, blocking in front of Luo Zheng!
That net was like a heavenly net, covering the entire sky of the Huatian City.
"It's the Forbidden Sky Restriction …" Mu Mingxue reminded.
Facing this Forbidden Sky Restriction, Luo Zheng's expression didn't change at all. He waved the long sword in his hand again. The sword light was like a rippling wave.
"Dang, dang, dang …"
When Luo Zheng's sword light hit the net, it exploded with a crisp sound. This slash actually didn't destroy the Forbidden Sky Restriction, which made Luo Zheng's face reveal a trace of surprise.
Due to this slight delay, a vigorous and powerful laughter was transmitted from afar.
"Hahaha, this is really unprecedented. Someone actually dares to cause trouble in my Huatian City!" Not far away, more than ten rays of light flew over. The person in the lead was wrapped in a blood-red ray of light. That person was wearing a blood-red robe, and his eyes were emitting a fiendish aura. It seemed that he had cultivated some kind of extremely powerful evil technique!
Seeing that person fly over, Mu Mingxue, who was behind Luo Zheng, suddenly changed her expression. "Uncle, uncle …"
Hearing Mu Mingxue's voice, Luo Zheng sized up the warrior in the lead. His expression was very calm. "Divine Realm expert … He's your uncle?"
Mu Mingxue nodded. "Yes, he's also the City Lord of Huatian City …"

Chapter 1098
The scarlet-robed martial artist was a knowledgeable person.
At first glance, he was a little confused. However, after he sensed the Divine Beast bloodline in the Heavenly Luan Horse, he was certain that the young man was riding was an extremely precious Heavenly Luan Horse!
When a martial artist traveled through the Great World, it was far from enough to rely on his physical body to travel through the air.
Even for a Divine Realm martial artist, it would take a long time to travel through a Great World using the Space Tunnel.
If one wanted to move from one great world to another, one had to have an extremely high level of comprehension and be able to comprehend the Space Diversion Technique. Otherwise, one would have to borrow some top-grade treasures to fly. Other than that, some divine beast mounts were also quite effective.
For example, the Blue Smoke Bird and the Heavenly Luan Horse in front of him!
However, capturing a Divine Beast was already an extremely difficult task. If one wanted to suppress and tame a Divine Beast, the difficulty would be even more exaggerated!
The young man in front of him was riding a tamed Heavenly Luan Horse …
The scarlet-robed martial artist knew very well what a tamed Heavenly Luan Horse meant. It was something that even the Holy Lord of the Spiritual Martial Saint Land would be interested in.
If he took the Heavenly Luan Horse, the scarlet-robed martial artist would not take it for himself. It was not something that a person of his level could possess. However, if he offered the Heavenly Luan Horse, he would obtain unimaginable benefits.
At this thought, the scarlet-robed martial artist's greed grew uncontrollably in his heart like black vines.
However, the scarlet-robed martial artist was not a fool. The Heavenly Luan Horse could not have been captured from the wild. Even if an ordinary martial artist had the ability to capture it, it would be impossible for him to tame it.
Since the young man in front of him had a tamed Heavenly Luan Horse, his background was probably not ordinary.
Because of this, the scarlet-robed martial artist did not choose to attack directly. Instead, his expression became polite. "Mingxue, as your uncle, I have treated you well. Why do you want to escape?"
Since she had chosen to escape with Luo Zheng, Mu Mingxue knew that there was no turning back. Her expression turned cold. "Uncle, after I ascended, I traveled thousands of miles to seek refuge with you. However, you want to use me as a Human Cauldron. How can Mu Mingxue be willing to be someone else's Human Cauldron?"
The blood-robed cultivator tried his best to show an amiable smile. "Mingxue, why do you say that? Uncle has been working hard in the Upper Realm. The Upper Realm is much crueler than you can imagine. Being a furnace may not be a bad thing … I wonder who this young hero behind you is? "
The crimson-robed martial artist was not interested in Mu Mingxue's thoughts. His main purpose of saying these words was to find out the identity of the young man behind Mu Mingxue.
Luo Zheng's mind was as clear as a mirror. How could he not know what this scarlet-robed martial artist was thinking?
He smiled faintly and said, "Mu Mingxue and I ascended at the same time, and it was fate that brought us together. Your uncle is too vicious, he won't even spare his own niece."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the blood robed martial artist's heart skipped a beat!
He was actually an ascender?
The crimson-robed martial artist was also an ascender. Over the years, he had relied on his own hard work to become a City Lord of one of the thirty-six thousand cities in the Lingwu Holy Place. He was also considered a person with real power.
However, in the entire Lingwu Holy Place, he was not considered a big shot, but this did not diminish his ambition!
Since he was an ascender, this matter would be easy to handle …
Originally, he was only afraid of the power behind Luo Zheng. Now that he no longer had any scruples, the smile on his face gradually disappeared.
"Hehe, boy, naturally, I, as her biological uncle, will handle my niece's matters. As an ascender, I also want to warn you, don't meddle in other people's business in the Upper Realm, because meddling in other people's business will often lead to a considerable price!"
"What price?" Luo Zheng pretended not to know and asked.
"Pay with your life." The crimson-robed martial artist shook his head and sighed softly. However, he turned his back and waved gently at his several followers.
This crimson-robed martial artist had the cultivation of the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm. In his eyes, Luo Zheng, a little guy who had just entered the Divine Sea Realm, was not worthy of his attack. However, the six followers of the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm who followed him rushed forward!
"Puff, puff, puff, puff, puff …"
Just as the crimson-robed martial artist turned his back, he heard several muffled sounds coming from behind him. When he turned his head, he saw that his six followers were like kites with broken strings, smashing directly into Huatian City.
Luo Zheng still had a slightly ignorant smile on his face. He continued to ask the crimson-robed martial artist, "What price?"
Seeing this scene, the crimson-robed martial artist's eyes revealed a trace of surprise. With the cultivation of the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm, he killed six warriors of the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm in an instant? The original faint smile suddenly froze on his face. The strength of the boy in front of him had completely exceeded his estimation.
With the cultivation of the early stage of the Divine Sea Realm, he had such strength?
At this time, his gaze once again fell on the sword in Luo Zheng's hand.
After feeling the power of the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword, his eyes suddenly twitched! It was at least a Secondary First Grade Divine Weapon. No, it was very likely a First Grade Divine Weapon!
The crimson-robed martial artist had faintly sensed that Luo Zheng's identity was not ordinary. He had a Heavenly Luan Horse as his mount and a First Grade Divine Weapon in his hand! Even the core geniuses of the Lingwu Holy Place would not receive such treatment.
Was this guy really just an Ascender?
Mu Mingxue had just ascended not long ago. It was impossible for her to know any big shots. The possibility of this guy being an Ascender was very high. Moreover, he had just admitted it very frankly.
The question was, how could an ordinary Ascender have such strength? How could he have a First Grade Divine Weapon?
If the crimson-robed martial artist had calmed down and thought carefully, he might have stopped. The consequences of provoking someone he could not afford to provoke were obvious. He had lived for so many years. There was no reason for him not to understand this logic.
However, the sword in Luo Zheng's hand and the Heavenly Luan Horse behind him were constantly emitting a huge temptation.
Although the crimson-robed martial artist was the City Lord of Huatian City, any random treasure in Luo Zheng's hand was something that he could not obtain in his lifetime!
Killing people and seizing treasures were normal things in the world of martial artists. At this time, greed had already dominated his thoughts.
Sitting on the Heavenly Luan Horse, Mu Mingxue's eyes flashed with a strange light. From the first day she met this guy, he seemed to be constantly giving her surprises! She could not see through this guy's strength at all! However, when she thought of her uncle's ultimate Divine Realm cultivation, the light in her eyes dimmed a little. No matter how powerful Luo Zheng was, he would not be able to defeat her uncle.
The crimson-robed martial artist sized up Luo Zheng a few times and only lightly praised, "Interesting … but it's still not enough …"
Then, from the crimson-robed martial artist's body, a violent Evil Qi spread out like a tide. In the entire Huatian City, the weak martial artists and countless mortals were all trembling under this pressure. Most of them did not even dare to raise their heads.
The Life and Death Realm and Divine Sea Realm martial artists in Huatian City were quietly watching the battle in the sky from a corner.
"The City Lord of Huatian City actually personally took action …"
"His opponent seems to be a little guy who has just entered the Divine Sea Realm?"
"Don't underestimate that young man. He defeated six late-stage Divine Sea Realm martial artists in an instant. This guy can't be a core genius cultivated by some big Holy Land, right?"
A ball of fluctuating Blood Qi appeared in the crimson-robed martial artist's hand. The Blood Qi constantly curled up and gradually formed a blood-red spear!
The blood-red spear waved and pointed at Luo Zheng from afar. "You will soon know the price you will have to pay!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's expression also became serious. He did not speak again. This was the first time he faced an ultimate Divine Realm expert. The Thunder and Wind Divine Sword lightly flicked, and a bolt of lightning and a wind blade intertwined. At the same time, numerous Divine Shadows also surrounded the crimson-robed martial artist from different directions.
"Come on!"
The scarlet-robed martial artist roared, and the blood-red spear thrust out several times in an instant. Almost every spear thrust hit Luo Zheng's Serene Spirit Phantoms, shattering them in an instant!
Not only that, after breaking Luo Zheng's Divine Shadows, the crimson-robed martial artist's spear was like a dragon exploring the sea, heading straight for Luo Zheng.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1099
In the eyes of the scarlet-robed martial artist, Luo Zheng dared to attack him because he was overestimating himself. He was like a mantis trying to stop a chariot.
However, for Luo Zheng, this was a test of his own strength.
The strength of a Divine Realm expert had once again crossed a realm. In the lower realm, the strength of the seven Divine Realm experts of the Demi-Immortal Clan was something that Luo Zheng could only look up to. However, the scarlet-robed martial artist in front of him was just a City Lord of the Spirit Martial Holy Land. His strength should be on par with the seven Divine Realm experts of the Demi-Immortal Clan.
Seeing that his Nether God Shadow was instantly shattered, Luo Zheng's figure quickly retreated along the path of the Forbidden Sky Restriction.
"The strength of a Divine Realm expert is indeed more powerful than I imagined. This person is just a City Lord, so his talent shouldn't be that outstanding. However, the power of the blood spear is still beyond my expectations."
Luo Zheng kept thinking in his mind. Facing the sudden thrust of the blood red spear, Luo Zheng's long sword gently blocked. Numerous wind blades and lightning once again swept towards the scarlet-robed martial artist! However, the blood red spear was also very strange. In an instant, it transformed into numerous blood flowers, which blended with the Power of Law, and directly dissolved the wind blades and lightning.
"It's useless, kid. Among the Divine Sea Realm experts, your strength is indeed shocking. However, your understanding of the Power of Law is still very shallow … Hehe." After saying this, the scarlet-robed martial artist once again raised the blood red spear. In an instant, the Forbidden Sky Restriction of the entire Hua Tian City seemed to be linked with him in an instant!
The originally large black net, which was filled with Blood Qi, began to turn into a large dark red net. Under this large net, numerous hair-thin blood threads stretched out and constantly intertwined, forming a huge blood net cage, which trapped Luo Zheng and the scarlet-robed martial artist inside.
Mu Mingxue, who was sitting on the Heavenly Luan Horse, was also very nervous. Just now, Luo Zheng was forced to retreat again and again. Now that Luo Zheng was bound, there was no way out!
"It's over … Since you ascended at the same time as Mu Mingxue, I don't know how you obtained the first-grade divine artifact and the Heavenly Luan Horse in just one year. But that's not important anymore because everything you have will be mine!"
In this cage, the greed on the scarlet-robed cultivator's face gradually became more intense, and he no longer concealed it.
Luo Zheng just glanced at the scarlet-robed martial artist and didn't say anything. Instead, he put the long sword in front of him. After his pupils shrank slightly, his temperament suddenly changed dramatically.
That little change was completed in an instant …
The whole process took less than a breath's time. Almost before the scarlet-robed cultivator could react, Luo Zheng's sword had already stabbed out.
At this moment, the scarlet-robed cultivator waved his long spear gently. Endless blood flowers bloomed on his body and piled up on Luo Zheng. In his view, the Power of Law that Luo Zheng had stimulated was not worth mentioning at all. As long as he kept melting it, he could easily dissolve it, and then he could take this boy's life with a single strike!
But what he never expected was that Luo Zheng's seemingly casual sword strike was very different from before!
He felt a power that he could not understand!
It was just a very ordinary sword strike!
Even the Power of Law was not stimulated, only a narrow and sharp sword intent.
But it was this seemingly ordinary sword strike that contained a sword momentum that made him feel confused.
At this moment, he felt as if Luo Zheng wasn't a living being, but a piece of stone, a piece of stone without any life force.
But how could a stone wield a sword?
How could he stab out such a terrifying sword strike?
The most important thing was that this guy did not have any accumulation of power at all. This sudden stab seemed like a casual stab, but it made the scarlet-robed cultivator feel an unstoppable power.
Yi Shen's Sword …
The legendary sword strike that could injure a True God. Although Luo Zheng's performance was not perfect, the sword momentum contained within was already terrifying enough.
The terrifying part of the Yi Shen's Sword was not only its invincible power, but more importantly, compared to other killer moves, this sword strike did not require any accumulation of power at all. As long as it was completely comprehended, it could be used at will. Under the stimulation of the sword intent, it could destroy the world with a raise of one's hand.
The scarlet-robed cultivator was not anxious from the beginning to the end. In his view, Luo Zheng had shown great strength, but under this prohibition, coupled with his Dark Blood Skill, this Luo Zheng could only be a plaything.
But this reversal only happened in an instant!
Just as he was about to torture Luo Zheng bit by bit and let this kid understand the price he had to pay, this guy suddenly stabbed out with his sword.
When the confusion in his heart disappeared, he found that he had been completely penetrated by Luo Zheng's sword. Then, his entire body turned into pieces and began to rapidly dissipate.
The first to dissipate was his torso, and the last to dissipate was his head …
Even before his head was completely shattered, Luo Zheng's eyes were still indifferent as he asked, "Tell me, what price do I have to pay?"
The scarlet-robed cultivator's head unwillingly glared at Luo Zheng, and then began to break into pieces, slowly dissipating between heaven and earth …
"How could there be such a monstrous existence … One sword strike, and my main body was destroyed! Ah ah ah! "
At the same time, in the depths of the City Lord's Mansion of Huatian City, in a secret room filled with the smell of blood, a ball of blood essence floated in the air and transformed into a human figure, roaring towards the sky! This scarlet-robed cultivator cultivated the "Dark Blood Art", which could create the Blood Soul Body!
In the future, he could use this Blood Soul Body to condense a second body that was exactly the same as his own.
However, his main body had already fallen before he could complete his Blood Soul Body. This was a huge blow to him!
The blood man formed by the blood essence roared crazily. Suddenly, he seemed to have discovered something, and a surprised expression appeared on his bloody face. "Palace Lord! The Palace Lord seems to be flying towards my Huatian City. I will inform him of this news. After all, there is still a chance! "
His main body was accidentally killed. For the City Lord of Huatian City, it was a huge loss. However, if the Palace Lord was willing to protect him, this loss could be easily made up for!
A Palace Lord controlled a thousand cities. In the eyes of the City Lord of Huatian City, the Palace Lord was a high and mighty existence. He was also an extremely important figure in the Spiritual Martial Holy Land. After the blood man had completely absorbed the blood in the blood pool, he rushed out of the secret room.
The power that erupted from Luo Zheng's sword was too strong. It easily pierced through the body of the scarlet-robed cultivator and easily destroyed the Forbidden Sky Seal. The sword intent extended to an extremely far distance, spanning a distance of tens of thousands of miles. In the end, it was gradually dissipated by the sword force condensed by Luo Zheng.
Even after it dissipated, the power of the sword force was still felt by many experts.
All the experts within a radius of several hundred thousand kilometers all sensed the presence that erupted from the sword at the same time. The city lords of over a hundred cities all left their manors and raised their heads to look at the sword that had pierced through from the west. They all became extremely stern.
Under normal circumstances, there would not be any major disputes in the Spiritual Martial Holy Land. After all, ordinary warriors would not dare to fight within the Spiritual Martial Holy Land. However, who was the one fighting just now? Only when a Divine Realm expert went all out would they be able to erupt with such power.
Many City Lords felt the remaining power of the sword and rushed towards Huatian City. The fastest and the first to arrive was a Palace Lord of the Spiritual Martial Holy Land.
This Palace Lord's surname was Shen. He was an old acquaintance of Luo Zheng. When Luo Zheng left the mine, it was Palace Lord Shen and another Palace Lord He who led Luo Zheng all the way to the Holy City.

Chapter 1100
Just now, Luo Zheng's sword attack had indeed alarmed Hall Master Shen.
It wasn't that Luo Zheng's sword attack was very powerful in Hall Master Shen's eyes. In fact, because they were too far away, Hall Master Shen didn't see Luo Zheng's sword attack.
He sensed an extremely unique concept from that might!
Therefore, after feeling that power, he hurriedly searched for it and came all the way to Hua Tian City.
Just as he arrived at Hua Tian City, he saw a bloody man rushing towards him.
Hall Master Shen's eyes faintly glanced at him and frowned slightly. "Mu Jinglun? Why do you look like this? "
Hall Master Shen was in charge of thousands of cities, but only thirty or so of them were controlled by ascenders. Mu Jinglun was one of the city lords he was more familiar with.
"Hall, Hall Master … My physical body was destroyed by a brat!" Hua Tian City Lord said sadly, but then his eyes flashed with a ray of hope. "But Hall Master, that brat has precious treasures on him. He shouldn't have escaped from my Hua Tian City yet!"
"Oh?" Hall Master Shen's face revealed a trace of confusion. How could someone with precious treasures dare to cause trouble in the Spiritual Martial Holy Land?
"Hall Master, I'll take you there now!" Hua Tian City Lord was leading the way. Now that his physical body was destroyed, it was impossible for him to covet Luo Zheng's Heavenly Luan Horse and the first-grade divine weapon. However, if he gave these treasures to Hall Master Shen first, he believed that Hall Master Shen would help him.
Luo Zheng's sword attack had killed Hua Tian City Lord and then broken the entire Hua Tian City's restriction on flying. Mu Mingxue, who was sitting on the Heavenly Luan Horse to the side, was stunned speechless.
What had Luo Zheng experienced in this one year?
Could it be that the resources in the Upper Realm were so abundant? He had only wandered in the Spiritual Martial Holy Land for less than a year, but he was able to raise his strength to such a level?
This was absolutely impossible. The moment this thought arose in Mu Mingxue's mind, she immediately dismissed it …
Because she had sought refuge in Huatian City, Mu Mingxue indeed did not have the opportunity to properly understand the Upper Realm. However, her uncle's talent was not bad. At least, he was now a City Lord. However, after staying in the Upper Realm for so many years, he was still killed by Luo Zheng in one move. Just from this point alone, Mu Mingxue's guess was denied.
But how did Luo Zheng improve his strength so quickly? Mu Mingxue couldn't figure it out.
She couldn't imagine that Luo Zheng had already traversed more than a thousand Great Worlds and circled around before returning to this place …
Luo Zheng was not very satisfied with the sword that his opponent had just slashed out. There was even a somewhat dispirited expression on his face. Although this Sword of God Yi did not require any accumulation of power, he still needed to adjust his state of mind. The moment he slashed out that sword, Luo Zheng tried to completely seal off his seven emotions and six sensory pleasures.
It was easier said than done. No matter how hard he tried, the moment he slashed out that sword, his state of mind was still somewhat shaken.
Luo Zheng himself also speculated that if he used a stone to perform this sword, he might be able to achieve the perfect effect. Unfortunately, he was not a stone, nor was he a true god-like existence. After all, he was a living person!
"Let's go." Luo Zheng smiled faintly and prepared to leave with Mu Mingxue.
Mu Mingxue nodded slightly, but her gaze towards Luo Zheng clearly carried a trace of reverence.
When she had just ascended, Mu Mingxue's heart was still quite superior. After all, compared to other ascenders who had no one to rely on, she still had an uncle in the Lingwu Holy Land. It could be considered that she had someone to rely on.
Now that she thought about it, she shouldn't have rushed to seek refuge with her uncle. In comparison, Luo Zheng was actually more reliable.
However, now that Mu Mingxue understood, she already had this kind of reverence towards Luo Zheng. When facing Luo Zheng, she couldn't be so casual …
However, at this moment, another two streaks of light flew over from the horizon.
Seeing the people who were flying over, Mu Mingxue's slightly relaxed heart suddenly tensed up again!
This was because she saw that the bloody figure in front of her was actually her uncle. As for the cultivator following behind him, she couldn't see through his cultivation at all. As long as one wasn't a fool, they would know what was going on in this situation!
"What should I do …" Subconsciously, Mu Mingxue had already regarded Luo Zheng as her pillar of support.
Luo Zheng's gaze swept over lightly, but there was a faint smile on his face. Probably because he felt Mu Mingxue's nervousness, he said, "It's okay, I seem to have met an acquaintance."
Hall Master Shen followed the Essence Blood body of Huatian City's City Lord and quickly rushed to the west side of Huatian City. He was originally very confused, but when he arrived at his destination, his eyebrows suddenly raised and he looked up and down at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's appearance didn't change at all, but after breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm, Luo Zheng's cultivation realm had advanced by leaps and bounds. His temperament had also improved, which made Hall Master Shen a little confused.
Moreover, Luo Zheng should have been taken away by the Palace Lord of Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. Why did he return to the Lingwu Holy Land alone? So for a moment, Hall Master Shen was a little uncertain!
"Hall Master, it's this brat. He has a First Grade Divine Artifact and a Heavenly Luan Horse as his mount," Huatian City's City Lord said in a low voice, "And the woman beside him is the Heavenly Origin Body that I have arranged for you. She is an excellent cultivation vessel for a cultivator …"
Hearing her uncle's words, Mu Mingxue was also anxious and angry. Her uncle had asked her to become a cultivation vessel for some important person. It seemed that the important person her uncle was talking about was this person in front of her. Mu Mingxue felt extremely sad in her heart!
And this person's cultivation was far stronger than her uncle's. She didn't know if Luo Zheng could still deal with him … Although Luo Zheng's performance just now was shocking, but no matter how monstrous a cultivator was, there was still a limit. Crossing a major realm to defeat her uncle was already shocking enough. Luo Zheng couldn't cross two realms to defeat this person in front of her, right?
What Mu Mingxue never expected was that after that person sized up Luo Zheng a few times, his face was filled with smiles. He cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng and said, "Luo Zheng, long time no see."
Seeing Hall Master Shen, Luo Zheng only nodded slightly, "Hall Master Shen, long time no see."
Under the two's intimate greeting, Mu Mingxue and Mu Jinglun were both stunned. But after the shock, Mu Mingxue revealed a smile with a relaxed expression on her face.
But Mu Jinglun's expression at this time was as ugly as it could be!
What kind of person was Hall Master Shen? When had he ever shown them, City Lords, any respect?
But he was so polite to this young man in front of them. Even if one used their buttocks to think, the current situation was very unfavorable to them …
Hall Master Shen sized up Luo Zheng, his face still somewhat doubtful. He asked, "Young hero Luo Zheng, I heard from the Holy Lord that you were brought to the Thirteen Palaces, right? Why did you return to the Spirit Martial Sacred Land in such a short time? "
This Luo Zheng was someone that even the Holy Lord thought highly of, so Hall Master Shen was naturally very polite.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "I just happened to pass by this place. I didn't expect that a friend who ascended with me back then would be imprisoned by my uncle. He said that he was going to treat her as some important person's cultivation vessel!"
Cultivation vessel …
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Hall Master Shen suddenly felt cold sweat break out on his back!
Hall Master Shen didn't know that Luo Zheng had already joined the Lingyan Pavilion, nor did he know what happened to Luo Zheng in the Thirteen Palaces. After all, with Hall Master Shen's status, he didn't have the qualifications to join the Thirteen Palaces.
But Luo Zheng's status and background was something that Hall Master Shen couldn't afford to offend. Even the Holy Lord of the Spirit Martial Sacred Land would treat the Palace Lord of the Misty Cloud Palace with the highest level of respect. Even the Great Realm Lord of the Spirit Martial Sacred Land had to personally greet him!
And he was just one of the thirty-six Hall Masters of the Spirit Martial Sacred Land.
A single word from the Holy Lord could consign him to eternal damnation.
And when Hall Master Shen saw that Luo Zheng was familiar with Mu Mingxue, although he wasn't sure of the relationship between Luo Zheng and that girl, it still made Hall Master Shen's heart tremble!
Give her to me as a cultivation vessel? Isn't that the same as killing me?
This Luo Zheng was personally taken away by Yun Luo. Although he hadn't seen Yun Luo in person and didn't have the qualifications to do so, the daughter of the Heavenly Venerables of the Alliance, the Palace Lord of the Misty Cloud Palace, only needed a glance to kill him a hundred times if she wanted his life!
Hall Master Shen smiled and nodded at Luo Zheng. "Young hero Luo Zheng, please don't mention the matter of the cultivation vessel again. It was my fault for disciplining my subordinates, so I'll apologize to you first." After speaking, he glanced indifferently at Mu Jinglun and said, "This person is extremely sinister and doesn't even recognize his own family. How can such a person be qualified for the position of Spirit Martial Sacred Land's City Lord?"
At this time, Mu Jinglun's face was filled with terror. That extremely bad premonition of his had now become reality!
Thinking of this, Mu Jinglun actually turned around and fled. He originally had a body of essence blood, so relying on it to escape was extremely fast.
